Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.17

Chapter 1601
"Hahaha, brat Luo Zheng, come again!"
The smile on Old Man Feng's old face became increasingly bright. The movements of his hands became faster and faster, and the astral energy became increasingly dense!
The astral energy he used had a great origin. It was called the Astral God Energy and was a transcendent divine might of the Demi-Immortal race. Although he was casually using it, its strength was still terrifying.
At this time, Old Man Feng's eyes were filled with seriousness. His hand turned into a claw and the astral energy became incomparably sharp. The sound that erupted in the air also became shrill, as if a strong wind was blowing through the valley!
Si si si …
The astral energy was like countless tiny black snakes that swept towards Luo Zheng!
"80% of his strength?" Seeing these snakes of astral energy, Empyrean Azure Sky's eyes widened.
"It's 100%! The Old Patriarch is already going all-out. If this Luo Zheng can really receive it, then that's too … "Empyrean Mirage shook his head, as if he thought the Old Patriarch was going too far.
The Old Patriarch's strength was obvious to all. He was an existence on par with Original Sin, Divine Mandate, Thunder Punishment, and other Mandate of Heaven, perhaps even surpassing them!
However, the fierceness of an Empyrean lay in the Mandate of Heaven that they inherited!
At this time, the Old Patriarch hadn't used his own Mandate of Heaven, but even if he only used his own martial skill, his strength still couldn't be underestimated. Even an ordinary Empyrean would have to use their full strength to deal with this!
But standing across from the Old Patriarch was Luo Zheng. He had just stepped into the World Lord realm. Could he really receive it?
The astral energy rushed towards Luo Zheng, baring its fangs and brandishing its claws. It was like a black snake slithering through the darkness.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. The nine stars in his dantian had already begun to slowly revolve.
In truth, the Old Patriarch's strength wasn't much in Luo Zheng's eyes. Although the Old Patriarch was an Empyrean, in terms of pure strength, within the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, he should be similar to the Mountaincrumbling Clan's Thunder Toad!
Using the strength of the nine stars was only a precaution!
When those small snakes formed by the Gang-strength shot towards Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng still stretched out his right hand, with his index finger pointed forward and his thumb slightly raised!
The moment the small snake at the front rushed over, Luo Zheng's hand shook slightly, and the small snake's head was directly caught in his hand. He grasped the position very precisely, and then he squeezed lightly!
"Crack!"
In Luo Zheng's hands, the small black society formed by the Supreme Force is broken inch by inch.
Immediately, Luo Zheng's right hand turned into a shadow, waving in front of him. Although there were many small snakes, as long as they touched Luo Zheng's finger, they would be broken in an instant!
"Ka ka ka ka ka …"
A series of cracking sounds could be heard.
The Celestial Venerables could see the ingenuity of Luo Zheng's technique. Whether it was speed, power, or even the angle he grasped, it was flawless.
However, Ning Yudie and Mu Mingxue couldn't see it at all. The speed was too fast.
They only saw a black gust of wind floating in front of Luo Zheng, and Luo Zheng's hand reached into that gust of wind, constantly waving it, as if he wanted to use his hand to disperse the gust of wind in front of him.
Just as they thought, as Luo Zheng's hand waved faster and faster, the color of the gust of wind also gradually faded, from a thick black color to a light black color, and finally completely disappeared in front of the two women's eyes …
In fact, during this process, Luo Zheng's right hand crushed thousands of small black snakes formed by the Gang-strength!
After Luo Zheng crushed the last small black snake formed by the Gang-strength, he took two steps back, a faint smile on his face, and cupped his hands toward the old patriarch.
Old man Feng's expression was extremely shocked, and after a while, he faintly sighed. "From the information he comprehended from my bloodline, the Lord of Dayan asked me to do my best to nurture the ten Dao Children born between heaven and earth, and then send them to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement …"
"Only these ten Daoists have the qualifications to save this world. Of course, I won't go against the wishes of the Lord of Dayan, but I think that the Lord of Dayan's way of thinking is a little naive." Old man Feng continued, "Counting on the young Daoists to grow up is a matter of time. How can they fight against the Sacred Race?"
The goal of the Demi-Immortal Clan was to protect the entire world from the Sacred Race's invasion.
This was also one of Luo Xiao's methods of self-preservation. The key to Luo Xiao's series of arrangements lay with Luo Zheng and the other nine Dao Children …
The old patriarch naturally understood this point, but he also understood that completely placing all of his hopes on the Dao Children was a completely unreliable thing to do.
Therefore, in the past few years, when the Sacred Race began to invade the world on a large scale, two different ideas had emerged in the Demi-Immortal Clan.
Nearly half of the martial artists requested to actively prepare for war, and not place any hopes on the Dao Children.
Before the Dao Children entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, their cultivation was only at the Extreme Divine Realm. How could they be expected to return to the world in a short three years and save the world?
However, there was another group of people who chose to believe in old man Feng's arrangements. After all, this was a plan from the Lord of Dayan, and the Lord of Dayan was ultimately the Saint who had built the world.
His strategies and arrangements were naturally not something ordinary people could understand.
Although the old patriarch clearly supported the Lord of Dayan, he was still rather worried. Fortunately, Lord Startail stood on the old patriarch's side without hesitation, and let the old patriarch act according to Luo Xiao's plan.
Although there weren't many people in this group, almost all of them were the top group of people in the Demi-Immortal Clan. Therefore, no matter how quickly the Sacred Race occupied the world and how arrogant they were in the world, the Demi-Immortal Clan remained biding their time.
Now, the old patriarch finally understood that there was no problem with Luo Xiao's arrangements …
Their vision was still too shallow. They had never thought about how terrifying a Saint's power was, or how terrifying his inheritance would be.
Now, the old patriarch deeply experienced it.
Luo Zheng had walked around the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, and by the time he returned, he had undergone earth-shaking changes.
Just now, Luo Zheng still hadn't fully displayed his strength in that test!
The test for the Dao Children had already been arranged before the Dao Children returned.
The Demi-Immortal Clan needed to understand the Dao Children's strength, soul, and True Essence after they returned …
And in the first test, Luo Zheng passed the old patriarch's test in an unfathomable manner. Or rather, he surpassed the old patriarch.
"The Lord of Dayan's gaze has long since fallen on a place we can't see." Old Feng smiled faintly. "Actually, my test ended long ago. Now, it's time for Celestial Mirage to act, but I still want to try!"
Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback. He didn't know what old man Feng wanted to do.
The other Celestial Venerables also looked surprised, but didn't say anything.
A golden light flashed between old man Feng's brows, and a profound power of law dissipated from the top of his head.
There were 128 Heavenly Wills in the world, and the laws contained within them were often strange and bizarre.
There were also differences in strength between the laws.
For example, the Resurrected Celestial Venerable of the Demi-Immortal Clan wasn't very strong. His combat strength was only equivalent to an ordinary lower Celestial Venerable. Some of the peak Great Realm Lords could even fight him to a standstill!
In terms of combat strength, he was considered a weaker Celestial Venerable.
However, the Resurrected Celestial Venerable was one of the top Celestial Venerables. He was second only to old man Feng, Celestial Venerable of Original Sin, Celestial Venerable of Divine Mandate, and Celestial Venerable of Thunder Punishment.
The reason was that the Heavenly Will he inherited was extremely heaven-defying!
No matter how heavily injured a warrior was, even if they were already dead, the Resurrected Celestial Venerable could still bring them back to life.
This Heavenly Will of Rebirth controlled the lives of all living beings. It was also because of the Resurrected Celestial Venerable that the Demi-Immortal Clan could use this method to continuously obtain the Heavenly Will crown.
The old patriarch was able to become an upper Celestial Venerable because the Resurrected Celestial Venerable killed him 13 times. After selecting the 13 Heavenly Wills, he finally obtained the strongest Heavenly Will of Rebirth in the universe, the supreme law of karma!
"Celestial Venerable Qian Lan, take the two girls away." At this moment, Old Feng's aura surged around him, and the entire space became chaotic. It was as if he was preparing an extremely powerful technique. He was afraid that it would affect the two girls behind Luo Zheng.
"Patriarch, you plan to …" Celestial Venerable Huan Miao revealed a shocked expression.
The fact that they weren't able to test the limits of Luo Zheng's strength made the group of Celestial Venerables somewhat unhappy.
In the end, this was a good thing. The stronger Luo Zheng was, the more useful he would be in their future plans.
However, Luo Zheng's performance wasn't just strong. It was unfathomable. He had strength that even Celestial Venerables couldn't measure. It sounded a bit ridiculous.

Chapter 1602
Old Man Feng didn't answer. The golden radiance between his eyebrows was becoming more and more intense, like a shining star.
When the Heavenly Venerables saw that Old Man Feng was planning to use the Mandate of Heaven, they felt that it was absurd. However, they knew that persuasion was useless, so they could only let the old patriarch do as he pleased …
Whoosh!
Venerable Qian Lan's figure flashed and appeared behind Luo Zheng. He smiled faintly at Ning Yudie and Mu Mingxue. "The two of you, please step back for now …"
After saying that, a triangular barrier appeared in Venerable Qian Lan's hand and instantly enveloped Ning Yudie and the others.
Ning Yudie looked at the power that surged out of Old Man Feng's body and asked worriedly, "Husband, will he be alright?"
"Don't worry, the old patriarch naturally knows when to stop." Venerable Qian Lan smiled.
In fact, Venerable Qian Lan himself wasn't confident. Originally, it was just a simple test. He didn't expect that it would develop into such a situation where the old patriarch had to use his trump card!
After Venerable Qian Lan answered, he reached out and lightly tapped. The triangular barrier spun for a while and began to slowly rise.
The two women stood in the barrier and saw that they were getting further and further away from Luo Zheng. In the end, they almost floated to the top of Soaring Sky Mountain. At this distance, they could only see a small gray dot …
"Is there a need to send us so far?" Ning Yudie asked with a frown.
The Heavenly Venerables sent them out because they were afraid that they would be affected by the aftershocks.
Only by being so far away could they be considered safe. What was Old Man Feng planning to do? Was he going to kill Luo Zheng?
Thinking about it, it wasn't very likely. Moreover, there were so many Heavenly Venerables present. If he really wanted to kill Luo Zheng, he didn't need to care about the two women at all. He could just kill them all …
Besides them, the other Heavenly Venerables also retreated for the time being, leaving only Luo Zheng and Old Man Feng in the hall.
"Luo Zheng, if you can't handle it, I will naturally stop. Don't force yourself!" After Old Man Feng warned, his body suddenly shook, and an even more ferocious aura was suddenly unleashed!
Then …
A lotus flower suddenly appeared in the sky.
The thirty-six leaves of the lotus bloomed and spun slowly above his head!
"Thirty-six Petals Lotus Flower, this …" Seeing the lotus flower, Luo Zheng was stunned.
Generally, lotus flowers would only appear when martial artists were transcending heavenly tribulation.
At this moment, no one was breaking through to the next realm, let alone undergoing tribulation!
Why would a lotus flower appear out of nowhere?
Old Man Feng stared at Luo Zheng and said: "My Lord's Divine Dao is the Great Creation Divine Dao, and he has already comprehended the most important part of the Great Creation Divine Dao, the Great Creation Lotus! The Heaven's Will that I bear is the foundation of the universe!"
The process of a Saint constructing a universe to form a stable universe was rather complicated, and the methods used were also rather particular.
For example, he would first construct countless Greater Worlds. Following the evolution of these Greater Worlds, he would gradually construct the Upper Realm. Then, he would look at the fence between the Upper Realm and the Lower Realm and construct the Ascension Passage between the two realms.
There were so many Ascension Passages from the Lower Realm to the Upper Realm that even Heavenly Venerables could not construct. All the Ascension Passages were personally constructed by Saints.
After constructing the universe's five viscera and six bowels, he would need to cover the entire Heavenly Dao!
That was his supreme comprehension of the world!
The Heavenly Dao contained all sorts of Heaven's Wills, and they were all the Lord of Great Creation's lifetime comprehensions. Among them, the most important Heaven's Will was used to seal this universe!
This Heaven's Will was naturally the supreme Heaven's Will of the universe. It was also the profound meaning created by Luo Xiao after comprehending the Great Creation Divine Dao, the Great Creation Lotus!
After using the Great Creation Lotus to seal the entire universe, if others wanted to surpass this Heaven's Will, they would naturally need to comprehend the Mahayana Lotus Flower to be qualified!
Back then, Old Man Feng had been killed by the Resurrection Heavenly Venerate thirteen times in a row, and he had been resurrected thirteen times, bearing thirteen Heaven's Wills …
Among them, three of the Heaven's Wills were not bad! At the very least, they could help him become a top-notch Intermediate Heavenly Venerate!
The thirteenth Heaven's Will was the worst. It was a teleportation Heaven's Will. This Heaven's Will allowed its wielder to instantly appear anywhere in the universe. It was a pretty good supplementary Heaven's Will, but it did not meet Old Man Feng's requirements.
Old Man Feng gritted his teeth and after dying for the fourteenth time, he welcomed the Heaven's Will, the Great Creation Lotus!
It was a law above all the Heaven's Wills in the Heavenly Dao, a law of karma.
Under Old Man Feng's control, the lotus slowly pressed down on Luo Zheng!
"Whoosh!"
There was a terrifying power hidden within the lotus flower.
Heavenly Venerate Qianlan and Heavenly Venerate Huan Miao looked worried when they saw the lotus.
The Great Creation Lotus was the ultimate technique of a Saint!
Old Man Feng was qualified to use it because he had inherited this ultimate technique …
How could Luo Zheng resist it?
According to Old Man Feng, Luo Zheng would need to use pure strength to resist. Wasn't that a bit too much?
They knew that the old patriarch was stubborn, but they didn't expect him to be so stubborn in front of Luo Zheng.
After the old patriarch inherited this Heaven's Will, he had only made a move once!
At that time, the Demi-Immortal Tribe had just risen to power and had a conflict with the Demon Race.
In the Great Creation Universe, ordinary conflicts would not draw the attention of Heavenly Venerates. However, during that conflict with the Demon Race, two Heavenly Venerates of the Demi-Immortal Tribe had made a move and killed dozens of Extreme Divinity Realm martial artists!
At that time, the Demon Race was not the only one who didn't take the Demi-Immortal Tribe seriously. The other factions didn't take them seriously either.
The old patriarch had not been a Heavenly Venerate for long and didn't have much of a reputation in the universe. No one thought of him as an Upper Heavenly Venerate. It seemed …
After all, after so many years in the universe, there were only a handful of publicly recognized Upper Heavenly Venerates. Something like the Heaven's Will could only be chanced upon and not sought. No one thought that the Demi-Immortal Tribe would use the resurrection method of the Reborn Heaven's Will to sift out such a Heaven's Will for the old patriarch!
Several days later, the old patriarch represented the Demi-Immortal Tribe in action.
When that lotus flower descended, the entire Demon Race was shaken!
With the methods of the Great Creation Lotus Flower, the old patriarch defeated two demon Empyreans in a single move, severely wounding them!
In addition, he killed 19 demon World Lords and three Great World Lords …
The Demon Race had paid a great price. Fortunately, the old patriarch had held back and didn't kill the two demon Empyreans. As for Empyrean Demondawn and the others, they didn't even dare to let out a fart during this time …
After this battle, the several great influences of the universe had a whole new level of respect for the Demi-Immortal Tribe.
This rapidly rising race might seem a bit strange and mysterious, but the strength they hid behind the scenes caused the other influences to feel a deep dread.
Old Man Feng naturally didn't want to fight Luo Zheng right now. The reason he was so stubborn was because he wanted to see just what Luo Zheng's limits were!
After the Dao Children returned, the Demi-Immortal Tribe would definitely make new arrangements for the next step of the Dao Children. Each Dao Child would shoulder a great responsibility and they might even participate in the Demi-Immortal Tribe's God Slaying Plan. Thus, the evaluation of each Dao Child's strength was extremely important.
Hu hu hu …
The lotus flower slowly descended, pressing down on Luo Zheng.
"The strength that little friend Luo Zheng has displayed is extraordinary. I wonder if you have the strength to lift up my lotus flower!" The old patriarch faintly smiled.
The Great Creation Lotus Flower could be considered Saint Jiu's own comprehension of the Divine Dao. It was similar to the Ice Soul Devil Flame that Saint Jiu had comprehended; it was a special skill of a Saint!
The old patriarch's lotus flower was far inferior to Luo Xiao's; there was simply no comparison.
However, in this universe, it was already close to the limit of power that a martial artist could cultivate. If one wanted to go further, they would have to break the laws and surpass the Heavenly Dao.
In other words, only a True God could surpass the old patriarch's Great Creation Lotus Flower.
The lotus flower continued to descend, pressing down heavily on Luo Zheng's head.
Luo Zheng's expression became serious again. He crossed his arms, blocking the lotus flower above him, holding it at the bottom!
"Power …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly felt the heaviness of the lotus flower.
This heaviness couldn't be compared to the heaviness of the Chiliocosm Greatsword. If the Chiliocosm Greatsword was as heavy as a chiliocosm, then this lotus flower would be as heavy as ten or even a hundred …
Beneath this terrifying pressure, there was an irresistible force.
"Crackle …"
The stone beneath Luo Zheng's feet instantly shattered, and his feet sank deep into the ground.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1603
The old patriarch had never fully released the power within, so the world didn't know much about the Mandate of Heaven that the old patriarch bore.
This lotus flower seemed to contain an unimaginable power.
Under the pressure of the lotus flower, Luo Zheng's body was sinking rapidly.
Facing the old patriarch's pressure, Luo Zheng didn't move. The power in his body was also mobilized. It was like seawater flowing in reverse, crazily flowing through every one of his meridians!
The nine stars in the world within his body spun faster. The power source within the stars turned into threads and continuously flowed into Luo Zheng's meridians.
After experiencing the Craftsman Saint's body tempering, Luo Zheng's physical strength had increased greatly. However, even with the current strength of Luo Zheng's body, it couldn't contain all the power source within the nine stars.
At the current stage, Luo Zheng could only bear about thirty percent of the power source. This was already the limit of Luo Zheng's body. If more than thirty percent of the power source was applied to Luo Zheng's body, his body would also be on the verge of collapse!
In order to resist the slowly descending lotus flower, Luo Zheng extracted more and more power source. The stars spun faster and faster!
Ten percent …
Twenty percent …
Because the power was rapidly flowing into Luo Zheng's body, his body began to emit the sound of bones crisscrossing. His body had been tempered with steel, and the strength of his bones had already surpassed that of a supreme artifact. The sound of his bones crisscrossing was like a mountain sliding, violent and ferocious!
The lotus flower stopped its downward momentum. In the end, it was still stopped by Luo Zheng!
"He really stopped it …" Heavenly Sovereign Huan Miao, who wasn't far away, was extremely speechless when she saw this scene.
"What kind of power does this kid have? This is a Sage's technique. No one below the level of a true god should be able to stop it!" Heavenly Sovereign Qian Lan exclaimed.
Old Man Feng's eyes flashed. He gently pushed with both hands and extended two index fingers, but they were pointing downwards slightly.
"Ka ka …"
The lotus flower, which was forcibly stopped by Luo Zheng, began to descend abruptly.
"So heavy!"
Luo Zheng instantly felt the weight of his hands increase by a lot!
This Old Man Feng's Mandate of Heaven was indeed miraculous. A single lotus could actually transform into such a heavy object. It was countless times heavier than the Mountain Crumbling Race's "Golden Fork Strange Rock"!
Seeing this heavy object bearing down on him, Luo Zheng had no choice but to draw out the power source from his inner world!
"Thirty percent!"
Thirty percent of the power of nine stars was the limit that Luo Zheng could bear.
As this power surged out, the meridians on Luo Zheng's body coiled around like earthworms. These meridians were as tough as dragon tendons, and after bulging, they were as thick as a thumb. It was a terrifying sight to behold!
"Pop pop pop …"
The skin between the bones crackled, and the space squeezed out between the gaps of the bones squeezed each other, bursting out with a sound similar to the sound of insects.
At this moment, Luo Zheng seemed to have turned into a human-shaped musical instrument. Every part of his body was making strange sounds …
"Rise!"
Luo Zheng flipped his hand, and the lotus flower, which was originally pressing down on him, was forcibly pushed up by Luo Zheng!
"Ah …"
Celestial Lord Huan Miao's mouth was wide open.
Celestial Lord Qian Lan also had a similar expression.
These Celestial Lords were all old monsters who had lived for tens of millions of years, or even hundreds of millions of years. After countless years, it was very rare to see such an expression on their faces. In their long years, they had seen all sorts of new things in this world. What was there to be shocked about?
Such an expression had not appeared on their faces for countless years …
However, the surrounding Celestial Lords all had the same expression.
As for Old Man Feng himself, he could only watch as the lotus flower was lifted up by Luo Zheng!
He noticed that Luo Zheng's physical body seemed to have reached the limit of what it could bear, and there were faint signs of it collapsing. However, this might not be the limit of Luo Zheng's strength. This brat seemed to still have some strength left!
But it was almost there.
"Forget it." The Old Patriarch waved his hand slightly, and the lotus flower flew out of Luo Zheng's hand. It spun non-stop and soon disappeared into the air. He sighed. "Today … I was too stubborn. However, Luo Zheng, your performance was indeed a pleasant surprise!"
After the Old Patriarch withdrew the lotus flower, Luo Zheng smiled and said, "Old Patriarch praised me instantly. I was already at my limit. Fortunately, Old Patriarch held back. Otherwise, I would have made a fool of myself."
The Old Patriarch glanced indifferently at Luo Zheng and chuckled. "This brat is not telling the truth. This old man is curious, but he knows his limits …"
In fact, Luo Zheng was also unable to measure his own limits. If a third of a Saint's strength was used at full strength, he could not be sure of how powerful it would be.
Moreover, strength was only one aspect of a martial artist's strength. Moreover, when this strength was used purely or when it was used with a certain cultivation technique, the effect would be very different.
This kind of limit could only be considered in battle.
After the Old Patriarch's strength test, it was Heavenly Lord Huan Miao's turn.
At this moment, Heavenly Lord Huan Miao was already mentally prepared.
He was testing the strength of Luo Zheng's soul, and there were no tricks in this kind of test.
Heavenly Lord Huan Miao's figure floated down and arrived in front of Luo Zheng. His eyes met with Luo Zheng's, and the Golden Battle Soul in his body was like a lurking vicious beast, suddenly releasing a strong pressure.
A martial artist's soul was also a very important aspect.
Strength, True Essence, soul …
When two martial artists fought, it was very likely to be a three-in-one attack.
For example, he could use his True Essence to attack his opponent and then find an opportunity to approach his opponent and engage in a physical battle. If these two methods were still unable to deal with his opponent, he could use a soul attack to break the deadlock.
However, Heavenly Lord Huan Miao's test only lasted for a few breaths of time. His majestic soul pressure covered the area. Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a golden light, easily resisting Heavenly Lord Huan Miao's soul pressure. His expression was extremely calm.
"Hula …"
Heavenly Lord Huan Miao's soul pressure disappeared without a trace.
Then, Heavenly Lord Huan Miao's face revealed an expression of not knowing whether to laugh or cry. "Luo Zheng, you already have a Golden Battle Soul?"
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. He did not deny Heavenly Lord Huan Miao's speculation.
"Then … there's no need to test me." Heavenly Lord Huan Miao shrugged. He was not as stubborn as Old Man Feng.
As for True Essence …
The Demi-Immortal Clan still knew Luo Zheng's background. His cultivation method was special, far beyond the scope of ordinary martial artists. There was no need to test him.
Thus, today's small test for Luo Zheng came to an end.
In fact, the test for the Dao Children was just a short test. It was only because Luo Zheng's performance was too shocking that it caused such a big commotion.
Heavenly Lord Qian Lan waved his hand, and Ning Yudie and Mu Mingxue, who were temporarily hiding on the mountain peak, descended and returned to Luo Zheng's side.
At that moment, the Resurrected Heavenly Lord, who had been silent all this time, suddenly spoke. "Since Luo Zheng already has such strength, I have a suggestion."
The Resurrected Heavenly Lord had a somewhat reclusive personality and was usually a man of few words. Now that he had spoken, everyone's eyes naturally gathered on him.
"What suggestion?" the old patriarch asked faintly.
"Help Luo Zheng carry a Mandate of Heaven!" The Resurrected Heavenly Lord stared at Luo Zheng and said.
"A Mandate of Heaven?" The old patriarch was slightly taken aback.
The Resurrected Heavenly Lord nodded and smiled. "Luo Zheng does not have a Mandate of Heaven, but his strength is already comparable to a Heavenly Lord. If he is selected to carry a Mandate of Heaven, how strong will he be?"
Hearing this, the faces of the several Empyreans were immediately moved.
Ever since the Human Alliance had taken action against the Sacred Race and lost three Heavenly Lords, the Human Race had fallen into a passive state.
At the realm lord level, the Human Race was unable to resist the Sacred Race. At the Heavenly Venerate level, the Human Race had far fewer Heavenly Venerates than the Sacred Race. After all, there were 128 Heavenly Venerates in the Great Yan Universe, and the Human Race only occupied slightly more than 50 seats. The only optimistic ones were the old patriarch, Heavenly Lord of Thunder Punishment, Heavenly Lord of Oracles, and Heavenly Lord of Original Sin. These four upper Heavenly Venerates were all part of the Human Race.
However, the Great Yan Universe had more Mandates of Heaven than the Great Yan Universe to begin with, and all the Heavenly Venerates were members of the Sacred Race. Therefore, the Sacred Race still had an overwhelming advantage in terms of Heavenly Venerates.
The only ones in the Human Race that could match the Sacred Race were the four peak Heavenly Venerates.
If Luo Zheng carried the Mandate of Heaven and became a Heavenly Venerate, he would be a great help in a short period of time.
"No!"
The old patriarch immediately rejected the Resurrected Heavenly Lord of Resurrected's suggestion.

Chapter 1604
What the Resurrected Exalted Celestial was thinking about now was the current crisis.
But the old Patriarch knew that Luo Zheng was far too important in Sage Luo Xiao's plans. It was impossible to confine Luo Zheng to the universe.
Once a martial artist shouldered the Mandate of Heaven and became an Empyrean, there was no longer any possibility of jumping out of the universe!
Then Luo Zheng would lose the qualifications to enter the Realm of the Gods …
Now, in order to deal with the crisis of the saints, Luo Zheng had to shoulder the Mandate of Heaven, allowing Luo Zheng to become an Empyrean.
Not to mention whether or not this could really save the universe, even if Luo Zheng played a huge role and drove the saints back to the Great Pole Universe, that would only solve the current crisis!
The Sage Halls of the Realm of the Gods would never let Luo Xiao go.
Now, Mu Haiji was making trouble for the Great Pole Universe. There was no guarantee that in the future, there would be other Sages breaking through the Wall of Sighs and attacking the Great Pole Universe. This would be endless. Even if Luo Zheng had three heads and six arms, he still wouldn't be able to block the methods of the Sages.
Hearing the old Patriarch's words, the Resurrected Celestial fell silent.
Giving Luo Zheng the Mandate of Heaven was indeed a path that wouldn't work.
Once the Mandate of Heaven was shouldered, it would leave behind a brand. This brand would be deeply engraved into the soul, impossible to get rid of. One would also lose the qualifications to become a True Divinity.
"We will discuss this matter later!" The old Patriarch smiled.
Just as his voice fell, a voice floated up from not too far away. "I have a suggestion!"
"Lord Startail!"
"Lord Startail!"
"…"
Hearing that voice, the Empyreans also became respectful and reverent.
Not too far away, a white figure floated over. This old man wore a white robe and was untainted by even a speck of dust. It was as if the dust that approached him would automatically retreat. This was the only True Divinity of the Demi-Immortal Clan, Startail.
When Luo Zheng saw Startail, he bowed and called out, "Lord Startail."
When Luo Zheng first entered the Demi-Immortal Clan, he didn't understand their goal. He even had some suspicions in his heart and had a strong resistance towards them.
But now, when he saw Startail, there was a different thought in his mind.
This True God Xingwei … was Luo Xiao's disciple, his father's disciple!
If not for the presence of these Celestial Venerables, Luo Zheng really wanted to ask him what kind of person his father was.
Luo Zheng's impression of his father since he was a child had gradually become blurred over the years, but his father's stern voice still lingered in his ears.
A dignified Saint had transformed into an incarnation in his body, turning into a loving father to teach him.
Now, Luo Zheng had two completely different views of this "father". On the one hand, Luo Xiao was a high and mighty saint. On the other hand, he was a loving father with flesh and blood. Which one was his real father? Luo Zheng couldn't explain it clearly either …
Xingwei glanced at Luo Zheng and said, "Luo Zheng, do you still remember the Destined Crown from back then?"
"The Crown of Destiny …"
Upon hearing Xingwei's words, Luo Zheng immediately nodded.
Back then, Luo Zheng had alarmed so many Celestial Venerables in the universe because of this Destined Crown.
However, he hadn't inherited the Destined Crown. After all, the lowest requirement to inherit the Destined Crown was to step into the realm of Sector Lords. At the time, Luo Zheng's realm hadn't been high enough.
"Sir Xingwei, you're talking about the Destined Crown!" The old patriarch's eyes lit up.
The Destined Crown in Luo Zheng's hands had been passed down from the previous evolution cycle. In other words, it was the 129th Destined Crown in the universe!
The Crown of Heaven's Will was still in Luo Zheng's immortal abode …
Xingwei nodded and said faintly, "When the previous evolution cycle was destroyed, a Celestial Venerate wanted to escape the great extinction of the universe. Just as he was about to fail and die, I saved his life and preserved his Destined Crown."
A Celestial Venerate's lifespan was limited. A long-lived Celestial Venerate could live up to 300 million years, but they still couldn't withstand the erosion of time and would eventually die one day.
Xingwei's goal in saving that Celestial Venerate was to preserve the Destined Crown. This crown was prepared for Luo Zheng!
"That Celestial Venerate's life! You saved him? "Luo Zheng was stunned.
Xingwei nodded.
When Luo Zheng had entered the Wheel of Samsara, he'd been rather curious.
The evolution cycle that occurred every 3.6 billion years was accompanied by the collapse of the Heavenly Dao and the extinction of all life …
From Celestial Venerates to mortals, no living being could escape.
Why had that Celestial Venerate survived by hiding in the Wheel of Samsara?
Refined Shenzi had also survived along with that Celestial Venerate. But because Refined Shenzi was a puppet, he couldn't be considered a living being. Thus, he had survived the previous cycle of the Heavenly Dao until now. But in the end, that Celestial Venerate had fallen into the Celestial Decay and perished.
"The Heavenly Dao that Destined Crown belonged to has already been shattered and reformed into the current Heavenly Dao. You can already bear the Destined Crown and won't be branded. It also won't affect you when you cross the Heavenly Dao in the future. There's no need to worry." Lord Xingwei smiled.
Hearing this, everyone present, including Luo Zheng, couldn't help but sigh. Luo Xiao's plan was extremely meticulous. This plan had likely started tens of billions of years ago and had taken into account almost every detail!
The only thing that had been miscalculated was when Mu Haiji attacked the Great Evolution Universe.
The time of the Sacred Race's invasion had been brought forward by nearly 300 years.
A few hundred years sounded like a lot of errors, but when compared to a span of tens of billions of years, it was already quite accurate!
Luo Zheng also revealed a deep interest in the Destined Crown. He smiled at Lord Xingwei and said, "Then I'll go and take the Destined Crown now!"
After thinking for a moment, Luo Zheng said to the Old Patriarch, "My friends are still in the Immortal Mansion. They aren't able to adapt to the origin energy pressure of the upper realms. Is there any way for them to adapt?"
The Old Patriarch nodded. "This is a trivial matter. Qianlan, go and help Luo Zheng …"
Then, Luo Zheng was led by Venerable Qianlan to the Immortal Mansion.
After waiting at the entrance of the Immortal Mansion for a while, a group of Demi-Immortal Clan martial artists flew over. The martial artist in the lead held a small purple and gold bottle in his hand.
The group of martial artists found it strange. The thing in the bottle was only for martial artists in the lower realms to adapt to the origin energy of the world. Why did they need to personally deliver it to the Empyrean?
Venerable Qianlan reached out and took the bottle, handing it to Luo Zheng. He said indifferently, "This bottle contains the Dust Cleansing Pill. The pressure of the world's origin energy requires martial artists to adapt to it. Our Demi-Immortal Clan is unable to change it. However, the Dust Cleansing Pill can neutralize the surrounding origin energy of the world. After eating this pill, they will be able to travel in the upper realms without hindrance for a period of time."
"How long will this Dust Cleansing Pill last?" Luo Zheng asked.
Venerable Qianlan smiled faintly. "This Dust Cleansing Pill is a high-grade pill. It should last for about 30 years …"
Luo Zheng thought to himself. It would already be good if this pill could last for dozens of hours. When he heard that it could last for 30 years, he was speechless.
As expected of a Demi-Immortal Clan, he took the Dust Cleansing Pill and stepped into the Immortal Mansion.
Luo Nian, Granny Yu, and many other people in the Cloud Hall had been waiting for a long time.
They had been cooped up in the Immortal Mansion for several years. Although the environment in the Immortal Mansion was good, it was still like a prison to them.
Luo Zheng took out the Dust Cleansing Pill and stuffed it into everyone's hands. After everyone swallowed it, Luo Zheng went into the Immortal Mansion and took out the Crown of Destiny.
The moment Luo Zheng's hand touched the Crown of Destiny, the entire Heavenly Dao shook slightly!
The Empyreans of the Demi-Immortal Clan were fine. They already knew that it was Luo Zheng who had touched the Crown of Destiny. However, the other Empyreans in the universe looked at the sky in surprise. The Heavenly Dao was shaking again …
Luo Zheng felt somewhat helpless about this shaking. The Crown of Destiny had a trace of connection with the Heavenly Dao. The slightest carelessness would cause it to shake endlessly.
After taking out the Crown of Destiny, Luo Zheng left the Immortal Mansion with Luo Nian and the others.
Luo Nian was bored to death in the Immortal Mansion. He could only use his innate talent to decipher the golden Sanskrit characters. However, he was only an 11-year-old child. He was full of curiosity about this world.

Chapter 1605
Seeing Luo Nian's excited appearance, Luo Zheng felt a little ashamed in his heart.
He wasn't a dutiful father, and he'd never even properly accompanied him. But in his heart, he made up his mind to take Luo Nian on a trip.
After handing the Crown of Destiny to Xingwei, Xingwei said that the Crown of Destiny still needed some time to be processed.
As for the old patriarch, he ordered people to arrange a residence for Luo Zheng and the people of Cloud Hall ….
After these trivial matters were taken care of and everyone was settled, Luo Zheng didn't rush to discuss the next step. Instead, he set off with Luo Nian and Ning Yudie.
"One Greater Thousand World as a region!"
"One thousand regions as a Greater World!"
"This Greater World is truly vast and boundless!"
Sitting on Luo Zheng's shoulder, Luo Nian's young voice let out a cry of surprise.
In the distance, huge mountains stretched out like sleeping elephants, exuding a majestic aura between heaven and earth. Just looking at them made one feel heroic.
"One thousand regions as a Greater World? Who told you that? "Luo Zheng asked.
"It's in a book!" Luo Nian replied with a smile.
"Is it a book from the library?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Mm!"
Luo Nian continued, "With a million Greater Worlds as our feet, a hundred thousand Greater Worlds as our hands, the stars in the heavens as our internal organs, the stars as our breathing, forming a whole …."
"What's the name of this book?" Although Luo Nian's voice was childish, the content was extraordinary. Luo Zheng seemed to understand something from it.
"Summary of the World Structure," Luo Nian answered truthfully.
It wasn't hard to understand from the book's title. Naturally, it was about what one saw and heard while constructing the Greater World ….
Constructing a Greater World and stabilizing it above the borders of the Divine Realm was originally an extremely complicated process.
This book should have been recorded by Master Gu Bei during the process of constructing his Greater World.
That library was truly a treasure trove. Unfortunately, Luo Zheng hadn't read all the ancient books inside. It was a bit of a waste to leave the library to Luo Zheng.
Little Luo Nian was intoxicated by it, but he had gained a lot.
"As a father, you're really ignorant in front of your son," Ning Yudie said with a gentle smile on her face. Luo Nian's tone was like a little adult's, which made her feel proud. This little guy who used to give her a headache had finally begun to show his talent.
Luo Zheng chuckled. It was no big deal to lose to his son. As a father, he also felt comforted.
Half a day later, after passing through this stretch of mountain range, they came face to face with a majestic city that was as bustling as the capital …
Luo Zheng originally wanted to take Luo Nian to take a detour. He wanted to take Luo Nian to see more magnificent scenery. However, Luo Nian grabbed Luo Zheng's hair and pointed to the magnificent city. "Father, I want to go into the city!"
"Okay." Luo Zheng naturally agreed to such a small request.
The couple led Luo Nian into the majestic city …
There were tens of millions of mortals in the majestic city.
Even though the Sacred Clan was shouting and killing every day, the vast majority of the common people still went on with their lives. They weren't interested in the roars of the Sacred Clan. What they cared about was their daily lives.
A considerable number of them didn't even know about the existence of the Demi-Immortal Clan. They only knew that the place they lived in was called the Majestic City. Outside the Majestic City was the horizon. If you wanted to go to the horizon, you had to use a transmission channel. But 99% of the mortals had never used a transmission channel in their lives.
Therefore, to them, a city was their entire world.
Because they were small, it was easier to be happy.
After Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie concealed their cultivation, they carried little Luo Nian and slowly walked down the street.
The peddlers' cries reached their ears, and a burst of fragrant aroma assailed their nostrils. There were those selling osmanthus cakes, those selling hundred flower dumplings, and those selling golden cloud swallows …
Seeing these snacks, Luo Nian's little face was extremely excited.
Since childhood, his meals had been personally customized by Ning Yudie. Together with Grandma Yu's care, the Cloud Hall had collected all kinds of treasures to make them. Although these treasures had allowed Luo Nian to have a physique that far surpassed that of his peers, their taste was nothing to be proud of.
Seeing these snacks, Luo Nian naturally thought they were delicacies of the world.
"Dad, I want to eat this, this, and this …" Luo Nian's fingers constantly danced on the snacks, his cheeks flushed red. In his eyes, these were all delicacies.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. "Buy all of these …"
"Eating too much of these grains is not good for the body." Ning Yudie wasn't willing to let Luo Nian go.
Martial artists paid particular attention to the purity of their bodies, so after reaching the Xiantian realm, the requirements for food were very high. There were too many impurities in grains, and after swallowing them, they would settle down in the body. For Ning Yudie and Luo Zheng, this was naturally fine. But Luo Nian's cultivation was still shallow, and he hadn't yet fasted, so eating too much wasn't good for him.
Seeing Luo Nian's little mouth curl up, Luo Zheng waved his hand and smiled. "These impurities can't trouble a father. Little Die's worries are unnecessary. Nian 'er can eat to his heart's content today …"
"No." Ning Yudie might have been very arrogant and indulgent towards Luo Nian in the Cloud Hall, but in this aspect, she was very strict.
Luo Zheng stood in front of Ning Yudie and patted Luo Nian's butt. Luo Nian made a face at Ning Yudie, then rushed over like a gust of wind.
Seeing Luo Zheng's pampering, Ning Yudie was also quite helpless. She could only glare at her husband with hidden bitterness.
When it was time for the peddlers to settle the bill, they all stretched out their hands to Luo Zheng. "Guest, eight coins of silver!"
"Guest, this apricot cake is four coins of silver …"
"…"
Seeing the peddlers stretch out their hands, Luo Zheng was slightly surprised, and his face suddenly revealed an embarrassed expression.
Where was he going to find the silver?
Not only did he not have silver, he didn't even have gold.
Gold and silver were the most widely circulated currencies in the hands of mortals. The currencies between martial artists were True Origin Stones and Zhen Yuanyu.
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, the peddlers also felt quite strange.
Looking at Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie, one had an imposing appearance and extraordinary temperament, the other was an outstanding beauty. Ordinary people could tell at a glance that these two were not ordinary people. It couldn't be that they couldn't take out a few taels of silver, right?
The peddlers didn't dare to say anything, and only waited for Luo Zheng to settle the bill. But Luo Zheng really didn't know what to use to settle the bill. It couldn't be that he had to take out a piece of Zhen Yuanyu, right? This piece of Zhen Yuanyu was probably enough to buy a hundred streets in the city.
Luo Nian didn't care about this, and had already buried his head in eating.
"Xiao Die …" Luo Zheng could only cast a pleading gaze towards Ning Yudie.
Ning Yudie, on the other hand, turned her head away with a faint smile on her face, but didn't respond.
"Xiao Die." Luo Zheng pulled on the corner of her clothes. He was a Sector Lord now, he couldn't possibly run away for a few taels of silver, right? That was too shameful …
Seeing her husband so embarrassed, Ning Yudie's heart softened. She rolled her eyes at Luo Zheng, and then touched her spatial ring.
Actually, Ning Yudie wouldn't carry such vulgar things as gold and silver with her. But women always carried many small accessories, and some of them were made of gold. The moment she took out a gold hairpin, she melted it into a small gold bead, and handed it to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng saw this handful of gold beads, and let out a sigh of relief. This could be considered a solution, but he didn't expect that when he handed over this handful of gold beads, it would scare the peddlers.
"Dear customer, you don't need so much …"
"These are extremely pure gold beads, it's enough to buy this shop of mine. Moreover, I don't have change …"
"Eight taels of silver is enough, eight taels of silver. Do you have money, dear customer?"
Hearing the peddlers' words, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a headache coming on. With a slight shake of his hand, he controlled his strength perfectly, and the gold beads fell into the pockets of the peddlers. Luo Zheng hugged Luo Nian, and said, "Keep the change." Then, he flew away.
Luo Nian held on tightly to the snacks in his hand, completely ignoring the surrounding movements. This child's greatest ability was his focus …
Ning Yudie walked leisurely behind. Seeing her husband's actions, she only felt that it was amusing. As for the peddlers, they were all dumbfounded.
Four or five gold beads fell into each of their pockets, which was equivalent to several years of their business. It was only after the immortal-like couple had gone far away that they woke up from their dream and realized that they had struck it rich. Only then did they become extremely excited. They had originally guessed that the couple was not ordinary people, and now it was confirmed. After all, this was a city that belonged to the Upper Realm. It was not impossible to occasionally encounter those martial artists who were generous with their money.

Chapter 1606
Luo Zheng ran far away, and Ning Yudie chased after him, covering her mouth and giggling at the side.
The pedestrians on both sides of the street didn't know what was going on, and they all looked over in surprise.
Only Luo Nian was extremely calm, and focused on the snacks in his hands …
With this lesson, Luo Zheng learned to be smart. When he brought his son out to play, he would always prepare some money.
Over the next few days, the three of them traveled to three or four cities, and along the way, they encountered a few cultivators with weak cultivations who had taken a fancy to Ning Yudie's beauty and seemed to have evil thoughts about her. However, Luo Nian immediately ran out and stood in front of his mother, and these cultivators were all frightened away by Luo Zheng's calm and collected demeanor.
During this trip, Luo Zheng enjoyed the rare joy of being with his family.
It was a pity that the situation in the universe was still critical, or else he would be willing to take out ten or twenty years to accompany Luo Nian as she grew.
In these few days, there were often roars in the universe …
Ever since the Sacred Race invaded the universe, every time they occupied a large world or killed a Sector Lord, they would announce it to the entire universe in the form of a roar, and use this to suppress the morale of the entire universe.
Those voices often rang in the ears, and it was indeed annoying.
However, the content of today's speech was targeted at Luo Zheng …
Luo Zheng raised his head and frowned when he heard those words.
…
…
During this period of time, the rolling clouds of blood mist in the sky above the Seven Supreme Immortal City had completely disappeared, and the sky returned to its original azure blue color.
The originally strong smell of blood had faded by quite a bit, and the faint smell of blood had a refreshing and sweet feeling to it.
After the blood curse array was destroyed by Luo Zheng, tens of millions of mortals were chased out of the Seven Supreme Immortal City. To the Sacred Race, there was no sense of accomplishment in killing these mortals, and without the blood curse array's effect, they would only be tainted with baleful aura for no reason …
It was better to let these mortals fend for themselves.
The mortals naturally understood that their lives were saved, and they all began to migrate back to their hometowns. As long as they weren't ravaged by the Sacred Race martial artists, these mortals' vitality was still rather tenacious!
When they passed by a hillside a hundred miles away from the Seven Supreme Immortal City, they discovered that there was a massive sinkhole there!
Standing at the edge of this sinkhole, it was nearly impossible to see the bottom!
After Luo Zheng sneaked into the immortal mansion, he used a teleportation token to move the node of the mansion away, causing Maha to fall into a rage.
This caused Maha to become enraged. He didn't believe that Luo Zheng had such a miraculous ability to sneak away in front of him and two saint Empyrean masters!
This wasn't an ability that a Realm Lord could possess. Even an Empyrean couldn't!
Therefore, at that time, he was certain that Luo Zheng had used some kind of concealment technique to hide underground …
"We have to find him even if we have to dig three feet into the ground!" This was Maha's order.
But the Sacred Race didn't just dig three feet into the ground. ......... com. on......... e...
After Luo Zheng returned to the universe, he broke the Sacred Race's blood curse array formation. At the same time, in front of Maha, in front of the entire universe, he humiliated Maha and ruthlessly suppressed the morale of the Sacred Race. Then, he safely fled …
To Maha, this was an almost unbearable defeat!
Following that, Maha urgently summoned all the saint Empyreans in the Great Expansion Universe for an emergency meeting.
In this attack on the Great Expansion Universe, the Sacred Race hadn't sent out all their forces. They had only sent 44 Empyreans, Maha, and many other saint Dao Children, as well as tens of thousands of World Lords …
In terms of the overall situation, the Sacred Race wasn't anxious at all.
In terms of overall strength, they held the absolute advantage. The Sacred Race could easily and gracefully occupy the entire universe …
Occupying more than 3,000 great realms in three years was already quite fast.
According to the Sacred Race's plans, even if they spent 300 years, it still wasn't slow.
As a Dao Child, Maha was the leader of the Sacred Race's expeditionary army. His strength might be inferior to some Empyreans, but he had the authority to command Empyreans. Thus, Empyrean Darkglow and Empyrean Myriad Poison were obedient subordinates in front of Maha.
However, not all Empyreans were like this. "Maha! You actually allowed that human Dao Child to escape in front of you! This is a serious dereliction of duty! " The one who spoke was Empyrean Sacred Altar. This Empyrean was an extremely old man in the Sacred Race and had a transcendent status. Moreover, he was an upper Empyrean!
When he heard that Maha had allowed a human Dao Child to escape, he naturally criticized him without the slightest bit of mercy.
Maha glanced indifferently at Sacred Altar Empyrean. He didn't refute Sacred Altar Empyrean.
"The Extreme Lord has ordered his disciples to repeatedly instruct that no one else in the Great Expansion Universe is important. Only the Dao Children are to be killed with all their strength! For your negligence, I will send a message back to the Great Expansion Universe! "Empyrean Sacred Altar said.
The Extreme Lord he spoke of was naturally the architect of the Great Expansion Universe, Saint Mu Haiji.
The truth was that from a purely racial perspective, the Sacred Race didn't have a strong motive to invade the Great Expansion Universe …
The Great Expansion Universe was already so large that it was enough to accommodate the Sacred Race. There were even many great worlds in the Great Expansion Universe that the Sacred Race had yet to occupy or explore.
Invading the Great Expansion Universe wasn't a good thing for the Sacred Race.
However, the Sacred Race was ultimately created by Mu Haiji. Mu Haiji was the Sacred Race's father. If he asked the Sacred Race to break through the Wall of Sighs and occupy the Great Expansion Universe, the Sacred Race had no reason to refuse!
Before the Wall of Sighs opened, Mu Haiji had already sent a message through his disciples. His primary goal was to occupy the entire Great Expansion Universe, and his other goal was to kill all the Dao Children …
If they occupied the Great Expansion Universe, they would be able to kill Luo Xiao.
If they killed the Dao Children, they would sever any hope of Luo Xiao turning the tables.
This was a game between Saints. As for the Sacred Race, they didn't know the inside story, nor did they need to know!
Maha shrugged his shoulders and leapt up from his throne. He walked in front of Empyrean Sacred Altar, the sneer on his face becoming increasingly thick. "On that day, Empyrean Sacred Altar wasn't too far away from the Seven Despair World! Since Empyrean Sacred Altar says that I was negligent and allowed Luo Zheng to escape, why don't you say that Empyrean Sacred Altar didn't appear to stop Luo Zheng!? "
Maha's words pushed all of the blame onto Empyrean Sacred Altar.
How could Empyrean Sacred Altar not hear this? Just as he was about to retort, Empyrean Darkshade and Empyrean Myriad Poison spoke up at the same time.
"Your Highness Dao Child is right. That boy's space magic tool is no ordinary object. We tried our best but we still weren't able to break through that immortal mansion! That immortal mansion is likely something from the Divine Realm! "
"Empyrean Sacred Altar, you make it sound so easy. If he were here that day, I fear he would also be helpless!"
With the support of the two Empyreans, Maha shrugged his shoulders once more. "He naturally knows that he is helpless, so that's why he didn't appear that day. Hehe … now he actually wants to complain to the Extreme Sovereign. Empyrean Sacred Altar, your Mandate of Heaven is formidable enough, but your methods seem even more formidable?"
Maha, Empyrean Darkshade, and Empyrean Myriad Poison had clearly colluded beforehand. With the three of them echoing each other, they stirred up the waters and pushed a portion of the blame onto Empyrean Sacred Altar …
Empyrean Sacred Altar naturally had his own thoughts.
Within the saint race, he was one of the top 10 ranked Empyreans. After the Wall of Sighs was broken through, he should have been the leader of the expeditionary force and come to attack. But, he never imagined that the position of leader would be given to a child whose skeletal age was less than 100 years old!
He naturally felt that this was unfair.
The saint race wasn't a monolithic entity; there were all sorts of factions scattered throughout. He had long found this Maha displeasing to the eye, but the entire saint race was controlled by the Brahma lineage. Maha's father was also the number one Empyrean of the Extreme Realm.
Although Empyrean Sacred Altar was furious, he could only swallow this anger.
This was a rare opportunity and he planned to directly complain to the Extreme Lord. The Extreme Lord placed great importance on the ten Dao Children of the Extreme Realm. For Maha to let that Dao Child Luo Zheng escape was no small crime …
But just as he was about to make trouble for Maha, he was immediately bitten back by this brat. Empyrean Sacred Altar was also depressed!
Maha indifferently glared at Empyrean Sacred Altar, as if he didn't place this upper Empyrean in his eyes at all. Then, his figure flickered and he boldly sat down on his throne. He looked around at the other Empyreans present and stroked his chin. "Everyone, today I called everyone here to think of a solution, not to quarrel. In this situation, what ingenious plans do you all have?"
Although the saint race occupied over 3000 great worlds of the Human Dao Alliance, the truth was that they had been dragged into a quagmire.
If the saint race wanted to continue expanding into the Gods No Thought Of Great Worlds, they had to stabilize the great worlds they controlled. Thus, they had to use the Blood Curse Array … but the Blood Curse Array was troublesome and expensive to use, greatly slowing down the saint race's progress.
Now, the three great human influences, including the Demi-Immortal races, the demon races, and the smaller races, had all declared war on the saint race. If the saint race continued to hesitate, it would greatly affect their morale!

Chapter 1607
Many Empyreans stood on either side of Maha, maintaining their silence.
In truth, the Empyreans present were somewhat unconvinced of the World Lord realm Maha.
In any situation, Empyreans were lofty and lofty existences. They stood at the peak of the world, shouldering the mandate of heaven and representing the Heavenly Dao …
A junior at the World Lord realm, even if he was a Dao Child, even if he was a Highness, without absolute strength, it was impossible for Empyreans to willingly submit to him.
It was only because of Maha's background that the Empyreans maintained a respectful attitude on the surface.
Now that Maha had encountered trouble, if he wanted the Empyreans to come up with a plan, they might not be very cooperative.
Maha had gathered the Empyreans here today to gather their thoughts. But as he saw this situation, his smile became increasingly cold …
"It looks like no one has any concerns …" Maha sneered. "Fellow Empyreans, after arriving in the Great Yan Universe, have you not discovered anything strange?"
The Empyreans remained silent.
Only Empyrean Dim Darkness spoke up. "Of course there is. This world is filled with malice towards us."
Maha nodded. "Yes, malice!"
The universe was originally an organic whole, just like a complete person.
If a person was afflicted with an illness, all the functions in the person's body would be mobilized to fight against the illness. If the human body could win against the illness, the illness would naturally be cured without treatment. If the human body lost, the illness would worsen.
The Great Yan Universe was a 'person'. And when the saints broke through the Wall of Sighs, that was the same as breaking through this person's skin.
As a foreign race, the saints were like countless illnesses that began to corrode this' human body '.
The Great Yan Universe didn't need the command of a 'Saint'. It would instinctively launch attacks.
A blade of grass, a tree, a river, a lake, a sea …
Space, stars.
As well as the countless living beings and races of the Great Yan Universe, they all maintained an intense hostility towards the saints.
Even if they walked through the grass, they would 'occasionally' trip over the grass …
Ever since the saints invaded …
They had already encountered 70,000 earthquakes in the various great worlds.
As they traveled through the great worlds of the Human Alliance, they encountered over 600 beast tides, over a thousand insect tides, over 10,000 tsunamis …
And these were all in the statistics. The true number of disasters far surpassed this number.
These inexplicable disasters were all launched against the saint martial artists. This was Huan Yu's counterattack!
A mere disaster didn't cause any damage to the saint martial artists.
But apart from this, there was another type …
For example, a bolt from the blue.
Three months ago, a bolt of lightning suddenly struck down in the Three Pure Realms.
If this was an ordinary bolt of lightning, it would be fine, but the Thunder Laws contained within that blood-red bolt of lightning were as high as the ninth level. Its might was terrifying to the extreme. Even if an Empyrean encountered such a massive bolt of lightning, they would still have to retreat!
This single bolt of lightning had killed over 10,000 saint race martial artists, including four Great World Lords and 17 World Lords!
Another example was the spatial storm …
The spatial passageways built by the Human Race were originally very stable.
It was possible that a spatial storm would not erupt for thousands of years. Even if it did, the scale of the spatial storm would not be large.
However, when it was the saint race's turn, the large scale space storms had already erupted 76 times, sweeping away countless saint race troops. Over 20 sector lords had died in the space storms, and one of them was a great sector lord …
There were also falling stars, astral winds, golden streams, and …
These malicious intents that came from Huan Yu were nearly impossible to guard against, causing the saint race martial artists to have a headache.
"The stronger the 'potential' of Huan Yu, the stronger the retaliation will be! Why would I, Maha, want to seize so many roaring tokens and roar about in this Great Yan Universe?! " Maha's eyes blazed, two balls of flames rising in his pupils. "As long as the morale of Huan Yu is extinguished, the potential of Huan Yu will also weaken!"
Some time ago, the malicious intents that came from Huan Yu had indeed lessened by a great deal. As Maha said, this was all thanks to him!
"Humph! We all understand what you're saying! Moreover, we are doing the same thing! If it weren't for us, would those old fellows of humanity have allowed you to so casually extinguish their potential? " Empyrean Sacred Altar coldly snorted. "Moreover, the morale that you personally extinguished was destroyed by that Luo Zheng!"
Maha's expression didn't change at all. "This Huan Yu was created by a Saint. If it could be casually destroyed then that would be strange! That is why I have gathered everyone here today. I hope that everyone can come up with a plan! "
"Hehe, Your Highness Daozi possesses extraordinary wisdom. I'm sure you already have a plan, so why bother asking for advice from us old fellows?" Empyrean Seven Kills said, his voice dripping with sarcasm.
The saint race Empyreans present were divided into three groups. The group of Empyreans led by Empyrean Sacred Altar simply didn't like Maha. Empyrean Seven Kills was also one of them.

There was also a group of people that belonged to the 'Brahma Demon' faction. For instance, Empyrean Darkshade and Empyrean Myriad Poison completely followed Maha and obeyed his every word.
Hearing Empyrean Seven Kills' words, a sharp light flashed in Maha's eyes. He faintly sighed and said, "It looks like Empyrean Seven Kills doesn't know what cards the human race has!"
"What do they have?" Empyrean Seven Kills disdainfully said.
Empyrean Sacred Altar and Empyrean Seven Kills had always spoken with strength. As for the other human Empyreans, they didn't spare them a glance.
The only one they feared was the True Divinity behind the Demi-Immortal Clan, Startail.
"The Mandate of Heaven that the old patriarch of the Demi-Immortal Clan inherited was the Great Expansion Lotus Flower that Saint Luo Xiao comprehended! This lotus flower can use the potential of the universe! " Maha's smile became increasingly bright. He stared at Empyrean Seven Kills and said, "As for how strong the potential of the universe is, even I don't know. But, if you Empyreans encounter this old man in the future, you must be careful!"
Hearing this, the Empyreans present all had solemn expressions.
As Empyreans, they certainly understood that the Mandate of Heaven was divided into strong and weak. Some Mandates of Heaven were ridiculously strong and some Mandates of Heaven were pitifully weak. Because of this, Empyreans were divided into high, middle, and low grades.
The lotus flower in the Grand Evolution Universe and the Alba Fern Tree in the Grand Evolution Universe had a saying in the Saint Race that they were called 'objects of the heavens'. After the Heavenly Dao was constructed, these objects were used to suppress it. This was also the strongest Heaven's Will in the Heavenly Dao!
And in the universe where the saints resided, this Heaven's Will belonged to Maha's father!
"Your Highness Daozi, where did you obtain this information?" A saint race Empyrean asked.
"Where I obtained this information from is not important. What is important is everyone's attitude. If we were to fight with those Empyreans, the ones rushing up to die will not be me, but all of you!" Maha sneered.
"Hmph, when that time comes, how can His Highness Daozi stay out of this?" Holy Altar retorted coldly.
"Oh?" The smile on Maha's face grew even wider. "It would be a pity if my heavenly treasure body were to perish, but my father has prepared another body of the Sacred Woodfern Fruit for me. So what if I perish in the Grand Evolution Universe? If all of you perish here … hehe. "
When the saints sent Maha into the Grand Evolution Universe, they had already prepared for both sides. Even if Maha died, he still had another escape route.
Since his father controlled the Sacred Woodfern Tree, he had also taken a Sacred Fruit from the tree and created another body of Maha's. The Sacred Woodfern Fruit body was even more perfect than the heavenly treasure body!
After Maha finished speaking, the Empyreans present fell silent once more.
But the meaning behind this silence was different.
Before, they didn't want to cooperate with His Highness Daozi, but now they were calculating the situation they would face …
After an incense stick of time, Maha leaned back on his throne. A small golden scimitar appeared in his hand. The scimitar was very exquisite. Maha's finger gently stroked the tip of the scimitar.
He narrowed his eyes and swept his gaze over the many Empyreans, an arrogant smile on his face.
From the start, His Highness Daozi had never placed these Empyreans in his eyes!
After a long time, Empyrean Silverfrost, who had maintained a neutral stance, finally spoke up. "If we want to destroy their momentum, we must first strike the humans!"

Chapter 1608
In the grand scheme of things, the Sacred Race did have the upper hand.
However, the human race's strategy was still commendable!
After sacrificing the Human Alliance's territory, the Sacred Race fell into a quagmire and was unable to take another step forward. Even now, they were still lingering at the boundary between the Human Alliance and Gods No Thought!

My father and I combined are only forty-four years old, half of your age. It's not fair for you to challenge my father to a duel. If my father cultivated for another fifty years, when he reached your age, he would still beat you until you were looking for your teeth on the ground! "

Chapter 1609

A World Lord could live for 10 million years. As for an Empyrean, their lifespan was measured in hundreds of millions of years!
With such a long lifespan, one could see the evolution of time and the birth and death of a race. To a mortal, this was an eternal existence.
92 years old …
According to a World Lord's lifespan of 10 million years, this was like a little chick that had just broken its eggshell. It had only peeked its furry head out from the broken eggshell and was only able to open its eyes to see the world …
During this process, Maha had already cultivated to the World Lord level. Moreover, he was a true Dao Child!

Chapter 1610
