Dragging this out would only be a slow suicide.
"According to Luo Zheng's idea, if we take a risk, perhaps … we can increase our chances of victory by 10%," the Old Patriarch said.
The Empyreans still had worried looks on their faces. But thinking about it carefully, Luo Zheng's method was their only choice, and it could also be called the correct choice.
Facing a powerful opponent, they could either rely on their greater strength to crush them, or they could only take advantage of the situation and fight with everything they had!

Chapter 1613
The Demi-Immortals had already told Luo Zheng some information about the demon race and the demon race.
Although the three great human factions hadn't gotten involved, they were still paying close attention to the war between the demon race and the demon race.
The two races had never been on good terms with each other since the Primordial Era. In addition to the fact that their realms intersected with each other, there had never been a lack of war between the two races. There had even been several full-scale wars!
The three great battles recorded in history were the War of the Setting Sun, the War of the Demon Slayers, and the Bloody War of the Mirage Realm. The demon race and the demon race had each invested hundreds of millions of their people in these battles, and had lost several Celestial Venerables as well.
The War of the Demon Slayers was a battle between the demon race and the Human Dao Alliance. The demon race had suffered heavy losses in that battle.
However, the demon race had always played a shady role in history.
They were a reckless and violent race that wasn't welcomed in the universe. Whether it was the major factions or the smaller races, they were all wary of the demon race!
But this time, the demon race had suddenly erupted with a strong sense of bloodlust in the face of the Sacred Race's invasion. They swore to fight to the death against the Sacred Race, playing a very rare role.
On the other hand, the demon race had suddenly declared war on the human race and joined the Sacred Race's side.
"According to the Craftsman Saint's speculations, the demon race is a pawn created by Mu Haiji to hide in the Great Yan Realm. Could it be that the demon race is a race created by Father to be used in times of need?"
Luo Zheng continued to analyze the situation as he searched for information.
In the grand scheme of things, this was very likely. After all, creating a race wasn't very difficult for a Sage. However, Luo Zheng had no idea why Luo Xiao would create such a violent and detestable demon race.
Right now, the Yao Night Clan had completely given up on their territory. The two Kings of the Yao Night Clan were leading the elites of the clan to clear out the enemies and head towards the center of the universe!
However, if the demon race wanted to enter Gods No Thought's territory, they would first have to pass through the demon race's territory. They could also use the Sifting Yin Realm to join forces with the Sacred Race.
However, the demon race had obviously discovered this path and had set up layers of defenses in the Luminous Dragon Realm and the Autumn Cry Realm.
After several paths were blocked by the demons, the Yao Ye Clan's advance was forcefully slowed down.
It was just that in this process, the demons had also paid a considerable price.
Right now, the Demon Race had also mobilized their entire race, and all of their elites were stationed in the Blood Devil Realm, blocking off Qingyu's Demon Night Clan's team. However, the Punishment King was leading another team to meet up with Qingyu. When the time came, it was likely that a fierce battle would break out …
After Luo Zheng quickly understood the current situation, he continued to obtain information from the World Will.
Luo Zheng had deep feelings for the Fiend Night Clan. This time, he was most likely going to be an enemy rather than a friend. In the past, he had made a promise to Liu Yu and the Phantom. Faced with this situation, Luo Zheng didn't know how to fulfill his promise.
Luo Zheng, who had his eyes closed, was constantly moving through the massive amount of information of the World Will.
In order to understand the root cause, he did his best to collect memories related to Yao. Bit by bit, these memories flashed through Luo Zheng's mind …
He saw the birth of an ordinary Fiend Night Clan baby girl. That baby girl was Yao. After a test, Yao's bloodline caused a commotion in her Fiend Night Clan. She possessed an extremely pure ancient Fiend Night Clan bloodline, so she was qualified to compete for the throne.
The scenes of Yao's life were condensed into fragments, and they drilled into Luo Zheng's soul one by one. In the end, they stopped at two years ago …
In that instant, Yao's Soul Needle Seal was awakened.
At the same time, Luo Zheng was undergoing body refinement in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement by the Craftsman Saint. Meanwhile, Xun was using her great willpower to erase her Soul Needle Seal, along with all of her memories.
Soon, Luo Zheng saw the scene of Yao gathering members of the Fiend Night Clan. He saw the expressions of disbelief on the faces of the Fiend Night Clan members, as well as their strong emotions of resistance. Even the Heavenly Venerables of the Fiend Night Clan didn't approve of Yao's actions.
"So that's how it is." Luo Zheng revealed an enlightened expression.
Just like the Craftsman Saint had said, the process of creating the Soul Needle Seal was extremely complicated. Even Saint Mu Haiji was unable to plant the Soul Needle Seal in too many people.
The Soul Needle Seal had only been passed down through the Kings of the Fiend Night Clan, generation after generation. In the end, it was activated in the generation of Xun, Yao, and Ying …
Thus, the Fiend Night Clan's declaration of war against the human race and their alliance with the Sacred Race was completely under the orders of the two Kings. The other members of the Fiend Night Clan weren't affected by the Soul Needle Seal.
The Fiend Night Clan viewed themselves as part of the universe. They originally hated the Sacred Race, but they had no choice but to obey the orders of the Kings!
"If that's the case, then I only need to kill Yao and Qingyu." A cold light flashed in Luo Zheng's eyes. He suddenly took out the Teleportation Token …
The Heavenly Venerables might have some misgivings about the Fiend Night Clan, but they were more willing to gather all of their strength to resist the Sacred Race.
After Luo Zheng returned, he hadn't revealed his full strength. Now was the time. And Luo Zheng's test stone was the Fiend Night Clan!
…
…
Fiend Night Clan, Clear Dream Boundary, Cage Island.
One by one, green cannon brands rose from the ground like giant chimneys.
In the sky above Cage Island, a bright light suddenly shone.
It wasn't lightning, but a spatial law storm.
The moment the storm bloomed, a human figure shuttled out from it!
Cage Island was one of the most important prisons of the Fiend Night Clan. Those who could be imprisoned here were all chosen by the Punishment King himself. They were all famous.
The martial artists guarding here naturally weren't weak …
In the past, there were 12 Fiend Night Clan Sector Lords guarding Cage Island.
However, after the two Kings Yao and Qingyu mobilized almost all of the Fiend Night Clan's experts, there were only two Sector Lords left guarding Cage Island.
On the highest observation tower of Cage Island, Sector Lord Feng Ling carried a longbow as tall as a person. She looked at the blazing white storm in the sky.
Her eyes turned pale yellow, and there were three red dots that kept circling. The three red dots finally converged into a ball and locked onto the rapidly descending figure in the sky.
"Invader, human martial artist! Die! "
Even Fiend Night Clan martial artists would be killed if they got close to Cage Island, much less a human martial artist.
Feng Ling didn't hesitate at all. Her slender arms lightly waved, and the longbow was pulled open. A blue spiral formed on the bowstring.
Xiu xiu xiu xiu …
As the spirals converged, a heart-palpitating energy fluctuation condensed.
Sou!
The spiral left a trail of tens of thousands of feet in the sky as it accurately shot towards the figure.
Although the opponent was a Sector Lord, he wouldn't be able to dodge her arrow.
Feng Ling was very confident in her archery skills. Plus, the longbow in her hand was given to her by the Island Lord before she left, a secondary grade 1 god artifact.
The helix shot out quickly approached and finally hit the target accurately. It exploded in the air, disrupting the sudden airflow and forming a violent storm, completely annihilating the target.
"Is he dead?" Feng Ling looked above with concern.
When the storm dissipated, she still saw a figure falling, or rather, 'falling'.
He should have been killed already. Human world overlords were truly unable to withstand a single strike. Feng Ling thought to herself. After that, she slung her bow over her shoulder and leaped into the air like a nimble rabbit. She soared through the air and flew towards her target.
When the target had just fallen to the ground, Feng Ling had already arrived at her destination. Then, an expression of disbelief suddenly appeared on her originally indifferent face.
The human Sector Lord was lying in the hole he created and was slowly climbing up. At the same time, he looked up and smiled at Feng Ling.
This guy was actually unharmed!
When Feng Ling saw that small moment, she felt a chill run down her spine. Almost like a conditioned reflex, she raised her hand and shot an arrow!
Forming the arrow, drawing the bow, aiming, shooting. These few actions had already become instinct in Feng Ling's mind. In one breath, the opponent didn't even have time to react.
Hua la!

Chapter 1614
The Yao Night clan's Sector Lord's eyes were filled with shock …
This person didn't use any sacred art.
He didn't use any protective True Yuan or God Light. He was able to take an arrow from less than 30 meters away, and he wasn't injured at all. Not even the corner of his clothes were torn …
Was this guy even human?
Luo Zheng slowly walked out of the crater. He couldn't sense any killing intent from him.
He didn't want to kill for no reason.
Although the Demon Night Clan was created by Mu Haiji, they had grown up together with the universe. They had become one with the universe and were considered a part of the universe. The key to the Demon Night Clan was the Soul Sealing Needle in the depths of the three kings' souls …
If he was facing a Saint Race Sector Lord, Luo Zheng wouldn't hesitate to kill him. However, Luo Zheng wouldn't do that against the Yao Night clan.
"Don't attack anymore, I don't mean any harm," Luo Zheng said as he walked towards the other party.
Feng Ling placed her hand on the longbow and stared at Luo Zheng. Her entire body was in a tense state, and her toes were constantly moving on the ground. This was the unique footwork of an Archer. Her whole body was constantly moving backward along with Luo Zheng's speed, always maintaining a distance of about ten feet from Luo Zheng.
She didn't shoot a third arrow.
The first two arrows weren't able to injure this youth at all. Could the third arrow kill him? At this moment, she felt extremely conflicted.
She had already determined that Luo Zheng was an enemy, so she had to attack him with all her might.
However, she didn't feel any hostility from him, which was quite strange.
"Trespassers of Cage Island will die! Tell me your goal! "Feng Ling was still extremely vigilant.
"I'm looking for someone," Luo Zheng replied as he walked.
"My Cage Island is filled with prisoners personally appointed by the King. The person you're looking for isn't here. Stop!" Feng Ling shouted.
However, Luo Zheng ignored her warning and continued to walk at an even pace.
At this moment, a True Yuan voice transmission sounded in Feng Ling's ears.
"Attack together and kill this person!"
The one who spoke was the other Yao Night clan Sector Lord, Shui Ying.
Compared to Feng Ling, Shui Ying was slightly stronger.
If the two of them worked together, they might have a chance.
Feng Ling was also a decisive person. Once she made a decision, she immediately attacked!
Her movement speed suddenly increased by several times. In the process of moving backward, the two ends of the longbow in her hand unfurled six black blades. It was as if the longbow had spread its wings, and a light began to flow along the surface of the longbow!
"Hiss!"
A bright golden light condensed at her fingertip and turned into a short golden arrow!
After the arrow appeared, golden wind blades crazily revolved around the short arrow.
"Perfect Storm Arrow!"
Feng Ling had already comprehended the profundity of the ninth level of the Laws of Wind and had perfectly integrated it into her archery.
"Wu wu wu …"
As the golden wind blade spun around the short golden arrow, it emitted a sound that resembled the wails of ghosts and howls of wolves!
"Sou!"
Immediately after, the arrow left the bowstring, drawing a perfectly straight line in the air, heading straight for Luo Zheng.
"Aren't you going to dodge?"
This Perfect Storm Arrow was self-created by Feng Ling. It was an archery skill that she was proud of and was also her strongest attack!
However, after shooting this arrow, Feng Ling's heart was still very conflicted.
She hoped to hit Luo Zheng and kill Luo Zheng, but at the same time, she was worried about the terrifying defense of this monster in front of her. If this arrow hit Luo Zheng head-on and he remained unscathed, the blow to her would be too great.
At the same time that Feng Ling shot this arrow, a black figure appeared.
That black figure was wearing a bright black tight-fitting night suit. Like a ghost, it left an afterimage in the air and then disappeared from sight.
In an instant, the small golden arrow nailed Luo Zheng's chest!
The golden wind blades that surrounded the small arrow followed Luo Zheng like a shadow. These wind blades began to crazily cut Luo Zheng's body!
At the same time, the black figure silently appeared behind Luo Zheng. The black figure held a silver dagger and stabbed towards Luo Zheng's back!
At this moment, Luo Zheng's entire body was covered in golden wind blades. To a sector lord, these golden wind blades were extremely powerful. However, the black figure also hid behind the world, so the golden wind blades didn't cause any damage to the black figure.
"Ding!"
The silver dagger stabbed into Luo Zheng's green shirt, but it only made a crisp sound. It didn't pierce through Luo Zheng's clothes at all!
The wind blades dissipated and the black figure retreated, standing side by side with Feng Ling.
This black figure was dressed in black, and the exposed parts of her skin were snow white. It was the other sector lord that stayed behind to guard Cage Island, Shui Ying.
At this moment, Shui Ying's expression was the same as Feng Ling's, extremely ugly.
She had a deep understanding of the Dao of Assassination. Just now, she wanted to cooperate with Feng Ling to kill this human youth in one strike. Whether it was timing or speed, she grasped them very precisely.
She didn't think that she wouldn't even be able to pierce through this person's clothes …
"The two of you can stop now. I'm really just here to find someone." Luo Zheng lightly said, the smile on his face fading a little.
The two sector lords of the Yao Night clan couldn't do anything to this invulnerable sector lord. With such a clear difference in strength, Feng Ling asked, "Who are you looking for?"
"Her name is Liu Yu. She's imprisoned on this island." Luo Zheng lightly said.
"Liu Yu?" Shui Ying's eyebrows rose.
The members of the Yao Night Clan who were able to be imprisoned on Cage Island were definitely not ordinary people. However, there were a few exceptions. The two Sector Lords naturally knew Liu Yu because Liu Yu's cultivation was probably the weakest amongst all the members of the Yao Night Clan who had been subjected to the torture. He was only at the Divine Ocean Realm.
Besides her, the others were all at the Divine Transformation realm. There were even a few sector lords!
When Liu Yu was escorted here and learned that she was an ascender, many people felt it was strange. Just how had this newly ascended clansman offended their king?
"Why are you looking for her?" Feng Ling asked.
Luo Zheng lightly smiled. "Of course I'm here to save her."
"Only the king has the right to take away anyone that enters Cage Island!" Shui Ying's eyes narrowed.
"You don't have the right to stop me." Luo Zheng shook his head.
Shui Ying and Feng Ling immediately fell silent …
This human youth in front of them was very restrained. They had used their killing moves several times in a row without holding back at all, but he didn't seem to have any intention of fighting back.
This fellow wasn't boasting. They really didn't have the right.
"Even if we don't have the right, we won't give up." Shui Ying's attitude was even more resolute than Feng Ling's.
Although all the elites of the Yao Night clan had come out, they were still the guardians of Cage Island. They wouldn't back down in the face of an invader.
Feng Ling stared at Luo Zheng. After sizing him up, she suddenly felt that this human youth's voice was very familiar …
He was a sector lord. He was so young, but he had such terrifying strength. There weren't many martial artists like him in the universe.
"Who are you?" Feng Ling asked.
Luo Zheng faintly smiled. He didn't plan on hiding his identity. "I'm Luo Zheng."
As expected …
Feng Ling's guess was confirmed.
As for Shui Ying, she stared blankly at Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng's voice often echoed in the universe. Everyone knew about the grudge between him and Yao Yao.
The most important thing was that Luo Zheng had another king of the Yao Night clan by his side!
"Don't make any unnecessary resistance. I don't want to implicate the innocent." Luo Zheng calmly glanced at the two women. He turned his head and walked in a direction.
It wasn't hard to find Liu Yu through the will of the world. He knew where Liu Yu was.
As for the two sector lords of the Yao Night clan, they looked at each other and finally learned to be smart. They didn't attack Luo Zheng anymore. At the same time, they felt very strange.
It should be Luo Zheng's first time on Cage Island. How could he accurately know where Liu Yu was?
After walking for tens of li, Luo Zheng finally stopped in front of a huge pillar.
Liu Yu's hands were behind her back. She was hanging on the pillar. Her body was covered in traces of corrosion. Her long hair fell to the ground. Her eyes were closed and her soul was extremely weak …
Seeing Liu Yu like this, Luo Zheng felt a bit apologetic.
He had given Liu Yu hope and promised that he would bring her out within three years. However, the situation before this and Luo Zheng's strength were not enough to save her. Instead, he had allowed her to suffer in this place!

Chapter 1615
Drip, drip, drip, drip …
Drops of black rain fell from the sky.
Cage Island was located in a black ocean. The water in the ocean was extremely corrosive and no living thing could survive in it.
The water evaporated and condensed in the air, gathering above Cage Island and finally falling down as raindrops.
The extremely corrosive raindrops were called the Rain of Pain by the Yao Night clan. After the seventh generation King of Punishment discovered the uniqueness of this place, he created this artistic punishment, which was second only to the Eternal Night Punishment.
Weng, weng.
Following two soft cries, Feng Ling and Shui Ying, who were behind Luo Zheng, both condensed protective true essence. The raindrops hit their protective true essence and made sizzling sounds. The Rain of Pain was corroding their protective true essence.
Even though they were sector lords, they didn't dare to let the Rain of Pain splash on their bodies. The corrosive rain could melt gold and penetrate iron. It could also touch a warrior's sense of pain and make them wish they were dead.
Luo Zheng didn't react at all to the raindrops.
When the raindrops landed on Luo Zheng, they didn't even corrode his body. They were like ordinary raindrops, sliding down Luo Zheng's body.
When the two sector lords of the Yao Night clan saw this, they glanced at each other …
What was this guy's body made of?!
The two of them were guarding this island. Even if it was a grade one divine artifact, they had to protect it well. They couldn't let it be exposed to the Rain of Pain!
Seeing Luo Zheng like this, he didn't care about the Rain of Pain at all!
Chi la!
A raindrop fell on Liu Yu's body.
Liu Yu, who was hanging on the railings like a zombie, suddenly trembled and let out a muffled groan.
Besides Liu Yu, all sorts of painful howls could be heard on Cage Island …
If one endured some pain for a long time, they would become numb to it. They wouldn't even feel pain anymore. This was a form of self-protection for warriors.
However, the huge charms on Cage Island contained strong Life Laws. These charms were created by the seventh generation Monarch of Life and were actually trees that were divine artifacts. These trees would also be corroded by the Rain of Pain, but they could rely on the Life Laws to repair themselves and the warriors tied to the charms …
The only difference was that these trees that had been forged into cannon brands had no sense of pain, while the martial artists tied to them were living a life worse than death.
"Ah …"
Drops of Rain of Pain splashed onto Liu Yu's body. On her snow-white back, a few black holes appeared, and a faint white smoke rose from her back.
At this moment, a strong life force was emitted from the torch, and the holes on Liu Yu's body were instantly repaired, forming new pieces of flesh of different colors.
But at the same time, new rainwater dripped down. The newly grown flesh was corroded and then repaired again.
This was the most painful process. It was a cycle that repeated endlessly.
The entire Cage Island was like purgatory at this moment. Countless members of the Fiend Night Clan made all sorts of noises from their throats. They roared in pain, screamed, cursed … and some others, like Liu Yu, remained silent.
Luo Zheng walked forward and gently flicked his hand. An invisible force appeared above Liu Yu's head, and all the Rain of Pain was instantly repelled by Luo Zheng's power …
At this moment, the Life Laws in the charms completely repaired Liu Yu in a few breaths of time, but she still didn't open her eyes.
Not long after, Liu Yu finally felt something strange.
As long as the charms emitted life force, it meant that the Rain of Pain would continue to drip down. There had never been an exception in the past few years.
She reached out to sweep the hair in front of her forehead and suddenly opened her eyes. In front of her was a handsome young man.
Liu Yu was slightly stunned, but she stared blankly at Luo Zheng …
Ever since they parted on the Illusory Battlefield, Liu Yu had deeply engraved Luo Zheng's promise in her heart. It was practically her greatest motivation to endure!
Every time the Roaring Command sounded in the universe, no matter how painful it was, Liu Yu would perk up her ears to listen …
But as the situation evolved, Liu Yu found that the situation was becoming more and more unfavorable.
Perhaps she knew very little of the truth, but through the Roaring Command, she still learned that a race called the Sacred Race had begun to attack the human race. Later on, she also learned that the Fiend Night Clan had allied with the Sacred Race, and the situation began to become more complicated.
Three years passed very quickly. Luo Zheng didn't come at the scheduled time.
However, Liu Yu didn't hear anything about Luo Zheng's death. She knew Luo Zheng's personality. As long as he was alive, he definitely wouldn't give up.
Recently, Luo Zheng's voice sounded in the universe again. He had begun to declare war on the Sacred Race. Saving her seemed to be even further away. In a few years, Luo Zheng probably wasn't strong enough to fight against the Fiend Night Clan, right?
She didn't expect that in less than a month, she would see Luo Zheng again. At this moment, she suspected that she was dreaming.
However, there were no dreams on Cage Island. In such a painful environment, no one could sleep peacefully. Even if there was, it would be a nightmare.
"Luo Zheng, you …"
Liu Yu's eyes were filled with confusion. Because she saw the two Fiend Night Clan Sector Lords behind Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng smiled at Liu Yu apologetically, "I'm very sorry, I didn't see you."
With that, Luo Zheng's finger tapped lightly.
The vines binding Liu Yu instantly broke. Liu Yu's entire body slid to the ground, weakly sitting on the ground …
After being eroded by the Rain of Pain for so many years, she was completely naked. Facing Luo Zheng, she could still maintain her shyness. She struggled to keep her legs together while staring at the two Sector Lords behind Luo Zheng. "But they …"
Luo Zheng could casually enter Cage Island. There was no way he wouldn't be stopped. Why were the two Sector Lords just standing there like idiots?
Luo Zheng took out a thick blanket from his spatial ring and covered Liu Yu's body. He then smiled faintly, "It's okay, they can't beat me."
Even if they couldn't beat him … they couldn't just stand there and watch right?
Luo Zheng's answer didn't solve Liu Yu's confusion. Instead, it made her even more confused.
Liu Yu naturally didn't know that it wasn't that the two Sector Lords didn't want to stop Luo Zheng. It was just that there was a huge difference in strength. The two of them couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng. On the contrary, if Luo Zheng wanted to kill them, it would be as easy as flipping his hand.
The two Sector Lords were like two three year old children. They used all sorts of methods, but Luo Zheng still didn't do anything. How could they fight in this situation?
They could only stand behind Luo Zheng and watch the show …
Yao's rules were extremely strict. None of the prisoners on Cage Island were allowed to escape. If Liu Yu was taken away by Luo Zheng, the two of them would probably be severely punished.
At this stage, the entire Yao Ye clan's elites were all out. Yao Ye couldn't care about this anymore.
After saving Liu Yu, Luo Zheng turned his head and his gaze landed on the two Yao Ye clan Sector Lords, "Do you know why I didn't kill you?"
Hearing this, the faces of the two Sector Lords revealed vigilant looks.
With Luo Zheng's methods, they indeed weren't Luo Zheng's match!
Luo Zheng smiled, "Because I see the Yao Ye clan as my friends."
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, the two Yao Ye clan Sector Lords had complicated expressions …
"Although the Yao Ye clan betrayed the entire universe, you weren't the betrayers. It was your king." Luo Zheng's voice gradually became cold, "And your Yao Ye clan doesn't only have two kings. There's a third …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a trace of blood red light. That was killing intent rolling within him. At this moment, he seemed to have inherited Fumigation's aura, "I will represent the King of Massacre and get rid of the other two kings!"
Liu Yu, who was wrapped in a blanket, looked at Luo Zheng's broad back. Hearing this, her originally tired and sickly face glowed with a trace of radiance and a gratified smile appeared on her face.
As for the two Sector Lords, they took in a deep breath …
If it was in the past, the Yao Ye clan would have already attacked someone who said such disgraceful words. In order to protect their king's honor, they were willing to sacrifice their lives. Moreover, the two of them belonged to the Saint Chain Sect, which supported the King of Punishment.

Chapter 1616
When a human's power reaches its peak, it will form a God Kingdom and a Holy Land.
The other races in the universe are more or less the same, but the Yaoye clan is a bit special. Strictly speaking, the Yaoye clan is similar to the esoteric sect in Gods No Thought.
However, the esoteric sects in Gods No Thought were only there to absorb the power of faith. They haven't developed to the point where they can rule over an entire force. The various esoteric sects still serve the four major families.
The Yaoye clan is completely controlled by the three kings.
The reason for this social structure is naturally Mu Haiji's intention. His goal is to use the Soul Sealing Needle to make the three kings lead the Yaoye clan in rebellion.
Although the Yaoye clan is absolutely loyal to the king, they are still intelligent beings. They don't agree with the king's actions.
However, Kaoru still hasn't returned to the throne. As the two kings are the rulers of the Yaoye clan, they can't go against their orders. They can only bite the bullet and follow their king.
Even if Feng Ling and Mizukage belong to the Holy Chain Sect, when they heard Luo Zheng's words, they couldn't help but feel agitated.
If it were another king, the Yaoye clan would be fine …
At this moment, the two of them simultaneously had the same thought.
Whether it's rationally or emotionally, they are willing to stand on the side of the human race and Luo Zheng.
But they can't betray their king!
The traitors of the Yaoye clan are called "abandoned by the king". This is the greatest shame of the Yaoye clan. Some people would rather bear the most painful punishment than betray their king! From the moment they were born, the honor of following their king has settled in their blood.
Therefore, when Feng Ling and Mizukage had this thought, they were frightened by their own thoughts! This kind of thinking is disgraceful!
But after this thought was born, it grew uncontrollably.
"We … will not betray our king." Mizukage shook her head stubbornly.
"Not now doesn't mean we won't in the future." Luo Zheng looked up at the sky.
A huge black cloud floated in the sky above this sea. The clouds gathered above Cage Island to form a storm, and the Rain of Pain fell …
At this moment, Luo Zheng's body suddenly rose from the ground, and his hands suddenly circled!
The nine stars in his body slowly rotated, and an invisible power spiraled between his hands!
The power radiated outwards, causing the surrounding space to distort crazily!
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng's fist suddenly shot towards the sky!
Boom!
A muffled sound came from the sky.
The black clouds that gathered above Cage Island were instantly torn apart by this tremendous force. Like a torn piece of cloth, they turned into strips of cloth that quickly drifted in all directions …
After a few breaths of time, the storm dissipated, and the black rain that fell from the sky also stopped.
Luo Zheng lowered his head and glanced at the two sector lords. With a flash, he charged towards the other cannon!
Feng Ming and the Mizukage obviously guessed what Luo Zheng wanted to do. The Mizukage's brows suddenly wrinkled, and she wanted to rush over, but Feng Ming reached out and grabbed her, then shook his head, "You can't stop him …"
"But we can't let him!" The Mizukage said angrily.
Feng Ming still shook his head, "The current situation isn't something we can control. Maybe … he's right."
There was no right or wrong in the conflict between races. From the Yao Ye clan's point of view, Luo Zheng was definitely their enemy. However, Luo Zheng just used Fumigate's aura to affect them.
Luo Zheng flew towards the other cannon. On this cannon, there was a Yao Ye clan sector lord tied up.
This sector lord was looking up at the dissipating black clouds in the sky. Her eyes were filled with shock. The clouds seemed to have been dispersed by someone, but how could such a thing happen on Cage Island?
Just as she was shocked, an extremely fast figure swooped down and stopped in front of her.
"Human?" The Yao Ye clan sector lord revealed a confused expression.
Luo Zheng waved his hand and the vines binding the sector lord were broken. He didn't stop at all and flew towards the other cannon …
The people he rescued were all prisoners of the Yao Ye clan. To be able to be imprisoned on Cage Island meant that they were all sinners to the Yao Ye clan. There were all sorts of strange reasons why they were imprisoned here.
Many of them weren't weak. Most of them were at the Divine Transformation realm and there were even some sector lords!
After being rescued, the Yao Ye clansmen were still very weak. However, with their cultivation here, they recovered quickly. Soon, some sector lords regained their ability to move and gathered towards Luo Zheng.
Liu Yu also stood up. She didn't say a word as she looked at the clansmen gathering around her. Liu Yu didn't know what Luo Zheng wanted to do.

However, their strength hadn't fully recovered and they hadn't gathered their true essence yet. They didn't have the ability to fight yet.

"Who are you? Why did you save us? "A Yao Ye clan sector lord stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
"Our Yao Ye clan won't follow a human." Another Yao Ye clan sector lord said.
Even at this stage, the Yao Ye clan was still noble. Even if they were in dire straits, they would still be extremely elegant. They had nowhere to go, but they wouldn't follow a human.
Weng …
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed red. In this situation, he could only borrow Xun's soul again.

There were many Sacred Spear Sect fighters on Cage island. They had followed Xun for a long time, but because they resisted, they were sent here by Yao.
They were familiar with Xun's aura, it couldn't be faked!
"Because he's Luo Zheng, our king is in his body!" Liu Yu said.
When they heard Liu Yu, all the cultivators from the Demon Night Clan instantly came to a sudden understanding, and their gazes towards Luo Zheng were filled with even more reverence!
Even though they were imprisoned here, the battle between Luo Zheng and Yao a few years ago had spread across the entire universe. They had also heard it loud and clear! Luo Zheng was Liu Yu's hope, and at the same time, a glimmer of hope in their hearts. If Luo Zheng returned with Xun Sha and successfully returned to the throne, they would finally be able to see the light of day again …
"Our king's soul still exists, why can't she appear?" One of the Yao Ye clan sector lords asked.
If it was before, Xun could appear as a sword spirit.
But now, Xun was just a remnant soul in Luo Zheng's body. She had even lost her memories, how could he make her appear?
"There's a problem with her soul …" Luo Zheng couldn't explain too much. If Xun didn't destroy her soul, she would end up like Yao and go against the entire universe. However, destroying her soul would cause her to lose all her memories.

"Someone from our Yao Ye clan?"
The Yao Ye clan members were all anxious …
Luo Zheng nodded and replied, "Your Yao Ye clan's Empyrean, Divine Arrow Empyrean."
The three kings were the true leaders of the Yao Ye clan, but the Empyrean's status wasn't low either. As a member of the Yao Ye clan, Divine Arrow Empyrean's status and rallying power weren't low either.
Divine Arrow Empyrean only went into hiding to avoid Yao's encirclement. He hadn't shown himself for so many years.

"I want to see him!"
"… …"

Chapter 1617
Everything within the range of the will of the world was no secret to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng had already found where Divine Arrow Empyrean was hiding.

But in the end, Luo Zheng was still an outsider. Now that Xun's soul had shown no reaction, he could only place his hopes on the Demon Night Clan's Divine Arrow Empyrean.
The Demon Night Clan members present were still a bit confused.
Divine Arrow Empyrean had disappeared for many years. After all, in a sense, following Luo Zheng was the same as betraying the Demon Night Clan. Everyone followed behind Luo Zheng and passed through this space.
This space seemed to have marked out the entire upper realm of the universe. It was like a huge map. Being in it made one feel very small.

Chapter 1618
This Purple Snow Realm was the westernmost part of the Yao Night clan's territory. There weren't any important mines or rich heaven and earth essence here. It wasn't a strategic location either.

Just now, with a tap of Luo Zheng's finger, he opened up a ten mile long well that went straight down!
When Luo Zheng reached the bottom of the well, he did the same thing again and pointed downwards. The well continued to extend downwards.

The caves a thousand miles below the ground were all formed tens of millions of years ago. There should have been a rich underground river here …
"Which path should we choose?" Someone asked.
The caves were like a natural giant maze. They suspected that Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to find the correct path.
It was indeed difficult for others, but to Luo Zheng, it couldn't be any easier.
With the memories in his mind, not only could he accurately identify each cave, he could even find out how they were formed and how they evolved.
Luo Zheng wasn't an omnipotent existence in this universe. That was an ability that only Luo Xiao had. But if the will of the world covered the entire universe, he would be in a state of "omniscience". He would be able to see through any secret in this world. This was a terrifying ability!
"Just follow." Luo Zheng replied. He chose a cave and flew towards it.
In the depths of the underground, there were already very few living beings that lived here. However, as they passed through the winding caves, they still encountered over ten different types of ferocious beasts. Most of them were not a threat to martial artists, but there was a type of frog without eyes that almost caused a Sector Lord to lose his life. These colorful little frogs had extremely terrifying venom.

When they reached the end, they realized that the cave was very flat. It was obviously carved out by human hands. It seemed like there were people living here!
Luo Zheng stepped on the ground and walked forward slowly. His face was filled with caution.

Just as Luo Zheng turned a corner, a golden stream of light suddenly shot towards him!
"Si — —"

Luo Zheng didn't even have time to react!
"It's Sun Flaming Arrow!"
The Yao Night clan fighter's excited voice came from behind!
"Divine Arrow Empyrean is here!"
Divine Arrow Empyrean's Dream God Arrow. The Yao Night clan was the most proficient archer in the universe. Before inheriting the Mandate of Heaven, Dream God Arrow was the strongest archer in the clan!
Sun Flaming Arrow was one of Dream God Arrow's ultimate techniques!
"Lord Divine Arrow! I'm Qian Ling! "
The Yao Night clan's Great Realm Lord impatiently called out. His voice reverberated throughout the cave, loud and clear.

This Great Realm Lord called Qian Ling was related to Divine Arrow Empyrean by blood!

Chapter 1619
When Qian Ling was pulled back by Luo Zheng, there was a bloody gash on her forehead and her face was deathly pale.

Qian Ling believed that her cultivation was higher than Luo Zheng's, so she should be stronger than Luo Zheng as well. If even she had almost died to the Sunflare Arrow, then Luo Zheng would be in even more danger.
Luo Zheng smiled noncommittally, then walked towards the corner.
The members of the Demon Night Clan stared at Luo Zheng with wide eyes, their eyes filled with worry. They'd followed Luo Zheng here and finally found Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial. But this Luo Zheng couldn't possibly die to an Exalted Celestial's arrow, right?
Only Liu Yu's gaze remained calm. She knew Luo Zheng's personality. He definitely wouldn't act rashly. Since he'd rushed over, he definitely had a certain level of confidence.
Luo Zheng slowly approached the corner. The instant he did, he suddenly sped up, his speed explosively increasing!
The current strength of Luo Zheng's body was no longer the same as it was in the past. What's more, he was wearing a cloak made from the quintessence of his Quintessence. It shouldn't be too much of a problem for him to block the Sunflare Arrow!
Whoosh!
As he dodged, a golden light flashed through his eyes!
The arrow was simply too fast. Even Luo Zheng couldn't react in time. He could only rely on his instincts to protect his head.
After all, his entire body was covered by the cloak. Only his head was exposed. If he were to be shot in the head by the Sunflare Arrow, that would be an injustice!
"Pa!"
Just as Luo Zheng covered his head, a heavy blow suddenly came from his body. It was as if a heavy hammer had directly hit his body. His whole body was abruptly thrown to the side of the cave.
This arrow hit Luo Zheng right in the chest!
However, it was just as the Craftsman Saint had said. Tong Xinyi was forged with the Origin Divinity, so it was impossible to destroy!
The terrifying power contained in this arrow only pushed Luo Zheng several dozen feet away.

A doubtful voice came from the darkness.

"God …"

The two Sunflare Arrows shone with dazzling golden light and hit Luo Zheng's Tong Xinyi. The golden arrow shafts were smashed into pieces, but the huge force pushed Luo Zheng back several dozen feet. He crashed heavily into the wall of the cave, causing the entire cave to shake.

The moment he broke free from the wall, a sword appeared in his hand!

Luo Zheng's Da Qian Heavy Sword suddenly danced. This heavy sword, which was comparable to a large world, was abnormally light in Luo Zheng's hand.

"Kacha, kacha!"
With two crisp sounds, Luo Zheng forcibly shattered the two Sunflare Arrows!
"What!"
An extremely surprised voice came from the darkness again.
The Yao Ye clansmen hiding on the other side had expressions of disbelief on their faces!
The Great Realm Lord Qian Ling's exquisite eyes were also wide open …
Luo Zheng waved the Da Qian Heavy Sword in his hand. When he saw the expressions of the Yao Ye clansmen, he felt a little disdainful. Wasn't it just breaking two arrows? What was there to be surprised about?
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that the name "Divine Arrow" had a great history …
The Empyrean Lords in the universe all had different weapons, but only Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow used bows and arrows as his main weapon!
Bows and arrows couldn't display the full strength of a martial artist, so even if there were Realm Lords or Empyrean Lords in the universe who were proficient in bows and arrows, they could only use bows and arrows as support.
However, Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow's "Mandate of Heaven" was called the "Eye Fixed Divine Ability". This divine ability was an extremely powerful law of karma. When it was used on his arrows, his arrows completely ignored the Laws of Space. As long as it was within the range of Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow's eyes, his arrows would definitely hit!
If Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow's eyesight could reach infinitely far and he could see to the other side of the universe, then his arrows would definitely be able to cross an infinite distance and hit a target on the other side of the universe! This was the terrifying aspect of the "Eye Fixed Divine Ability".
In the Great Evolution Universe, no one could stop his arrows. Not even the human race's upper Empyrean Lords could!
Before this, Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow's first and second arrows had been dodged by Luo Zheng and Qian Ling. Because they had hidden in a corner, they had escaped Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow's "line of sight". But now, Luo Zheng was standing completely within Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow's line of sight.
With a swing of his sword, he was actually able to break two of Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow's Sunfire Arrows. This was somewhat unimaginable.
To a certain extent, Luo Zheng had broken the karmic law of the "Eye Fixed Divine Ability". Or rather, he had broken the karmic law of the universe!
Luo Zheng held the Great Thousand Heavy Sword horizontally in front of his chest and shouted into the darkness, "Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow, I am Luo Zheng. I am … fuck!"
As the golden light flashed in front of him, Luo Zheng flipped over and pulled the Great Thousand Heavy Sword in his hand. As the longsword swung through the air, it accurately broke the two Sunfire Arrows again!
At this moment, Luo Zheng was somewhat impatient. This Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow was hiding in the darkness and shooting at him with all sorts of sneak attacks …
"Whoosh!"
With the Great Thousand Heavy Sword in hand, Luo Zheng's figure began to rapidly dash into the darkness.
Every time a golden light flashed in the darkness, Luo Zheng's Great Thousand Heavy Sword was able to capture the trajectory of the golden light and easily cut it into several pieces …
Finally, Luo Zheng rushed into the darkness and raised the Great Thousand Heavy Sword in the air, pressing it against the enemy's neck.
"Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow, I didn't mean to offend you. I am …"
"You are Luo Zheng. I already know." The old voice resounded in the darkness. Suddenly, the light became brighter and brighter, revealing a lonely and thin old man.
This old man was sitting on a stone bed with a longbow in his hand. He was like a person playing a zither, but his eyes were abnormally calm.
The old man's appearance was very old, but his eyes were very clear. It was as if he had the eyes of a goshawk!
"Since you know, why do you still …" Luo Zheng asked again.
Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow smiled and placed the longbow to the side. Luo Zheng also put the Great Thousand Heavy Sword back into its scabbard. After all, this sword was so heavy that Luo Zheng had to hold it in his hand for a long time.
"I'm very curious. How did you break my arrow?" Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
Ever since Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow inherited the Mandate of Heaven, as long as someone was within his range of sight, no one could dodge his arrow. This was because of the law of karma. Because he was looking at the enemy, his arrow could not be dodged. This was the tyrannical power of the law of karma! As long as the 'cause' condition was met, the 'effect' would occur.
Although this Sunfire Arrow was not Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow's strongest archery skill, the law of karma was still the law of karma. It could not be violated. Therefore, Luo Zheng's performance was destined to shock Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow!
Hearing the conversation between Luo Zheng and Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow, the members of the Yao Ye Clan all rushed out from around the corner.
Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow's gaze was focused on Luo Zheng. "Can you tell this old man how you did it?"
Luo Zheng cupped his hands and said, "This … forgive this junior for not being able to tell you. There are some secrets involved."
In truth, Luo Zheng did not break the law of karma of the 'eye fixation divine ability'. He had used another method to break Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow's Sunfire Arrow. He had relied on the will of the Vast Thousand Realms and the memories of the Vast Thousand Realms.
Luo Zheng was in a state of omniscience. When Empyrean Lord Divine Arrow raised his hand, he already knew when he had shot the arrow and where the arrow's target was.

Chapter 1620
Divine Arrow took a deep look at Luo Zheng.
Back when Luo Zheng brought Xun into the universe, he had been paying close attention to any news regarding Luo Zheng.
Divine Arrow had been hiding deep underground for many years and had never seen the genius born in this great era.
This was the first time he had met Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng had actually used a heavy sword to break his Red Sun Arrow, breaking his Heavenly Fate's "Eye Fixation Divine Ability". What Divine Arrow Celestial Master felt was extreme shock!
The Fixed Gaze Divine Ability's karmic laws were bestowed by the heavens. How could it be broken so easily?
Yet, this youth had actually managed to break it by accident.
Luo Zheng refused to tell him the secret, so there was nothing Divine Arrow could do to Luo Zheng. Divine Arrow's gaze swept past Luo Zheng and swept across the many members of the Yao Night Clan behind him. He said, "I didn't expect the Yao Night Clan to have reached this point. It seems their foundation is already unstable …"
Ever since the battle between Yao and Xun began, the entire Fiend Night Clan had fallen into a state of internal strife. Now, the two remaining kings of the Fiend Night Clan were going against the heavens and forming an alliance with the Sacred Race. That was why Divine Arrow Empyrean said those words.
The Demon Night Clan's Great Realm Lord, Qian Ling, took a step forward and bowed deeply towards Divine Arrow Heavenly Sovereign, "Divine Arrow Heavenly Sovereign, the Demon Night Clan has fallen into a state of fire and water under that woman's control. I hope that Divine Arrow Heavenly Sovereign can help the Sacred Spear Sect!"
Hearing Qian Ling's words, Shui Ying and Feng Ling, who were following behind the group, revealed unnatural expressions.
Nearly half of the Yao Night Clan members present were from the Sacred Spear Sect, but not all of them.
Shui Ying and Feng Ling, for example, had followed Luo Zheng here. On one hand, they did not agree with Yao's current actions. On the other hand, all the prisoners on Cage Island had escaped. In the future, they would not be able to escape responsibility.
However, they were still members of the Holy Chain Sect. Since they were young, they had always believed in the King of Punishment. When they heard a member of the Yao Ye Clan describe their King in such a manner, they couldn't help but feel uncomfortable. However, deep in their hearts, they felt that Qian Ling was speaking the truth.
"I know." Divine Arrow Celestial Master nodded. Although he was hiding underground, the Roaring Command was pervasive. He was no stranger to the situation in the universe. "Where is Xun? Why didn't she come out to see me? "
Divine Arrow Celestial Master was naturally most concerned about Smog. He had been in hiding for many years and hadn't made a sound. When Luo Zheng returned to the universe with Smog, Divine Arrow Celestial Master was the first to respond.
At this moment, Luo Zheng had already arrived. Why didn't he see Xun?
The question that Exalted Celestial Divine Arrow asked was actually the question that all of the members of the Yao Ye Clan wanted to ask …
On Cage Island, they had clearly sensed Smoky's aura. Why didn't Smoky appear?
Upon hearing Divine Arrow Celestial Venerable's question, Luo Zheng's face turned gloomy.
When Divine Arrow Celestial Master saw Luo Zheng's expression, he realized that something was wrong and asked in a stern voice, "What happened to Smog?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed and a red light shot out from his eyes. Luo Zheng moved Smog's soul back into her body. At the same time, Luo Zheng also summoned his sword spirit. Smog's soul and sword spirit merged into one. This was the form that Smog had always maintained.
However, at this moment, Smog was floating in front of Luo Zheng with her eyes closed. She was like a marionette and didn't react at all.
"Plop!"
"Plop!"
"Plop …"
Behind Luo Zheng, Great Realm Lord Qian Ling, Liu Yu, and the other members of the Yao Ye Clan all knelt on one knee. Even Shui Ying and Feng Ling also knelt on one knee after hesitating for a moment. After all, Smog was the king of their Yao Ye Clan.
"How could this be?" When Divine Arrow Celestial Master saw this, he was quite confused. Smog's soul wasn't completely destroyed, so it wasn't completely silent. This Celestial Master could clearly feel the fluctuations of Smog's soul!
Smog's soul wasn't completely destroyed. It was just that the Soul Sealing Needle was located in the depths of her soul. In order to destroy the Soul Sealing Needle, all of Smog's memories had to be sacrificed. In other words, Smog's soul was now like a piece of white paper. There was no imprint on it, so naturally, there wouldn't be any reaction.
"Her memories have been destroyed along with the Soul Sealing Needle …"
Luo Zheng then told Divine Arrow Celestial Master about the Soul Sealing Needle and Mu Haiji's plan.
After hearing this, Divine Arrow Celestial Master let out a deep sigh. "So that's how it is … I've always thought that no matter how crazy Yao is, she wouldn't lead the Yao Ye Clan to this step! To think that our Yao Ye Clan was hiding such a secret! "
Yao was indeed very ambitious, but her ambition had always been restrained within the Yao Ye Clan.
But now, Yao was going against the entire universe and wanted to lead the Yao Ye Clan into the abyss. This madness was a bit abnormal …
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the Yao Ye Clan members kneeling behind him came to a sudden realization. Indeed, none of them would've thought that their Yao Ye Clan wasn't born in this universe!
"But now that Smog has fallen into such a situation, what should we do?" Divine Arrow Celestial Master asked as he looked at Smog's sword spirit body.
"I'm not sure either." Luo Zheng shook his head.
The soul was originally very complex and mysterious. No one in this universe had ever thoroughly researched it, let alone the memories within the soul.
How were they supposed to make up for the lost memories? Luo Zheng had no idea at all.
"I'll do my best to help her recover, but that's only if I can find a way." After saying this, Smog's sword spirit body instantly turned into countless red dots that drilled into Luo Zheng's body. At the same time, Smog's soul also returned to Luo Zheng's mind. Luo Zheng continued, "But right now, I think that since Divine Arrow Celestial Master can reappear, the Yao Ye Clan needs another leader."
Divine Arrow Celestial Master used to be the Celestial Master that protected Smog, and he was quite famous in the Yao Ye Clan.
According to the news Luo Zheng received, the Sacred Spear Sect had already fallen apart among the six factions under the Three Kings. The other five factions were also quite dissatisfied with Yao and Ying. Although the elites of these five factions still followed Yao and Ying and fought in the great world of the Devil Race, they were already faintly regretting it.
Unfortunately, the two Demon Kings were the absolute rulers of the Yao Ye Clan, and even the Celestial Masters followed them. Without a leader, it was very difficult for these people to resist the two Demon Kings!
If Divine Arrow Celestial Master appeared under Smog's banner, the situation would be different. This gave the Yao Ye Clan members a second choice!
"Haha …" Divine Arrow Celestial Master suddenly laughed loudly. There was even a hint of sadness in his laughter.
Luo Zheng stared at him and asked, "Divine Arrow Celestial Master, you're not willing?"
"This old man … has waited for this day for countless years!" Divine Arrow Celestial Master suddenly said. His hoarse voice suddenly became excited, and he raised his voice by at least twice as much. "Come with me!"
At this moment, this old Celestial Master seemed to explode with boundless vitality.
He abruptly got up and lifted the stone bed under his body with one hand.
Clang!
The heavy stone bed fell to the ground with a loud sound. Beneath the stone bed was a flight of stairs that led downwards. It turned out that there was a cave beneath the stone bed.
The moment Divine Arrow Celestial Master lifted the stairs to the cave, a thick smell of blood wafted out.
Divine Arrow Celestial Master took the lead and walked down. Luo Zheng followed closely behind him, walking down the stairs …
Although the other Yao Ye Clan members were curious, they didn't dare to follow.
After walking down the stairs, Luo Zheng discovered that the cave wasn't very large. In the center of the cave, there were three crimson bone arrows stabbed into the ground!
When Luo Zheng's gaze fell on these bone arrows, Luo Zheng felt a chill run down his spine. He felt that if these bone arrows hit him, they would definitely cause him great injuries or even kill him!
Seeing Luo Zheng's gaze, Divine Arrow Celestial Master lightly said, "These three bone arrows were made from my ribs. They are called the Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrows."
Staring at these bone arrows, Luo Zheng asked in confusion, "These three bone arrows … I feel as if they are alive?"
He could feel the aura of life from them.
Divine Arrow Celestial Master nodded and smiled. "These three Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrows are connected to my life force. Every time I shoot an arrow, my life force will decline by a third. Once these three arrows are shot, my life will come to an end!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face revealed a moved expression.
Arrows forged with the life of an Empyrean?
Just how powerful were these arrows?
Divine Arrow Celestial Master revealed a gratified expression. "Ever since I hid here, I have been forging these three bone arrows. Now, the day has finally come …"

Chapter 1621
Seeing the determination in Divine Arrow's eyes, Luo Zheng understood that this Empyrean was not hiding here because he was afraid of Yao or the Empyrean behind her.
His destiny was bestowed upon him by Xun. The only reason he was living underground was to help Xun take revenge!
"I, Divine Arrow Empyrean, came from the Yao Night Clan. I have never thought of betraying my clan." Dream God Arrow stared at Luo Zheng and said with determination, "However, if you want to lead my clan astray, I, Dream God Arrow, will be the first to oppose it!"
Divine Arrow gently patted the short bow in his hand, and the three Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrows trembled. After shaking for a while, they rose from the ground and flew toward Divine Arrow Holy Master.
"Pu pu pu!"
The Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrows shot directly into his chest and drilled into his body. However, they did not leave behind any wounds.
Divine Arrow did not even turn his head as he walked up the stairs. His entire body was emitting a sharp aura.
It had only been two hours since Luo Zheng came to find him. However, this Divine Arrow Empyrean who had been silent for countless years had already undergone a fundamental change. He was like an old arrow, a silent arrow. However, this did not mean that he had lost his former sharpness. As long as there was the slightest chance, he would burn away all of his life force!
Empyrean Divine Arrow appeared once more in front of the 3,000 Night Demon Clansmen. Within his wrinkled eye sockets, his pupils seemed as if they would burn. Although his appearance was old, one could still see the handsomeness of Dream God Arrow when he was young.
"You were all saved by Luo Zheng from Cage Island," Divine Arrow Empyrean said indifferently. "As a Heavenly Empyrean of the Yao Night Clan, I am not in charge of punishment. However, the King of Punishment has led my clan astray, which is very unfair. No matter what crimes you have committed in the past, I will forgive you in the future! I will pardon you of your crimes! "
The Yao Night Clan respected order. Even if they had escaped from Cage Island, since Yao had declared them guilty, they were still guilty.
Thus, Divine Arrow Empyrean still needed to follow this procedure. As a Heavenly Empyrean, he had the qualifications to do so.
"Right now, our race is in chaos because of the two kings. If everyone here were to follow me, do you know that the process would be extremely dangerous?" Divine Arrow Celestial asked.

"I'm willing!"
"I'm willing!"

The people on Cage Island had suffered all kinds of torture. They had tried countless times to free their lives but failed. Now that they could follow the Celestial Venerate, they would not hesitate to give up their lives for this great cause that could not be completed!
Empyrean Divine Arrow nodded and then muttered in a low voice, "Glory to the King!"
"Glory is my life!" His voice gradually grew louder, echoing in the spacious cave.
"With eternal slaughter, we guard the borders of our race. With merciless slaughter, we defend the prosperity of our race …"
As Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial spoke, the Demon Night Clansmen of the Sacred Spear Sect also began to softly chant.
This was the declaration of the Sacred Spear Sect!
For example, the declaration of the Sacred Chain Sect also began with "Glory to the King. Glory is my life." However, the next sentence was "With just punishment, we maintain the order of our race …"
Now that Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial had come out, he naturally used the title of the King of Slaughter to gather as many Demon Night Clansmen as possible under his banner …
Half a day later …
Under Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial's lead, many Demon Night Clansmen appeared in the Purple Snow Realm.
They traveled about twenty thousand kilometers north of the vast snowy mountains. Then, they appeared in front of the Demon Night Clan's Sacred Land.
The entire Purple Snow Realm was covered in purple snow. Only the Demon Night Clan's Sacred Land was filled with greenery. The purple snowflakes that floated in the air would quickly melt and disappear.
This was because the Purple Snow Realm was located at the edge of a great world. It was extremely barren and the strongest Sacred Land in the entire great world was only an eighth-grade Sacred Land. The Demon Night Clan's Sacred Land in front of them was the only eighth-grade Sacred Land. It was called the Purple Cloud Sacred Land.
This time, the Demon Night Clan was conscripting their elites. But, these elites were mostly Divine Transformation realm powerhouses and World Lords. The Purple Cloud Sacred Land didn't do anything at all. After all, the Sacred Lord of the Purple Cloud Sacred Land had just stepped into the Divine Transformation realm and didn't have the qualifications to be conscripted.
After the Purple Snow Realm entered winter, there would be small scale beast hordes that would erupt from the north. The Purple Cloud Sacred Land was established here to block these beast hordes. Thus, every winter, the Purple Cloud Sacred Land would be fully armed. All enchantments would be opened and all Demon Night Clan warriors would be on standby.
At the edge of the Sacred Land, dozens of Demon Night Clan martial artists held spears in their hands as they stood guard in front of a wooden house.
"Look! What is that!? "A Demon Night Clan warrior shouted out loud. He raised his spear and pointed it towards the horizon!
A large group of black dots appeared at the peak of the snowy mountain. Because they were too far away and it was difficult to see, the Demon Night Clan warriors mistook them for a flock of birds!
Birds that could fly through the snowy mountain were extremely fierce vicious beasts!
"Be on guard!"
"Wu wu …"
A Demon Night Clan member blew his horn.
The trees in the Holy Land began to shake, and the sound of agility interspersed among them. From time to time, the sound of bowstrings being pulled taut could be heard …
Sou sou sou …
From the depths of the Purple Cloud Sacred Land, several beams of light shot out.
The leader was a Demon Night Clan woman wearing a red battle robe and holding a blood red spear. She was the Purple Cloud Sacred Land's Sacred Lord, Flowsand.
She leapt onto the tallest tree and looked into the distance. Her eyes flashed with a red light; it was clear that she was using some sort of supernatural power.
As she looked around, Sacred Lord Flowsand revealed a puzzled expression.
"Sacred Lord, are those Blood Corpse Rocs?" A Sacred Land Elder below asked.
Blood Corpse Rocs were extremely fierce and violent birds. They were infamous in the Purple Snow Realm. They would often appear in flocks. In the past, a hundred or so Blood Corpse Rocs would cause quite a bit of damage to the Purple Snow Sacred Land. If there really were a few thousand of them, their rank 8 Sacred Land would be in trouble!
Sacred Lord Flowsand shook her head. Her expression became more and more puzzled, "No, no … it's a group of Demon Night Clan members!"
"What?" Hearing the Holy Lord's words, the elders of the Holy Land below revealed strange expressions.
At this time, it was impossible for Demon Night Clan members to come. The Elders all suspected that the Sacred Lord was mistaken.
However, Flowsand's tone became more and more resolute. She looked again and could even tell their cultivation level, "They are … very strong. There seems to be a Sector Lord among them!"
"Sector Lord?" The Elders below opened their eyes wide.
There were indeed many Sector Lords in the universe. Added together, there were hundreds of thousands of them. However, they shouldn't exist in the Purple Snow Realm.
The prerequisite for a Sector Lord was to have a rank 10 Sacred Land. For example, in their rank 8 Sacred Land, even if there were some peerless geniuses, they would be selected before they reached adulthood and leave the Sacred Land …
Thus, many people in the Purple Snow Sacred Land had never seen a Sector Lord in their entire lives.
"There seems to be more than one." Flowsand's expression became more and more puzzled. At the same time, she revealed a look of vigilance.
Although they were all from the Demon Night Clan, who knew what these people were doing? It was possible that they were enemies and not friends.
"How many are there?" The Elder asked again.
"Eight, no, nine … wait!" Flowsand's eyes flashed red. Her voice unconsciously became louder and her expression became more and more surprised, "So many Sector Lords? There seem to be two Great Sector Lords among them! "
Below Flowsand, the Purple Cloud Sacred Land Elders were all dumbfounded.
As for the Demon Night Clan members present, they stared blankly at the black dot in the sky. It seemed that this group of people was extremely fast. It wouldn't be long before they entered the Purple Cloud Sacred Land.
"What, what do we do?" An Elder said anxiously.
Flowsand retracted her gaze and closed her eyes for a while. When she opened them again, the red light in her eyes had disappeared. Her expression was calm as she jumped down from the tree.
"What can we do? No matter who they are, if they want to enter our Purple Cloud Sacred Land, we will welcome them! "Flowsand said indifferently.
Facing a group with a Great Sector Lord, the Purple Cloud Sacred Land had no choice but to welcome them.

Chapter 1622
In this group of 3000, there were close to 1000 sector lords.
The remaining fighters were mostly at the Divine Transformation realm. The one with the lowest cultivation was Liu Yu beside Luo Zheng. Other than that, there was also Qian Ling and another great sector lord …
Of course, the one with the highest cultivation was naturally the leader, Divine Arrow Heavenly Sovereign!
As they got closer, the elders of the Purple Cloud Sacred Land could clearly see them. Their eyes were filled with more and more shock.
Even in the scale of the entire Yao Night clan, 1000 sector lords was definitely not a small faction!
Every sector lord had the qualifications to become the sacred leader of a rank 10 sacred land. Their Purple Cloud Sacred Land was not even worth mentioning in front of these people. The people of the Purple Cloud Sacred Land felt more and more uneasy.
Sou sou sou …
Not long after, the 3000 people slowly descended on the Purple Cloud Sacred Land.
Purple Cloud Sacred Leader Flowsand took a few steps forward and bowed towards the crowd, "I am Purple Cloud Sacred Land's Sacred Leader Flowsand. My fellow clansmen have come to visit, please excuse me for not going out to welcome you!"
The elders behind Flowsand all bowed as well. As for the clansmen behind them, they began to whisper amongst themselves …
"These people's auras are so strong!"
"Nonsense, they are all sector lords!"
"What's the cultivation of that old man in the lead? He seems to be their leader? Is he also a great sector lord? "
The Yao Night clan warriors lowered their voices. But how sensitive were the eyes and ears of the people present? They could even hear the chirping of insects tens of miles away. When Flowsand heard these voices, her face revealed an awkward expression. She turned around and shouted, "Silence!"
Under the sacred leader's scolding, it instantly became quiet. Only palm-sized purple snow could be heard falling to the ground, creating a rustling sound.
Flowsand once again turned around and looked at Divine Arrow Heavenly Sovereign with a puzzled look on her face …
The Yao Night clansmen were extremely familiar with the three kings. Whether it was Xun, Yao, or Ying, the moment they appeared in front of them, they would be recognized by the clansmen. This was because the three kings that they had worshipped since they were young were extremely familiar with their appearances and auras.
But Heavenly Sovereigns were different. Many Heavenly Sovereigns had only heard of their names. Most of the Yao Night clansmen would never have the chance to meet them in their entire lives.
Moreover, the difference between an Empyrean and a Great Realm Lord wasn't in terms of cultivation, but in terms of destiny …
Moreover, Divine Arrow Empyrean had been hiding for so many years. It was normal for Flowsand not to recognize him. Therefore, Flowsand thought that Divine Arrow Empyrean was the third Great Realm Master.
Aside from Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial, the other members of the Demon Night Clan were still looking at Luo Zheng. Why was there a human among them? Wasn't the Fiend Night Clan going to attack the human race? Could this person be a captive? From the looks of it, it didn't seem like it.
Divine Arrow Holy Master didn't waste any time. He looked at the people in front of him and said, "I want to borrow your king statue."
Flowsand and the elders' expressions changed slightly when they heard this. The King Statues were the core of the Nightfiend clan, and all the King Statues were connected to each other. Unless there was something urgent, the Nightfiend clan wouldn't use the King Statues. Moreover, these people were so strong, but they didn't know what their goal was.
Flowsand couldn't refuse, so she could only ask about the other party's background. "May I ask which member of our clan you are? Which of the six great sects do you belong to? "
Based on Flowsand's experience, only the six great sects were capable of gathering so many experts. However, the six great sects had accompanied the two Kings into battle and entered the Demon World. How could they have appeared here?
Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial calmly replied, "The Holy Spear Sect."
"Holy Spear Sect?" Flowsand was stunned. Although she wasn't strong, as a Holy Master, she could still obtain some information. Wasn't the Holy Spear Sect already destroyed …?
Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial then added, "The Dream God Arrow."
Holy Spear Sect, Dream God Arrow …
"Ah …" Flowsand's expression froze.
Many of the elders present also looked as if they had seen a ghost. However, there were still a few who were confused. The Dream God Arrow had been hidden for a long time, so the elders had naturally forgotten that there was an Empyrean in the Demon Night Clan. It wasn't until someone poked them in the waist and whispered, "It's Lord Empyrean." Only then did the elders wake up from their daze.
"Lord Empyrean!"
Flowsand suddenly knelt down on one knee and bowed towards the Dream God Arrow.
The elders behind her naturally followed suit and knelt down as well …
In the Demon Night Clan, no matter their position, even in front of a Holy Master, they only needed to bow. Only the three Kings and the Empyrean could make them bow so deeply!
The Dream God Arrow nodded. "Rise. Lead us to the King Statue!"
Flowsand suddenly stood up and cupped her hands towards the Dream God Arrow. "Lord Empyrean, please follow me!"
As soon as she finished speaking, Flowsand led the way while Luo Zheng, Liu Yu, Qian Ling, and the others followed closely behind her. The 3,000 members of the Daynight Clan followed behind them in a formidable array ….
Along the way, Flowsand's heart was filled with apprehension.
The Dream God Arrow had been hidden for so many years. Although the clan knew that it was because of the conflict between the two Kings, under Yao's rule, the Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial was described as a traitor. Now that the Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial had suddenly appeared, she didn't know what he was up to!
If a small Purple Cloud Sacred Land like them were to get involved in the conflict between the top figures, their end would be quite ugly.
However, this thought only lingered in her mind for a moment before disappearing without a trace. No matter what the outcome was, it wasn't something that a mere rank 8 Sacred Land like her could control …
Very quickly, Flowsand led the Dream God Arrow and the others to the Purple Cloud Sacred Land's Saint Tree.
This Saint Tree was very similar to the one Luo Zheng had seen on the Sea God Continent. However, it was much more majestic!
In a spacious hall, three gigantic King Statues stood silently. These three King Statues were golden and translucent, as if they were carved from some kind of precious amber. The Demon Night Clan had tens of thousands of King Statues. They were all made from different materials, but they were all made from the best materials they could find.
After Flowsand led the Dream God Arrow and the others to this place, she stood to the side and didn't leave.
Luo Zheng's gaze fell on the Smoked Statue. At this moment, the statue didn't seem to have any abnormalities. This time, the Soul Sealing Needle hadn't damaged the Soul Sealing Needle too much. However, the Soul Sealing Needle had completely destroyed the Soul Sealing Needle. That was the biggest problem.
After all, if the Soul Sealing Needle was damaged, Luo Zheng could find a way to repair it. With the help of the Demi-Immortal Clan, it wouldn't be too difficult.
However, the Soul Sealing Needle's memories were simply too mysterious. He couldn't think of any way to repair them ….
Divine Arrow looked at the statue for a moment, then turned to Luo Zheng and said, "Get ready. The next step will cause a huge commotion in the Demon Night Clan."
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. "I'm ready."
Whether it was the operation against the Demon Night Clan or the final battle against the Sacred Race, there would always be a step forward.
What he needed to do was face it head-on. At this moment, he wasn't nervous at all.
"Cause a huge commotion? What is Divine Arrow planning …. "When Flowsand heard this, her heart trembled.
Divine Arrow reached out towards the Smoked King Statue and placed his hand on the statue's forehead!
Bzzz!
Suddenly, the King Statue began to emit a demonic red light, as if it had come to life.
At the same time ….
In all of the Demon Night Clan's Holy Lands, from the Upper Realm to the Lower Realm, the statues of the King of Slaughter began to tremble.
The most crude of these statues were carved from stone, while others were forged from gold. There were also statues made from crystal, wood, bronze …. There were all sorts of materials, and at this moment, all of them began to emit a demonic red light.
In addition to the statues in the Holy Lands, the two groups led by Ying and Jade also had people carrying the King Statues on their backs. After all, praying to the King Statues every day was compulsory for the entire Demon Night Clan. The King Statues also began to change!
"It's up to you," said Divine Arrow.
Luo Zheng nodded, then took two steps forward to face the King Statue.
