After doing this, Luo Zheng took two more steps back. Now, it was Divine Arrow's turn.
At this moment, Divine Arrow was going to represent Smoked and issue a King Edict to the entire Demon Night Clan. Only the three Kings were qualified to issue such an edict.

Chapter 1623
Blood Demon world, Blood Plains …
The Blood Plains was known to be cursed by the gods, if one dug up the soil, there would be red blood flowing out, the entire plains would be barren all year round.
However, ever since the Yao Night clan took over this place, thick purple trees began to grow on the Blood Plains!
These purple trees had leaves that were as sharp as blades, wrapping around the entire tree, forming a natural fortress.
These trees were called Jade Feather Trees, a plant unique to the Yao Night clan. From a palm sized seed, it would only take a few days to grow into a 400 to 500m tall tree!
The sharpness of the leaves on the Emerald Feather Tree's crown was comparable to that of a sacred weapon.
This tree was impervious to water and fire. No matter if the seeds were buried in solid rocks or bitter seawater, it could still spread its well-developed roots and grow healthily.
So the Jade Feather Trees were the palaces of the Yao Night clan!
While they were in a stalemate with the demons, the Yao Night clan set up camp on the Blood Plains. In less than half a month, the Blood Plains were filled with Jade Feather Trees …
"Sou!"
In the sky above the Blood Demon world, dozens of silver light dots suddenly appeared.
These circular spots of light were like suns, illuminating the dark Blood Demon World as if it was daytime. They streaked across the sky, past the mountains and rivers, and headed straight for the Blood Plains. Their target was the Emerald Feather Trees on the Blood Plains!
The first dot of light exploded on the plains, forming a powerful ball of annihilation that enveloped over ten Emerald Feather Trees.
"Dong!"
Following a slight explosion, the dozen or so Emerald Feather Trees completely disappeared!
Every Jade Feather Tree had over a thousand Yao Ye clansmen guarding it. When this sphere of light smashed down, the Yao Ye clan lost over 10,000 people, including many sector lords!
On the tallest Jade Feather Tree on the Blood Plains, a blue clothed woman suddenly waved a flail in her hand!
The Law of Thunder rushed out, condensed into thistle-like bolts of lightning, and shot towards the round light spot in the sky that had yet to fall.
"Pa pa pa …"
"Dong dong dong dong dong …"
Every time a lightning tendril came into contact with a circular light spot, the light spot would explode in an instant!
As all of the circular motes of light exploded in the air, the light became 10 times brighter than before, and intense heat spread out. The Halcyon Feather Trees closest to the circular motes of light were instantly set ablaze by the heat, turning into crackling fire trees. Many of the Yao Ye clan members escaped from the Halcyon Feather Trees, accompanied by cries of agony.
…
Whoosh whoosh whoosh!
Three streaks of light flew towards the largest emerald feather tree. They were the three sector lords of the Yao Night clan. The moment they reached the top of the tree, they knelt down in front of the blue robed lady.
"My king! Setting up camp on the Blood Plains was part of the Devil Race's plan. In just a few days, more than half of our people have been killed or injured. Can we retreat first? "
"Your Majesty, the Demon Race may seem reckless, but they are actually very cunning. They are trying to exhaust our Demon Night Clan here!"
"Your Majesty …"
The three sector lords of the Yao Ye clan were the three main generals of the Yao Ye clan's expedition army!
After entering the Blood Devil Realm, the Yao Ye clan had suffered heavy casualties. They saw it with their own eyes and felt pain in their hearts!
On the surface, the Yao Ye clan was stronger than the Demon Race, whether it was in terms of intelligence or strength. However, when the battle truly began, the Yao Ye clan realized that the Demon Race's tenacity was far beyond their imagination.
The Demon Race that looked stupid and ugly suddenly seemed to be enlightened and began to be resourceful. This was something that the Yao Ye clan didn't expect.
The Demon Race had set up a powerful barrier in the Blood Devil Realm and left the Blood Plains to the Yao Ye clan. They were using the Yao Ye clan as living targets.
Under the Demon Race's bombardment, the Empyrean Exalts might not be afraid, but the Yao Ye clan's lower level cultivators would suffer heavy losses …
Yao Yao was standing at the end of the branch. Her long dress was hanging down from the trunk for a distance of several feet. Both of her feet were only standing on a branch as thick as a little finger. It was as if she was weightless.
Despite the persuasion of the three generals, her face remained unmoved. "I understand. You may leave!"
"Your Majesty!"
"Your Majesty!"
Despair flashed across the faces of the three generals.
As members of the Yao Ye clan, they were absolutely loyal to the ruler of their clan.
Even if Yao Ye had suddenly turned against them and led the Yao Ye clan to become enemies with the Sacred Race, they wouldn't hesitate in the slightest …
However, Yao Ye didn't have the slightest bit of pity or love for her own people. She had slaughtered her way through the Demon Race's great world, practically using the lives of countless Yao Ye clan members to pave this path!
Yao Ye's current actions caused the hearts of her clansmen to turn cold!
"Leave!" Seeing the three generals still kneeling in front of her, Yao Ye's face flashed with a hint of ruthlessness.
However, the three generals still didn't move. Their faces were filled with despair as they stared fixedly at Yao Ye.
"What … do you want to do?" A cold smile suddenly appeared on Yao's face. An invisible pressure spread out, and in the surrounding space, there were faint crackling sounds of lightning.
Everyone knew what the Punishment King's methods were, but the three generals had already thrown caution to the wind.
Seeing their clansmen fall one by one and suffer such an unexpected calamity was worse than death. In order to persuade their king, they were willing to bear Yao Ye's punishment!
"Your Majesty! We beg you to temporarily retreat from the Demon Race's vanguard! "The leading general continued.
"Pa!"
Streaks of lightning suddenly appeared on the general's body and crazily flowed through her body. Under the lightning's devouring, the general suddenly rolled and then hung motionlessly on the side of a tree. Her entire body trembled, and a burnt smell came from her body.
Yao Ye's expression was cold as she looked at the other two. The two Great Realm Lords' expressions were still resolute, and they didn't waver in the slightest even after seeing their comrade's miserable state …
"Ai …" Seeing this, Yao Ye deeply sighed. "In two more days, Ying's team should be able to reach the Blood Devil Realm. Then, we'll be able to break through this barrier. I believe that the road ahead will be unobstructed!"
"The road ahead? Next, we'll have to face the Demi-Immortal Tribe? Or even Gods Have No Thoughts? "The general questioned.
In fact, the strongest team of the Fiend Night Clan should be the King of Slaughter, who was Smoky and the Sacred Spear Sect that she led. They were the strongest swords of the Fiend Night Clan. However, after Smoky fell from the throne, the Sacred Spear Sect either died or was imprisoned.
Given the Demonnight Clan's current strength, it would be very difficult for them to deal with any one of the major human factions. Not to mention, the three major human factions and the Demi-Immortals had long since joined forces. The Demonnight Clan was like a moth flying into a flame!
"The Demi-Immortal Tribe won't just watch from the sidelines. The final victory will belong to us," Yao Ye replied.
"Does this victory mean anything to us?" The general gritted his teeth.
Yao Ye paused …
Meaning …
To the Demon Night clan, it was indeed meaningless.
But to the Demi-Immortal Tribe, to the person who had planted the Soul-Sealing Needle in her and created the Demon Night clan, it was meaningful!
This was Yao Ye's current mission.
As such, she didn't feel the slightest bit of pity for the deaths of two or three of her clansmen.
"You don't need meaning," Yao Ye replied coldly.
"Our Queen only needs us to die?" The general asked.
"Yes," Yao Ye replied coldly.
"Your Majesty, you've gone mad!" The general suddenly rebuked.
No one from the Demon Night clan had ever dared to speak to their Queen like this. Even if some of their fellow clansmen had committed heinous crimes and betrayed the Demon Night clan, they still wouldn't be dissatisfied with their Queen …
But now, the general was reprimanding the King of Punishment. This was something that had never happened before in the Demon Night clan.
"How dare you!"
"Pa!"
A bolt of lightning flashed in Yao Ye's eyes. The general was struck by lightning and fell to the ground …
There was only one general left in front of Yao Ye.
"You want to reprimand this Queen as well?" Yao Ye stared at the general and asked.
Just as the general was about to speak …
But at that moment, an intense killing intent erupted from the Emerald Feather Trees.
As the palace of the Daydream Clan, every single Emerald Feather Tree had a king statue enshrined within it. At this moment, the king statue that belonged to the King of Slaughter was emanating intense red light, and then an ancient yet familiar voice sounded out.
"I am the Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial. On behalf of the King of Massacre, I am here to issue a decree!"

Chapter 1624
If he wanted to spread his voice across the entire Yao Night Clan, he could do so with his command.
However, issuing a King's Edict was a ritual of the Yao Night Clan, and it had to be done through the King Statue.
At this moment, regardless of whether it was the upper or lower dimensions, the Hallowed Grounds, all the clansmen of the Yao Night Clan had been alarmed ….
"It's the Dream God Arrow! The Dream God Arrow has returned? "
"Why is he issuing a King's Edict on behalf of the King of Massacre?"
"What is he trying to do?"
"......"
Some of the elders in the Yao Night Clan were paying close attention to this matter. When they heard the words' King's Edict ', they realized that a great change was about to occur in the Yao Night Clan!
"On behalf of the King of Massacre, I declare the King of Life, Ying, and the King of Punishment, Yao, traitors! They have been controlled by outsiders, and they have led our clan to betray the Great Yan Realm and lead our clan to destruction! "
Divine Arrow's voice echoed throughout the Yao Night Clan's Sacred Grounds.
All of the Yao Night Clan members were visibly moved!
They hadn't expected Divine Arrow's King's Edict to be so strict.
"The people of the Demon Night Clan should understand the current situation in the universe. They should follow the King of Slaughter and withdraw from this war. They should regroup and recuperate to become stronger once again …" Divine Arrow continued.
The three Kings were actually equal in status. Thus, the King of Massacre didn't have the right to declare the crimes of the other two Kings. This sort of King's Edict was invalid.
However, Divine Arrow knew that the Yao Night Clan members had reached the limit of their patience with Ying and Yao.
Perhaps the people of the Yao Night Clan were extremely loyal and filled with faith in their King, but their faith wasn't unlimited.
Previously, Xun hadn't made an appearance, and Ying and Yao had started a war. They'd already lost their trust in their clan. However, the people of the Yao Night Clan were born to follow their King. When Yao and Ying acted in concert, they had no other choice.
However, the King of Massacre's appearance had given them a second choice. A second path.
If they didn't betray their clan, it was very easy for them to make this choice. That was to follow the King of Massacre …
Sacred Master Flowsand of the Purple Cloud Sacred Ground was dumbfounded when he heard Heavenly Venerate Divine Arrow's words.
Flowsand understood that since Empyrean Divine Arrow had led everyone here, he would surely cause a great commotion. It was likely that he would be drawn into the dispute between the Kings. But, she never imagined that Empyrean Divine Arrow would directly declare the other two Kings as traitors!
The severity of the situation had far exceeded her imagination!
After Divine Arrow Celestial Master finished speaking, Luo Zheng smiled and said, "I believe that woman will fight back soon."
Empyrean Divine Arrow's complexion was light. "What I fear is that she won't fight back. Our people are intelligent and they are the most loyal warriors, but they are not fools. They know what choice they should make."
What Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerate was doing right now was like peeling a fruit. He was stripping Ying and Yao from the Demon Night Clan and then thinking of a way to kill them.
What he did was only the first step …
"Clang!"
The statue of the king was smashed into smithereens, the severed head rolled in front of Yao Yao. With a stomp, the head was smashed into pieces!
At this moment, Yao's face was ashen, and that beautiful face of hers was completely distorted!
Just as Luo Zheng had said, Yao Yao immediately launched a counterattack …
Within the Demon Night Clan's Holy Lands, the statue of the King of Punishment emitted rays of blue light. At the same time, lightning flashed, and Yao Yao's voice spread out. "Hmph, Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerate, you are nothing but a little mouse hiding in the ground. Now, you finally dare to walk out from that dark corner. What right do you have to represent Xun and issue a King's Edict? Besides, even if it's Xun, what right does he have to declare me a traitor?! "
Hearing this, all of the Demon Night Clan members in the Holy Lands fell silent …
Yao Yao had mobilized all of the elites and geniuses of the various Great Worlds. In this meaningless war, bad news would come every day. The ones who died in the war were their sisters, brothers, and children …
This sorrow was constantly spreading and accumulating.
When Mu Haiji had created the Demon Night Clan, he had made ample considerations!
He had given his most loyal faith to this clan! When the time was right, he would activate the Soul Sealing Needle. With the three Kings having the same goal, this enormous clan had no room for doubt. They could only blindly believe in their King.
Unfortunately, Mu Haiji had miscalculated something. One of the Kings had forcibly destroyed the Soul Sealing Needle, allowing him to slip through the net. Because of this, a huge hole had been created, and this hole was fatal!
Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerate's voice once again spread out through the King's statue, spreading to all of the Demon Night Clan's Holy Lands. He had completely predicted Yao Yao's counterattack. It could be said that he had been waiting for Yao Yao to say this.
"I, Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerate, am indeed not qualified. Even Xun is not qualified. The ones who are qualified are the countless people of the Demon Night Clan! I believe that you will make the right choice! "
After saying this, Yao Yao's expression sank once more. As for Qing Yu, who was leading the troops to the Blood Devil Realm, the expression on her face was extremely ugly.
Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerate's words were like grasping a snake's weak spot. They had directly hit their vitals!
They didn't even have a chance to refute!
At this moment, all of the Demon Night Clan members suddenly realized that a new path had appeared in front of them. Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerate's words were equivalent to giving the choice back to each of their clansmen.
It wasn't just the ordinary Demon Night Clan members who were shaken. Even Yao Yao and Ying's loyal followers were shaken.
"We are willing to follow the King of Slaughter …"
"Those two sluts want to bring our Demon Night Clan to a dead end!"
"They have never cared about our lives!"
"......"
Yao Yao's body stiffened. Lightning continued to flicker in her eyes, and her gaze was filled with intense killing intent. She couldn't even refute Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerate's words …
The general was still kneeling on the tree trunk. When she heard Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerate's words, a smile appeared on her face that was filled with despair. From her angle, she could see Yao Yao's stiff back. However, this Great Realm Lord felt very gratified. In her heart, Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerate and Xun Ran had already become the saviors of the Demon Night Clan!
* Buzz buzz buzz … *
A purple light appeared behind Yao Yao. As the light flashed, it condensed into a human figure. This person was the Demon Night Clan's Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu.
The status of Heavenly Venerates in the Demon Night Clan was quite special. They received orders from the King, but they weren't absolutely loyal to the King.
As such, the Demon Night Clan's Kings would nurture their own people to carry the Mandate of Heaven. For example, Divine Arrow Heavenly Venerate was only able to become a Heavenly Venerate with Xun Ran's support. As for Broken Shadow Heavenly Venerate, he was nurtured by Yao Yao. However, Broken Shadow Heavenly Venerate was trapped by Luo Zheng's desperate battle and ultimately died.
"Yao Yao, give up," Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu said calmly.
Yao Yao suddenly turned around and stared at Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu. She was filled with rage. "You also want to betray the Demon Night Clan?"
Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu's eyes flickered as he replied, "I didn't betray the Demon Night Clan."
"Then why are you telling me to give up?" Yao Yao asked.
"It's very simple. Because I won't help you anymore," Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu replied.
In truth, the Demon Night Clan's Heavenly Venerates were also strongly opposed to attacking the Demon Night Clan. However, the two Kings had already made up their minds. Even the Heavenly Venerates had no choice. However, this didn't mean that the Heavenly Venerates were completely willing.
"You want to help that bitch?" Yao Yao asked again.
Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu calmly said, "It's just as the Dream God Arrow said. This is my right!"
She hadn't betrayed the Demon Night Clan. She had merely chosen to support the other King …
Yao Yao stared at Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu. Lightning crazily swirled in her eyes. The Holy Lightning Chain Hammer in her hand suddenly trembled. A thick bolt of lightning shot towards Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu like a python!
"Boom!"
Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu nodded slightly. A silver mirror appeared in front of her.
When the thick bolt of lightning struck the mirror, it was reflected back into the depths of space and disappeared without a trace …
This mirror was Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu's "Absolute Reflection". Under the protection of this law of karma, Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu could reflect almost any attack back!
"I won't attack you." Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu looked at Yao Yao. Killing intent faintly appeared in his calm eyes. "However, next time, it's hard to say."
After saying this, Heavenly Venerate Cang Yu's figure slowly disappeared, leaving Yao Yao standing there in a daze.
On the Blood Plains, the Demon Night Clan's expeditionary army had already begun to lose control. The Demon Night Clan's soldiers surged out of the Emerald Feather Tree. This army that had never feared death began to collapse.

Chapter 1625
Empyrean Divine Arrow and Luo Zheng hadn't expected that the King's Edict would have such an effect.
In these past years, Xun had disappeared for a long time. As for Ying, she had reincarnated as' Whisper 'and was obsessed with studying the Laws.
In truth, after Yao had taken control of the entire Fiend Night Clan, she had gone against the flow and attacked those who disagreed with her. The Council of Elders had long since lost patience with her. But, as the King of Punishment, Yao had special methods of suppressing her own people. Thus, the Fiend Night Clan's anger and dissatisfaction were forcefully suppressed!
But, the anger and dissatisfaction didn't disappear. Rather, it accumulated bit by bit, like a silent volcano, causing the internal pressure to grow greater and greater.
After the saints invaded and Yao boldly announced that she would join forces with them, the entire Fiend Night Clan had reached the end of their patience.
In their bones, they viewed themselves as a part of the universe. If it was in the past, they would have chosen to wage war against other races for the sake of competing realms and resources. After all, this was a conflict for the benefit of their own race. But now, with the invasion of other races, the entire universe had joined together. Only the Fiend Night Clan had chosen to rebel …
This was no longer going against the flow. They believed that their King had gone insane.
Even so, the Fiend Night Clan members still endured. After all, without a leader, the Empyreans chose to remain silent. The Council of Elders also chose to remain silent …
But, the disappointment in their hearts was rapidly increasing, almost reaching a critical point!
That was why the three commanders of the Night Demon Clan had requested for Yao Yao to retreat for the time being.
But at this time, Empyrean Divine Arrow's King's Edict was like a spark that had been thrown into a pot of boiling oil. The emotions that the Fiend Night Clan members had been suppressing for so long finally erupted!
"Let's go, we've already made a new choice!"
"We don't need to work for such a cruel and cruel King!"
"I can follow the King of Slaughter …"
The World Lords and third transformation martial artists all began to flee …
During this process, some people tried to stop them.
Yao had been in the Fiend Night Clan for so many years and had cultivated many trusted subordinates!
The military commanders of this expeditionary army were almost all Yao's subordinates and her loyal followers!
"Pa!"
A whip shadow exploded like a firecracker!
A Fiend Night Clan military commander waved his whip and reprimanded the fleeing Fiend Night Clan soldiers, "Those who flee before battle are punished by death!"
The punishment of the Fiend Night Clan was extremely severe. If the warriors led by the Warlords escaped, they would have to bear a great responsibility!
However, there were simply too many of the fleeing Yao Night Clan cultivators. With a single lash of the Warlord's whip, over a hundred Yao Night Clan cultivators fled. As the saying went, the law could not be enforced when numerous people were present. How were they supposed to stop them?
When the Demon Night Clan Military Governor saw that his warning was ineffective, he raised the whip in his hand and lashed out at a Divine Transformation Realm martial artist!
"Pa!"
The Overseer also had a World Lord realm cultivation and the might of his whip was extraordinary. Caught off guard, the Divine Transformation realm martial artist was struck by this whip and nearly half of his body exploded, falling to the ground of the Blood Plains …
Kill one to warn a hundred!
This was the only way to deal with this situation.
Unfortunately, killing one to warn the others required absolute strength. If this military governor was a Celestial Venerable, then killing him would have been no big deal. It was quite possible that he could even intimidate the fleeing Yao Night Clan warriors.
However, she was only a World Lord and there were many World Lords amongst the fleeing Yao Night Clan cultivators. There were even a few Great World Lords amongst them!
When the fleeing Yao Night Clan cultivators saw their comrade die, their expressions changed drastically. Dozens of hateful gazes focused on the Military Governor!
"She's Yao's dog!"
"She's the main culprit! Let's kill her! "
"Tear her apart …"
Perhaps these warriors did not have the courage to face Yao, but they did not fear a Military Governor!
The expression of the whip-wielding Overseer changed drastically. These fellows, who only knew how to escape, had turned into man-eating demons in the blink of an eye. She could feel their anger!
After retreating a few steps in the air, the Military Governor turned around and tried to fly away.
Unfortunately, it was too late. The enraged Yao Night Clan cultivators swarmed forward and tore the Military Governor to pieces!
When the other Military Governors saw this, their faces turned green with fright. How could they dare to stop them?
Thus, more and more Yao Night Clan cultivators began to flee …
This chaos continued for a long time …
At the end of the Blood Plains was where the Demon Race cultivators were stationed.
In the face of the Demon Night Clan's invasion, the Devil Race was under even greater pressure. Although they had killed as many of the Demon Night Clan's clansmen as possible, the two Demon Night Kings and the Celestial Exalts were definitely stronger than the Devil Race.
If they allowed the Yao Night Clan to break through the Blood Devil Realm, the Demon Race would have no way to retreat. They would probably have to abandon the Demon Race's territory and join the Demi-Immortal Race.
Thus, the Demon Race had set up a large number of troops in the Blood Devil Realm to stop the Yao Night Clan on the Blood Plains!
However, Yao Yao's army was already strong enough. If the Yao Night Clan had another army to reinforce them, the Blood Devil Realm would not be able to hold on for long …
At this moment, the higher ups of the Demon Race were gathered together to discuss how to deal with the situation.
"Before the Yao Night Clan's King of Life arrives, we have to deal a heavy blow to this army. Only then will we have a chance of stopping the Yao Night Clan." A Demon Race Great World Lord with golden hair suggested.
Empyrean Demondawn didn't speak. To the Demon Race, this was an extremely dangerous matter. As Empyreans, they could not afford to die here, because Empyreans were the decisive forces of both races!
But if they didn't take this risk, the Demon Race would fall into a situation where they would definitely be defeated!
Finally, Empyrean Demondawn made a difficult decision. But just as he was about to give the order to attack, he suddenly received news from a Demon Race spy.
"Reporting! The Yao Night Clan has collapsed without a fight. The Yao Night Clan cultivators are currently frantically fleeing! "
Hearing this news, the higher ups of the Demon Race were all stunned. At the same time, they revealed incomparably puzzled expressions …
How was this possible!?
In this battle with the Yao Night Clan, the Demon Race finally realized just how terrifying the Yao Night Clan was.
The members of the Yao Night Clan weren't good with words, but they were all silent and cold-blooded killers. They didn't fear death nor did they fear pain. They were willing to burn their last drop of blood for their race!
The Demon Race had always been known for their bravery and ferocity. But after this all-out war with the Yao Night Clan, compared to the Yao Night Clan cultivators, the Demon Race was as weak as a kitten.
In truth, it wasn't that the Demon Race was weak, but that the Yao Night Clan cultivators didn't care about their lives at all.
How could such a race collapse and flee?
Empyrean Demondawn's face was filled with disbelief. His massive figure shot into the skies, tearing apart the tent. His eyes were the size of bells as he looked into the distance, his expression incomparably wonderful …
He could clearly see countless Yao Night Clan cultivators fleeing towards the other side of the Blood Plains.
Several other Great World Lords also flew up and looked into the distance. At such a distance, if they wanted to see the end of the plains, they would need to use some supernatural abilities …
These Great World Lords also had wonderful expressions.
"Are these Yao Night Clan cultivators possessed?"
"I don't believe they would actually run away …"
"Could something have happened on the Blood Plains?"
These Great World Lords began to speculate.
"This is a good chance. We should take advantage of this victory and chase down those fleeing Yao Night Clan cultivators!" A Great World Lord's eyes flashed with a bright light.
Empyrean Demondawn shook his head. "No, this might be a scheme of the Yao Night Clan!"
"Lord Empyrean is right! The Yao Night Clan is full of schemes. If they lure the enemy in then we will be in even greater trouble. "Another demon Great World Lord nodded in agreement.
Taking advantage of the Yao Night Clan's retreat to chase them down was an opportunity for the Demon Race. But, the risks involved were far too great. They planned to continue watching …
Not long after, most of the Yao Night Clan's expeditionary forces on the Blood Plains had fled.
In the end, only several hundred martial artists remained …
These people were the core followers of the Holy Chain Sect and they maintained absolute loyalty to Yao. But, at this time, none of them dared to approach her palace.
Yao was still standing alone on the Emerald Feather Tree. Her azure blue eyes were staring into the distance. Her face was filled with desolation. Her long dress was fluttering in the gentle breeze. At this moment, she felt an unprecedented chill.
But a moment later, she leapt down from the emerald feather tree and landed on the ground like a bolt of lightning. She swept her eyes over her followers, the killing intent on her face growing increasingly thick. "Help me inform Qingyu that we are returning to the Yao Night Clan!"
"My Queen, we are currently returning …" A follower began to speak.
Pipa!
A bolt of Lightning Laws as thick as a water bucket descended like a dragon's tooth, striking the trusted aide. He was instantly reduced to charcoal, standing there like a charred wooden stake.
As the other followers saw this, their eyelids twitched. But, none of them dared to move.

Chapter 1626
Within the Purple Cloud Sacred Land …
"Right now, the first step is to isolate the two kings from the Night Demon Clan," Divine Arrow said calmly as he stood in the hall.
"What's the second step?" Luo Zheng asked.
Divine Arrow calmly asked, "What do the kings of the Yao Night clan rely on?"
Luo Zheng thought for a moment before replying, "Faith Ponds."
Xun, Yao, and Ying were only at the Realm Lord level. If they didn't use the power of faith, they were only at the Realm Lord level. However, if they used the power of faith, they could even kill Empyreans.
The reason Xun had lost to Yao was because her Faith Ponds had been sealed, so there was a large gap between them.
"Yes," Divine Arrow nodded, "If we want to kill Yao and Ying, we have to destroy their Faith Ponds. That's the second step!"
Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial's words shocked everyone present.
The 3,000 Demon Night Clan martial artists who had followed him all stared at Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial with astonishment …
Destroying the Faith Ponds … that was too crazy!
Compared to the kings of the Yao Night clan, the Faith Ponds were the foundation of the Yao Night clan!
The kings of the Yao Night clan had changed many times, but the Faith Ponds were still the same three!
As long as the Faith Ponds existed in the Yao Night clan, it was equivalent to having three extra Empyreans. In the entire universe, no other clan had such a unique resource!
"Lord Empyrean, you can't do that!"
"We can kill the two kings, but we can't destroy the Faith Ponds. That's the foundation of our Yao Night clan!"
"… …"
Under Realm Lord Qian Ling's lead, the other Yao Night clan members all tried to persuade him.
As for the Purple Cloud Sacred Land's Sacred Leader, Flowsand, after hearing Dream God Arrow's words, she felt even more incredulous.
She felt that everything she saw today was very strange. So many Yao Night clan experts had appeared out of nowhere, and there was even an Empyrean among them. Now, this Empyrean wanted to destroy the Faith Ponds. She didn't know if she had gone crazy or if the world had gone crazy.
Dream God Arrow didn't back down at all. He calmly looked at everyone, "From now on, our Yao Night clan doesn't need three kings. One is enough!"
The Yao Night clan had a limited amount of faith power. The three kings shared the faith power. If only one Faith Ponds remained, all the faith power would belong to one person, greatly increasing their strength …
Everyone chose to remain silent.
Empyrean Divine Arrow's words weren't without reason. The Demon Night Clan had three kings, and although they all had different roles, there had been many instances of them fighting each other throughout history. If there was only one king left, the situation would be very different.
"Then where is the Pond of Faith?" Luo Zheng stared at Divine Arrow and asked.
Everyone in the Night Demon Clan knew about the existence of the Faith Pond in their clan. Anyone who possessed the bloodline of the ancient Night Demon Clan had the right to use the power of the Faith Pond. However, most people did not know where the Faith Pond was located.
"On the King-Conferring Platform, the center of the Xixia World. That's the core of our Yao Ye Clan's Great World, and it's also the most heavily guarded place." Dream God Arrow stared at Luo Zheng and said, "We need to hurry. If Yao isn't an idiot, she'll be able to guess where we are!"
Divine Arrow looked at the members of the Demon Night Clan and announced, "I will bring Luo Zheng with me and use Greater Teleportation to rush to the Dawnperch World. It will be a long journey, so everyone can return to the Jade Flute World where my Sacred Spear Sect is located."
When they heard Dream God Arrow's words, everyone looked at him with strange expressions.
Dream God Arrow noticed everyone's expressions and was also a bit confused. He continued, "Everyone, the journey to the Dawnperch World is a long one. I cannot bring everyone with me …"
Heavenly Exalts could use Greater Teleportation to cross several Great Worlds, but Dream God Arrow could only bring two or three people with him at most.
"Cough cough …" Luo Zheng coughed twice and clenched his fist, interrupting Dream God Arrow. "I want to say that there's no need to go through so much trouble."
"What do you mean?" Dream God Arrow asked with a strange expression.
A faint light flashed in Luo Zheng's hand, and the Spatial Laws within the Teleportation Token continuously condensed into a blazing white whirlpool of light. It continuously revolved in front of Luo Zheng.
Dream God Arrow was still a bit confused. He could sense the profound Spatial Laws contained within the whirlpool of light, but he still did not understand what Luo Zheng was trying to do. Even Heavenly Exalts would find it hard to imagine that such a wondrous item like the Teleportation Token would exist in the universe.
In truth, just this Teleportation Token alone was comparable to the Will of the Heavens.
"Follow me." Luo Zheng stepped into the Teleportation Token, instantly disappearing before everyone's eyes.
The other Demon Night Clan martial artists present had already seen the Teleportation Token's miraculous effects, so their expressions did not change much. However, Dream God Arrow was even more curious. He carefully stepped forward, and his entire body instantly sank downwards, entering that strange space …
When he saw the vast universe beneath his feet, the shock on his face became even more intense.
"Where, where is this place?"
Heavenly Exalted Divine Arrow was an experienced and knowledgeable person. After seeing the various Great Worlds, he immediately asked, "Could it be that through this space, we can teleport to any location?"
Luo Zheng nodded.
Heavenly Exalted Divine Arrow looked deeply at Luo Zheng once more.
In the short time that he had known Luo Zheng, this was the second time that Luo Zheng had surprised him …
He was hidden underground and could only rely on the roar of the Teleportation Token to determine the situation in the outside world. In reality, he did not know much about Luo Zheng.
In Heavenly Exalted Divine Arrow's opinion, the only reason that Luo Zheng had taken the initiative to find him was because of his soul!
This was because releasing a King's Edict required a soul, and stepping onto the Throne Bestowment Platform also required a soul.
As for other matters, Dream God Arrow did not have much hope. If he could successfully destroy the Pond of Faith, then he would personally take care of Yao and Ying.
Thinking back to what he had obtained before, Dream God Arrow felt slightly embarrassed. He could only chuckle. "This little guy has quite a few good things."
Naturally, this would save him a lot of time …
Luo Zheng nodded noncommittally. Immediately after, Liu Yu, Qian Ling, and the other Demon Night Clan members all entered this space. Everyone began to advance towards the Xixia Realm.
Not long after, the Laws of Space suddenly appeared in the sky above the northern part of the Xixia Realm. This Laws of Space tore open a crack, and Luo Zheng's figure was the first to pass through the crack. After everyone had arrived, the crack disappeared.
The Xixia Realm and the Purple Snow Realm could not be mentioned in the same breath.
This was the core realm of the Demon Night Clan, and the coronation ceremony of the clan's King had always been held here. This was the birthplace of the Demon Night Clan.
Just the Xixia Realm alone had nine tenth-rank Sacred Lands, as well as countless other Sacred Lands below the tenth-rank. The population of the Demon Night Clan members here was several times that of the other Great Realms.
The entire Xixia Realm was verdant and lush, and the plants in the Sacred Lands were flourishing and growing, almost covering the entire Great Realm …
After the King's Edict was released, all of the Sacred Lands were greatly shaken.
The core Xixia Realm was naturally no exception!
At this moment, all of the Sacred Lands were in complete chaos …
Although the majority of the Demon Night Clan members had chosen to support Divine Arrow and Xun, there were still a small number of Yao Yue's supporters in the Xixia Realm. Even though Yao Yue had done something so perverse, the Demon Night Clan members who valued order believed that it was normal for the three Kings to perform their duties.
Thus, it was completely unreasonable for Divine Arrow to directly declare Yao Yue and Xun Ying to be traitors.
In response to this, there were even several conflicts within the Dawnperch World. However, even more people from the Yao Night Clan chose to kneel and pray on the Platform of Bestowment. They hoped that the Yao Night Clan would be able to smoothly survive this calamity under the protection of their king.
Divine Arrow led Luo Zheng and the others directly across the Sacred Lands. If this were in the past, there would definitely be guards from the Sacred Lands coming up to inspect them. However, no one even noticed them today. Or rather, even if they did, they couldn't be bothered to pay them any attention.
During this period of time, the faith of the Demon Night Clan had practically collapsed.
Even when Empyrean Divine Arrow arrived at the Regal Bestowment Platform, some people still recognized the Dream God Arrow!

Chapter 1627
This King Bestowment Platform was a place of pilgrimage for the Demon Night Clan.
Many Demon Night Clan martial artists were weak, but out of their reverence for their king, they would gather here from all over the great worlds to cultivate. These Demon Night Clan members worshipped their king every day and even gave up on cultivating.
These people were called pilgrims.
The more powerful pilgrims were much better off. They would often spend several years migrating here from other great worlds. As for the weaker pilgrims, they would spend their entire lives on the road. It would be difficult for them to enter Dawnperch World before their lifespan ran out, much less see the King Bestowment Platform with their own eyes!
The King Bestowment Platform was incomparably large, and around it was a massive platform that was hundreds of thousands of feet wide. This platform was filled with Demon Night Clan martial artists. They were prostrating themselves on the ground, just like the disciples of Gods No Thought. In their hearts, their king was a supreme existence, a True God that protected them …
Although the Holy Land of Dawnperch World was in chaos, the massive platform around the King Bestowment Platform was still solemn and quiet. No one dared to make a ruckus.
"It's the Dream God Arrow! Empyrean Divine Arrow has come to the King Bestowment Platform! "
Someone shouted out from the crowd.
Empyrean Divine Arrow had led a group of several thousand people here. This naturally attracted the attention of some people.
Although the Dream God Arrow hadn't appeared for many years, there were still some people who recognized him.
That shout instantly broke the silence. Countless members of the Night Demon Clan opened their eyes and looked up into the sky ….
Empyrean Divine Arrow had issued the King's Edict. In a short period of time, his name had spread throughout the Demon Night Clan.
Some people viewed him as the only savior of the Demon Night Clan, someone who could save them from this calamity. But at the same time, some people viewed him as a traitor who had broken the rules that the Demon Night Clan had established over countless years!
In the dense crowd, there were over ten people who were staring at the Dream God Arrow. They nodded to each other, a cruel and ruthless look on their faces!
One of them poured his true essence into his voice and roared towards the skies, "You traitor! My Demon Night Clan's King of Slaughter has already fallen! You have faked the King's Edict! Your crime is death! "
The faith of these pilgrims was very blind. They had even given up on cultivating and only blindly contributed their faith. These blind martial artists didn't have the ability to distinguish right from wrong and were easily bewitched!
The debates were confined to the various Holy Lands, and they didn't affect the Demon Conferral Platform or the pilgrims. They might have heard rumors about the King's Edict, but most of them didn't know who was right or wrong. To them, it didn't matter who the King of the Yao Night Clan was!
"Why don't you kneel on the King-Bestowing Platform and beg for forgiveness on behalf of our Demon Night Clan's Kings!" someone shouted from the crowd.
"Divine Arrow Celestial Venerable seeks the throne! He wants to destroy the Pond of Faith and annihilate the Fiend Night Clan … "
"Sisters and brothers of the same clan, we will pay with our lives to stop this evil man outside!"
Some of the martial artists in the crowd kept shouting.
The martial artists were hidden in the crowd. They were using their true essence to stimulate their voices, trying their best to make everyone in the Yao Night Clan hear them. Their words were filled with a strong bewitching intent!
As he heard these voices, Luo Zheng frowned. The voices coming from below were clearly abnormal. Someone had to be deliberately spreading these rumors …
This was an extremely underhanded method, but it was extremely effective at this moment!
The emotions of the pilgrims below were quickly swayed by these words. They all glared angrily at Divine Arrow Celestial Venerable in the skies!
There were nearly a million pilgrims in the square. The vast majority of them were weak, and there were even a few who were only Xiantian lifeforms. In the Yao Night Clan, Xiantian lifeforms were the same as mortals …
But these people didn't fear a mighty 'Celestial Venerable' in the slightest. Under the protection of their King, these pilgrims didn't fear him at all.
"Shut up!"
Dream God Arrow coldly snorted. He lightly raised the short bow in his hands. His movements were so fast that no one could see how the arrow was shot out. All they saw was a dozen golden arrows appear in front of Dream God Arrow, instantly shooting out in all directions!
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
Puff puff puff puff …
Although these warriors were hiding in the crowd, the Dream God Arrow's judgement was extremely accurate. The golden Sunfire Arrow flew into the crowd and accurately pinned down the warriors who were spreading the deceptive words!
Although these sun arrows were casually shot out by Dream God Arrow, no one below Great Sector Lord had the ability to block them!
Blood splattered in the crowd as some of the pilgrims screamed. Unfortunately, Dream God Arrow's actions caused them to be even angrier. Some of the warriors in the crowd jumped out and cursed at Dream God Arrow!
"Dream God Arrow, you dare kill your own people on the king bestowing platform! This is disrespectful! "
"Our clan's King of Slaughter actually chose someone like you to become Celestial Venerable. What a blind dog …"
"......"
Luo Zheng looked at the increasingly noisy crowd and asked, "What are they doing? There doesn't seem to be much point in doing this. "
Dream God Arrow squinted his eyes and thought for a while. Suddenly, he seemed to have thought of something and his expression changed. He grabbed Luo Zheng and flew towards the king bestowing platform, "We're in trouble, let's go!"
Now that Divine Arrow was so anxious, Luo Zheng was even more confused, "What is the meaning of this?"
"It seems like Na Yao is already prepared. She has arranged a bunch of reckless people here. It seems like they are going to activate the Trial of Faith to stop us from entering the King-Conferring Platform. I almost forgot about that!" Dream God's Arrow and Luo Zheng stopped in front of the enormous platform as soon as he finished his sentence.
Luo Zheng finally had the time to ask, "What is trial of faith …"
Dream God Arrow's face showed a trace of regret as he stared ahead and said helplessly, "That is trial of faith."
The king bestowing platform was the foundation of the kings. Once it was destroyed, the king's strength would greatly decrease! Therefore, the king bestowing platform was probably the most heavily guarded place, and the outermost layer of defense was this trial of faith!
The right to activate this trial of faith wasn't given to a single person, but to the millions of pilgrims on the king bestowing platform!
Once these pilgrims decided that they couldn't approach, the trial of faith would activate!
Yao Yao had already arranged for some death warriors to spread rumors among the pilgrims. After Divine Arrow Empyrean appeared, those death warriors risked their lives to slander Divine Arrow Empyrean. This way, the pilgrims would easily hate Dream God Arrow!
Na Yao had even calculated that Dream God Arrow would easily kill these death warriors. Therefore, Na Yao deliberately arranged several groups of death warriors. When the first group of death warriors was killed by Dream God Arrow, the second group of death warriors would follow.
Under the constant slander, some of the pilgrims who didn't know the truth viewed Dream God Arrow as an enemy and glared at him. They really thought that Dream God Arrow was an enemy of the Yao Ye clan! Not all pilgrims were so brainless, but this world was always made up of people who didn't know the truth.
When the majority of the pilgrims viewed Dream God Arrow as an enemy, the first layer of defense of the king bestowing platform, the trial of faith, activated …
The enormous king bestowing platform was a triangular tower. The intersection of the triangles was the staircase to the king bestowing platform. As the trial of faith was activated, three ribbons of light appeared from the tower.
These three ribbons of light were red, blue, and green. These were the colors of the power of faith of the three kings of the Yao Ye clan.
After the three ribbons of light floated in the air, they began to condense, and soon, they formed human figures!
Dream God Arrow retreated a few steps when he saw these human figures slowly condense. "The power of faith is condensing into the bodies of faith of the kings of the Yao Ye clan. Be careful!"
"How strong are they?" Luo Zheng asked as he stared at the balls of light.
"Comparable to a Great Realm Lord," Dream God Arrow replied.
"Just a Great Realm Lord. You're a Heavenly Venerable," Luo Zheng chuckled.
Dream God Arrow glared at Luo Zheng. "I'm warning you to be careful!"
As soon as he finished speaking, a red figure took form. This red figure was the 13th King of Slaughter of the Demon Night Clan. Although the body of faith was rather blurry, one could still make out the woman's shocking appearance through the blood-red mist!
"Whoosh!"
This King of Slaughter suddenly raised her spear and charged towards Dream God Arrow.
Before she could counterattack, Luo Zheng was one step ahead. Like a gust of wind, he suddenly reached out and lightly waved his hand at the 13th King of Slaughter. A terrifying power erupted from his fingertips, and this ball of faith was unable to withstand it and instantly dissipated. At the same time that he destroyed this body of faith, Luo Zheng turned his head and smiled at Divine Arrow. Dream God Arrow's warning was unnecessary.

Chapter 1628
Luo Zheng's strength seemed to always exceed Dream God Arrow's expectations.
After the Trial of Faith was activated, the Bodies of Faith formed from the power of faith were also very difficult to deal with!
These Bodies of Faith had a portion of the memories of the previous Kings. At the very least, they retained a portion of the martial skills they cultivated and a portion of their strength. It was a bit of an exaggeration to say that they were comparable to a Great Realm Lord. After all, a Great Realm Lord's strength had to be measured comprehensively.
Bodies of Faith were just another way to use the power of faith. In a certain aspect, these Bodies of Faith were indeed comparable to a Great Realm Lord.
"Whoosh!"
After Luo Zheng defeated the King of Massacre, another King of Massacre rushed over in the blink of an eye. This spear was burning with raging flames and contained a violent killing intent. The tip of the spear was aimed at Luo Zheng's forehead!
Luo Zheng's figure suddenly floated. Without any hesitation, he grabbed the spear and spun around four or five times. Taking advantage of the momentum, he approached the King of Massacre. His right hand suddenly reached out and grabbed the King of Massacre's neck!
"Pop!"
The King of Massacre was squeezed by Luo Zheng's enormous strength and instantly collapsed into strands of power of faith, which flowed towards the Platform of Bestowing King …
Luo Zheng's side was like crushing dry weeds and smashing rotten wood. Naturally, Divine Arrow Exalted Celestial didn't slow down either.
Dream God Arrow looked like a thin and shriveled old man, but at this moment, he was abnormally agile. At this moment, he was moving back and forth like a ghost. With one hand holding the short bow, his other hand strummed the strings of the zither as if he was playing a zither!
Every time he strummed the strings, a Red Sun Arrow would shoot out. The instant it pierced through a Body of Faith, the Red Sun Arrow would explode and the Body of Faith would collapse!
As the two circled in the air, they were like crushing dry weeds and smashing rotten wood. As long as a Body of Faith was condensed, it would instantly shatter. Dream God Arrow had defeated the previous King of Punishment and King of Life, while Luo Zheng's main target was the King of Massacre …
"Whoosh!"
A new Body of Faith condensed. It was another King of Massacre. The crimson body spun a spear and fiercely stabbed towards Luo Zheng. The spear tip transformed into a formless energy that pierced through space.
Luo Zheng didn't dodge and allowed the invisible penetrating force to touch his body. Other than a crisp sound, his body was almost unscathed. Then, Luo Zheng moved closer and was about to crush the body of faith!
But the moment he got close to the other party, he was stunned!
....................................
The King-Bestowing Platform had a memory. Every time a King was bestowed the title of King, the King-Bestowing Platform would record it down. When the Trial of Faith began, every generation's King would appear to protect the King-Bestowing Platform. Naturally, Xun was no exception.
. At this moment... Luo Zheng didn't know where to start...
..... But........ She raised her spear and stabbed at Luo Zheng again. The aura on her blurry face was almost the same as the real Xun!
While Luo Zheng was in a daze, a golden light suddenly shot out from beside his ear. The Dream God's Arrow accurately pierced through Xun's Body of Faith. Xun's Body of Faith collapsed in front of Luo Zheng.
"Don't be fooled by these Bodies of Faith," the Dream God's Arrow warned.
Luo Zheng frowned. His mind raced as he asked, "Does this King-Conferring Platform contain the memories of all the Kings?"
The Dream God's Arrow shrugged. "Who knows? Apart from the King, no one has ever entered … "
Hearing Divine Arrow's uncertain tone, Luo Zheng's eyes lit up.
At this stage, Luo Zheng couldn't find a way to recover Xun's memories. Xun was like a puppet now. Luo Zheng didn't know what to do. If he could recover Xun's memories on the King-Conferring Platform, that would be great.
Judging from these Bodies of Faith, the King-Conferring Platform should have some memories. For example, Xun's Body of Faith's spear was Xun's spear technique. But whether or not it had a complete memory depended on luck!
The trial of faith was a great threat to Realm Lords and Great Realm Lords. However, it wasn't too much of a problem for Divine Venerables and Luo Zheng. But if they couldn't get rid of these Bodies of Faith, they wouldn't be able to enter the King-Conferring Platform. This would greatly delay their time.
Generation after generation of Demon Night clan kings appeared and were defeated. From the ancient Demon Night clan until now, the Demon Night clan's kings had been passed down for countless generations. If one were to calculate based on age, an ordinary Great Sector Lord's lifespan could reach tens of millions of years. After billions of years, all the kings added together were probably more than a thousand!
The battle on the King-Conferring Platform quickly alarmed the nine tenth rank Sacred Lands of the Xixia World!
At this moment, those Sacred Lands that were left behind rushed towards the King-Conferring Platform. There were many Great Realm Lords in these Sacred Lands and they were all members of the Elder Council.
This time, the Elder Council didn't follow Yao and Ying on their expedition. As for Yao and the others, they didn't force them either. In Yao's opinion, the Elder Council's group of old fellows wasn't strong enough to begin with. Plus, the Elder Council had many conflicts with her.
In the sky above the Xixia World, two Divine Venerables stood there, calmly looking down.
Currently, the Demon Night Clan had a total of five Divine Venerables. Besides Divine Arrow and Cang Yu, there was also Divine Venerate War Spirit who was with Ying. As for the two Divine Venerables above the Xixia World, they were Divine Venerables and An Ying. The Demon Night Clan's situation was special. Even Divine Venerables weren't suitable to participate in the battle between Kings. They had always maintained a neutral attitude.
"It seems that Dream God Arrow is very determined. He really does plan on destroying the two faith ponds." Divine Venerate Juehuo glanced down, a wisp of fire dancing in his eyes.
"I admire this guy." Divine Venerate Shadow smiled. "I think we should do something. Yao and Ying should be on their way back … should we stop them?"
"I won't participate in their battle." Divine Venerate Juehuo shook his head.
Divine Venerate Shadow asked, "Are you really going to watch as the Demon Night Clan is destroyed?"
Divine Venerate Juehuo smiled. "Why must it be our Demon Night Clan that is destroyed? What if the human race fails? "
Yao had already told the other Divine Venerables about the soul sealing needle. Divine Venerate Juehuo and Divine Venerate War Spirit also understood why Yao and Ying suddenly changed their minds and chose to join forces with the Sacred Race.
From this point of view, Yao's choice wasn't necessarily wrong. If the Sacred Race really destroyed the Great Expansion Universe, their Demon Night Clan might still have a place. They might even be able to enter the Great Expansion Universe!
Hearing this, Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow pointed at Luo Zheng with a dangerous glint in his calm eyes. "If that's what you think, then let's join forces and kill that little guy down there!"
"Kill Luo Zheng?" Divine Venerate Juehuo raised an eyebrow.
Now, the entire universe knew the importance of the Dao Children. Luo Zheng, who was ranked first, might become the key to turning the situation around.
Although the two Demon Night Clan Divine Venerates were chatting casually, their attitudes were very important …
Dream God Arrow also understood that if he directly brought Luo Zheng to destroy the faith ponds, it wouldn't be that easy. The Demon Night Clan didn't only have the two Kings; there were also Divine Venerates. He used Xun to issue the King's Edict to divide the Demon Night Clan.
If Divine Venerate Bluesfeather couldn't bear it anymore and chose to leave Yao …
But Dream God Arrow's move was also extremely risky. If he rashly charged into the Throne Bestowment Platform, Dream God Arrow was betting that the other Divine Venerates wouldn't attack him.
However, the neutral Divine Venerates all had their own judgments. Although they didn't support any King, they still considered the overall interests of the Demon Night Clan.
When Divine Venerate Shadow proposed the idea of killing Luo Zheng, Divine Venerate Juehuo also carefully weighed the pros and cons.
"If we kill that boy, will the Sacred Race win?" Divine Venerate Juehuo asked.
Divine Venerate Shadow shook his head. "Not necessarily. But from the reactions of the Demi-Immortal Race and the Sacred Race, that boy is very important …"
Divine Venerate Juehuo looked down. "Then … kill him?"

Chapter 1629
Empyreans had been in high positions for a long time and liked to control the situation.
As peak characters of the universe, they didn't like their fates being manipulated …
Before Luo Zheng issued the declaration of war, the only races that actively resisted in the universe were the Demon Race and the Human Race. The total number of Celestial Masters of these two races added up to no more than 50. The other Celestial Masters who lived in seclusion, as well as the Celestial Masters of smaller races, had always maintained a wait-and-see attitude.
But with Luo Zheng's rallying cry, some of the smaller races finally began to move.
The Demon Night Clan's Empyrean Darkshadow and Empyrean Juehuo were in a state of uncertainty. They didn't support the Demon Night Clan's current decision, but they also didn't want to join the resistance against the saints.
This sort of wavering mentality was like a scale that swung back and forth between both sides. They were constantly weighing the impact of the current situation.
To them, Luo Zheng was like a weight. When they discovered that removing this weight would be beneficial to them, they made their choice decisively!
"I'll leave Luo Zheng to you. I'll kill the Dream God Arrow," Empyrean Juehuo lightly said.
Empyrean Darkshadow raised an eyebrow and glanced at him. "You're quite confident? Although this Dream God Arrow was once heavily wounded, his strength cannot be underestimated. "
In the eyes of the two Empyreans, Luo Zheng wasn't worth mentioning. As for the Dream God Arrow, he was already incomparably old. In addition, he had once been heavily wounded and was no longer as strong as he was in the past …
"No more nonsense, let's go."
As Empyrean Juehuo's voice fell, he turned into a blue flame and burned away …
As for Empyrean Darkshadow, he turned into a pitch black shadow and vanished.
In front of the King Bestowment Platform.
The three ribbons of light continued to tumble and condense in front of Luo Zheng and the Dream God Arrow …
After being repeatedly defeated by the two, the power of faith became increasingly difficult to condense and the speed at which it condensed into form became increasingly slow.
Pa pa pa!
With three faint cracking sounds, the ribbons of light formed from the power of faith finally broke apart. All of the power of faith returned to the King Bestowment Platform. They had successfully passed through the trial of faith.
Beneath the King Bestowment Platform, the millions of pilgrims revealed looks of despair.
These pilgrims did not allow anyone to enter the Platform of Bestowing King. It was a stain on their king, but they were weak. Other than using the Trial of Faith, they had no other way to stop it.
As such, the Dream God Arrow didn't kill any of the pilgrims. At his level, he didn't want to involve ordinary warriors in a battle.
"That's it? It's easy. "Luo Zheng smiled.
Dream God Arrow shook his head. "We're just lucky. Come with me, we'll ascend the Regal Bestowment Platform …"
Just as Dream God Arrow's voice fell, his expression changed drastically.
Under the golden light in his eyes, he suddenly shot out in the air. Under the acceleration of his body, he turned around and shot an arrow at the position where he was just now!
This arrow wasn't the golden Red Sun Arrow, but the black Sun Extinguishing Arrow!
Among the Dream God Arrow's archery skills, the Red Sun Arrow was the archery skill that made him famous. However, it wasn't the most powerful archery skill. As for the Sun Vanquishing Arrow, it was a powerful killing move that he had learned after becoming a Celestial Venerable!
An arrow piercing through the rainbow, extinguishing the sun and moon with a raise of his hand!
Dream God Arrow would only use the Sun Vanquishing Arrow when fighting against a warrior of the same level.
The black arrow was like a black ray of death as it shot out. However, when this arrow flew to the position where Dream God Arrow had been standing, it strangely floated in the air. The black arrowhead wavered, unable to move forward at all!
"Pop."
A strange blue flame appeared in the sky.
As this blue flame continued to burn, it enveloped Dream God Arrow's Sun Vanquishing Arrow. The fire grew bigger and bigger, and as it continued to burn, the flames formed the outline of a person. This person's hand was holding the black arrow shaft!
Not long after, the flames dissipated, and a slender young man appeared.
The youth had a faint smile on his face. He held the black arrow shaft with one hand and gently twisted it. The arrow shaft turned to dust and scattered with the wind.
"Absolute Fire!"
Dream God Arrow's face revealed a fierce expression. The thing he was most worried about had happened.
These Empyreans were not as easy to fool as ordinary warriors, especially Empyrean Absolute Fire and Heavenly Shadow. If they wanted to interfere, things would become troublesome …
"Long time no see, Dream God Arrow," Empyrean Absolute Fire said with a smile.
"Why are you stopping me? You want to help them? They are … "Dream God Arrow stared at Empyrean Absolute Fire.
Empyrean Absolute Fire shook his head and interrupted Dream God Arrow's words. He smiled and said, "Keep your words to yourself. I know exactly what you want to say."
"But …" Dream God Arrow was unwilling to treat Empyrean Absolute Fire as an opponent. At this moment, he was trying his best to persuade him.
However, Empyrean Absolute Fire did not give Dream God Arrow the chance. "But we have already made our choice!"
"We?" Dream God Arrow's eyebrows suddenly jumped. Empyrean Absolute Fire had also arrived. He instantly turned his head and warned, "Luo Zheng! Be careful! "
As his voice fell, a shadow suddenly appeared behind Luo Zheng. Within the shadow, a black broken blade silently reached for Luo Zheng's neck!
Facing Empyrean Absolute Fire, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. Then, he used his elbow to strike behind him.
This backhand elbow strike seemed ordinary, but the strength that Luo Zheng had poured into it was extremely terrifying. Since Dream God Arrow was facing an Empyrean, then the one attacking him must also be an Empyrean. Luo Zheng looked extremely calm, but he had also used the power of nine stars!
30% of his strength!
The frightening power of this elbow strike was like a collapsing dam, flocking towards the back of his body ….
"Peng!"
It seemed like a casual strike, but it actually exploded with a loud bang!
Immediately after, in front of everyone's eyes, a figure appeared behind Luo Zheng. It stumbled in the air and finally stabilized itself. However, that person's face was already deathly pale. After holding back for a while, there was a 'pu' sound as a mouthful of blood sprayed out from his mouth!
"Be careful of what?" Luo Zheng asked Dream God Arrow with a smile on his face. He didn't even turn around! He didn't even want to face a dignified Empyrean!
"…"
Dream God Arrow stared blankly at Luo Zheng. Along the way, he had repeatedly underestimated Luo Zheng's strength. In his heart, he had also repeatedly raised his evaluation of Luo Zheng!
When he first saw Luo Zheng, he only thought of Luo Zheng as a new Sector Lord. However, Luo Zheng was able to break through Dream God Arrow's' Fixed Gaze Divine Ability '. This made Dream God Arrow think highly of Luo Zheng!
However, following Luo Zheng's subsequent performances, he quickly adjusted his impression of Luo Zheng. After all, Luo Zheng was a Dao Disciple. Even if he had just stepped into the Sector Lord realm, he wasn't an ordinary Sector Lord. At the very least, he was a peak existence amongst Sector Lords. He could sweep away any fighter below the Great Sector Lord realm.
When Luo Zheng fought together with him against the Trial of Faith, Luo Zheng's performance once again exceeded Dream God Arrow's expectations. This fellow was extremely outstanding even amongst Great Sector Lords. He was indeed worthy of being the number one Dao Disciple!
Now, Dream God Arrow discovered that in front of Luo Zheng, he was like a farmer who had never seen the world. He couldn't understand Luo Zheng's true strength at all!
In any case, Dream God Arrow was an Empyrean. His experience and strength were proportionate. However, a Sector Lord's casual attack had actually caused an Empyrean to spit out blood. This sort of thing … was unheard of.
It wasn't just Dream God Arrow. Even Empyrean Exalted Fire was stunned.
Empyrean Exalted Fire believed that his strength was slightly higher than Empyrean Dark Shadow's. This was why he had allowed Empyrean Dark Shadow to deal with Luo Zheng while he dealt with Dream God Arrow.
Given Empyrean Dark Shadow's strength, dealing with Luo Zheng … it could only be said that he had no difficulty in doing so.
However, the scene in front of him immediately confused Empyrean Exalted Fire. He even had a trace of doubt in his heart. Was Empyrean Dark Shadow's brain sick? Was he acting? Even if he was acting, he still needed Luo Zheng's cooperation. Looking at Luo Zheng's smile and arrogant expression, it was obvious that Empyrean Dark Shadow had suffered a great loss!
At this moment, more and more people from the Yao Night Clan had gathered around the Bestowing King Platform. Apart from the weaker pilgrims, the Sector Lords of the Elder Council had also gathered around. When the Elder Council saw the two Empyreans attacking Dream God Arrow, they were all anxious.
The majority of the Elder Council stood on Dream God Arrow's side. After being suppressed by Yao for so many years, they hated Yao to the bone. However, from the perspective of the entire Yao Night Clan, they didn't wish for the clan's experts to kill each other. Therefore, these Elders had come to mediate.
However, as soon as they rushed over, they didn't even have time to speak before they saw this ridiculous scene …
"That youth should be Luo Zheng! He actually made Empyrean Dark Shadow spit out blood with a single punch! How … how did he do that? "An Elder's eyes were wide open, his eyeballs almost popping out of their sockets.
"You're asking me? Who am I supposed to ask … "An elder at the side was also dumbfounded.

Chapter 1630
Liu Yu and the other Sector Lords on Cage Island were also watching this scene from the other side of the platform.
Among them, only Cage Island's Warden Feng Ming and Mizukage had fought with Luo Zheng before.
At this moment, Feng Ming and Mizukage finally understood why they couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng even after using all their methods. This guy's strength wasn't what they had imagined at all …
As for Liu Yu himself, he was unusually calm.
This youth who had slaughtered his way through the Sea God Continent was now a match for a Heavenly Venerable in the blink of an eye …
As for Heavenly Venerable Shadow himself, he was extremely excited.
He had suffered a huge loss just now because he had been too careless.
Assassinating from the back was Heavenly Venerable Shadow's specialty. He never would have thought that Luo Zheng could predict the future and counterattack first!
Therefore, Heavenly Venerable Shadow was completely unprepared for Luo Zheng's counterattack.
If it had been a normal counterattack, it would have been fine. However, Luo Zheng had exploded with power at this moment. Even as a Heavenly Venerable, Heavenly Venerable Shadow still had to suffer. Under that seemingly careless attack, the power contained within it surged through his body and damaged his internal organs. He couldn't help but spit out a mouthful of blood.
He had really lost a lot of face today!
At this moment, Heavenly Venerable Shadow was feeling very strange. What kind of method did Luo Zheng use to know that he would attack from the back?
Heavenly Venerable Shadow's heavenly fate was called "Shadow Dance." After hiding himself, he would be in a state of absolute concealment. In theory, no one in the universe could find him. This heavenly fate was both weak and strong. However, when used at the right time, its effects were extremely terrifying. After all, he could get close to anyone without anyone noticing. Even Heavenly Venerables had to worry about being assassinated by him!
Naturally, he didn't know that within the range of the world's will, everything in the world was transparent to Luo Zheng.
In truth, when Luo Zheng and Dream God Arrow faced the Trial of Faith, he already knew of the existence of the two Empyreans. If these Empyreans hadn't come to provoke him, then it would have been fine. But now that they wanted to attack, Luo Zheng wouldn't show any mercy. There was almost no hesitation in his actions!
Heavenly Venerable Dark Shadow wiped the blood from the corner of his mouth and stared at Luo Zheng with scorpion-like eyes. "How did you discover me?"
Only then did Luo Zheng turn his head, the corners of his mouth slightly raised. "Why should I tell you?"
"If you don't speak, you'll definitely die!" Heavenly Sovereign Umbra Shadow threatened.
Luo Zheng's smile deepened. "Do you think you'll spare my life if I tell you?"
"In your dreams!"
Heavenly Sovereign Umbra Shadow was running out of patience.
Arguing with a little fellow at the World Lord Realm was futile. Under the extreme humiliation he felt, he only wanted to kill Luo Zheng as soon as possible!
"Shua!"
Heavenly Sovereign Umbra Shadow suddenly split into two pitch-black shadows and disappeared on the spot!
Heavenly Sovereign Umbra Shadow had just disappeared and was about to carry out a second assassination …
Dream God Arrow's reaction was extremely fast. Luo Zheng's performance had surprised him greatly, but at the same time, he was slightly relieved. At least this sudden change wasn't as troublesome as he had imagined. He still had a chance!
If he could quickly finish off Heavenly Venerable Juehuo, then it wouldn't be impossible for him to work with Luo Zheng to kill Heavenly Sovereign Umbra Shadow!
"Sou sou sou!"
He raised his hand and three streaks of black light shot out in the blink of an eye!
"Fixed Gaze Ability!"
Heavenly Sovereign Divine Arrow's eyes flashed with golden light as he stared at Heavenly Venerable Juehuo. In his field of vision, the three Yang Annihilation Arrows would definitely hit. There was no way to dodge them!
Heavenly Venerable Juehuo and Dream God Arrow both came from the Fiend Night Clan, so he was very familiar with Dream God Arrow's ability. He knew he couldn't dodge, but he didn't even think of dodging!
"Peng!"
Green flames burned vigorously on his body …
Then, the three Yang Annihilation Arrows nailed into Heavenly Venerable Juehuo's body, completely piercing through him!
But as the green flames gradually extinguished, Heavenly Venerable Juehuo disappeared from where he stood. Then, a scorching heat spread out from the skies. All of the martial artists around the King-Bestowing Platform felt as if they were standing next to a furnace as a wave of heat spread out!
"Go go go!"
The members of the Council of Elders didn't dare to stay any longer!
When Empyrean powerhouses fought, every move they made could destroy the heavens and earth. At this moment, both sides were restraining themselves. But, if they truly brought out their trump cards, it wasn't impossible for them to tear apart the entire Xixia World!
Liu Yu and the others also began to retreat. No one wanted to stay here and be caught up in the crossfire.
But, the pilgrims below the King-Bestowing Platform didn't move …
The vast majority of these pilgrims were weak and couldn't escape too far in a short period of time. Moreover, these pilgrims didn't want to leave this place at all. They simply whispered a prayer, using the Fiend Night Clan's unique language to pray for the protection of their King.

The Heavenly Fire Wheel surged forward, crushing down on the Dream God Arrow. The heat emanating from the Heavenly Fire Wheel was enough to melt the earth!

As this heat roasted down, the pilgrims below the King-Bestowing Platform were set ablaze one by one, transforming into raging flames …
It had to be said that these pilgrims were extremely devout. Some of the pilgrims who were further away weren't affected. When they saw the pilgrims in front of them in a pitiful state, they didn't scream or run away. In the face of death, they remained calm and collected!

Crimson Sun Arrow, Sun Annihilation Arrow, Profound Fixation Arrow, Spirit Seizing Arrow, and Vast Sky Arrow!

Against Dream God Arrow's arrows, one could only use other methods to neutralize them or even forcefully resist them. There was no way to dodge them even if one wanted to!

Heavenly Venerable Juehuo's plan was similar to Dream God Arrow's. As long as Heavenly Venerable Shadow captured Luo Zheng and joined forces with him, dealing with Dream God Arrow wouldn't be difficult. Thus, the one who should be anxious right now should be Dream God Arrow, not him!
Dream God Arrow's five arrows didn't work, so he turned his gaze to Luo Zheng on the other side.
Heavenly Venerable Shadow appeared and disappeared like a ghost. He didn't know if Luo Zheng could avoid his assassination.
The only thing that comforted Dream God Arrow was that Luo Zheng's reaction was shockingly fast. This kid had even broken his Eye Fixation Divine Ability.
Dream God Arrow was quite puzzled because breaking the karmic law was something that defied common sense.
Although Luo Zheng hadn't told him this secret, Dream God Arrow had been analyzing Luo Zheng's method of breaking the Eye Fixation Divine Ability. In the end, he only came up with one possibility. Perhaps this Luo Zheng had a divine ability that could see through a person's mind and knew where he was going to shoot the arrow. Thus, he was able to predict the future and destroy his arrow!
If his guess was correct, then it would be very difficult for Heavenly Venerable Shadow to take advantage of Luo Zheng …
After Heavenly Venerable Divine Arrow stopped, Heavenly Venerable Juehuo also looked over.
Luo Zheng was still floating in the air, but he was constantly turning in a certain direction. There was a mocking smile on his face as his eyes stared into the void.
After activating the Dance of Shadows, Heavenly Venerable Shadow was in a state of absolute concealment. In theory, he couldn't be detected. It was another matter if he could kill his target, but by relying on the karmic law, he could approach anyone without being detected!
However, that kid Luo Zheng was staring at him!

Chapter 1631
In front of the world's consciousness, all concealment was useless.
This world was made up of complex materials, such as rocks, air, water, mountains, and all kinds of living beings …
The world's consciousness recorded all materials, so Luo Zheng could instantly tell how many rocks there were in a large world, how many mountains there were, and how many people were there!
However, the world's consciousness had its limits.
When the world's consciousness first covered the entire world, it couldn't detect the movements of martial artists from the Upper Realm.
Now, the world's consciousness had covered all of the large worlds of the Yao Night Clan, so Luo Zheng couldn't detect human martial artists.
However, this world's will was still spreading. In the future, if it spread to the entire universe, Luo Zheng would truly be an omniscient god in this universe. The entire universe would no longer have any secrets to him. He would be able to know everything from the past to the present through his memories. The only thing he couldn't detect was the Sacred Race. After all, the Sacred Race came from the Upper Realm, so the world's consciousness couldn't enter the universe of other Saints.
Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow was born in the Yao Night Clan, so he naturally couldn't escape the world's consciousness.
In fact, Luo Zheng didn't just see Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow's current state. Through the world's consciousness' memories, Luo Zheng could even understand Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow's past. Most of those memories were something even Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow himself couldn't remember.

Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow kept moving in the shadows …
This brat in front of him was much more difficult to deal with than he'd imagined. At this moment, he was even depressed about Heavenly Venerate Extreme Fire's choice. With his strength, facing the Dream God Arrow was much better than facing this brat.
Although Luo Zheng was difficult to deal with, Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow didn't think that he couldn't do anything to him.

Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow moved faster and faster in the shadows. With Luo Zheng as the center, he opened up a distance of several hundred feet and quickly circled around him.
Luo Zheng floated in the air, spinning faster and faster, and the smile on his face gradually disappeared …
Even if the will of the world could capture anything, if Heavenly Lord Dark Shadow's speed could surpass Luo Zheng's reaction speed, he could still pose a huge threat to Luo Zheng!
"Huff huff huff …"
Heavenly Master Dark Shadow seemed to have understood this as well. He simply gave up on concealing himself. After retreating from the shadows, his speed suddenly doubled.
