Because of his extreme speed, these afterimages were actually able to connect head to tail, forming a huge halo that surrounded Luo Zheng.
At this moment, Dream God Arrow also frowned. Just like how Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow and Heavenly Venerate Juehuo understood him, Dream God Arrow also understood Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow's methods. This fellow could be considered an assassin among Heavenly Venerates. He liked to prepare for a long time before attacking, but once he attacked, he pursued a one-hit kill!
Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow had an advantage in speed against Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng would most likely be in a disadvantageous situation.
At this thought, Dream God Arrow's eyes flashed with a golden light. He raised his hand and was about to shoot an arrow at Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow. He couldn't just watch Luo Zheng be killed. After all, without Xun's soul, he couldn't enter the Platform of Bestowment!

With such a fast speed, even the afterimages he left behind disappeared. This speed didn't leave any mark on the eyes because it was simply impossible to catch!
When Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow disappeared, Luo Zheng also shouted out loud!
His right hand was still on his spatial ring. As soon as Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow's figure disappeared, Luo Zheng had already pulled out the Great Thousand Greatsword!
Luo Zheng didn't have time to think. Relying purely on instinct, he slashed the Great Thousand Greatsword to the side!
Strictly speaking, this couldn't even be called a 'slash'. After all, Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow's speed was simply too fast. When Luo Zheng used the world will to lock onto Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow, the two sides were already extremely close. He wasn't able to adjust the Great Thousand Greatsword's edge. He could only use the Great Thousand Greatsword as a stick and whip it towards Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow!
Peng!
It was accompanied by a muffled boom!
The Great Thousand Greatsword accurately smashed into Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow's body!
The weight of the Great Thousand Greatsword was extremely terrifying. It contained the weight of a great thousand worlds. Even if it were a stick, it would still have a terrifying lethality. Moreover, Luo Zheng had also erupted with his own strength as he drove the Great Thousand Greatsword!
The back of the Great Thousand Greatsword smashed into Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow's chest. A terrifying strength poured out, instantly shattering all of Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow's ribs!
Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow was like a broken kite as he was sent flying backwards by Luo Zheng's Great Thousand Greatsword. The speed at which he was sent flying backwards was even faster than the speed at which he came.
As he was sent flying backwards, he flew for 3000 miles before crashing into a Holy Land that was closest to the Throne Bestowment Platform. Along the way, he smashed through tens of thousands of trees and even some Yao Ye clansmen.
There was a 10,000 foot wide crater in the Holy Land. Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow lay in the center of the crater, his expression indescribably wonderful.
He suddenly flipped himself up and felt a sweet taste in his throat. Puff!
He spat out another mouthful of blood!
This time, the amount of blood he spat out was more than 10 times more than before!
Not a single rib in Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow's chest was intact. As for his internal organs, they were a mess. If it was any other cultivator, they would have been on the verge of death. How could they still jump up like this?
He gritted his teeth and took out a golden pill. He slapped it into his mouth and swallowed it after chewing for a while.
In a short period of time, he had been beaten to the point of spitting out blood twice by a Realm Lord. This was no longer just a great humiliation, it was a great humiliation!
Sou!
Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow jumped out of the crater, his face filled with the word 'unwillingness'. His gaze shifted and he discovered that there were several Yao Ye clan cultivators hiding in the bushes around the crater. His face darkened and he casually slapped out with his palm!
When this palm wind swept past the cultivators of the Yao Night Clan, the cultivators who had been sneaking glances at them all shuddered and their bodies stiffened. Not long after, cracks appeared on their bodies as blood flowed out from the cracks. Then, their bodies shattered into pieces and completely collapsed.
"Hmph!"
Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow coldly snorted and flew towards the Bestowment Throne once more.
After being struck by Luo Zheng's sword, Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow felt sad and wronged. This Luo Zheng was too unreasonable. What qualifications did he have to make an Empyrean spit out blood twice?
Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow even began to doubt his own life. He began to doubt his and Heavenly Venerate Juehuo's judgment. Should they have gotten involved in this mess?
If Luo Zheng was allowed to continue growing, then in a few more years, that little fellow would likely be able to defy the heavens. At that time, helping the human race defeat the Sacred Race might not be impossible …
However, people at their level never had any thoughts of 'regret'. Once they made up their minds, they would walk down a dark path. He and Heavenly Venerate Juehuo had already formed a grudge with Luo Zheng. There was no possibility of reconciliation!
"Not bad …"
Heavenly Venerate Darkshadow used greater teleportation and appeared in front of Luo Zheng once more. His deathly pale face was filled with absolute seriousness. "It looks like I shouldn't have treated you as a junior, nor should I have treated you as a Realm Lord. You … actually already possess the strength to rival an Empyrean!"
His evaluation was already extremely high.

Chapter 1632

After all, the power of 100,000 shadows couldn't be concentrated on a single point.
Even if a Heavenly Venerate forcefully withstood it, he would probably be on the verge of death. For Luo Zheng to be able to withstand it without a single injury was indeed too illogical …
For a moment, Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow wanted to retreat!

Thinking of this, he gritted his teeth and threw caution to the wind. He folded his hands together, opened his mouth, and spat out a ball of essence blood. This ball of essence blood danced in his hand and condensed into a thumb-sized essence blood sword.

Chapter 1633
Although Heavenly Venerable Dark Shadow was only a lower Heavenly Venerable, he was by no means an ordinary person.
Someone once said that each Heaven's Will had its own use and that there was no difference between them. If one could use their Heaven's Wills to their limits, then even a lower Empyrean would be able to fight against a upper Empyrean.
And vice versa. Some Mandates of Heaven were indeed powerful, but if used incorrectly, they wouldn't be very effective.
Heavenly Venerable Dark Shadow's Mandate of Heaven combined with his assassination techniques was actually very terrifying, but neither Absolute Concealment nor Shadow Dance were very effective. They were completely restrained by Luo Zheng's World Will. At this moment, he could only burn his essence blood to fight Luo Zheng!
But he didn't know that this was exactly what Luo Zheng hoped for the most!
In this world, Luo Zheng might not be the strongest at the moment, but in a close combat, it would be difficult to find a second opponent!
If Heavenly Venerable Dark Shadow escaped, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to do anything to him. But at this moment, Heavenly Venerable Dark Shadow was obsessed with Luo Zheng and wouldn't give up until he killed him. This gave Luo Zheng a chance!
When Heavenly Venerable Dark Shadow's small essence blood sword condensed in his hand, he felt a figure flash in front of him. He knew that this kid was reaching for a yard after taking an inch. Did he really want to take him down? Heavenly Venerable Dark Shadow couldn't take it anymore. He immediately laughed. "Come!"
Then, the small essence blood sword in his hand shook violently and was about to shoot out. At the same time, a blazing white light flashed under his feet, and he was about to teleport away …
But at this moment, Luo Zheng, who was rapidly approaching, flicked his finger lightly, and a rainbow-colored belt of light suddenly floated out. At the end of the belt of light was a shackle!
"Life Ending Shackle!"
Luo Zheng hadn't used this shackle since he obtained it.
According to Afo's explanation, the Life Ending Shackle was similar to the belt of light in the Immortal Mansion. Once locked, one could only escape by killing the other party. Otherwise, even a Heavenly Venerable couldn't escape!
However, ever since he ascended to the Upper Realm, Luo Zheng had never had the chance to use it.
Luo Zheng's speed wasn't slow either. Opponents at the same level as him were rarely able to escape from him. But when facing an Empyrean, the situation was different. No matter which Empyrean faced Luo Zheng, as long as they fled, it would be impossible for Luo Zheng to catch up to them with his speed!
Now, the Life Ending Shackles would naturally come in handy!
"Shua!"
The ribbon of light broke free from Luo Zheng's hand at an extremely fast speed and swept towards Heavenly Venerable Dark Shadow.
The speed at which the ribbons of light moved was also extremely fast. It simply exceeded the limit of the reaction of living creatures. Back when Luo Zheng was hiding in the immortal mansion, those Celestial Venerables were still quite a distance away from the immortal mansion, but they were also unable to avoid the ribbons of light, which was why they were caught in a desperate battle.
At this moment, Heavenly Venerable Dark Shadow naturally couldn't react in time. One end of the light belt firmly bound him, while the other end naturally bound Luo Zheng!
The Life Ending Shackles bound the two of them together. As long as one of them was alive or dead, there was no possibility of escape!
Therefore, it was naturally impossible for Heavenly Venerable Dark Shadow to escape with greater teleportation. Half of his body had already sunk into the ball of blazing white light. He originally thought that he could escape without a trace, but now he was forcibly pulled back by Luo Zheng!
"Whoosh!"
At the same time, the Blood Essence Sword that Heavenly Venerable Dark Shadow had shot out also shot towards Luo Zheng's face.
Luo Zheng had long been on guard against this Blood Essence Sword. He fiercely swung the Heavy Sword of Da Qian, which turned into a brown sword arc and fiercely collided with the Blood Essence Sword!
It was unknown what kind of secret technique this Blood Essence Sword was. It was merely condensed from a ball of blood, but its strength was extremely frightening. Even the Heavy Sword of Da Qian couldn't break it. But under the effect of the powerful force, the Blood Essence Sword was sent flying hundreds of thousands of miles into the sky …

Chapter 1634

Chapter 1636

The moment he passed through the firebird, Dream God Arrow finally found the opportunity to shoot this arrow. This Heavenly Sage let out a roar!
"Yao!"
The Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrow that was already on the bowstring began to slowly burn!
On the arrowhead of this bone arrow, a blood-red flame lit up!
Hearing Dream God Arrow's words, Yao, who had been watching Luo Zheng, slightly raised her head. When her eyes met the Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrow, her eyelids suddenly twitched.
This arrow …
The Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrow emitted a power that caused her heart to palpitate!
"This old man!"
A fatal sense of danger arose in Yao's heart!
"Let Luo Zheng go! I won't kill you! "
Dream God Arrow pulled back the bowstring and stared at Luo Zheng as he spoke.
At this moment, he was staring at Yao. Theoretically speaking, there was no way Yao could dodge this arrow. She could only choose to take this arrow head on!
This Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrow was refined from Dream God Arrow's own ribs.
The refining process was similar to burning one's blood essence. However, when ordinary martial artists burned their blood essence, they could greatly increase their strength in a short period of time. However, Dream God Arrow had used his own blood essence to create these bone arrows over a long period of time. The power of these bone arrows was even more terrifying!
Once this bone arrow hit its target, the blood-red flame on the arrowhead would escape into the target's body and use the target's blood as fuel to burn away!
However, the Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrow was originally a part of Dream God Arrow's life force. Every time he shot an arrow, he would burn away a third of his life force. If he shot three arrows … he would directly die!
The price was simply too great.
However, if Luo Zheng died now, Dream God Arrow wouldn't be able to escape either. In comparison, this price was nothing.
"Heh …"
A cold smile appeared on Yao's face. Her face was so beautiful that it could make people fall into a trance.
"Determination alone isn't enough. Are you sure this arrow can kill me?"
Yao asked calmly. Her tone didn't show any signs of backing down.
"Won't you know if you try?"
Dream God Arrow replied. The golden light in his eyes grew brighter and brighter. As long as Yao made any strange movements, he would shoot this arrow without hesitation.
However, Yao didn't seem to care about Dream God Arrow's Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrow …
The expression on her face became more and more relaxed. She smiled coldly. "I … I really want to try!"
After saying this, Yao didn't even look at Dream God Arrow. She continued to swing the Holy Lightning Chain Hammer in her hand. The lightning vortex spun faster and faster as the Dongxuan Holy Lightning continued to brew.
Seeing this, Dream God Arrow sighed. His face revealed a look of determination. In the end, he released the bowstring in his hand!

Chapter 1637
Xiu!
The Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrow turned into a blood-red trail as it shot towards Yao!
Divine Arrow Empyrean's arrow could not be dodged. This arrow could follow to the ends of the earth …

These Dao Children all had heaven-defying luck. This luck was specially given to them by the universe, so Yao didn't relax in the slightest.

Chapter 1638
Luo Zheng's power was sealed, and his body was bound by the Life Extinguishing Shackles. At this moment, he had no way to escape.

When Empyreans exchanged blows, many killing moves were often completed in an instant!

All living creatures within a hundred thousand miles and a million miles saw an intense ball of light explode in half the sky. An enormous burst of power exploded over the horizon …
Even the living creatures on the stars in the deep sky hundreds of millions of miles away from the Xixia Realm were astonished when they looked down. They saw a certain great realm down below suddenly emit a dazzling light. This light was even brighter than the stars in the deep sky!

Luo Zheng bathed in this lightning and sat down cross-legged with his eyes tightly closed.
His physical body was indeed powerful, but the power of Yao Yao's Dongxuan Holy Lightning was too absurd. It was too much for him to use his physical body to withstand it.
However, Luo Zheng was currently in the Xixia Realm, an area covered by the will of the realm. Luo Zheng could use another ability.
"Power transfer …"
As the will of the realm continued to expand, it would occupy a new great realm every once in a while.
At present, the will of the realm covered the territories of the demon race, the monster night clan, and many other smaller races. It rarely touched human territory, but still covered dozens of the Human Alliance's great realms!
These great realms were all occupied by the Sacred Beast Clan.
Luo Zheng's goal was to save the Great Yan Realm. If he used his power as he pleased, it would be a form of damage to the realm itself.
When Luo Zheng had been in the lower realms, he'd once destroyed all the mountain ranges in an entire realm.
Unless he had no other choice, Luo Zheng wouldn't use it so easily.
Luo Zheng had chosen one of the Human Alliance's great realms, one that had already been occupied by the Sacred Beast Clan …
"Boom!"

The lightning had already been transferred to the great realm by Luo Zheng …
Luo Zheng, who was being baptized by the lightning, had a sliver of understanding at that moment.
Due to the will of the realm, he could transfer his power. Then, the willow leaf that bound him probably had the same effect? It allowed him to transfer his power elsewhere through the willow leaf.
At that moment, Luo Zheng remained motionless, completely unharmed. However, the situation in another great realm was completely different …
In the Skywolf world that was three thousand great realms away from the Xixia Realm, an unprecedented disaster was happening.
After the Sacred Beast Clan warriors invaded the universe, they would face all sorts of natural disasters every few days. In order to resist the malice in the Great Yan Universe, they were constantly extremely careful …
After occupying a great realm, they would try their best to shrink their forces. Then, they would use whatever materials they could find to create all sorts of arrays and barriers.
This way, it was not too much of a problem to deal with ordinary heavenly lightning and earthly fire …
But today, lightning bolts as thick as water buckets suddenly appeared in the Skywolf world!
"What kind of lightning is that!?"
A Sacred Beast Clan warrior looked into the sky and shouted. The light that erupted in the sky had alarmed the Sacred Beast Clan warriors stationed there.

The Skywolf world's warriors were in complete chaos at that moment.
While they were in a state of chaos, the lightning snakes as thick as water buckets had already swept over …
All the lightning rampaged aimlessly across the land. The rumbling lightning plowed through the entire sacred land. The tenth-grade sacred land occupied by the Sacred Beast Clan was extremely vast, but the lightning snakes only took a few seconds to destroy it. Finally, they left behind a scene of devastation as well as the mangled corpses of countless Sacred Beast warriors.

Chapter 1639

Upon hearing this, Heavenly Venerable Juehuo and Dream God Arrow were similarly astonished …
This was especially true for the Dream God Arrow. His originally dim eyes now shone with the light of hope.

Unfortunately, he and the Dream God Arrow had failed. Now, against Yao and Ying, he could only temporarily sacrifice some Great Realms. At the very least, he could kill some Sacred Race experts while sacrificing himself.

Yao suddenly shook her head. She felt a strong sense of defeat when she saw the unharmed Luo Zheng!

"Dong!"

Yao was not the only one who was shocked. Ying, who was not far away, as well as Heavenly Venerable Juehuo and Dream God Arrow high in the sky, were also staring at Luo Zheng in disbelief.

In the countless years that these Heavenly Venerables had lived, they had never seen such an ability before. In this cycle of the Heavenly Dao, there had never been such a terrifying Mandate of Heaven!
Moreover, Luo Zheng had not inherited the Mandate of Heaven. He was only a World Lord. Although he was a Dao Child, he had yet to condense a divine spark. The supernatural ability that Luo Zheng displayed was already incomprehensible.
In their hearts, Luo Zheng had risen from a freak to an incomprehensible fellow …

And in another great world occupied by the Sacred Race, the Sacred Race martial artists were suffering an unexpected calamity. Invisible forces exploded, and the Sacred Race World Lords suffered heavy blows. At the same time, they were accompanied by a terrifying power of thunder. For a time, the Sacred Race martial artists in that great world suffered tragic casualties!
The weight of the Sacred Lightning Chainhammer was also extremely terrifying. After overusing her strength, she finally stopped. She gasped for breath, her chest heaving up and down … her beautiful eyes revealed a helpless expression.
At this moment, Yao was indeed feeling helpless. She had finally caught Luo Zheng with great difficulty. This boy didn't have the strength to fight back, nor did he have the ability to escape. However, she couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng. This scene even made her feel that it was funny. How could such a thing happen in the world?
"Let me try …"
At this moment, Ying floated down and floated in front of Luo Zheng. Her gentle eyes stared at Luo Zheng and smiled, "If I'm not wrong, you seem to have the same sacred art as my Ten Thousand Fold Willow Leaf?"
The willow leaves that sealed Luo Zheng's strength were a type of plant that Ying had created herself.
The Ten Thousand Fold Willow Leaf could absorb any energy, and then through an inconceivable link, transmit this energy to the Ten Thousand Fold Willow Tree's trunk. Ying had planted tens of millions of Ten Thousand Fold Willow Trees in a certain corner of the universe.
Therefore, when Luo Zheng used his strength, it would be absorbed cleanly by all the Ten Thousand Fold Willow Trees …
Hearing Ying's words, Luo Zheng smiled, "Perhaps it's the same sacred art."
Luo Zheng also had this conjecture. However, the Ten Thousand Fold Willow Leaf could only absorb the energy to those tree trunks, and all the Ten Thousand Fold Willow Trees had an upper limit. If that upper limit was broken, all the Ten Thousand Fold Willow Trees would be stuffed to death!
As for Luo Zheng's "Power Transfer", any place that was covered by the world's will could be transferred away. From this point of view, Luo Zheng's Power Transfer was countless times more powerful than Ying's method!
Ying didn't continue to ask. The green light in her eyes flashed, and with a light tap of her hand, a green ripple spread over Luo Zheng's body.

Chapter 1640
After completely wrapping Luo Zheng, only his head was left outside.
Those golden vines were called "Bone Melting Vines". Once entangled, they could continuously absorb the opponent's life force until their prey was sucked into a dried corpse.
Just now, Luo Zheng had even withstood the Dongxuan Holy Thunder. Ying, who was not good at fighting, naturally would not use brute force to break Luo Zheng's magical power!
If these "Bone Melting Vines" were really effective and completely absorbed Luo Zheng's life force, they would naturally achieve their goal of killing Luo Zheng.
"Sorry." Ying stared at Luo Zheng and smiled sweetly.
She was not a person who liked to kill, and she was not interested in Yao's ambition. Unfortunately, she had no choice but to kill Luo Zheng …
Luo Zheng's expression did not change at all, but in the next moment, he suddenly felt a strange power emitting from the vines. The vines seemed to be covered with countless small vortexes, and they began to absorb life force …
After a while, little green dots began to flow along the vines!
These Bone Melting Vines could actually absorb life force …
"It's effective." Ying smiled faintly.
"Not bad." Yao nodded and praised. She did not expect that she would still have to rely on Ying to solve the goal that she could not achieve even after exhausting all her strength.
Who knew that Ying would just glance at Yao indifferently and ignore her.
Although the Soul Sealing Needle made her choose to stand on the same side as Yao, she did not like Yao after all, so her reaction was naturally very cold.
The Bone Melting Vines continued to absorb life force, and those vines grew more and more plump. Seeing this scene, Ying reached out and gently pointed. One of the vines lifted up like a snake and drilled into Ying's hand.
That green life force was continuously transmitted from Luo Zheng's body to Ying's hand.
Ying liked this green life force very much.
From the moment a life was born, it was a small ball of life force. This life force constantly condensed the matter of this world, turning into the most primitive form, and began to absorb all kinds of nutrients, constantly growing, and this life force would become a real life …
After a while, more and more life force condensed in Ying's hand, and soon, a ball of green light appeared.
"Huh?"
Ying stared at the green light with a strange expression on her face.
This ball of green light was a complete life force. In other words, the life force condensed within this ball of light came from the same life form!
If Ruo Ying had already extracted all of Luo Zheng's life force, it would form this ball of green light. However, at this moment, Luo Zheng's complexion did not change at all …
At first, Ying thought that Luo Zheng's life force was very strong, and she would not be able to extract it all in a short time. But now, she realized that something was wrong. This … was not Luo Zheng's life force!
Could it be that there were other lifeforms in this brat's body?
Thinking of this, Ying urged the "Bone Melting Vine" to continue extracting …
After about ten breaths of time, a complete ball of life force appeared in Ying's hand again. Two balls of green light circled around her white and tender palms, slowly pulsating.
When Ying saw this, she pouted …
He continued to draw …
An incense stick's worth of time passed …
In Ying's hands, there were already more than forty dancing green balls of light!
Even Yao Yao could sense that something was amiss. "What's going on?"
Ying furrowed her brows and fell into confusion again.
She was clearly absorbing life force from Luo Zheng's body. However, there were already more than forty complete balls of light. Could it be that there were more than forty independent lifeforms hidden in Luo Zheng's body? Or could it be that this brat had more than forty lives …
And these balls of light were all very strong. They were at least sector lord level lifeforms that had such strong life force!
She was holding the lives of more than forty sector lords in her hands!
Among the three kings, Ying was the smartest. However, at this moment, she was completely confused.
If Luo Zheng's god art was the same as her ten thousand fold willow leaf, it could absorb all kinds of energy. However, right now, she was not releasing any form of energy. Instead, she was extracting life force …
Ying naturally did not expect that Luo Zheng's "energy transfer" could transfer the absorption method of the "Bone Melting Vine" to other big worlds.
Hence, in the big worlds covered by the world will, a portion of the sacred clan warriors became scapegoats again …
The sacred clan warriors were horrified to discover that their companions had unknowingly turned into dried corpses. There were no wounds on their bodies. Their life force had been sucked clean! And such a strange thing only happened to sector lords!
After a period of time …
There were already more than eighty green balls of light in Ying's hand. At this moment, she looked at Luo Zheng speechlessly. Luo Zheng still had a faint smile on his face. He did not look like his life force was declining at all.
At this moment, Ying finally understood that her method did not have any effect on Luo Zheng.
The depressed Ying did not waste the life force she had absorbed. After processing it, she directly poured it into the dying Dark Shadow Sky Sage …
"Let me try …"
Heavenly Sage Juehuo also descended from the sky, leaving Dream God Arrow alone above. At this stage, their battle was meaningless. Out of curiosity, Heavenly Sage Juehuo volunteered himself.
In the beginning, Heavenly Sage Juehuo had thought that Luo Zheng was easy to deal with. That was why he had left Luo Zheng to Heavenly Sage Juehuo. Who would have thought that such a strange thing would happen to this brat?
Thus, Heavenly Sage Juehuo, Ying, Yao … and the recovered Heavenly Sage Juehuo also joined in.
As for Dream God Arrow, he floated alone in the sky. He seemed to have become an outsider. He did not know what to say about the scene in front of him. It was no wonder that the Demi-Immortal Clan valued Luo Zheng so much. Since this fellow had such a remarkable ability, in the future, this brat would be able to protect the universe from destruction!
The two kings and two Heavenly Sages of the Demon Night Clan used all sorts of methods, but in the end, they failed.
In the end, Yao chose to give up. She helplessly shook her head. "Let me activate the Eternal Night Punishment and seal him into the eternal night …"
The Eternal Night Punishment was the cruelest punishment of the Demon Night Clan. It was also an extremely powerful sealing method.
So far, only a few dozen people in the Demon Night Clan had been subjected to the Eternal Night Punishment. Using the Eternal Night Punishment required a considerable amount of faith power. Even for the King of Punishment, the price was quite high. Thus, under normal circumstances, Yao was unwilling to use the punishment.
Not killing Luo Zheng would be a great disaster. Even if she sealed Luo Zheng into the eternal night, it would not be done once and for all. Yao had no other choice.
Yao once again arrived in front of Luo Zheng. Facing this stubborn monster, she felt a headache coming on. "It's a pity that you won't cooperate with me and die. I can only let you enjoy the strongest punishment of the Demon Night Clan!"
Luo Zheng's expression was calm. While he was being beaten, he was also using the will of the world to investigate the situation inside the Platform of Bestowment. At this moment, a faint smile appeared on his face. "You didn't notice anything?"
When Yao heard this, she ignored Luo Zheng. Right now, she only wanted to seal Luo Zheng into the eternal night.
Punishment was what Yao was best at. Killing people was what Yao should do …
But just as Yao began to gather faith power, she frowned slightly. She was actually unable to gather faith power!
At the same time, Ying's expression changed greatly. She seemed to have noticed the problem as well. Both of them could see a trace of panic in each other's eyes. The two women simultaneously looked towards the Platform of Bestowment!
"Rumble …"
The earth began to tremble violently.
The Platform of Bestowment also began to tremble. Soon, huge cracks appeared on its surface. It actually looked as if it was about to collapse!
"What happened?" Dream God's Arrow stared fixedly at the Platform of Bestowment.
This Platform of Bestowment was probably one of the sturdiest structures in the Great Yan Universe. Even a Celestial Venerable would not be able to force his way in. When Luo Zheng fell earlier, he only managed to create a large crater on the surface of the Platform of Bestowment.
At this moment, the Platform of Bestowment was shaking. It was actually able to cause the entire Platform of Bestowment to shatter. Just what had happened?
"Rumble …"
At this moment, the Platform of Bestowment actually exploded from the middle. This Platform of Bestowment with a long history was now completely destroyed.

Chapter 1641
That enormous body was that of an adult True Dragon!
A pair of yellow horns protruded from the True Dragon's head like awls, and a pair of demonic red eyes exuded a Dragon's Might that spread out like a raging wave, causing one to involuntarily feel a sense of awe and veneration.
Dark green dragon scales were arranged closely together, forming a tight layer of dragon armor that enveloped the True Dragon's enormous body. Under the refraction of the sunlight, it emitted a dreamy luster.
To determine the potential and grade of a True Phoenix, one relied on the number of feathers on the Phoenix's tail. But to determine the potential and grade of a True Dragon, one looked at the True Dragon's claws!
Ordinary True Dragons generally had three claws, and the stronger ones had four claws. In the True Dragon Realm, the strongest True Dragons had five claws!
In the entire True Dragon Realm, there were only two True Dragons with five claws. One of them was the eldest of the nine brothers and was once the strongest existence in the True Dragon Realm, the Five Claws Golden Dragon. The other was the Five Claws Black Dragon!
As for the Evil Dragon captured by the Demi-Immortal Tribe, it was the third True Dragon with five claws!
And this dark green True Dragon had six claws!
"Roar!"
The True Dragon soared into the sky, raised its head, and roared!
With a dragon's roar, the winds and clouds of the entire Xixia World changed. The tremendous roar constantly reverberated throughout the great world. The weak creatures couldn't help but feel a sense of submission in their hearts. As for the low-level vicious beasts, they trembled in their own caves!
This was the suppression of natural instincts. Weak creatures were born with a sense of awe and veneration towards powerful creatures. This instinct was accumulated bit by bit through generations of evolution, passed down time after time!
Even the Celestial Venerables present, as well as the two Kings, felt their hearts tremble when they heard this dragon roar!
"Huff huff …"
The enormous True Dragon circled in the air once, then curled up on the Platform of Bestowing Kingship. At the same time, it lowered its enormous dragon head.
The woman on the dragon's head fell downwards. When she was halfway down, she rolled a full circle. Her legs gently stepped on the ground, and she landed firmly in the pit. Her movements were as light as a swallow returning to its nest …
At this moment, Yao, Ying, and Celestial Venerable Juehuo were also nearby. Celestial Venerable Shadow was also bound to Luo Zheng by the Life Extinguishing Shackles. However, the woman ignored them and walked towards Luo Zheng!
Seeing the woman walking towards him, Luo Zheng called out softly, "Smoky Smoky."
This woman gave Luo Zheng a strong feeling of unfamiliarity. She was as pure as a newborn baby. Her entire body exuded a perfect aura, giving people a feeling of unreality.
But no matter how she looked at it, she was exactly the same as Kaori. She was Kaori.
It was normal for Luo Zheng to feel unfamiliar with her. After all, ever since Xun had accompanied him, she had always appeared in the form of a floating soul. Even if she was a sword spirit, she was still illusory.
Therefore, in Luo Zheng's impression, Smoky Smoky should be like this.
Now that Smoky Smoky had a brand-new body, Luo Zheng felt that she was a stranger …
As soon as he finished speaking, Smoky quickened her pace and rushed forward. She hugged Luo Zheng, who was sitting cross-legged on the ground, and pulled his head deep into her embrace.
Luo Zheng did not struggle, nor did he speak. He buried his head deep into her chest and felt a firm touch on his face. Although Luo Zheng was unfamiliar with her aura, a long-lost sense of peace rose in his heart. It was as if all the troubles and disputes in the world were gradually leaving him at this moment, and all the responsibilities and burdens had disappeared from his shoulders.
Xun, who was holding Luo Zheng in her arms, looked at Yao who was not far away. A murderous aura slowly swirled in her mesmerizing eyes. She opened her mouth and said, "This day has finally come … Bitch."
The muscles all over her body were extremely tight. She was like a statue, standing there motionlessly.
Yao Yao stared at Smoky with her teeth tightly clenched together. This was the only way to conceal the earth-shattering shock in her heart!
How could this be?!
Even if this brat Luo Zheng possessed remarkable abilities and was unable to kill him, it was not a big deal.
After sealing Luo Zheng with the Eternal Night Punishment, he would hand him over to the Sacred Race to be dealt with.
As for the Dream God Arrow, he had already lost all will to fight. A Heavenly Venerable who had fallen into Heaven's Decay had no value at all. He could easily kill him …
Everything was under Yao's control. Even Heavenly Venerate Juehuo and Heavenly Venerate Anying, who had always maintained a neutral stance, were helping him. It was as if the heavens were helping him!
Why was there such a huge reversal in such a short time?
Why would such a huge True Dragon appear? And why was it a Six-Clawed True Dragon? Wasn't Five Claws the strongest of the Dragon Clan …
Why was Xun, who had disappeared for so long, standing in front of him in such a form?
Why did the power of faith in her Faith Pool completely disappear in an instant …
Countless doubts were like sharp arrows that could not be avoided, riddling her state of mind with thousands of holes.
Just a moment ago, she felt that she was in control of everything. But in the next moment, she discovered that she was only in control of a crumbling building. In the blink of an eye, the building had collapsed.
Under extreme disappointment, Yao Yao lost all reason and almost went crazy. She suddenly turned into a bolt of lightning and rushed towards Smoky!
"Spear," Smoky whispered to Luo Zheng in her arms.
Then, she pulled out the Holy Slaughter Spear from Luo Zheng's spatial ring!
"Hu …"
The moment she grasped the Holy Slaughter Spear, her aura underwent a great change. A rich killing intent spiraled up the Holy Slaughter Spear. At the same time, slender bone spurs condensed on Smoky's back!
Those bone spurs radiated out in two pairs of fans, forming two irregular bone wings.
This pair of bone wings was condensed from the power of faith. This was Smoky's battle form!
In the Yao Night Clan, the King of Life and King of Punishment didn't fight outside of their duties. All external battles were led by the King of Slaughter. This was because the King of Slaughter was born for battle. Thus, only the King of Slaughter had this special battle form.
Smoky had used this form once before. After borrowing the power of faith in the Asura World, she condensed this pair of bone wings. However, Yao Yao quickly sealed Smoky's Faith Pool again.
"You … are the bitch!"
The expression on Yao Yao's face was crazed to the extreme. That perfect face of hers was now incomparably distorted. She spiraled into the air and madly channeled her own True Yuan into the Holy Lightning Chained Hammer. The might of this attack was not small either …
However, the reason why Yao Yao was so strong was because she relied on the power of faith in her Faith Pool.
After losing the power of faith and using vital essence to fight, she was only a Sector Lord. Her strength was at most comparable to a peak Great Sector Lord. This kind of attack was not even worth mentioning to the current Smoky …
The Holy Slaughter Spear lightly spun and a huge spear image appeared on the surface of the Holy Slaughter Spear!
"One Thought Without Turning!"
The spear image was blood red. It was many times stronger than Smoky's One Thought Without Turning. However, she didn't shoot it out. Instead, she pointed it out in a straight line. With a "dong" sound, it blocked the Holy Lightning Chained Hammer!
"Crackle …"
From the Holy Lightning Chained Hammer, countless lightning slithered down the Holy Slaughter Spear like little snakes.
Smoky lightly shook the spear and all the lightning was drowned out by the killing intent …
Yao Yao's hammer didn't work. She turned around again and the Holy Lightning Chained Hammer changed its angle and smashed down at Smoky.
Smoky's expression was extremely calm. She pulled the spear back and raised her arm slightly. The spear pointed out once more and hit the Holy Lightning Chained Hammer …
"Dong dong dong dong …"
Yao Yao smashed out dozens of times! Smoky also stabbed out dozens of times!
However, one was using all her strength while the other was casually attacking. The difference in strength between the two was obvious!
"Whoosh!"
Probably tired of Yao Yao's pestering, Smoky suddenly flicked the Holy Lightning Chained Hammer out of Yao Yao's hand. It spun in the air and smashed a small hole in the bottom of the giant pit.
Yao Yao's hands were empty. The crazed look on her face disappeared without a trace.
After losing the Faith Pond and the Holy Lightning Chained Hammer, she didn't have any strength to fight back against Smoky.
"Whoosh —"
The Dream God Arrow in the sky also shot down …
Just now, he was quietly floating in the air. When he saw this series of scenes, he, who was originally in complete despair, was completely dumbfounded!
At this moment, he felt like he was in a dream. He felt like everything he saw was unreal!
"King!" The Dream God Arrow floated down beside Smoky.
Smoky glanced at Yao Yao and then turned to look at the Dream God Arrow. Seeing the Dream God Arrow's old face, a gentle look appeared on her face. "Dream God Arrow, you … have aged a lot."
At their level, there was only one concept of "aging", and that was that they had slipped into the Five Decay of Heaven and Man. The Dream God Arrow would inevitably fall into the abyss of death, and there was no way to save it.

Chapter 1642
Dream God Arrow's destiny had been bestowed upon him by Xun, so his relationship with Xun was naturally different from the other Heavenly Venerables of the Yao Night clan.
Even though Heavenly Venerables were the pinnacle of existence in the universe, they would never submit to anyone.
Although the other Heavenly Venerables of the Yao Night clan respected their king and were led by their king, most of the time, they were on equal footing.
However, Dream God Arrow had willingly submitted to Xun, treating Xun as his master, his king.
For Xun's return, Dream God Arrow was willing to give up everything. Even if his life was at stake, he wouldn't regret it!
Hearing Xun's simple greeting was already worth it to him.
The conflict in front of them hadn't been resolved yet, so it wasn't the time to reminisce.
Yao stood in front of Xun, her face still filled with unwillingness. Her personality was stubborn and filled with ruthlessness.
Even though she knew she had been utterly defeated, she still couldn't accept it …
As for Ying, her expression was calm. No one could tell from her expression what she was thinking.
Heavenly Venerate Juehuo stood nearby, his heart filled with bitterness.
He and Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow were originally neutral Heavenly Venerables. They had never participated in the internal conflicts of the Yao Night clan. They would only act when the clan was invaded.
This time, they had acted based on their judgment of the situation. In addition, Dream God Arrow had brought a Realm Lord to the Xixia Realm, so they had nothing to worry about …
Who knew that Luo Zheng's strength had far exceeded their expectations. In the end, even after the two kings had returned, they still couldn't do anything to him.
They hadn't expected such a change in the Bestowment Platform, so they were caught off guard.
Heavenly Venerate Juehuo looked at Xun cautiously. He was prepared to use greater teleportation to escape at any time. If Xun became the ruler of the Yao Night clan, he wouldn't be able to stay there. However, as a Heavenly Venerate, finding a place to hide in the universe wasn't a problem.
The most depressed person was probably Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow.
Although he had completely recovered with Ying's help, the problem was that one side of the damned chain was still on him. He couldn't escape at all …
Xun's gaze slowly swept across the few of them, and finally stopped on Ying. "Ying, you also want to be my enemy? Why? "
After pondering for a while, Xun suddenly asked such a question. Everyone was stunned … This Xun didn't seem to know anything?
Ying's face revealed a noncommittal expression. She was not as depressed and unwilling as Yao. Instead, she smiled freely. "Since our positions are different, naturally I will be your enemy." Ying was not good at fighting, nor did she like fighting. However, under the control of the Soul Sealing Needle, she had no choice. After all, she could not resist the intention of the Soul Sealing Needle.
"Stand? What position? "Smog was even more confused, as if she had no idea what was going on.
In the end, she could only look at Luo Zheng with pleading eyes. "I only remember when you went to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. I don't remember what happened after that..."
In order to erase the Soul Sealing Needle, Kaoru had completely lost her memories.
However, the King-Bestowing Platform was originally connected to the King. It contained all the memories of the past Kings, including all of Smoky's memories. However, these memories were limited to the Dayan Universe.
After Smoky followed Luo Zheng into the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, her memories did not exist. When Luo Zheng returned, Smoky's soul was like a wooden stake. It was even more impossible for her to remember anything.
When Luo Zheng stuffed the Ancestral Gragon in, he also attached Smoky's soul to the Ancestral Gragon.
This action was very risky. Luo Zheng did not know if it would work.
However, the prerequisites to enter the King-Bestowing Platform were there.
First of all, the Ancestral Gragon could travel through space. Ordinary barriers and arrays could not stop this little thing. That was its innate ability.
And Smoky's soul was the prerequisites to enter the King-Bestowing Platform. The Dream God Arrow waited for so many years and did not act alone. Because Smoky was not by his side, he could not enter the King-Bestowing Platform and destroy the Faith Pond.
All these conditions were met. Luo Zheng quietly gave the Ancestral Gragon some instructions. Then, he could only resign himself to fate.
Unexpectedly, the results were far beyond Luo Zheng's expectations. Not only did the Ancestral Gragon bring Smoky with him, but he also obtained her memories on the King-Bestowing Platform. The Ancestral Gragon also devoured the power of faith in Ying and Yao's Faith Ponds!
This power of faith was accumulated by the Yao Ye Clan for countless years. How terrifying was the amount of power of faith? After the Ancestral Gragon devoured it, his body underwent a shocking change. Finally, he became what he was today.
When the Ancestral Gragon devoured Ying's Faith Pond, he found a perfect spirit embryo in the Faith Pond.
Previously, Ying wanted to use this spirit embryo to exchange for Smoky's Sacred Massacre Spear. However, she was rejected. Smoky, who had recovered her memories, naturally did not hold back. She took the spirit embryo for herself.
This spirit embryo was nurtured in the Pond of Faith, and it took a lot of effort for it to be completed. It was a truly perfect body, so small that every meridian and every hair tip was a perfect existence. There were no flaws or impurities, and this spirit embryo was in a state of acceptance of souls.
Smoky took over the spirit embryo without any hindrance. After she took over, the spirit embryo began to grow crazily. In the end, it became Smoky's current appearance …
Thus, the perfect temperament that Luo Zheng felt from Smoky's body did not come from Smoky's soul. Instead, it was because of the spirit embryo.
However, Smoky was not clear about the situation in the universe. She also did not know the position of the Celestial Venerables in the Yao Ye Clan. After taking over the spirit embryo and watching the Ancestral Gragon devour Ying's Faith Pond, Smoky felt a little guilty. After all, there was not much conflict between her and Ying.
After Smoky and the Ancestral Gragon came out of the King-Conferring Platform, the first thing she noticed was Luo Zheng. Then, she looked at Yao. Naturally, she saw Yao as her opponent. After all, the conflict between her and Yao had never stopped!
Luo Zheng saw everything that happened in the King-Conferring Platform through the Will of the World. This was why he was stalling for time. He was waiting for Smoky to break out of her cocoon. However, he did not know how much of Smoky's memories were in the King-Conferring Platform. Seeing Smoky so confused, Luo Zheng used his True Essence Voice Transmission to tell her what happened in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. He also told her how she lost her memories. He also told her about the drastic changes that happened in the Yao Ye Clan.
After hearing Luo Zheng's words, Smoky finally understood the current situation of the Yao Ye Clan.
She also understood what Ying meant. Due to the existence of the Soul-Sealing Needle, Ying's position was different from hers. After Smoky destroyed the Soul-Sealing Needle, she did not even know what the Soul-Sealing Needle was …
The problem of the Soul-Sealing Needle was a bit more complicated. She would deal with it later. Smoky's eyes looked past Yao and locked onto Empyrean Juehuo.
As the Queen of Slaughter, Smoky did not have much compassion. Even if an Empyrean of her clan had committed an unforgivable crime, she would still act decisively without hesitation!
But under Smoky's gaze, Empyrean Juehuo, who had already prepared to escape, instantly activated the Greater Teleportation Law Formula!
"Hiss …"
A blazing white power of Space Laws bloomed beneath his feet. His figure instantly dissipated!
"You want to escape?"
Luo Zheng and Dream God Arrow both frowned. When an Empyrean used the Greater Teleportation Law, they could instantly move a great distance. Therefore, it was very difficult to kill an Empyrean if they wanted to escape. Luo Zheng's Life Extinguishing Shackles could lock an Empyrean, but the other end of the Life Extinguishing Shackles was also locked onto Empyrean Shadow.
However, just as Celestial Venerable Juehuo used Greater Void Transference to escape, the Ancestral Dragon coiled on the King-Bestowing Platform began to move. It suddenly raised its neck and its dragon head shot out. It opened its dragon mouth and bit into the void!
Although the Ancestral Dragon's body was huge, its movements were unimaginably fast. It simply did not match its huge size!
With this bite, everyone saw something appear in its mouth. Then, they heard Empyrean Juehuo's angry roar. A green flame exploded in the Ancestral Dragon's mouth. Clearly, this was Empyrean Juehuo's counterattack!
However, the Ancestral Dragon still closed its mouth, including the green flame. After chewing for a while, its angry roar became softer and softer …
The dignified Empyrean Juehuo had been crushed and swallowed by the Ancestral Dragon!

Chapter 1643
The Greater Teleportation Art allowed a martial artist to instantly appear in another location.
Once one reached the World Lord realm, they would be able to grasp this ability. In a battle between World Lords, or between Empyreans, it was usually very difficult to kill each other.
Martial artists who were proficient in the Greater Teleportation Art were well aware that the so-called 'teleportation' of the Greater Teleportation Art wasn't completed in an instant.
Ordinary space channels used the Space Laws to construct a path, folding both ends of space together!
The stronger the Space Laws, the higher the degree of folding.
For example, the space channels in the Great Thousand Domains could reduce a distance of a thousand miles to ten miles. As for the space channels in the Upper Realms, they could reduce a distance of ten thousand miles to ten miles. As for the space channels that crossed between realms, they were even more exaggerated. They could reduce a distance of a million miles to ten miles.
The Greater Teleportation Art used by an Empyrean was in essence to construct a space channel. However, this space channel could only allow a small number of people to pass through. Moreover, this space channel couldn't exist for a long time. It could only exist for an extremely short period of time. However, it could reduce a distance of millions of miles to a foot!
In other words, when an Empyrean used the Greater Teleportation Art, they only needed to move a foot to traverse millions of miles.
Once someone used the Greater Teleportation Art, they wouldn't actually disappear. Instead, they would traverse millions of miles at an extremely fast speed!
The extreme speed of traversing space couldn't be captured. Thus, once an Empyrean used the Greater Teleportation Art, the pursuing Empyrean could only use the Greater Teleportation Art to chase after them, hoping that their speed could catch up.
However, the Ancestral Dragon's understanding of the Space Laws had already surpassed their imaginations!
Empyrean Juehuo used the Greater Teleportation Art to travel through space. In less than the blink of an eye, he was able to escape from the Dawnperch World.
However, he never expected that the moment he entered the spatial passageway and began to move, he would be bitten by the Ancestral Dragon and dragged out of the spatial passageway after just a few thousand feet!
At that moment, Luo Zheng and the others were stunned. As for Empyrean Juehuo, he was both shocked and angry. Thus, he immediately chose to counterattack.
However, the Ancestral Dragon had absorbed all the power of faith from the two faith ponds. At present, the Ancestral Dragon was in the 'youth' stage and possessed extremely terrifying strength. Celestial Exalted Juehuo struggled in the Ancestral Dragon's mouth for a while before ultimately perishing. He was swallowed alive by the Ancestral Dragon.
After Empyrean Juehuo died, the Heavenly Dao trembled.
All of the Empyreans in the Great Yan Universe felt it at the same time. They naturally understood that another Empyrean had fallen.
They didn't know which Celestial Exalt had died. Almost five Celestial Exalts had died in the Dayan Realm in the past three years. Three of them belonged to the Human Dao Alliance, while the other two belonged to minor clans …
In a short span of less than twenty years, the number of Empyreans that had fallen in succession had already exceeded ten. This was practically unimaginable in the former Great Yan Universe.
Just from this, it could be seen that the entire Grand Evolution Universe was undergoing a violent change.
After Ancestral Gragon swallowed Heavenly Venerate Juehuo, his huge body shook violently like a pendulum. Then, he suddenly opened his mouth and coughed twice. "Ka, ka …"
The coughing was like a thunderclap. As he coughed, a large amount of azure flames came out of his throat. Heavenly Venerate Juehuo had died with great reluctance. He was still struggling in his death throes after being swallowed into the Ancestral Gragon's stomach.
Heavenly Venerate Juehuo's azure flames were an extremely powerful type of flame. As the Ancestral Gragon coughed repeatedly, the azure flames coming out of his mouth rapidly decreased. However, it seemed that he couldn't completely clear them away …
Not long after, the Ancestral Gragon, who had just reached adulthood, was enraged. He suddenly raised his head and opened his mouth!
"Roar, roar, roar …"
A demonic red dragon breath suddenly burst out of his mouth!
There were also traces of green flames mixed in the dragon breath, but those green flames were like bubbles in a river, disappearing in the blink of an eye.
After expelling all of the azure flames from his body, the Ancestral Gragon revealed a satisfied expression and continued to sit on the Platform of Bestowment. As for the dragon breath he spat out, it spread into the sky, spreading out into demonic red clouds that didn't dissipate for a long time.
Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow swallowed his saliva when he saw this. He only felt fear and trepidation. This enormous dragon made even Heavenly Venerates feel reverence.
At this moment, Xun shifted her gaze to Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow once again …
Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow's strength was originally inferior to Heavenly Venerate Juehuo's. Moreover, he specialized in concealment rather than assassination. Now that he was bound by the Life Extinguishing Shackles, his strength was greatly reduced, and he couldn't use his concealment ability.
"It's your turn," Xun said coldly.
Now was the time for Xun to settle the score. She had just learned about the current situation from Luo Zheng. She also knew that the Fiend Night Clan was in dire need of manpower. A Heavenly Venerate's value to a clan was immeasurable. However, Xun didn't plan on letting Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow live.
Moreover, as long as Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow didn't die, the Life Extinguishing Shackles wouldn't last. Luo Zheng couldn't keep delaying Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow's movements …
"My Queen, please spare my life!"
Heavenly Venerate Dark Shadow's heart had completely collapsed. He didn't have a trace of the strength that a Heavenly Venerate should have.
Xun's expression didn't change at all. Her state of mind was as cold and firm as ice!
When she was hiding in Luo Zheng's body as a soul, she had thought about it countless times. In this life, she would no longer have the slightest bit of mercy for anyone other than Luo Zheng …

Chapter 1647

She didn't expect another Saint to appear the moment Mu Haiji appeared, and that Saint was Luo Zheng's father?
Yao could tell at a glance that Luo Zheng wasn't ordinary, but she didn't expect Luo Zheng to be the direct descendant of a Saint. She could only accept her loss wholeheartedly. The arrogance that often appeared on her face also dissipated at this moment, and the flickering lightning in her eyes dimmed and dissipated …
Luo Zheng stared blankly at the dissipating energy fluctuations. He suddenly remembered that Luo Xiao was still behind him. He was shocked and afraid that Luo Xiao would disappear as well, so he turned around and said, "Father!"
Luo Xiao's silhouette didn't disappear, but he didn't respond either. He remained silent …
Luo Zheng revealed a nervous expression and asked, "Father, are you still there?"
"Xing 'er, I'm here …" Luo Xiao responded.
Hearing his father's response, Luo Zheng let out a sigh of relief...
When Luo Zheng was young, he didn't know much. All these years, he thought that his father was already dead, but he had accumulated too many doubts in his heart. Although Luo Zheng more or less understood the general situation through other channels, there were still some things that he wanted to ask in person!
"Huff huff huff …"

The originally erratic silhouette became more and more solid. When the silhouette shrunk to the size of Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng could already recognize Luo Xiao from the silhouette!
Seeing his father's heroic appearance, Luo Zheng almost cried.
After experiencing the tempering of wind and frost and the hopeless situation of life and death, Luo Zheng's state of mind was already as hard as iron. He knew that all negative emotions were useless. For the sake of becoming stronger, for the sake of his own growth, he'd already completely abandoned these emotions.
But standing in front of his father, Luo Zheng felt that he was still that weak child …
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, Luo Xiao's face also revealed a trace of guilt.
The wisp of Soul Aura that he kept in Huan Yu could actually give Luo Zheng more pointers. But when a martial artist cultivated, whether it was in the Domain of the Gods or Huan Yu, if they wanted to go further, or even surpass Luo Xiao, they could only rely on themselves to walk further on the martial path!
So he had no choice but to create the illusion that he had fallen. He had no choice but to create one crisis after another, trapping Luo Zheng in it and watching him struggle desperately, like a wounded beast, licking his wounds again and again to become stronger and stronger.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng did not disappoint him, and even performed better than he expected. But he also understood that doing this was too cruel to Luo Zheng.
"You … come with me." Luo Xiao turned around and waved his hand, an illusory door appeared in front of him!
The door was like a heavenly palace, but also like a mirage, slowly floating in front of Luo Zheng.

Luo Zheng jumped up from the ground and looked back at Xun, his lips moved slightly, "Wait for me."

As for Ying, Xun didn't make things difficult for her.
After all, Ying had led the rebellion because of the soul sealing needle, she didn't have much ill intentions. Plus, in this king bestowing platform, Xun had relied on the spirit embryo in Ying's faith pond.

The faith ponds' water needed to accumulate over a long period of time, and the elders all understood that Xun was the only king left in the Yao Night clan. It would be difficult for the three kings of the past to reappear.

The Heavenly Dao!

"In the Great Evolution Universe, it's also known as the Mandate of Heaven," Luo Xiao added.

Chapter 1648
This universe was created by Luo Xiao himself.
Therefore, the beings in the Great Yan Universe were unable to cultivate to the peak of strength. This was a boundary that secondary life forms could not surpass, and it was also a restriction.
There was only one kind of person that could surpass this restriction, and that was a Dao Child. Once a Dao Child crossed this boundary, they would be able to condense a Godhead and leave the Great Yan Universe.

Luo Zheng's eyes sparkled under the illumination of the golden lights.
On the screen, streaks of golden light constantly flowed. Each streak of golden light contained a karmic law of the Heavenly Dao. Some of the streaks of golden light contained exceptionally powerful auras, while others had no auras at all.
There were strong and weak Wills of Heaven. Some Wills of Heaven couldn't even be used in battle. For example, the Resurrection Heavenly Mandate of the Resurrection Heavenly Venerate could only resurrect fallen martial artists, but it was of no help in battle.
However, there were some Wills of Heaven that were specifically used in battle. The aura emitted by these Wills of Heaven was naturally much more powerful!
Luo Zheng's gaze swept over and he could faintly discern the function of some Wills of Heaven!
For example, the Wills of Heaven on the left side of the screen emitted a bit of chill. There were even specks of frost condensing on the screen. This Wills of Heaven might be the complete power of the Ice Law.
However, with the comprehension of Sages, they also had their own comprehension of perfect Laws.
Therefore, there was more than one complete Law of Ice. Complete Laws of Ice could also be extended into several extremely powerful profundities.
For example, the Ice Soul Cold Flame that Luo Zheng had comprehended from Saint Jiu was a special kind of Ice Law Profundity.
Not far from the Ice Law, multicolored flames spouted out. These multicolored flames formed little kittens that walked down the screen and played with each other. These multicolored flames were definitely the mysteries of the tenth layer of the Fire Law.
Even an Empyrean would never be able to pry into the mysteries of the Heavenly Dao. This time, following his father into the Heavenly Dao, Luo Zheng feasted his eyes on it.

Presently, the Grand Yan Universe was facing a crisis. An additional Celestial Venerable was an additional portion of combat power!
Each Empyrean was an existence that couldn't be underestimated. If a few powerful Heaven's Wills were utilized to the extreme, they could even fight against a True God!

Luo Zheng had asked this question when he was very young because he did not have any impression of his mother.

Sometimes he would answer that his mother was dead, and sometimes he would answer that his mother was missing. After asking several times, Luo Zheng was sternly reprimanded by Luo Xiao.

Although Luo Zheng had such a speculation, he was still not sure in his heart. At this moment, he asked hesitantly.

"Indeed, it's up to you to believe it or not," Luo Xiao continued to deny it.
However, the more Luo Xiao denied it, the more certain Luo Zheng was in his heart!
He definitely had a mother. If his mother really died of an illness when he was young, Luo Xiao would certainly be able to answer truthfully at this moment.

Therefore, at this moment, Luo Xiao's stiff expression and perfunctory answer gave Luo Zheng a glimmer of hope.

Therefore, Luo Zheng changed the topic and asked, "Father, is Luo Yan okay now?"

In Huanyu, the arrangement of the siblings was the most important, followed by Hua Tianming and Luo Xi Xuan, and then Xi Youqin and Xuanyuan Chenfeng … Every step was personally arranged by Luo Xiao. However, no matter how the others performed, the most important thing was still Luo Zheng.

"The Ten Thousand Laws Enemy is divided into two sections: the Profound Path Chapter and the Profound Path Chapter!
"The Profound Path Chapter is to cut off the cause, and the Profound Path Chapter is to destroy the effect!"

For instance, the Roar Command also stimulated a Karma Law.

In the Book of Ten Thousand Laws, the 'Cutting the Cause' was to cut off the connection between cause and effect!
If the karma was split into two and no longer connected, the Mandate of Heaven would lose its effect.
