Luo Zheng's long sword danced around him. Wherever the sword edge passed, it dragged out strands of black hair. These strands of black hair were like tangles, wrapping around his entire body!
Any Wills of Heaven that approached Luo Zheng were instantly cut off by Luo Zheng's karmic connection, turning into a beam of golden light that returned to the screen!
This' Cutting Karmic Connection 'was only one move. Once one thoroughly comprehended it, they would completely understand it!
To others, these Wills of Heaven's karmic connections were the highest level of strength. But in front of Luo Zheng's sword, they were fragile.
Although Luo Zheng had completely grasped this move, Luo Xiao still didn't relax in the slightest …
Seeing his son's nimble figure, Luo Xiao was originally overjoyed. But before long, the smile on his face gradually faded and a trace of worry appeared in his eyes.
"Shua!"
After cutting off the last Wills of Heaven, Luo Zheng's long sword spun in a circle in the air before being placed into Luo Zheng's spatial ring. He turned to look at Luo Xiao. As he noticed his father's expression, he immediately asked with concern, "Father, what's wrong?"
His father was normally extremely dignified. For him to have such an expression at this moment, there must be something extremely important on his mind.
Luo Xiao didn't evade Luo Zheng's question. Instead, he stared at Luo Zheng and lightly said, "You are the universe's number one Dao Child. You have the protection of the Demi-Immortal Clan as well as the support of your father. Although you have experienced some tribulations in this universe, overall your journey has been smooth sailing, but …"
Luo Xiao paused for a moment and then continued, "If the Universe of Dayan survives this crisis, then when you step into the Realm of the Gods in the future, your encounters won't be so simple."
The tribulations that Luo Zheng had experienced these past years could be described as frightening but not dangerous. Moreover, many of these frightening dangers had been secretly arranged by Luo Xiao. But as a father, Luo Xiao had always been protecting Luo Zheng from behind, never leaving his side!
In Luo Xiao's eyes, Luo Zheng was still a toddler learning to walk. As a father, he had always placed his hands on Luo Zheng's shoulders. Whenever Luo Zheng was about to fall, he would help him up.
This kind of care was the fatherly love that Luo Xiao silently gave. But Luo Zheng would eventually grow up and leave the universe.
In the Universe of Dayan, Luo Xiao was the master. He was the omnipotent and omniscient Lord of Dayan. This was also equivalent to Luo Zheng's home field, Luo Zheng's home …
If Luo Zheng really stepped into the Realm of the Gods, Luo Xiao's hands would no longer be able to stretch so far. Then, who would help Luo Zheng?
The Realm of the Gods was a true and complete world!
Even a Saint couldn't transform the entire Realm of the Gods. If Luo Zheng stepped into the Realm of the Gods, then the cruel competition would truly begin. That would be Luo Zheng's starting point. Falling down in the Realm of the Gods meant complete death!
"Father, is it because of that fellow Mu Haiji?" Luo Zheng asked.
Luo Xiao's meaning was similar to Mu Haiji's. Luo Zheng was able to run amok in the Universe of Dayan and walk step by step until now. Although he relied on himself, his father also played a significant role. Once he stepped into the Realm of the Gods, it wouldn't be so simple …
"No," Luo Xiao shook his head. "Mu Haiji is telling the truth. The Realm of the Gods will be more difficult than you think … If Zheng 'er enters the Realm of the Gods and can't find a chance, don't force yourself …"
Luo Xiao's purpose in raising Luo Zheng was indeed to save himself.
Luo Zheng had smoothly reached this step and had never let him down.
But at this point, Luo Xiao had thoughts of retreating. He didn't want Luo Zheng to take such a huge risk to go against the Templars for his sake. He would rather Luo Zheng settle down in some corner of the Realm of the Gods and live peacefully for the rest of his life.
Hearing his father's words, Luo Zheng's expression suddenly became serious. He stared at Luo Xiao and said, "According to father's words, why doesn't Zheng 'er give up now?"
"Give up now?" Luo Xiao's eyes narrowed slightly.
"Father has carefully cultivated this child, so naturally, there is still a chance for survival. This child must do his best to live up to Father's expectations. If Father doesn't care about your own life and death, why did you let this child suffer so much and let Luo Yan separate from this child? Then what's the point of the Martial Dao that I've always strived for? "Luo Zheng stared at Luo Xiao and continued.
Seeing Luo Zheng's serious gaze, Luo Xiao was stunned for a while before he sighed deeply.
What Luo Zheng said was indeed correct.
The current Luo Xiao was just cherishing Luo Zheng, but his fatherly love was too narrow.
When a person cultivated to this level, what they pursued was no longer a comfortable life, nor was it glory, splendor, or wealth. Instead, they wanted to pursue their own heart that had never changed!
Luo Xiao's apprehension not only showed that he was looking down on his son, but also erased Luo Zheng's heart. He shouldn't have done so.
Moreover, since Luo Xiao had made this arrangement, he was like an arrow that had been shot. There was no turning back.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1651
Hearing his father's sigh, Luo Zheng smiled. His eyes quietly lit up as he clasped his hands towards Luo Xiao and said, "Father, believe in me!"
The confidence in his eyes was unprecedentedly strong, as if he could overcome any difficult problem!
Luo Xiao was able to become a Saint, so he naturally understood the difficulties. Although in his eyes, Luo Zheng's confidence was incomparably childish, it was purely a newborn calf that was not afraid of tigers.
However, under the encouragement of Luo Zheng's aura, he felt slightly comforted and nodded. "Mm! Next, I will teach you how to construct the inner world … "
Letting Luo Zheng cultivate the "Primal Chaos Secret Technique" was a risky and crazy move.
After all, no one had ever tried to transform their own physical body into such a way that it could really contain the primal chaos within it.
Because of this, Luo Xiao and Gu Bei, the two Saints, even argued about it. Luo Xiao felt that there was no need to take this risk!
Luo Xiao was in favor of Luo Zheng following the cultivation system. After all, the entire cultivation system had been passed down in the divine realm and was already a very mature system. As Sages, they had reached the pinnacle of the cultivation system!
Luo Xiao, Gu Bei, Jiu …
Three Saints devoted all their efforts to nurture Luo Zheng. This was probably a treatment that was hard to come by.
At that time, Gu Bei and Luo Xiao held completely opposite views!
"Have you ever seen a Saint's son become a Saint?"
Gu Bei refuted Luo Xiao with one sentence, leaving him speechless.
In countless divine eras, some Saints had fallen and the universe had been shattered …
Whether it was the current Saints or the fallen Saints, there had never been a second Saint among their direct descendants.
If Luo Zheng wanted to fight against Mu Haiji, his goal must be to become a Saint. However, Gu Bei felt that this probability was too low. Even if Luo Zheng and Luo Yan's origins were extraordinary, it would not be easy to become a Saint!
If Luo Xiao followed the Cultivation system, the possibility of him saving himself or even taking revenge was too low.
Back then, Sage Jiu had also been on Gu Bei's side. Sage Jiu was an expert in the art of deduction. After deducing according to Luo Zheng's plan, he quickly came up with an answer. If he let Luo Zheng cultivate step by step, the probability of Luo Zheng creating a miracle was less than one in three hundred thousand …
This probability wasn't low. Amongst the countless lives of the world, the number of martial artists that could become Saints was one in a trillion, or even one in a trillion. Every Saint was an absolute favorite of the Realm of the Gods. They had to go through countless trials and tribulations in order to grasp onto this slim chance of becoming a Saint, and only then would they be able to occupy a place in the Holy Temples!
The probability of one in three hundred thousand was indeed not low. This was because of Luo Zheng's special background and the careful guidance of the three Sages.
When Saint Jiu was deducing, he had already set these conditions in advance!
The key was that Luo Xiao only had one chance to save himself!
Once the plan started, there was no stopping it …
From this point of view, this hope was a little too slim.
Thus, Gu Bei and Sage Jiu stood on the same front. If they wanted to save Luo Xiao, if they wanted to create a miracle, they could only take an unconventional path and take an even greater risk. Because only in this way could they create a miracle.
The final result was what was happening right now.
Luo Zheng had lived up to expectations and had walked his own path within the 'Primal Chaos System'. No one knew what achievements Luo Zheng would be able to achieve in the future, but at least for now, it seemed that he had succeeded!
Unfortunately, even with Gu Bei's intelligence, he was unable to predict just how far the 'Primal Chaos System' would develop. In truth, if the 'Primal Chaos Secret Technique' was placed within the True Essence system, it could only be considered a 'beginner' level cultivation method.
It only taught Luo Zheng how to open up the Primal Chaos Sea and how to control the Primal Chaos Qi. As for the application of the Primal Chaos Qi and the construction of the world, it was all something that Luo Zheng had discovered by himself.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng's exploration of the path had not gone wrong. At the very least, the world within his body had expanded to a considerable extent. Moreover, it was a real world that was comparable to the Realm of the Gods. The creatures within were not formed from True Essence, but were constructed from Primal Chaos!
The problem that puzzled Luo Zheng was how to expand this world and how to construct it … He had no prior experience and could only bitterly fumble around by himself.
As the architect of the universe, Luo Xiao's understanding of the world within his body was far richer than that of an ordinary martial artist. Naturally, he could help Luo Zheng.
Hearing his father's words, Luo Zheng nodded without the slightest resistance. After all, he still had many questions regarding the world within his body that he had opened up.
After the expansion of the world within his body, Luo Zheng had secretly conducted some experiments.
For example, when he took out the fishes within his body, the fishes did not disappear. According to the level of the Primal Chaos World, these creatures formed from Primal Chaos should all be "Same Level Creatures." Theoretically, these creatures were all qualified to survive in the Realm of the Gods and were not restricted by the Heavenly Dao.
After that, Luo Zheng had conducted another experiment. He had captured a low-level vicious beast, the "Fiery Poison Crab," and placed it into his own world.
Casually placing creatures into his world within his body still made Luo Zheng somewhat uncomfortable. The moment that low-level Fiery Poison Crab was placed into his world, a strange scene appeared.
Luo Zheng felt that this world began to produce a feeling of rejection. At this moment, this world within his body had become a whole, rejecting the entry of the Fiery Poison Crab. Immediately, cracks began to appear on the surface of the Fiery Poison Ant. This kind of sturdy ant became abnormally fragile, and its body began to disintegrate.
That Fiery Poison Crab first broke into relatively large fragments, then into bits of powder, and then into dust.
In the end, not even a trace of dust was left. It turned into a faint wisp of True Essence, dissipating into Luo Zheng's world within his body!
After other martial artists opened up their world within their body, they could place creatures into their world within their body. Some Saints even had their own family members living within their bodies, but Luo Zheng's world within his body could not. Entering his world within his body was equivalent to entering the Realm of the Gods. The only consequence of forcibly crossing the Heavenly Dao was to be disintegrated and reduced into a wisp of True Essence!
"Don't have any thoughts of resistance. Lead me into your world with you," Luo Xiao said.
When Luo Xiao approached, Luo Zheng's thoughts moved, and he took his father's wisp of Qi into his world within his body. At the same time, Luo Zheng also brewed an internal incarnation!
As a Saint of the Realm of the Gods, Luo Xiao naturally would not be rejected like the Fiery Poison Ant when he entered Luo Zheng's world within his body …
After a short while, Luo Xiao's wisp of Qi floated high in the sky of this world, looking at the nine massive stars not far away. At this moment, he could not help but exclaim in admiration, "Bringing the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier into his world within his body as a Realm Lord, truly a miraculous existence …"
After some True Gods opened the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier, those nine stars floated above the True God's dantian.
As the saying went, "Abdomen like a drum of stars, radiant with nine radiances." Thus, True Gods who refined their bodies only had nine protruding pearls flickering with light in their abdomens. That was the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier.
However, True Gods did not have the qualifications to bring the nine stars into their world within their body. Only some top-level True Gods could merge their world with the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier when they constructed their universe and started to become Saints!
Luo Xiao had experienced this process and was very clear how difficult it was to merge the nine stars with the world within one's body.
Now, Luo Zheng was at the Realm Lord tier and had already merged the two into one. If this happened in the Realm of the Gods, it would probably make countless True Gods go crazy. This was really too inconceivable.
Cultivating the Primal Chaos Secret Arts was admittedly an unorthodox path, but now it looked like it was indeed a wise choice. Luo Xiao also sighed inwardly. He could not imagine how much potential his son still had to be unearthed!
He and Luo Zheng glided downwards towards the world within their bodies. The aura that greeted them was completely different from that of the Great Expansion Universe.
Luo Zheng himself did not realize the uniqueness of this aura, but Luo Xiao was very clear … this was the aura of the Realm of the Gods!
In essence, the entire Realm of the Gods was actually formed by the Qi of Primal Chaos. However, the living beings in the Realm of the Gods were unable to control the Qi of Primal Chaos, and could only settle for the next best thing, thus developing a complete cultivation system!
"This place can really be called a small Realm of the Gods," Luo Xiao said slowly as he sized up this world. As Luo Xiao further sized up the world within Luo Zheng's body, he felt more and more that Gu Bei and Jiu's choice back then was correct. These two crazy old friends proposed a crazy plan, but it allowed his son to walk a path that no one had ever walked before …
Luo Zheng also had a certain degree of understanding of the Realm of the Gods, so he naturally understood Luo Xiao's meaning. At this moment, he suddenly remembered something and bowed slightly towards Luo Xiao. "Father, after I stepped into the Realm Lord tier, a strange thing happened in the world within my body."
"Oh? What strange thing? "Luo Xiao asked, puzzled.
"Father, follow me and you'll know." After saying this, Luo Zheng led the way in front, and Luo Xiao followed closely behind.

Chapter 1652
After Luo Zheng stepped into the World Lord realm, his inner world had a true boundary.
However, an ordinary World Lord's inner world would only be covered by a transparent curtain of light that divided it into specific boundaries. It was similar to the transparent barriers of the boundless universe. However, the barriers in Luo Zheng's inner world were a bit special!
Above the barrier, huge golden Sanskrit characters were floating!
With Luo Zheng's current knowledge, he was still unable to decipher the meaning of these golden Sanskrit characters. Thus, he was extremely curious about their origins.
This time, his father wanted to guide him in constructing the Divine Realm, so Luo Zheng naturally led his father here.
On the endless Chaos Sea, Luo Xiao and Luo Zheng floated above it …
Although Luo Xiao was only a soul, the strength of a Saint's soul far surpassed that of an ordinary Empyrean. It was enough to rival a True Divinity existence. Even from far away, he could already see these golden Sanskrit characters!
At this moment, as Luo Xiao saw this scene, his eyes were filled with disbelief!
"These … these are their characters!"
The 'they' that Luo Xiao spoke of referred to those 'life forms that leapfrog levels'.
A long time ago, some peak characters of the Divine Realm had already discovered the existence of these life forms.
The people of the Realm of the Gods had never stopped researching these 'leapfrogging life forms'. However, even after so many divine epochs had passed, no one knew the origins of these leapfrogging life forms. Even their appearance, gender, composition, and so on were unknown!
This race seemed to be completely above the knowledge of True Divinities and Saints!
So far, there were still many life forms in the Divine Realm that had a strong interest in this race. They seized every opportunity to research the origins of this race and obtain information about it!
Among them, Saint Jiu and Gu Bei were outstanding figures!
For example, Luo Xi Xuan's page of thunder Sanskrit characters and the golden Sanskrit characters on Luo Zheng's body. This was the first time they had deciphered and used these Sanskrit characters.
However, the effects were astonishing!
After Luo Zheng used these golden Sanskrit characters to transform his body, the strength of his body had already far surpassed that of a living creature. It was also because his body had become so tyrannical that he could withstand the chaos energy in his inner world and cultivate the Primal Chaos Secret Technique.
"Father, why would these golden Sanskrit characters appear at the edge of the inner world?" Luo Zheng asked.
Luo Xiao stared at those golden Sanskrit characters, floating motionlessly in the air. He didn't answer Luo Zheng's question. Instead, he flew along the edge of his inner world, his gaze constantly sweeping over those golden Sanskrit characters …
His father's flying speed was naturally incomparably fast, but as the creator of this inner world, Luo Zheng didn't need to circulate the Laws of Space at all. With just a thought, he could appear anywhere!
As Luo Xiao flew around the edge of the Inner World, Luo Zheng's figure kept flickering behind him.
At this stage, Luo Zheng's Inner World was not very wide. It was only about the size of four Great Chiliocosms.
In Luo Xiao's Great Evolution Universe, each Great World had over a thousand Great Worlds, and each universe had over a hundred thousand Great Worlds. In comparison, Luo Zheng's internal world was incomparably tiny.
In less than an incense stick of time, Luo Xiao had already circled around Luo Zheng's Inner World. Then, his footsteps suddenly came to a stop.
When Luo Zheng appeared in front of him, Luo Xiao stared at Luo Zheng with a strange expression, his eyes filled with incomprehension and disbelief.
"Father, what's wrong?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"There are a total of 1,080 golden Sanskrit characters. These golden Sanskrit characters should have been deliberately left behind," Luo Xiao concluded.
"Deliberately left behind? Why would they leave behind these Sanskrit characters? "Luo Zheng asked again.
Luo Xiao shook his head, "With my current Soul Qi, it is impossible for me to decipher the mysteries of these golden Sanskrit characters, but there must be some important information left behind. They want to tell you something."
Golden Sanskrit characters were a very unique type of characters. Each character contained a wealth of information. Just by looking at the shape of the characters, one could not obtain any information. One had to combine all of them together to make a judgment. Moreover, the entire process of deciphering these Sanskrit characters was very time-consuming and exhausting!
However, deciphering these Sanskrit characters would greatly benefit a martial artist.
For example, the Highest Artifact Refining Technique … had been deciphered by Jiu Sheng. As for Luo Xi Xuan's Thunder Sanskrit, it had been deciphered by Gu Bei …
The Realm of the Gods was an isolated island in the Primal Chaos. Apart from Saints who could roam the Primal Chaos, even the most powerful True Divinities were unable to enter.
And after experiencing countless Divine Epochs, the cultivation system's cultivation methods, cultivation methods, Laws, Divine Dao … had all been thoroughly studied. It was very difficult to make any major breakthroughs.
In the Realm of the Gods, countless martial artists had their eyes fixed on these golden Sanskrit characters. They wanted to comprehend a cultivation method that could allow them to surpass their level and obtain a strength that had never appeared in the Realm of the Gods!
This sort of thing had happened before. There had once been a martial artist who had become a Saint because he had deciphered an extremely powerful cultivation method from these golden Sanskrit characters.
Previously, when Luo Zheng had returned to Immortal Mansion and met the Book Immortal, the Book Immortal had told Luo Zheng that his son, Luo Nian, would definitely be a treasure in the Realm of the Gods. The Book Immortal had always praised Luo Nian's talent in deciphering Sanskrit characters …
At the time, Luo Zheng hadn't taken the Book Immortal's words to heart. Now that he heard his father say this, he realized that Luo Nian truly possessed an amazing ability.
Luo Xiao studied the golden Sanskrit characters for a long time. Although he had made this conjecture, he was still unable to decipher the meaning of these golden Sanskrit characters. He was even more unable to understand why these golden Sanskrit characters had appeared here.
"If you have the chance, you must decipher them when you go to the Realm of the Gods." Although Luo Xiao was unable to decipher these golden Sanskrit characters here, from his point of view, the Sanskrit characters that had appeared in Luo Zheng's Inner World definitely contained something extraordinary.
"Mm." Luo Zheng nodded. After all, this was his Inner World. No matter what the purpose was, Luo Zheng had to figure it out himself.
Luo Xiao wandered around this Inner World. Then, he shook his head at Luo Zheng. "Your Inner World is still too small …"
"Too small?" Luo Zheng asked, puzzled.
Luo Zheng didn't know just how large other people's Inner Worlds were …
What he didn't know was that when one cultivated to the World Lord realm and a boundary appeared in their Inner World, World Lords would try to expand their Inner World as much as possible. After all, World Lords had extremely high requirements for the amount of true essence they had. The wider their Inner World was, the more true essence it could hold. This was a very urgent requirement for World Lords.
However, this problem didn't exist for Luo Zheng.
For other martial artists, their Inner Worlds swallowed the heaven and earth origin energy of the universe, accumulating it bit by bit. If their true essence was overly consumed, they would need to constantly meditate and absorb the heaven and earth origin energy of the outside world into their own true essence sea.
Of course, there was an even simpler and faster method. That was to directly crush a top grade true essence stone and absorb the true essence within …
And Luo Zheng's Inner World had an extremely dense amount of chaos energy. To Luo Zheng, it was simply inexhaustible!
Moreover, Luo Zheng could use the roots of the World Tree to collect the chaos energy of the universe. Thus, expanding his Inner World wasn't an urgent requirement.
But in Luo Xiao's eyes, expanding his Inner World was an extremely important matter.
In the end, he was a Saint who had constructed a universe. In this aspect, he could be said to be at the forefront of the Divine Realm civilization. When Luo Xiao noticed the massive ice tree within Luo Zheng's Inner World, a surprised smile appeared on his face. "This is the profound meaning of the Icy Soul Cold Flame. You actually comprehended it from Saint Jiu!"
Under Luo Xiao's guidance, he passed on the essentials of constructing a Inner World to Luo Zheng.
For example, the Laws that one comprehended should be transformed into Law Tablets and passed down to the living beings within, guiding the living beings within one's Inner World to become powerhouses. One should take the initiative to create several continents and not allow one continent to expand without limit.
Constructing a universe was originally a very trivial matter. As Luo Xiao passed on the knowledge, Luo Zheng listened attentively.
While Luo Xiao was passing on the technique, Luo Zheng noticed that the aura coming from his father's body was becoming weaker and weaker.
His figure was condensed from Soul Aura, but his entire body seemed to be translucent. At this moment, in front of Luo Zheng's eyes, he was becoming more and more transparent, and his voice was as soft as a mosquito's …
Soul Aura was a unique method of a Saint's descent. Luo Xiao had left behind a total of fourteen Soul Auras. After the Great Evolution Universe's Cycle of Evolution began, his Soul Auras would move and look for opportunities. This Soul Aura was being consumed very quickly. Ten years ago, he had helped Luo Zheng resist the calamity of the Luck Destroying Phoenix Serpent, and this time, he had clashed with Mu Haiji's Soul Aura. Now, he was an arrow at the end of its flight.
"Father?" Luo Zheng called out.
Luo Xiao smiled faintly at Luo Zheng, but shook his head. "It's about time. This Soul Aura can only last for so long … This is the last Soul Aura I left behind in this world. If I can't wake up one day, I'm afraid I'll really fall."

Chapter 1653
Luo Xiao gazed at his son, his eyes filled with love.
In the end, Luo Zheng only saw Luo Xiao's lips move, but he was unable to tell what his father had said until Luo Xiao's soul completely dissipated.
The brown Primal Chaos Sea was slowly roiling. A few large Vicious Beasts would occasionally poke their heads out of the water to size up Luo Zheng, but after sensing Luo Zheng's aura, they would be frightened and dive back into the depths of the sea.
Luo Zheng stood motionless for a long time as he looked in the direction his father had disappeared.
It had been twenty years since he had last seen his father. To Luo Xiao, twenty years had passed in the blink of an eye, not even an instant. But to Luo Zheng, it had taken up two-thirds of his entire life.
In this period of time, he had completed his transformation from youth to youth, and his vision and strength had increased by countless times.
"We will meet again …" Luo Zheng smiled at the place where Luo Xiao had disappeared. These words seemed to be for Luo Xiao's ears, but they also seemed to be self-encouragement.
Following which, he flew towards the Central Continent of his inner world.
"Hualala …"
As Luo Zheng flew, the Primal Chaos Sea below him began to spin, turning into a waterspout that condensed in Luo Zheng's hands.
All the Primal Chaos Qi in his inner world was under Luo Zheng's control. He could freely visualize and change the shape of this Primal Chaos Qi.
The seawater constantly changed in Luo Zheng's hands, quickly forming massive tablets that floated in front of him.
Following which, Luo Zheng stretched out his finger and drew in the air at these tablets. Wherever his finger drew, Laws began to take shape, and at the same time, the tablets were coated in a layer of light gold.
Bzzz …
After the first stele was completely inscribed, countless bolts of lightning flowed through the stele. This stele recorded the power of Luo Zheng's Thunder Laws that he had comprehended!
The second tablet emitted golden light, and this golden light blossomed with Stellar Energy. This was the power of the Metal Laws.
The third tablet …
One after another, Law Tablets began to appear around Luo Zheng.
After Luo Zheng entered the Central Continent, Luo Zheng casually tossed a Law Tablet down.
"Boom!"
The Law Tablet embedded itself into the ground!
Luo Zheng did not stop. He continued to fly across the continent, constantly tossing Law Tablets down.
On this day, the living beings of the Central Continent saw the 'World Supporting Venerable' descend from the skies and bestow sixteen 'Divine Tablets' to enlighten the people.
All the martial artists were like thirsty travelers in the desert. They gathered around these tablets, desperately trying to absorb the Law energy within them....
Before this, a considerable portion of the martial artists in Luo Zheng's inner world had already grasped the power of Laws. But, these powers of Laws were comprehended according to the Laws of this world. There was a considerable degree of deviation and they weren't complete!
Now, with these Divine Tablets that recorded the Laws, a whole new world was presented to these martial artists. Some of the geniuses among them were able to create even stronger cultivation methods based on the Laws, and once they cultivated to a higher realm, they would be able to comprehend even deeper Laws.
This was a virtuous cycle that allowed the living beings of his inner world to rapidly advance in strength!
Luo Xiao's pointers were naturally effective. Following Luo Xiao's instructions, Luo Zheng still had many things to do in his inner world. But after placing down these Law Tablets, he dispersed his incarnation.
When Luo Zheng opened his eyes, he returned to the Heavenly Dao. He indifferently glanced at the Heaven's Will on the screen, no longer hesitating. He resolutely walked towards the door outside the Heavenly Dao!
Bzzz …
The moment Luo Zheng pushed open the door, an illusory door appeared in the air above the center of the Fiendnight Clan's Xixia World.
Luo Zheng had just walked out of the Heavenly Dao when he saw the environment around him. He was stunned.
Before Luo Zheng had followed his father into the Heavenly Dao, this place had still been in ruins. The massive Throne Bestowment Platform had disintegrated, and the only thing that was still intact was the Pond of Faith!
It had only been a few days, but the environment in front of him had undergone a tremendous change.
Huge trees completely covered the ruins, and the thick trunks of the trees intertwined with each other, forming a palace made of trees in this place.
All of this was due to Ying's efforts.
Although Ying's Pond of Faith had been cleanly devoured by the Ancestral Dragon, Ying had discovered some loopholes in the universe through her own exploration. These loopholes had existed because of Luo Xiao's fall. Ying had relied on these loopholes to perfectly control the Laws of Life.
In this sense, Ying had the ability of a Heavenly Venerate.
Luo Zheng swayed a few times above the palace of trees. The two giant trees in front of the palace suddenly swayed and split apart. Vines intertwined in the depths of the palace, forming a path that extended towards Luo Zheng, all the way to Luo Zheng's feet.
Luo Zheng smiled when he saw this. He stepped on the path formed by the vines and walked towards the depths of the palace of trees.
After passing through the dense thicket of trees, Luo Zheng saw a huge tree vault. The countless branches were horizontal and vertical, seemingly casually forming an exquisite structure.
Just as Luo Zheng was sizing up the place, a tall figure quietly approached him from behind. That figure was extremely nimble, shuttling through the trees without making any sound. She also concealed her aura to the extreme, completely blending into the surroundings.
However, just as that figure approached Luo Zheng, he turned around and pulled that figure into his arms, pressing his lips against hers.
Within the range of the world's will, no concealment ability could fool Luo Zheng. Naturally, concealing one's aura was useless. Luo Zheng had already discovered Xun, who was quietly approaching him.
Xun's personality was not as cheerful as Xi Youqin's, nor was she as gentle as Ning Yudie's. Behind her decisive personality was a hint of shyness. When Luo Zheng pulled her into his arms, her body seemed to have been struck by lightning, and she immediately tensed up!
However, the people of the Night Demon Clan were more civilized than the people of the Human Clan. Although Xun was nervous, she didn't run away like a human girl. She made up her mind, and with a twist of her waist, the two of them fell down the intertwined branches.
The instant the two of them fell, countless soft branches rapidly extended from below, weaving into a net in the air at an extremely fast speed. It firmly caught the two of them, and then completely wrapped them up …
On the other side of this enormous tree palace, Ying was sitting on the top of a tree. Her white legs swayed gently under her emerald green dress. There was still a trace of the Law of Life in her hand. She curled her lips helplessly. "It's broad daylight, and you're already like this …"
This palace had been set up by Ying using the Law of Life, so she could naturally sense all the minute movements in this palace. As for the moving vines and branches, they were all personally controlled by Ying.
"Roar …"
At that moment, a huge creature suddenly turned its head and rapidly extended towards her. However, when the nose of the creature was a foot away from Ying, it stopped steadily!
"Roar roar roar …"
This creature was the Ancestral Dragon. At that moment, it was roaring at Ying.
"Alright, alright, I got it …" Yao waved her hand at the Ancestral Dragon.
Ying waved her hand at the Ancestral Dragon. She placed her slender fingers on the tree trunk, and countless strange flowers and strange fruits instantly condensed from the side of this enormous tree, emitting a sweet and rich smell.
After comprehending the perfect Law of Life, Ying had the ability to "create". This ability could not only create things that she had seen before, but also things that existed in her imagination.
These strange flowers and strange fruits were the perfect food in her imagination, and now, she was using them to feed the greedy Ancestral Dragon.
"Aren't all true dragons exceedingly intelligent? It's been so long, yet it still hasn't even learned the human language. How stupid and greedy … "Ying said softly.
The Ancestral Dragon opened its mouth wide and swallowed the fragrant fruits. When it heard Ying's words, it seemed to be greatly dissatisfied. It let out a "huchi huchi" sound, protesting against Ying's evaluation …

Chapter 1654
Due to the battle between Luo Xiao and Mu Haiji, the entire Xixia Realm was on the verge of collapse.
Both the higher and lower realms possessed the ability to repair themselves. However, the repair process was extremely long. Due to the severe damage done to the Xixia Realm, it was very likely that it would disintegrate on its own.
The tenth rank Sacred Lands in the Xixia Realm could only evacuate their clansmen first before finding a way to stabilize the Xixia Realm.
The responsibility of repairing the Xixia Realm naturally fell on Ying's shoulders.
During this period of time, she had spread countless Sacred Trees throughout the Xixia Realm. These enormous plants could extend their roots endlessly. Once their roots entered the ground, they would entangle with each other and pull at each other. They would be able to close the cracks and stabilize the Xixia Realm.
However, this was not something that could be done in a day. Only time could heal the wounds of the Xixia Realm.
Peace returned to the Demonnight Clan for the time being. Two of the countless king statues in the Sacred Grounds of the Demonnight Clan had been permanently extinguished … The members of the Demonnight Clan knew very well that there would only be one king in their clan now and in the future!
Apart from that, Xun had also pardoned the crimes of the Yao Night Clan members such as the Sacred Chains.
The six great factions of the Yao Night Clan instinctively supported their King. The root of the problem was still Yao.
The pardoning allowed the Yao Night Clan members who had followed Yao to heave a sigh of relief. Although they knew that with Yao's death, it would be very difficult for them to gain Xun's trust again, and it was almost impossible for them to be put in an important position in the Yao Night Clan, they were still extremely grateful to Xun for being able to keep their lives and even regain their freedom.
In truth, Xun was not a benevolent person. However, she knew that the Yao Night Clan was already in a precarious situation after experiencing such severe internal strife. They could not endure any more suffering.
The hammock made of branches swayed gently like a swing. Xun curled up her long legs and tried her best to curl up into a ball. She curled up in Luo Zheng's arms.
She had spent most of her time curled up in Luo Zheng's body over the past few years. She basically treated Luo Zheng's body as her own home. Now that she had recovered her true body through Ying's Spirit Embryo, she could not 'live' in Luo Zheng's body.
Luo Zheng gently caressed Shaun's silk-like skin as he asked, "Have the members of the Sacred Spear Sect been rescued?"
He hadn't forgotten the promise he had made on the Illusory Battlefield. Not only did he have to save Liu Yu, but he also had to save Huan Ling from the Demon Night Clan.
"Yes!" Smog nodded.
The Sacred Spear Sect had always been Smoky's most important supporter. Over the past few days, after Smoky had gathered the members of the Council of Elders, the first thing she did was to clear the name of the Sacred Spear Sect. Now, in the entire Fiend Night Clan, three Heavenly Venerables were supporting Smoky, and she was the only king of the Fiend Night Clan. How could the other factions dare to say "no"? They could only follow Xun's arrangements obediently!

Hearing Luo Zheng mention the Dream God Arrow, the smoky face on Luo Zheng's chest suddenly flashed with a trace of sadness.
Although she had returned to her throne, the Dream God Arrow had fallen into the Five Decay of the Celestial Being. This was probably the worst news she had heard during this period of time …
In truth, burning one's life force normally wouldn't cause a martial artist to fall into the Five Decay of the Celestial Being. Ning Yudie's life force had nearly been exhausted, but she hadn't aged at all. But, the Dream God Arrow's Blood Extinguishing Spirit Devouring Bone Arrow had overdrawn his life force and also caused him to rapidly age!
The Five Decay of the Celestial Being was like a giant pit that one couldn't climb out of. If the Dream God Arrow had remained at the edge of the pit and hadn't fallen in, then Smog would have done everything she could to extend his life! But, once he fell into the pit, it would be impossible to pull him out.
This was the rule of the Great Evolution Universe. No one was an exception.
"He is in seclusion within the Spirit Spirit Holy Lands' Holy Tree …" This so-called seclusion was only an excuse. Smog naturally knew that the Dream God Arrow had been depressed during this period of time.
Everyone wished to live longer. Eternal life was an eternal topic in this universe.
For an Empyrean, falling into the Five Decay of the Celestial Being was an extremely painful process. Their bodies would rapidly age and a strange stench and filth would constantly gush out from their bodies. This filth simply couldn't be removed!
Thus, when some powerhouses fell into this state, they would find an excuse to go into seclusion. In truth, they didn't want their image to be known to the public. They could only choose to silently perish in a corner.
"The Five Decay of the Celestial Being … is actually a law of cause and effect, right?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
Smog thought for a moment and then silently nodded. This wasn't wrong. It was just that no one was willing to accept this law of cause and effect, but no one could escape it!
"Perhaps I can solve it." Luo Zheng faintly smiled.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Smog blinked, as if she didn't know how to react. Once she understood, her first reaction was to doubt Luo Zheng. In this world, just who could escape from the passage of time?
But then she thought about it again. Luo Zheng's status was special, not to mention that he never did anything he wasn't confident in!
At this moment, the thoughts in her mind circled around several times before she came back to her senses. "Luo Zheng, do you really have a way to solve it?"
If Luo Zheng could really solve the Five Decay of the Celestial Being, then Ying had a way to help the Dream God Arrow recover its life force.
"I can try." Luo Zheng wasn't one hundred percent sure. Although the 《Ten Thousand Laws Rival》 could sever cause and effect, the Five Decay of the Celestial Being wasn't performed in the form of the Mandate of Heaven. Thus, he didn't speak too much.
Hearing this, Smog's face revealed a smile as bright as a pearl. With a light flip, the gorgeous red satin dress instantly wrapped around her slender body. This was the first time Luo Zheng had seen such an outfit.
She originally had a body that was close to perfection, and with the embellishment, she looked elegant and refined. That spirit embryo had only started to grow rapidly after Smoky had taken over, so if one were to measure the age of her bones … Smoky was not even a month old!
"Then let's go now!"
…
…
The Fang Ling Sacred Land was the closest tenth rank Sacred Land to the Platform of Bestowment. This Sacred Land didn't have a Sacred Master. The entire Sacred Land was directly controlled by the Council of Elders.

After entering the Fang Ling Sacred Land's Sacred Tree and passing through a narrow passage, they found the Dream God Arrow, who was in seclusion.

After inheriting the Mandate of Heaven, they couldn't become Dao Disciples, much less obtain a Divine Spark.
Rather than saying that he was in seclusion, it was more accurate to say that he was hiding here.
When Luo Zheng and Smog entered, they smelled a foul stench. This sort of disgusting smell was impossible to get rid of.
The Dream God Arrow had been handsome and refined in his youth. How could he tolerate others seeing him like this?

"I'm trying to help you get rid of the Five Signs of Heaven's Decay!" Smog smiled.
"What!?" The Dream God Arrow's dim yellow eyes rolled up. "My Queen, are you trying to make fun of me …"
It wasn't that the Dream God Arrow was being too presumptuous, but rather that he had never heard of such a thing. Could the Five Signs of Heaven's Decay be removed? If there really was a way to remove it, then it wouldn't be so terrifying …
Moreover, removing the Five Signs of Heaven's Decay meant eternal life. Before becoming a True God, this was clearly impossible.
"That's why I'm just giving it a try. Besides, I have a certain amount of confidence," Luo Zheng said.
The Dream God Arrow's heart skipped a beat when he heard Luo Zheng's words. He had personally witnessed the demeanor of Luo Zheng's father. Luo Zheng was the master of this universe. All the laws in this universe were set by this brat's father. If he thought about it this way, then there really was a sliver of possibility!

Chapter 1655

Sever all karma …
What a bold claim!

The Five Decay of Celestial Being was a special state. Everyone only knew that warriors above the Divine Sea Realm would suddenly descend when they reached a certain age. As for why this state occurred and how to break this state, no one knew.
Even Luo Zheng himself wasn't sure …
The moment Luo Zheng's sword slashed at the Dream God Arrow, a mournful wail came from his body!
This strange mournful wail startled both Dream God Arrow and Smokescreen!
"Ah ah ah ah ah …"
As an Empyrean, the Dream God Arrow understood his own body very well. If something was hiding in his body, there was no way he wouldn't know. At this moment, he was looking left and right. All he could hear was screams coming from his body, but he didn't know where these screams were coming from!
"Drip drip drip …"
In addition to the screams, a filthy liquid flowed out from under the Dream God Arrow's armpits. The place where he was in seclusion was already very smelly, but now the stench was even stronger …

Not long after, filth began to flow out from his ears, eyes, mouth, and nose. This filth also contained the body parts of some special life form. An old man appeared in front of Luo Zheng and Smokescreen. This old man wore a white robe, and there wasn't a single speck of dust on his body. Although his face was filled with wrinkles and he looked abnormally old, his eyes shone brightly, and his spirit was hale and hearty!

For a dignified Empyrean to bow so deeply to Luo Zheng, this kindness was unimaginably great. After all, Luo Zheng's actions were the same as granting the Dream God Arrow a second life. This was the grace of rebirth!

Martial artists at the Divine Sea realm and above all had their lifespan end after the Five Decay of Celestial Being appeared. Everyone's time of descent was different, but they would all face it sooner or later. This was a fair rule.
But now that the Dream God Arrow's Five Decay of Celestial Being had been removed by Luo Zheng, did this mean that the Five Decay of Celestial Being would no longer descend? From this point of view, did the Dream God Arrow possess an eternal life?
The question that the Dream God Arrow raised also struck Luo Zheng. He thought for a moment and then faintly smiled. "That does seem to make sense. If the Five Decay of Celestial Being no longer descends, then Empyrean Divine Arrow, you might possess the ability of a True Divinity … eternal life!"
The pursuit of eternal life was an eternal topic. But, this was a boundary that only those who stepped into the Realm of the Gods and became a True Divinity had the qualifications to touch upon. In the Great Yan Universe, there had never been a life form that possessed eternal life!
Just like the Refined God Child, using the body of a puppet to pursue eternal life was indeed an alternative method. But, a puppet could no longer be defined as a life form …
Smokescreen faintly smiled. "Right now, the entire universe is facing a crisis. First, we must withstand the repulse of the saints. Otherwise, let alone eternal life, whether or not we can survive another thousand years is a problem."
The war with the saints was the greatest problem placed before the various races of the universe.

The morale of the universe was getting lower and lower, and the situation was becoming more and more unfavorable for the Demi-Immortal Tribe.
After resolving the internal disputes of the Demon Night Clan, Luo Zheng also understood that the time to contend with the saints had arrived.

Chapter 1656
Sunflower Water Realm was located at the intersection of the Human Dao Alliance and Gods No Thought. Its geographical location was very special.

Whether it was saint race martial artists or human martial artists, after a challenge, no one else could help …
If the winner was to shout out loud, they would even use the roar command to announce it to the universe!
The loser would directly lose their lives!
As for the martial artists of both races, they only fought for glory. This was because even if they defeated their opponent, they wouldn't receive any rewards …
It was just that there were far too many crazy people in this world. Some martial artists were born to fight, and they didn't care about anything else. They only wanted to enjoy the joy of trampling other races beneath their feet!
Sunflower Water Realm would have countless gambling fights every day.

Chapter 1657

Huu …

The war puppet's body was as thick as a small mountain. In addition to the demon race's unique explosive strength, the impact of this collision wasn't small.

"She must have a way to deal with it, otherwise she wouldn't be so stupid …"
"Humph, maybe it's just a diversion. If this war puppet doesn't fall for it, it can directly smash that sacred race woman to pieces!"
During this period of time, many towering pavilions had appeared on the side of Iron Moon City that faced the hill. Many cultivators had gathered in these pavilions. There were both humans and saints. They exchanged information and even challenged each other to a duel …
However, these pavilions and towers were man-made. Spending money in them required true essence stones, and the price wasn't cheap either.
However, the martial artists that dared to wander the Sunflower Water Realm had long since regarded wealth as mere worldly possessions. After all, challenging the sacred race meant that one could perish at any time. No matter how many true essence stones and Zhen Yuanyu they had, what use was that?
Just as these people's voices fell …
The War Puppet's two axes suddenly charged towards Shi Kongwu.
But the moment they collided, a strange scene occurred. Shi Kongwu's entire body was as light as a sheet of paper, without the slightest resistance. She was like a willow leaf dancing in the wind, brushing against the War Puppet's axes as she floated past!
The Puppet Fighter was also shocked. He felt as if he had crashed into a piece of cotton under his charge. The other party was not affected at all. No matter how strong he was, he could not cause any harm to Shi Kongwu!
As a Sector Lord, the Puppet Fighter's reaction was extremely fast!
Since the collision had no effect, the two axes in his hands chopped down at Shi Kongwu at the fastest speed possible!
Shi Kongwu was as light as a sheet of paper, but the War Puppet's two dark golden axes were like scissors. They crisscrossed left and right, wanting to "cut" Shi Kongwu in half.
However, when the War Puppet's two axes were about to chop down, Shi Kongwu did not show the slightest hint of panic on her face. Her soft body slightly twisted, and her whole body actually slipped through the gap between the two giant axes. She actually directly stuck herself in the War Puppet's bosom. She stretched out her hand and gently stroked the War Puppet's strong chest muscles, leaving a wisp of fragrance on the War Puppet's chest. She giggled, and there was a hint of contempt in her eyes. At this moment, if she had a dagger in her hand, the War Puppet's stomach would have already been cut open by her!
Although her smile was beautiful and her laughter was pleasant to the ears, the War Puppet had no intention of appreciating it. He knew that this woman in front of him was definitely a fierce character. Now that she was playing with him, it was clear that she didn't place him in her eyes!
In a fit of anger, he gave her a bear hug. If he could grab this Sacred Clan woman, he would definitely be able to crush her with brute force.
But at this moment, Shi Kongwu was like a slippery fish. She slipped out of the War Puppet's arms. Her clothes fluttered, and she pulled away from the War Puppet. At the same time, she raised her hands and gently clapped.
The War Puppet was startled. Only then did he realize that he was unknowingly surrounded by those black beads. These beads were arranged in an orderly manner in a formation. The moment Shi Kongwu clapped her hands, they were activated!
"Bang, bang, bang, bang, bang …"
The black beads exploded in an instant, bursting out beams of black light that completely enveloped the War Puppet!
Almost all of the weapons that Shi Kongwu possessed were single-use magic treasures. The black beads that were used to deal with the War Puppet were called "Black Flame Beads". The black flames that were produced after these black beads exploded had an extremely strong annihilation power. After the black flames exploded, they released a powerful annihilation power in the air.
After a while, the black flames gradually dissipated. Two black objects fell from the sky. They were the two axes of the War Puppet. These two axes were no longer in shape, and the dark golden light had also faded away. As for the War Puppet itself, it had been completely annihilated by the black flames.
"Boring … is there anyone else willing to challenge me?" Shi Kongwu asked.
Her gaze circled around the hill. Outside the hill, there were still many martial artists with powerful auras, but they all maintained their silence. It seemed that no one dared to challenge her today. She turned around and followed Maha …
In truth, those powerful martial artists were not afraid of this enchanting Sacred Clan woman.
It was just that this woman had countless single-use magic treasures on her, and they were all extremely powerful. Wanting to defeat her was indeed very difficult. Moreover, these martial artists thought highly of themselves. Their goal in coming to Sunflower Water Realm was to challenge Maha. They didn't have much interest in anyone else!
"Ai, let's not even talk about taking down Maha. In my Great Evolution Universe, I can't even take down a Sacred Clan woman!"
"It seems that the number of martial artists that have been defeated by this Sacred Clan woman is at least ten, right?"
"She hasn't tasted a single defeat!"
The martial artists of Iron Moon City sighed in low voices. There were even some that were drunk, casually making a sad song …
But just as Shi Kongwu turned around, a person climbed up the hill.
This person was dressed in black, and his entire body was wrapped in a thick robe. Only a pair of star-like eyes could be seen through a gap. When this martial artist reached the top of the hill, he faintly asked, "If I defeat you, can I directly challenge Maha?"
Maha's current fame was too great. In addition, he only accepted three challenges a day. Now, those who wanted to challenge Maha had to queue up.
Hearing the black-clothed man's question, Shi Kongwu turned her head again, an interested expression on her face. At the same time, she shook her head. "Actually, it's not like that!"
"What do you mean?" The black-clothed man coldly asked.
Shi Kongwu charmingly smiled. "If you defeat me, Big Brother Maha will naturally help me. Of course, he will challenge you!"
"That's good!"
The black-clothed man's goal was to target Maha. He had no interest in the word games Shi Kongwu played. With a shake of his arm, the robe covering his body shattered into pieces and drifted away with the wind. At the same time, his true appearance was revealed. It was a young man.
The young man's eyes under his sword-like eyebrows shone like stars, revealing endless pride. At the same time, he stretched out his hand and a fiery red spear appeared in his hand!
"That's … Xuanyuan Chenfeng!"
"Hahaha, I knew it. Our human race's Dao Child will finally make a move!"
"This Xuanyuan Chenfeng is an existence second only to Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming. Before Luo Zheng appeared, he was the number one Dao Child!"
"This kind of monstrous figure is terrifying when he improves. After he stepped into the realm of Sector Lord, his strength increased greatly. Now, he will only be more powerful!"
Because the Dao Children were unwilling to show up, countless warriors in the universe were disappointed.
As the saying goes, the brave win in the battle of martial arts. Many warriors understood that failure wasn't scary. What was scary was being afraid before the battle. It was also because of this that the morale of the universe was greatly affected …
These warriors didn't expect that instead of Luo Zheng, Xuanyuan Chenfeng would appear!
For a time, in the entire Iron Moon City's pavilions, high and low, there was a burst of cheers, as if victory was right in front of them. Even some extremely drunk warriors woke up with a start.
"Xuanyuan Chenfeng?"
The corners of Shi Kongwu's mouth curled up slightly. Her gentle and charming eyes immediately revealed a strong interest.
She understood the background of the ten Dao Children very well. When Luo Zheng returned from the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, she immediately recognized Luo Zheng's origin.

Chapter 1658
"That is me."
The spear in Xuanyuan Chenfeng's hand trembled as he looked straight at Shi Kongwu.
At this stage, the 'potential' of the Great Expansion Universe had already fallen to a freezing point under Maha's suppression.
But whether it was the old Patriarch or the many Empyreans, none of them had a good method to deal with this. After all, the saints held an absolute advantage at this stage. Facing Maha's scheme, there was no way to break it!
As long as an Empyrean took action, the final battle with the saints would immediately begin. However, facing the unfathomable saints, the demi-immortals had no assurance of victory!
After discussing for a long time, they could only send out Xuanyuan Chenfeng to take a risk.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng cultivated the Divine Dao of Truth, and his comprehension of it had reached a deep level. If he could succeed in one strike and eliminate Maha, it would be enough to buy some precious time for the universe.
Maha, who had already left the hill, suddenly stopped. He turned his head, a cold and gloomy smile on his face. "Heh, a group of sly old fellows are beginning to test me …"
Xuanyuan Chenfeng sensed Maha's gaze. The tip of the spear, which was originally tilted downwards, swayed slightly. With a "hu la" sound, it gently drew a line in the air. It flashed out a dazzling spear flower, but it was pointed directly at Maha.
This was a very casual provocation, but when this long spear was pointed in the air, Maha Dun felt a faint pain on his right chest!
The right chest was the weak point of Maha's body. Back then, when his father was refining this body for him, there was a bit of carelessness, which caused his body to have this weak point. He didn't expect that Xuanyuan Chenfeng would see through it with a single glance!
"Interesting." Maha narrowed his eyes and said in a low voice, a faint trace of vigilance in his heart. It seemed that besides Luo Zheng, the other Dao Children weren't bad. He had underestimated the Dao Children of the Great Expansion Universe.
However, Maha didn't choose to interfere. Since Xuanyuan Chenfeng wanted to challenge Shi Kongwu, he could also observe.
The voices in Iron Moon City suddenly became louder. During this time, the saint martial artists held the absolute advantage. Whether it was the human race or the demon race, when they challenged the saint martial artists, they lost more often than they won.
As for those who challenged Maha, they were defeated again and again. This was no different from sending themselves to their deaths.
Under these circumstances, it was normal for them to place their hopes on Xuanyuan Chenfeng!
"A Dao Child has finally arrived. It truly is unexpected." Shi Kongwu revealed a moving smile. Her waist was perfectly straight as she slowly floated in the air.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng's expression did not change in the slightest. "Enough nonsense. Let's do it. I'm in a hurry."
Shi Kongwu didn't care about Xuanyuan Chenfeng's attitude. Wei Shi smiled faintly, "Hasn't it already started?"
"Sou!"
The moment she finished speaking, a black pearl shot towards Xuanyuan Chenfeng's face!
Xuanyuan Chenfeng frowned slightly. His reaction was extremely fast. Facing Shi Kongwu's sneak attack, he leaned back and fell down.
The moment he fell, the "Black Flame Bead" instantly exploded. Its power was naturally not ordinary, but Xuanyuan Chenfeng had already escaped without a trace.
After Xuanyuan Chenfeng fell, he suddenly stopped. The long spear in his hand turned slightly, and once again rushed towards Shi Kongwu!
"That's all you've got …"
Seeing that Xuanyuan Chenfeng only continuously dodged and didn't dare to fight head-on, Shi Kongwu had a rough estimate of Xuanyuan Chenfeng's strength. She thought that the Dao Children of the universe were only so-so!
"Sou sou sou sou …"
With a wave of Shi Kongwu's hand, the Black Flame Beads that looked like soybeans were thrown out by her one after another …
Although this' Black Flame Bead 'was not as precious as the' Life Sacrificing Bead ', it was still not an ordinary magic treasure. Let alone ordinary martial artists, even ordinary saint race Great World Overlords wouldn't be able to squander it like this. In the eyes of the saint race martial artists, this Shi Kongwu was using her wealth to smash others!
This time, Xuanyuan Chenfeng did not choose to dodge. Moreover, with so many black beads falling down, it was impossible for Xuanyuan Chenfeng to pass through them. Other than using greater teleportation to avoid them, he could only forcefully break through them!
Xuanyuan Chenfeng came to save the "influence" of the universe. Of course, he was not willing to use greater teleportation to escape.
Looking at the rapidly descending "Black Flame Pearls", a complicated pattern flickered in his eyes, and the entire world changed in his eyes.
Now that Xuanyuan Chenfeng's cultivation had greatly increased, his aura had also increased by a large margin!
"Insignificant skill!"
He didn't dodge at all. Instead, he raised his spear and pointed it towards the sky. Within a single breath, he had pointed out countless times. Moreover, every spear was grasped with incomparable precision, all of them pointing at the center of the black beads.
However, martial artists who used spears, after stepping into the Divine Sea Realm, could mostly do this, but the results would be vastly different.
These Black Flame Beads were a special method used by the Sacred Clan experts. The black flames were sealed within them. Even if they were shattered, the black flames would still erupt.
However, under Xuanyuan Chenfeng's storm-like spear thrusts, all of the Black Flame Beads cracked, but the black flames within did not erupt. This was because Xuanyuan Chenfeng could see through the flaws of these black beads. Following the flaws, he directly destroyed the entire Black Flame Bead!
"Good, as the first Dao Child, this Xuanyuan Chenfeng's strength is indeed extraordinary!"
"Let's see how that Sacred Clan woman deals with it!"
"Xuanyuan Chenfeng, kill that Sacred Clan woman!"
In the eyes of many, at this moment, Xuanyuan Chenfeng seemed to have already won. It was also because of Xuanyuan Chenfeng's move that the atmosphere in Iron Moon City became more lively.
"The Divine Dao of Truth is indeed powerful …" Shi Kongwu praised. With a light tap of her foot, she quickly retreated. She wanted to avoid Xuanyuan Chenfeng's attack and then slowly attack …
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng did not intend to give Shi Kongwu this opportunity. After he shattered those black beads, he raised his foot and stepped into the air!
"Bang!"
An explosive sound came from under Xuanyuan Chenfeng's foot. His speed suddenly increased by several times, and his momentum was even more astonishing. Almost no one saw how he moved, and he strangely appeared in front of Shi Kongwu.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng, who had a grave expression, did not have the slightest bit of pity. In his eyes, this Sacred Clan beauty before him had already turned into a thin line and a dot. In his eyes, she was nothing more than a mass of rules!
"Die!"
The tip of his spear suddenly flicked, and suddenly pierced towards one of Shi Kongwu's arms!
This scene caused the surrounding warriors to be somewhat confused. With such a good opportunity, why didn't he use his spear to destroy the Sacred Clan woman's head? Was he trying to cripple one of her hands? At this time, would Xuanyuan Chenfeng still have tender feelings for a foreign race woman? This was simply incomprehensible …
However, at this moment, Shi Kongwu's beautiful face lost all color!
If it were an ordinary method, even if Shi Kongwu's arm was destroyed, it would not harm her life.
However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng comprehended the Divine Dao of Truth. The weak point he comprehended could not be wrong. Even Xuanyuan Chenfeng himself felt that it was strange. Why would this woman's Mingmen be on her right arm? However, he did not have any doubts about what he saw, so this spear pierced out without any hesitation!
"Pop!"
This spear pierced through Shi Kongwu's right arm, and a pitch-black bead wrapped in a mass of blood slid out!
This pitch-black bead was Shi Kongwu's Life Sacrificing Bead!
Previously, she had seven Life Sacrificing Beads. These Life Sacrificing Beads could replace her to receive a fatal injury. When Luo Zheng returned to the universe, he broke four of her Life Sacrificing Beads with a single strike. This Shi Kongwu learned to be smart, and hid the remaining three Life Sacrificing Beads in different parts of her body.
However, when Xuanyuan Chenfeng used the Divine Dao of Truth to see through Shi Kongwu's Mingmen, he discovered that she actually had several Mingmen. This spear could be considered to have taken one of Shi Kongwu's lives!
"So that's how it is." Although Xuanyuan Chenfeng did not know the origin of this Life Sacrificing Bead, he understood that if he wanted to kill this woman, he would have to break all of her other Mingmen!
"Whoosh!"
Almost without any pause, he stabbed out with his spear, and this spear hit Shi Kongwu's calf.
"Pu!"
As a mass of blood spurted out, another Life Sacrificing Bead was broken by Xuanyuan Chenfeng!
These Life Sacrificing Beads were Shi Kongwu's backing, enough to protect her from death. At this moment, two of them were broken by Xuanyuan Chenfeng in a row. She was extremely terrified in her heart, and the thought of risking her life suddenly appeared in her heart.
Clenching her teeth, she flipped in the air, and suddenly spread out her hands. Two sword patterns appeared along her arms.
This Shi Kongwu's talent was second only to Maha in the Ultimate Universe. However, she did not focus on cultivation. When she encountered an enemy, she relied on magic treasures. However, her position in the Sacred Clan was extremely high, and she had countless powerful magic treasures in her hands. Those two sword patterns were her strongest killing move!
Xuanyuan Chenfeng felt the powerful aura from the sword patterns, but he still did not have any plans to retreat!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Countless single-colored small swords scattered around Shi Kongwu's body, forming a storm that surrounded Xuanyuan Chenfeng.
At this moment, the light in Xuanyuan Chenfeng's eyes instantly became intense, like two twinkling stars. Under his full strength, the Spear of Law was also activated to the extreme …
"Break!"
Xuanyuan Chenfeng did not care, and wanted to kill Shi Kongwu first!
However, at this moment, a voice filled with demonic nature suddenly rang out …
"Enough!"
Under this loud shout, whether it was Shi Kongwu or Xuanyuan Chenfeng, they immediately felt their minds surging!
As for the low-level warriors in the Iron Moon City that were very close to this place, their legs trembled as if it was a conditioned reflex, and they all knelt on the ground together …

Chapter 1659
Maha slowly descended from the other side, a lazy expression on his face.
But under the pressure of the Great Ruling Art, he was like a saint that had descended. All living beings in the world had to kneel before him!
The last time Maha used the Great Ruling Art, it had no effect on Luo Zheng. This was because Luo Zheng's soul was no weaker than Maha's. Moreover, Luo Zheng's soul had a "Sovereign Soul Seal", so his aura was no weaker than Maha's Great Ruling Art!
But Xuanyuan Chenfeng's soul was a bit weaker than Luo Zheng's, so it was difficult for him to resist Maha's Great Ruling Art. Thus, when Maha said "enough", his willpower instantly collapsed, and his spear was unable to pierce through!

Taking advantage of Xuanyuan Chenfeng's absent-mindedness, Shi Kongwu quickly retreated and hid behind Maha.
Her complexion was deathly pale, and her heartbeat was extremely fast. If Maha had made a move a step later, she would probably have lost her life here today. When facing the true experts of the Dayan Universe, the magic treasures that she carried with her to protect her life seemed to be completely useless …
"Ai, what a pity, Maha actually stopped him!"
"I almost killed that Sacred Clan bitch!"
"It doesn't matter, that Sacred Clan woman wasn't important to begin with …"
At this moment, the weaker martial artists in Iron Moon City were trembling as they crawled up from the ground. After all, Maha's shout was aimed at Xuanyuan Chenfeng, and the others were only slightly affected …
Xuanyuan Chenfeng didn't feel regretful that he wasn't able to kill Shi Kongwu. After all, his goal this time was Maha, and under the careful planning of his elders, he had already prepared an escape route. Xuanyuan Chenfeng didn't feel any burden in his heart.
"Great Ruling Art …"
Xuanyuan Chenfeng faintly smiled. With a wave of his hand, a thin string appeared on his chest. On the string hung a light green round stone. This small stone emitted a faint halo that enveloped Xuanyuan Chenfeng's head.
"Tianhun Relic?" Maha glanced at the small stone on Xuanyuan Chenfeng's chest and frowned.
"The Great Ruling Technique should be one of the most useless Divine Daos. Only brainless people would choose it," Xuanyuan Chenfeng said proudly.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Luo Zheng came from different backgrounds. Xuanyuan Chenfeng was born in the Xuanyuan family, and his temperament was more arrogant than Luo Zheng's. Because of his family, the treasures in his hands were as endless as Shi Kongwu's.
This Great Domination Art was able to make a martial artist with a lower cultivation submit to it with just the pressure of the soul. This effect was truly terrifying.
If he could raise his soul to a certain level, he would be able to make tens of thousands of living beings submit to him with just a glance …
In the Realm of the Gods, the Great Ruling Technique was one of the Divine Daos that Saints had to cultivate. It was also a method to control the gods. This was also the reason why the Great Ruling Technique was ranked in the top 30 of the 3000 Divine Daos!
If the opponent's soul strength was similar to his own, or even stronger, and the Great Ruling Technique had no effect, then it would be useless.
The reason Maha chose the Great Rule Art was because the saints wanted to raise him as the leader of the great universe!
But Xuanyuan Chenfeng had come prepared. He had used the Tianhun Relic to protect his soul. Maha's Great Ruling Technique was completely useless.
"Is that so?"
Maha coldly smiled. His right hand pinched the air, and a rhombus-shaped light constantly condensed in his hand. A light purple saber appeared in his hand. On the back of this saber was an evil dragon baring its fangs and brandishing its claws. And on the edge of the saber, a dark red light circulated. He said, "Thinking about it carefully, the Great Ruling Technique is indeed useless. If a Saint cultivates this Dao and can control the gods of the Realm of the Gods, then it won't be of much use to me. But …" Maha's expression suddenly changed. "I only cultivated the Great Ruling Technique for fun. Who said that a martial artist could only cultivate a certain Divine Dao?"
As Maha's words left his mouth, the martial artists in Iron Moon City began to discuss amongst themselves.
"Cultivating the Divine Dao for fun, this fellow …"
"Who doesn't know how to boast?"
"Indeed, he should be just boasting. But listening to this fellow, he should have cultivated many different Divine Daos!"
To be able to comprehend a single Divine Dao was enough for one to become a Dao Child. But, this world was unfair. Some monstrous geniuses could not only comprehend a single Divine Dao, but could also cultivate several Divine Daos at the same time.
Actually, this kind of phenomenon was not rare in the Realm of the Gods.
For example, Luo Zheng encountered some martial artists in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. While they cultivated the main Divine Dao, they would also cultivate the "Great Wishing Technique". And the Great Wishing Technique was a Divine Dao that all the gods of the Realm of the Gods had to cultivate. So, almost all the True Gods of the Realm of the Gods would choose two or more Divine Daos to cultivate.
Hearing Maha's words, Xuanyuan Chenfeng responded with a sneer.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Maha's characters were very similar. They both came from the top forces of the universe. They both had high hopes placed on them by their families, and they both had the same pride. It was just that Xuanyuan Chenfeng in the Great Expanding Universe had a slight deviation. In the end, he became Luo Zheng's "substitute".
And Maha's treatment in the Saint Clan was much better than Xuanyuan Chenfeng's in all aspects …
The two of them had similar environments, which created similar characters. So, Xuanyuan Chenfeng also understood that Maha's words were just to suppress him.
After the experience in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, in addition to being suppressed and "educated" by Luo Zheng again and again, his state of mind was no longer the same as before. At this moment, he was naturally not alarmed. He only indifferently said, "Then make your move."

He himself emitted a serious and solemn aura, and the Mo Saber was sharp and dazzling …
"Whoosh …"
Without wasting any more words, Maha instantly rushed towards Xuanyuan Chenfeng!
Xuanyuan Chenfeng did not hesitate at all. Facing Maha, he had already made sufficient mental preparations. What's more, it was not that he did not have a way out. At this moment, he understood that he only needed to concentrate on displaying his peak strength!
"Shua!"
The Mo Saber's blade was about two inches wide. Although it was called a saber, it was actually thinner than the blade of many swords. The blade and hilt were both six feet long, each occupying half the length. The total length of twelve feet was two feet and one inch longer than Xuanyuan Chenfeng's nine feet and nine inches long spear!
The Mo Saber's blade instantly slashed down, dragging out a dark red blade shadow as it chopped down towards Xuanyuan Chenfeng's head!
There was no technique, nor was there anything special. There was only an extremely fast blade technique and an extremely fierce aura!
The Mo Saber in Maha's hand was admittedly the "Fiend Dragon Saber", which was ranked fourth in the Great Extreme Universe. However, the spear in Xuanyuan Chenfeng's hand was also the "Heavenly Passage Spear", which was ranked seventh in the Great Extreme Universe's Primal Chaos Myriad Spirit Stele. In terms of weapons, the two did not have much of a difference!
Under Maha's attack, he originally thought that Xuanyuan Chenfeng would dodge first and then counterattack …
But what he did not expect was that Xuanyuan Chenfeng did not need to dodge at all. With a flash of his eyes, the Spear of Law had already pierced forward. This was actually a method of mutual destruction!
Although he could split Xuanyuan Chenfeng into two pieces, after being pierced by Xuanyuan Chenfeng, he probably would not be able to survive …
"This guy …"
He also did not expect Xuanyuan Chenfeng to risk his life like this.

Chapter 1660
Xuanyuan Chenfeng's spear attack without dodging made Maha feel extremely uncomfortable!

In Iron Moon City, from all directions, all kinds of martial artists began to boil. They had already placed the heavy responsibility of defeating Maha on Xuanyuan Chenfeng.
The entire universe understood that the arranged battle in Sunflower Water Realm was not a normal duel. Every step here was related to the fate of the entire universe. It was related to the fate of all living beings in the universe.
All the races and living beings in the Great Evolution Universe had already staked everything they had on the line ….
Seeing Maha escape, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's expression didn't change as he followed. The Heavenly Passage Spear in his hand was like a viper leaving its hole. It shot out in an arc in the air and continued to bite Maha's body without letting go!

Maha's heart was also filled with anger. Facing a Dao Child who wasn't much weaker than him and who cultivated the most dangerous "Divine Dao of Truth", this guy was still unafraid of death. It really made Maha feel a bit embarrassed!
The Mo Blade swept towards Xuanyuan Chenfeng's head.
If Xuanyuan Chenfeng was hit by this blade, his entire head and soul would be killed by his Mo Blade …
However, just like the first exchange between the two, Xuanyuan Chenfeng turned a blind eye to Maha's spear. He didn't care about his own life at all. No matter what, he was going to fight until both sides perished!
"Damned bastard …"
Maha clenched his teeth and could only swing the Mo Blade in his hand once again, and once again deflected Xuanyuan Chenfeng's spear!
As the two of them circled the hill, a strange scene unfolded as they chased after each other.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng acted like a desperate man. He wanted to kill Maha, but Maha was not willing to die under Xuanyuan Chenfeng's spear. Naturally, Maha was always at a disadvantage. Maha was always at a disadvantage.

However, in the eyes of some martial artists with extremely strong eyesight, they also felt that it was a bit strange.

Meeting a fellow who cultivated the "Divine Dao of Truth" who was so desperate was indeed a headache. Not to mention Maha, even if it was an Empyrean, if they couldn't stop Xuanyuan Chenfeng, once this fellow got close to them, their situation wouldn't be much better.
At this moment, Maha had circled around the hill to dodge Xuanyuan Chenfeng's hundreds of spears. At this moment, he finally couldn't endure it any longer. He turned around, and a fierce light flashed in his eyes!

Chapter 1661
