He was well aware that the moment he stepped into Sunflower Water World, he would trigger a powerful counterattack from the saints.
Even if he was an upper Empyrean, the chances of him dying here were infinitely high!
But he couldn't stare on helplessly as the corpse of his great-great-grandson was eaten alive by those wolves!
Beneath the obstruction of several Empyreans, Empyrean Original Sin actually slowed down …
At the same time …
In the skies above Sunflower Water World, several blood red space cracks appeared!
Dark red light outlined the outline of these space cracks. Faintly, one could see figures swaying within. A formidable aura leaked out from these space cracks; it was the saint Empyreans hidden within!
It was also this scene that caused Empyrean Original Sin to sober up.
He understood that there were many times when anger couldn't solve problems. If he were to rush into Sunflower Water World at this time, the best result would be him being killed by the saint Empyreans. If he were to fall here, then the 'Star Annihilation' that the four great families worked together to lay down wouldn't be able to operate and humanity's strength would be greatly reduced.
And the worst result would be that the saints would launch an all-out attack.
In this sort of situation, humanity's chances of winning were terrifyingly low. The consequence would be the destruction of the entire universe …
In that moment, Empyrean Original Sin constantly weighed the pros and cons and finally restrained himself.
Zizi …
The spatial crack created by the Original Sin Heavenly Lord's Greater Teleportation Technique had silently closed up. The aura that had originally dissipated had also dissipated without a trace.
When Empyrean Original Sin's space crack vanished, the several blood red space cracks also vanished at the same time.
Now that the saints had the upper hand, they weren't in a hurry. As long as the human Empyreans didn't attack, the saints wouldn't act rashly either. As long as Maha stood tall in Sunflower Water World, he would be able to suppress the 'potential' of the entire universe without fear.
This action would not only eliminate the 'malice' of the Great Evolution Universe towards the saints, but it would also suppress the 'treasure of heaven' in the hands of the old patriarch. From this point of view, it should be the humans that were in a hurry!
After Maha put on his silver overcoat, he slowly walked towards Iron Moon City. Although he didn't stay where he was and didn't even look back, he was well aware of what was happening behind him.
"That Empyrean is quite patient …" He faintly smiled at Shi Kongwu.
Shi Kongwu curled his lips. "What a pity."
If Heavenly Lord of Original Sin were to rush over, it would be exactly what Maha wanted.
"It doesn't matter. I want to see how much tolerance they have, and whether or not they are willing to send the Daoists over one by one." As Maha spoke, she held Shi Kongwu's small waist and stepped into Iron Moon City.
The Sacred Moon Ash-Wolves rushed forward and bit Xuanyuan Chenfeng's corpse. In the blink of an eye, the Sacred Moon Ash-Wolves had devoured the top genius of the Great Evolution Universe. These Sacred Moon Ash-Wolves could sense the Divine Dao aura from the corpse. They faced the sky and howled, revealing a satisfied expression. Then, they obediently returned to their spirit beast pouches.
When the human martial artists in Iron Moon City saw this scene, they all revealed expressions of grief and indignation. For the human race, this was bound to be a great humiliation, but the problem was that they could do nothing about it!
The saints were far too strong. The number one Daoist of the saints was simply impossible to defeat!
In the midst of grief and indignation, a person's potential would always be stimulated and they would have a strong resistance. At this moment, in Iron Moon City, whether it was the Fiend Night Clan, the human race, the demon race, or the martial artists of the smaller races, all of them maintained their silence. But, their gloomy faces revealed a special tenacity. They were like volcanoes that could erupt at any moment!
Not long after, the voice of the Realm of the Gods echoed throughout the Great Evolution Universe.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng's death had another effect. It was to stir up the hearts of all living beings in the universe. Just as Maha had guessed, he didn't need to announce this to the entire universe. The human Empyreans were bound to do the same.
Before this, many living beings in the universe already knew that Xuanyuan Chenfeng had gone to Sunflower Water Realm to challenge Maha. Moreover, Maha seemed to be at a disadvantage. But since then, the universe had remained silent. No one used the roar command to announce the situation. Countless living beings were waiting for this news.
Countless people who were paying attention to this matter waited for a long time, but no news came.
At this moment …
Empyrean Oracle's voice resounded throughout the universe …
"I am Empyrean Oracle. I represent the human race to announce some unfortunate news. Xuanyuan Chenfeng of the Xuanyuan Family has fallen at the hands of Maha. I represent the human race and the Great Evolution Universe. I hope that even stronger geniuses will step forward to avenge us!"
In fact, there were only one or two people in the younger generation who were stronger than Xuanyuan Chenfeng. The one who had defeated Xuanyuan Chenfeng was Luo Zheng, and the other was Hua Tianming. Now, Hua Tianming was also in seclusion, and Luo Zheng had just dealt with the matter of the Demon Night Clan. Besides them, there was no one else in the universe.
The reason she said this was to comfort the countless living beings in the Great Evolution Universe. It was also a kind of encouragement!
Heavenly Sovereign Shen Yu's cold and pleasant voice reverberated throughout the universe. All the races and intelligent lifeforms in the Great Yan Universe were in an uproar …
"Xuanyuan Chenfeng actually lost to Maha!"
"This Maha is really an undefeatable existence! Does an Empyrean have to take action? "
"Luo Zheng, we want Luo Zheng to fight!"
For a moment, the entire Great Evolution Universe was filled with righteous indignation …
Empyrean Oracle's voice reverberated throughout the universe, and naturally, it reached Luo Zheng's ears.
After dealing with the internal strife of the Demon Night Clan, Luo Zheng dived into the lower realm to retrieve the Da Qian Heavy Sword …
This sword could withstand Luo Zheng's power and was very suitable for Luo Zheng. However, once it left his hand and flew out, there was nothing in the world that could bear its weight. It followed the world all the way down to the lower world, burrowing into the depths of the earth. It only stopped when it touched the boundary of the Universe of Great Expansion, which was the Wall of Sighs.
After retrieving the Da Qian Heavy Sword, Luo Zheng activated the teleportation token and stepped into the Sunflower Water Realm. As for Xun, as well as Empyrean Cang Yu and the others of the Demon Night Clan, they had already set off for the Passing Water Realm to meet up with the human Empyreans.
After entering the Sunflower Water Realm, Luo Zheng chose a direction. With a flash of white spatial laws under his feet, he crossed hundreds of miles.
If one looked at Luo Zheng from a high place, they would see that he was like a blinking star. He repeatedly used the Great Teleportation Technique, maintaining a high speed as he moved towards the center of the Sunflower Water Realm.
"Xuanyuan Chenfeng has already fallen, but I wonder if his body has been destroyed. Can the Heavenly Lord of Resurrection's Will of Rebirth revive him?" He weighed this matter in his heart as he hurried on.
Back then, Jiang Zhengyi was almost crushed to pieces by the Ancient Sorcerer Race's Cilie Beast. In the end, he was saved by the Heavenly Lord of Resurrection. If he could get his hands on Xuanyuan Chenfeng's body, he should be able to revive him. However, he didn't know what happened to Xuanyuan Chenfeng. If he was burned to ashes, he didn't know if the Heavenly Lord of Resurrection could revive him …
"Boom!"
Just as Luo Zheng was hurrying on, Luo Zheng saw a bolt of lightning explode in front of him.
His eyes narrowed slightly as he saw two martial artists fighting not far away.
"Sacred Race martial artists." Luo Zheng frowned slightly. His figure gently floated as he sped towards the other side.
The entire Sunflower Water Realm had become a huge arena. Sacred Race and human martial artists were constantly challenging each other in the Sunflower Water Realm …
In the battle between the two, the Sacred Race warrior had the upper hand, while the human world lord was completely suppressed by the other party.
"Lightning Seal Art!"
The Sacred Race martial artist shot out a bolt of brown lightning and nailed the human martial artist to the ground!
"Hahahaha, inferior races aren't worth mentioning. Die!" The Sacred Race Great World Lord laughed wildly. Seeing that he was about to end the human Great World Lord's life, he summoned a bolt of golden lightning that fell from the sky. The human Great World Lord couldn't move at this moment and could only close his eyes and wait for death.
But this bolt of lightning only struck halfway …
A figure suddenly appeared in the air. With a light stretch of his hand, the golden lightning was cut in half!
The thick golden lightning instantly turned into thin lightning snakes that flowed along Luo Zheng's arm and scattered in all directions. Luo Zheng revealed a faint smile towards the other side. "Since the victor has already been decided, let's not engage in meaningless slaughter."
"Who are you! You dare to interfere in my challenge, this is against the rules. "The Sacred Race martial artist's face turned cold.
There wasn't a large scale chaotic battle in the Sunflower Water Realm. The human and Sacred Race martial artists were both holding back their anger. Normally, if one side won a battle, they would directly kill the other.
The Sacred Race martial artists were like this, and the human martial artists were also like this. Bystanders wouldn't interfere. This was an unwritten rule, but Luo Zheng had just arrived and immediately interfered in the battle of bystanders. This was against the rules.

Chapter 1665
As Luo Zheng hurried over, he saw two martial artists engaged in a fierce battle. There were also human and saint martial artists watching from the side. Naturally, he understood that these two martial artists were fighting one on one.
But Luo Zheng had hurried to Sunflower Water World in order to end Maha's challenge. As for this unwritten rule, he had no intention of abiding by it.
Only the weak would worship the rules as if they were gods. As for the strong, they would create rules and even trample on them!
"Boy, are you looking to die!?"
"In this Sunflower Water World, you can only fight one on one. If it weren't for this rule, our saint race would have already turned you humans to ashes!"
"If you know what's good for you then get the hell out of here!"
The saint martial artists watching from the side began to clamor as they saw this.
"Who made this rule?" Luo Zheng lightly swept his eyes around, his fingers gently rubbing against each other …
"This is the rule set by our His Highness Dao Child, Maha!" A saint martial artist arrogantly replied.
Most of the human martial artists that flooded into Sunflower Water World were here for Maha. However, there were far too many of them. It was impossible for Maha to challenge them one by one, so this heavy burden fell onto the shoulders of the saint martial artists.
Luo Zheng's lips curled up. "So that's how it is."
"If you know then hurry up and leave!" A saint martial artist shouted.
Luo Zheng shook his head again. "This is Maha's rule, not my rule."
"Then what is your rule?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the saint martial artists present all felt something was strange. In Sunflower Water World, the saints held the absolute advantage. When a human martial artist challenged a saint martial artist, they behaved according to the rules!
"My rule is that I don't talk about rules with the saints!" Luo Zheng smiled as he finished speaking. His eyes narrowed and his figure tumbled through the air. Like a light swallow, he shot towards the saint martial artist …
The saint martial artist didn't expect that Luo Zheng would dare to attack. He only saw a blur in front of him and Luo Zheng had already passed by him.
The saint martial artist was stunned. Before he could react, an Ice Soul Devil Flame bloomed in his chest and rapidly entered his body. A cold chill spread through his meridians, finally invading his inner world.
Within this saint martial artist's inner world, it had already become a world of ice and snow. Countless lives had turned into solid blocks of ice under the sudden drop in temperature. Even his true essence sea had been frozen solid. Without being able to revolve his true essence, he couldn't even revolve his cultivation method to resist this cold energy!
In the blink of an eye, the Ice Soul Devil Flames blazed and swallowed up his entire body. Finally, this saint race martial artist turned into an ice sculpture that couldn't be approached. That stunned look was permanently frozen on his face …
"How dare you!"
"How dare you sneak attack me!"
"Kill this human brat!"
How could the other saint martial artists have expected that Luo Zheng would suddenly attack?
After all, within the Sunflower Water World, the saints held the dominant position. They had never seen such an arrogant human before. Although they could see that the Ice Soul Devil Flame in Luo Zheng's hands was extraordinarily fierce, the saint martial artists still wanted to rely on their numbers to attack together!
The human martial artists were also slightly stunned. Where did this fellow come from? Not only was he strong, but he was even more arrogant than the saints!
As these human martial artists were hesitating over whether or not to help Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng had already rushed towards the saint martial artists. His figure was like a ghost and the Ice Soul Devil Flame in his hands was an extremely beautiful flower. Every time he passed by a saint martial artist, he would reach out and gently flick them.
This deep blue ice flame rapidly bloomed on the body of the saint martial artist, releasing an unstoppable chill!
"Sou sou sou ….."
The saint martial artists' counterattacks didn't even touch the corners of Luo Zheng's clothes. All of the saint martial artists turned into ice sculptures, the raging ice flames slowly burning around them. Luo Zheng slowly walked out from these ice sculptures, a faint smile hanging on his face.
Although his smile was amiable, the fact that he could kill the Saint Race's Sector Lords with just a wave of his hand gave the human martial artists an absolute shock. Seeing Luo Zheng walk out, the human martial artists couldn't help but take a few steps back, their faces revealing looks of awe.
"How far is Iron Moon City from here?" Luo Zheng asked indifferently.
"There, there's still two million miles ….." A human martial artist answered. This human martial artist was also a sector lord, but in front of Luo Zheng, he unconsciously lowered his seniority. It was as if he was facing an Empyrean.
"Mm." Luo Zheng nodded. With a light tap of his foot, his figure spiraled up like a hawk as he continued to use greater teleportation to hurry on his way.
After Luo Zheng left, the human martial artists felt as if a heavy burden had been lifted off their shoulders …..
"That guy is a sector lord!"
"Ordinary great sector lords can't do that. Could that guy also be a Dao child?"
"Hmph, are you all blind? That guy is Luo Zheng!" A human martial artist revealed Luo Zheng's identity.
Luo Zheng had once challenged the geniuses of the universe in the Illusory Battlefield. There were many people in the universe who remembered Luo Zheng's appearance.
The trip to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement had caused Luo Zheng's cultivation to rapidly increase. His aura had also undergone a drastic change. This group of human martial artists actually didn't recognize him. At this moment, after being reminded by others, the human martial artists present suddenly understood. At the same time, their faces became excited!
"He really does look like Luo Zheng!"
"He actually cultivated to such a level. How can that ice attribute cultivation technique be so terrifying!"
"He's going to Iron Moon City to challenge Maha. Everyone, there's a good show to watch!"
These human martial artists all flew up and chased after Luo Zheng.
A distance of two million miles was a distance that ordinary mortals would never be able to cross in their lifetime. However, to a sector lord, it was relatively close.
After an incense stick of time …..
A bumpy hilly area appeared in Luo Zheng's line of sight.
At the end of this hilly area, there was a half-moon shaped city that was built around this hilly area …..
"Whoosh ….."
Luo Zheng took a step forward. A blazing white light flashed, and he had already entered Iron Moon City.
The current Iron Moon City no longer had a so-called "city lord". The restrictions and divine runes that were originally installed in the city had long been abandoned. In fact, even if these restrictions were activated, they wouldn't be able to stop these ferocious sector lords.
Right now, Iron Moon City was filled with human and saint race Sector Lords. A portion of them came to observe the arranged battles between Sector Lords. However, even more of them came to provoke each other and challenge each other to a duel …..
After Maha killed Xuanyuan Chenfeng, the saint race martial artists were naturally pleased. Their arrogance was also unprecedentedly arrogant. They constantly provoked the other race martial artists in Iron Moon City. As for the human race, demon race, and demon race martial artists, their faces were tense. It looked like they could explode at any moment.
At this moment, Luo Zheng had just stepped in and attracted the attention of several saint race martial artists!
"The new human Sector Lord, hehe!"
"Daoyala, go challenge him!"
"I'll go try ….."
These saint race martial artists had mocking smiles on their faces. One of them walked towards Luo Zheng with large strides. "Hey, human brat, you came to Iron Moon City for ….."
Before this saint race martial artist could finish speaking, Luo Zheng had already brushed past him. However, the moment he passed by this saint race martial artist, a deep blue ice flower was left on his shoulder.
Luo Zheng's expression was as calm as water …..
That Ice Soul Devil Flame began to burn fiercely …..
Before the saint race martial artists could react, Luo Zheng had already increased his speed. That Ice Soul Devil Flame was like the scythe of a death god, beginning to reap the lives of these saint race martial artists!
"Puff puff puff puff puff ….."
Ice Soul Devil Flame blossomed one after another, disturbing the air and letting out low gushing sounds. In the blink of an eye, this group of seven or eight saint race martial artists were turned into burning ice sculptures, standing there motionless.
Luo Zheng didn't slow down his speed at all. He continued to pass through Iron Moon City …..
Because of his actions just now, Luo Zheng had already attracted the attention of the Iron Moon City martial artists. Groups of Saint Lords and Sector Lords rushed out. Seeing this scene, they were naturally enraged. After all, countless challenges had occurred outside Iron Moon City. However, this human martial artist in front of them had broken this rule!

Chapter 1666
Luo Zheng gripped the icy blue flame in his hand as he slaughtered his way through Iron Moon City.
Any Sacred Race martial artist that dared to obstruct him was infected by this unstoppable icy flame.
Only a few Sacred Race martial artists used powerful laws of fire to slightly resist the Icy Soul Devil Flame. However, the Icy Soul Devil Flame was ultimately a profound meaning comprehended by a Saint, and also Jiu Sheng's famed technique. How could these Sacred Race Sector Lords resist it?
A Sacred Race martial artist who used laws of fire conjured a pure gold flame, but was only able to resist for a moment before his true fire was swallowed up by the Icy Soul Devil Flame. After the internal world in his dantian was completely frozen, he could only stare at Luo Zheng in despair.
Luo Zheng's movements slowed down and he glanced at the Sacred Race martial artist. Under Luo Zheng's gaze, the Sacred Race martial artist was enveloped by the Icy Soul Devil Flame and quickly turned into a human ice sculpture. His life force also quietly disappeared.
Following which, Luo Zheng's finger lightly tapped on the ice sculpture. Countless cracks appeared on the Sacred Race martial artist's body, and he turned into a pile of ice shards that fell to the ground.
"What a bold human brat!"
"This guy dares to attack in Iron Moon City!"
"Kill him!"
Luo Zheng's actions finally alarmed half of the sacred race cultivators in Iron Moon City. One by one, the sacred race cultivators flew out onto the streets and quickly surrounded Luo Zheng layer by layer.
"It's Luo Zheng!"
"Luo Zheng has come to Iron Moon City!"
"The current Luo Zheng is so strong. A few years ago, he was only at the Divine Sea Realm. Now, he can easily kill a Sector Lord!"
The Sacred Race martial artists and other races finally recognized Luo Zheng. Some of the Sector Lords saw Luo Zheng's current strength and sighed in their hearts. When Luo Zheng was in the Illusory Battlefield, he was only at the Divine Sea Realm. They had been Sector Lords for hundreds and thousands of years. However, in just a few short years, Luo Zheng had become so terrifying!
There were 70 to 80 Sacred Race martial artists surrounding Luo Zheng, and they were all Sector Lords. However, when they saw the Sacred Race martial artists behind Luo Zheng, they didn't dare to charge forward.
When they heard the name "Luo Zheng", they became even more flustered.
After all, Luo Zheng was the true number one Dao Child of the Great Yan Universe. This fellow's strength might not be as strong as Maha, but he was probably much stronger than ordinary Sector Lords!
Facing these Sacred Race martial artists, Luo Zheng's face was still calm. He raised his right hand slightly and the Ice Soul Devil Flame that was originally the size of a fist suddenly jumped. The ice blue flame shot into the sky, and the temperature of Iron Moon City dropped by a lot!
As for the Sacred Race cultivators, they all took a step back in unison …
"So … the Sacred Race martial artists are so afraid of death." Luo Zheng said indifferently.
Hearing this, the Sacred Race martial artists weren't happy. A tall Sacred Race martial artist stood out and said, "Luo Zheng, if you want to challenge us, let's go up the mountain. I want to challenge you one on one. How about it?"
Luo Zheng glanced at the Sacred Race martial artist. With Luo Zheng's current personality, he wouldn't talk about rules with Sacred Race martial artists!
"You can challenge me." Luo Zheng smiled. "But it's me alone. Challenging all of you …"
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, golden light shot out from his eyes. The Emperor Soul imprint in his Golden Battle Soul emitted an aura of supremacy. This aura didn't match Luo Zheng's nature, but when released, it was enough to suppress Sector Lords. It wasn't much weaker than Maha's Great Ruling Art!
The majestic aura spread out, and all the Sacred Race martial artists felt their souls being suppressed. Some of the weaker Sacred Race martial artists felt a splitting headache, and even their vision went dark, unable to see anything!
At the same time, Luo Zheng's figure flashed. He grabbed the Ice Soul Devil Flame in his hand and shot towards the Sacred Race martial artists!
"Sou!"
He didn't stop beside the Sacred Race martial artists. The instant he approached them, the Ice Soul Devil Flame gently swept past them. A wisp of flame was left on their bodies, and in the blink of an eye, the Ice Soul Devil Flame burned fiercely on their bodies!
In just one or two breaths of time …
The vast majority of the Saint Race martial artists present had been infected by the Ice Soul Devil Flame. Only two or three Saint Race martial artists with relatively powerful soul abilities had managed to grit their teeth and use greater teleportation to escape.
"Hu hu hu …"
The ice blue Ice Soul Devil Flame emitted an extremely beautiful brilliance, slowly dancing on the bodies of these martial artists.
The temperature in Iron Moon City plummeted again …
The Sunflower Water Realm was like spring all year round, but at this moment, Iron Moon City seemed to have sunk into a land of extreme cold. Some of the low level martial artists and mortals in Iron Moon City were shivering from the cold.
As for the human Sector Lords behind Luo Zheng, their mouths were wide open as they stared blankly at Luo Zheng.
Just now, so many Sacred Race Sector Lords were planning to surround and attack Luo Zheng. They also wanted to help, but who would have thought that in just one or two breaths of time, all the Sacred Race Sector Lords would turn into ice sculptures.
"This … this guy is unbelievably strong!"
"He is indeed strong. This is already the strength of an Empyrean, but he doesn't have Heaven's Mandate!"
"Are you stupid!? Luo Zheng is a Dao Child. If his comprehension of the Divine Dao is deep, he will be far stronger than Heaven's Mandate! "
"Hahaha, that Maha fellow won't run away with his tail between his legs, right!?"
After the intense shock, there was extreme joy. The martial artists who dared to challenge the Sacred Race in Sunflower Water Realm all had the courage to face death with equanimity. They weren't afraid of death, but they were afraid of dying from cowardice and cowardice. After being suppressed by the Sacred Race for so long, these people's emotions were finally released. This was because Luo Zheng had allowed them to see hope!
After turning these Sacred Race martial artists into ice sculptures, Luo Zheng suddenly stomped his foot. An incomparably powerful force vibrated outwards, as if a giant mountain had smashed into Iron Moon City. The entire Iron Moon City was constantly shaking!
This vibration was like a huge wave that spread outwards, passing onto the ice sculptures. Countless cracks appeared on the surface of the Sacred Race World Masters that were frozen. They instantly collapsed and turned into a pile of ice shards …
At this moment, Luo Zheng slightly exhaled and asked a human martial artist present, "Where is Xuanyuan Chenfeng's corpse?"
When the human martial artist heard Luo Zheng's question, he forcefully suppressed the excitement in his heart and pointed towards the giant hill on the side of Iron Moon City, "It's there! But … Xuanyuan Chenfeng's corpse should have been eaten up by those wolves! "
Luo Zheng suddenly frowned. With a tap of his foot, he instantly rushed out of Iron Moon City and landed on the hill surrounded by Iron Moon City. His gaze slightly focused. Sure enough, he found four silver wolves on the side of the hill!
Just like what the bystanders said, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's body had already been eaten up by these silver wolves until there wasn't much left!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a trace of fury!
In the beginning, Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Luo Zheng were enemies and not friends. However, that was because they were in a competitive relationship. Although this guy's personality wasn't likable, he was still his comrade-in-arms.
Now that he saw Xuanyuan Chenfeng's corpse being eaten by these beasts, he couldn't help but feel furious!
In the sky, Luo Zheng instantly turned into a meteor and directly smashed towards those Sacred Moon Ash Wolves!
These Sacred Moon Ash Wolves were super divine beasts. Although they weren't fully grown super divine beasts, their strength wasn't weak. The moment Luo Zheng appeared in the sky, these Sacred Moon Ash Wolves had already noticed him. They lowered their bodies and stuck their heads to the ground, staring at Luo Zheng …
"Awoo!"
Without waiting for Luo Zheng to approach, several Sacred Moon Ash Wolves jumped up from different directions and bit towards Luo Zheng's neck, arms, and legs.
The teeth of these Sacred Moon Ash Wolves flickered with a metallic luster. The tips of their teeth were extremely sharp. Even divine tools could be bitten off in one bite. Relying on this kind of fighting instinct, ordinary Sector Lords were far from being their opponents!
They thought that by joining forces, they could tear this human martial artist to shreds!
The Sacred Moon Ash Wolves' intelligence wasn't much lower than that of humans, but they still underestimated Luo Zheng's strength.
"Ka ka ka ka …"

Chapter 1667
"A beast will always be a beast …"
Luo Zheng's hand shot out like lightning and grabbed the Holy Moon Wolf's head!
Unexpectedly, the Holy Moon Wolf's reaction was extremely agile. With a slight twist of its body, it dodged Luo Zheng's grab. At the same time, it roared and rushed towards Luo Zheng's face. The beast found that Luo Zheng's body was indestructible and wanted to go straight for Luo Zheng's face. At the same time, the other three Holy Moon Wolves were also biting tightly on the other side of Luo Zheng, trying to stall Luo Zheng.
"Oh?"
Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of surprise, and then he smiled coldly!
The power of the nine stars in his body gushed out crazily. Then his body suddenly twisted, and a wave of power turned into a whirlpool that spun wildly around him. This invisible whirlpool twisted the four Holy Moon Wolves, and a burst of cracking sounds were heard.
In the blink of an eye, the Holy Moon Wolves' heads, fur, and bones were all twisted into pieces …
Luo Zheng stood in the middle of these pieces and waved his hand calmly. The Sumeru Ring shone faintly, and all the pieces of the Holy Moon Wolves were absorbed into it.
Previously, Jiang Zhengyi was also smashed into pieces by the Cilie Beast of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe, but he was resurrected by the Resurrection Holy Lord. According to Luo Zheng's assumption, although Xuanyuan Chenfeng was eaten by the Holy Moon Wolves, these beasts should not have had time to absorb the pieces of his body.
If the Demi-Immortal Tribe could separate the pieces of Xuanyuan Chenfeng's body from the pieces of the Holy Moon Wolves, there might be a chance.
Although this matter didn't seem very reliable, Luo Zheng could only do this much. Whether or not Xuanyuan Chenfeng could be saved would depend on his luck.
After killing the Holy Moon Wolves, he slowly descended and stood on a protruding rock, his eyes falling on Iron Moon City.
While Luo Zheng was killing these Holy Moon Ashwolves, he was also secretly on guard against Maha's sneak attacks. However, Maha never showed himself, which only deepened Luo Zheng's vigilance.
On one side of Iron Moon City stood a magnificent palace. This palace was originally the City Lord's mansion of Iron Moon City.
However, after Maha had arrived at the Sunflower Water Realm, the City Lord had been killed by him, and the City Lord's Mansion had become Maha's temporary palace.
A pool in the mansion was steaming hot, and the brown water in the pool was boiling. A thick medicinal fragrance wafted through the mansion. The temperature of the medicinal water in the pool was far higher than normal boiling water. If an ordinary creature was thrown into the pool, they would be boiled in the blink of an eye.
However, there were two naked figures immersed in it.
Maha leaned against the edge of the pool and tilted his head back, looking up at the ceiling. Shi Kongwu leaned against Maha's side.
The two Realm Lords didn't use their protective true essence to resist the heat of the medicinal water. The scalding medicinal water boiled their skin until it was bright red, but they felt more energetic.
"Luo Zheng, that brat, seems to be a bit impatient," Maha said faintly.
Shi Kongwu used her delicate fingers to cup a handful of water and poured it on Maha's sturdy chest. She then said softly, "Brother Maha, don't be careless. He is very strong, much stronger than Xuanyuan Chenfeng."
Shi Kongwu had lost to Luo Zheng and Xuanyuan Chenfeng in succession. She couldn't defeat either of them, but the pressure they gave her was completely different.
She could exchange blows with Xuanyuan Chenfeng. In her eyes, Xuanyuan Chenfeng wasn't an unreachable existence. If she went all out, it wouldn't take long for her to defeat him.
However, the feeling Luo Zheng gave her was completely different. In front of Luo Zheng, she didn't have the strength to fight back. If Luo Zheng really went all out, she believed that Luo Zheng could instantly kill her. This kind of extremely strong pressure was even deeper than facing an Empyrean …
Maha smiled and rubbed Shi Kongwu's face. "Wu 'er, are you worried that I won't be his match?"
"Wu 'er isn't looking down on Brother Maha …" Shi Kongwu hurriedly explained. She was clear about Maha's extremely conceited personality.
She didn't think that Maha's smile would gradually disappear. He said faintly, "I don't have much confidence in my battle with Luo Zheng. I already have a premonition that he might be the strongest opponent I've ever met in my life!"
Under certain conditions, Maha could kill an Empyrean. This kind of evaluation already placed Luo Zheng in the ranks of Empyreans.
Shi Kongwu was stunned. She didn't think that Maha, who had always been conceited to the extreme, would give Luo Zheng such an evaluation. This was indeed outside of her expectations. Chapter 1668

Chapter 1669

Chapter 1670

Thus, for Luo Zheng, attacking like this could be considered a form of deterrence!
As for Maha, he could only scurry around underground, inwardly complaining …
But at this moment, Maha was unable to hold himself back. After traveling for dozens of miles underground, he suddenly came to a stop. Then, the earth in front of him suddenly split open. A sword intent pierced through his eyes, cleaving open a bottomless fissure in front of him.

Weng …

Chapter 1675

Chapter 1678

Chapter 1679
Empyreans were divided into upper, middle, and lower ranks; they weren't divided according to their cultivation.

Empyrean Sacred Altar's Heaven's Mandate belonged to the 'summoning' category of Heaven's Mandate. This sort of Heaven's Mandate belonged to the weaker category and was ranked towards the bottom of Empyreans. It couldn't even reach the level of a middle Empyrean!

She alone had killed nine Empyreans!
"Those four swords are coming again!"
"Empyrean Holy Altar! Help! "
"I'll block those four swords …"
In the eyes of these saint Empyreans, these four swords were as terrifying as death gods. Every time they appeared they would take the lives of one or two Empyreans.
When the four swords reached the top of the altar, they were stopped by the golden halo that spread out from the altar.
Xi Youqin sat on the battleship, her eyes indifferent. Her fingers danced in the air, and the giant swords shone with different lights as they began to attack the circle of golden light …
"Dong dong dong dong …"
In a single breath of time, the four great swords had already collided over ten thousand times!
With every collision, the golden halo that spread out from the altar dimmed a little.
Kacha!
With an ear-piercing shattering sound, the golden halo finally shattered!
The moment the light circle shattered, it wasn't just the four swords that rushed into it, there were also two people!
One of them was Luo Zheng, and the other was Celestial Lord Oracle!
Luo Zheng relied on Celestial Lord Cloudfox's Hollow Love Lock to appear behind a saint Empyrean.
This saint Empyrean's reactions were extremely sharp. The moment Luo Zheng appeared, the saint Empyrean brandished a trident towards Luo Zheng.
This saint Empyrean had stimulated his Mandate of Heaven to the limit. The might of this spear could pierce through all things in the world. Even if Luo Zheng's mortal body was tougher, he feared he wouldn't be able to block it. However, Luo Zheng didn't even need to dodge. Instead, he used his body to collide with the trident and slashed out with the Great Thousand Heavy Sword.
"Absolute Karmic Divine Light!"
A misty light gently flickered and severed all karma. After losing the support of the Mandate of Heaven, this Empyrean's trident posed no threat to Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng allowed the trident to pierce his body, but it only emitted a crisp sound without piercing through his vest.
After forcefully withstanding this trident strike, Luo Zheng's eyes were filled with a ruthless infernal energy. "Yi God's Sword!"
Puff!
Since this Empyrean couldn't use greater teleportation, he couldn't escape. He could only helplessly watch as Luo Zheng's heavy sword ruthlessly cut down at him. Beneath the tyrannical sword intent, his entire mortal body and inner world were turned to dust …
At the same time that Luo Zheng killed this Holy Race Celestial Venerable, the nearby Holy Race Celestial Venerable Oracle passed by another Celestial Venerable. The dagger in her hand only lightly scratched the Celestial Venerable's cheek, leaving a thin line of blood. It was like a provocation, but she didn't take his life.
In his rage, that saint Empyrean brandished his great axe and chased after Empyrean Oracle.
Celestial Lord Shen Yu's graceful figure was like a butterfly shuttling between flowers. There was a hint of a smile on his face as he constantly wandered between the axe-lights.
Soon, the wound on that Empyrean's cheek began to emit a faint purple light. This extremely small wound continued to expand along his cheek, extending all the way to his neck and all parts of his body!
After Empyrean Oracle inherited the Mandate of Heaven, she cultivated an extremely vicious cultivation method. It was one of Luo Xiao's ultimate techniques, the Self-Destruction Method.
Warriors at this level generally weren't afraid of all kinds of poisons. Whether it was their physical body or blood essence, they had all been cultivated to the extreme. Even if a violent poison entered their body, they could easily force it out or even directly swallow it.
The Self-Destruction Method was another form of poison. However, it wasn't poison, but an extremely special kind of energy. Any living being that was ambushed by Empyrean Oracle would die without a doubt, even if it was just a small cut.
However, strictly speaking, the opponent wasn't killed by Empyrean Oracle. This special kind of energy would directly guide all the energy in the opponent's body to destroy themselves. Whether it was the blood essence or the true essence in the dantian, they would all begin to madly destroy their physical body until they died.
This was considered suicide, so it was called the Self-Destruction Method. It was also because of this terrifying Mandate of Heaven that Empyrean Oracle was able to become an upper level Empyrean!
Luo Zheng and Celestial Lord Oracle killed the two Celestial Lords cleanly. They exchanged glances as they crossed each other's path.
Luo Zheng was still immersed in the Emotion Severing Divine Path. His emotionless gaze only lightly glanced at Empyrean Oracle. Empyrean Oracle's pair of beautiful eyes cast a smile at Luo Zheng. There was a look of appreciation in her eyes. Then, the two of them stepped into a spatial rift and escaped at the same time.
Empyrean Oracle used the Art of Great Teleportation, and Luo Zheng was directly pulled back by Empyrean Master Yun Hu.
In addition to the two of them, Empyrean Master of Original Sin, Empyrean Master of Thunder Punishment, and the others each took the lives of two Mandates of Heaven …
At the same time, on top of the Sacred Altar, Empyrean Sacred Altar suddenly clapped his hands. Half of the vital essence in his inner world poured into the Sacred Altar like seawater. The other Empyreans did the same. The inner worlds that they had painstakingly built had completely collapsed, and the living beings within had disintegrated by themselves.
After this group of Empyreans stepped into the Universe of Great Expansion, they couldn't join forces to set up a powerful formation. After all, the true elites were still in the Universe of Great Expansion. They were just pioneers. Because of this, they were at a disadvantage when fighting these Empyreans in the Universe of Great Expansion.
Fortunately, Empyrean Sacred Altar's Mandate of Heaven was extremely strong. Even with the Sacred Altar behind him, he could barely hold on. If it weren't for the Sacred Altar, all of the Empyreans would have been annihilated by now …
Under the joint efforts of several Empyreans, a huge amount of vital essence was poured into the Sacred Altar. Another object crawled out from the Sacred Altar. It was a small golden monkey. The moment the monkey appeared, it roared at the sky, and for a moment, golden light filled the sky!
The golden light was like swords and sabers, extremely sharp. It scattered in all directions at an extremely fast speed …
This golden light had previously killed two Empyreans from the Demi-Immortal Tribe and the Human Dao Alliance, but using the same method again was useless. The Empyreans in the Universe of Great Expansion had already fled in advance, so the golden light could only hit empty air.
After the golden light scattered, it once again condensed into a golden halo that wrapped around the Sacred Altar, giving the sacred race Empyreans a chance to catch their breath.
However, Xi Youqin controlled the four divine swords Long Vine, Buddha Emperor, Double Sun, and Skyfang to appear outside the halo once more …

Chapter 1682
Within the Great Yan Universe, the Empyreans were like a pack of wolves.
Every time the four swords broke through the golden circle of light around the Holy Altar, Luo Zheng and the others would rush into battle.
They would take the lives of several Empyreans at an extremely fast speed. When the little golden monkey appeared, they would quietly retreat …
Like this, Empyrean Holy Altar's resistance became nothing more than a joke.
He could only watch helplessly as his fellow Empyreans perished one after another …
Before long, the number of saint Empyreans guarding the Holy Altar began to decrease. Of the original 20 some Empyreans, only three remained.
"I didn't think that I, Empyrean Holy Altar, would perish here because this war was never fair to begin with. All you relied on was that True Divinity!" Empyrean Holy Altar roared out in despair.
His voice echoed through the Sunflower Water Realm, containing a trace of grief and indignation.
If it weren't for Startail using the Star Sealing Art to lock down the space around them, the 50 saint Empyreans wouldn't have been defeated so miserably. The saint Empyreans were imprisoned within the Sunflower Water Realm and were nothing more than moving targets.
Hearing Empyrean Holy Altar's words, Luo Zheng quietly appeared once more, standing tall in front of the Holy Altar. At the same time, Empyrean Oracle and the Saint Spear Smog also walked out from a space crack. More and more Empyreans gathered here …
"What shameless boasting. Your saint race has invaded our Great Yan Universe, so what fairness is there to speak of?" Empyrean Original Sin fiercely said. Xuanyuan Chenfeng had died at the hands of Maha. This great enmity was engraved in his heart and he hated the saint race to the bone.
"That's right. Those that aren't of my race must be evil by nature. Could it be that you plan on fighting us in the arena?" Empyrean Thunder Punishment taunted.
Wars between different races were often ruthless and cruel. Before the saints invaded, the human race, the demon race, and the demon race had never stopped fighting. The contradictions between different races were originally irreconcilable.
However, these three races were all beings that were born in the Great Yan Universe. Now that they faced an 'outsider' like the saints, they suddenly united together. After all, the saints came from a completely different universe.
In the eyes of the Empyreans, as long as they could protect the Great Yan Universe, there was no taboo in terms of methods.
Empyrean Sacred Altar stared at everyone present, a smile suddenly appearing on his despairing face. "There is indeed no fairness. Even if we die, we will drag you all down with us. How can a mere Great Yan Realm escape the grasp of our Saint Race!?"
Empyrean Exalt Elemental Altar believed that Grand Empyrean would take revenge for him, but he wouldn't be able to see that moment, so he could only mock him.
However, the saint Empyreans' words had no effect at all. More and more Empyreans arrived, all of them indifferently staring at Empyrean Holy Altar.
"Are you done with your nonsense?" Celestial Sovereign Shen Yu asked. The dagger in his hand glowed with a faint purple light.
"Hmph, you don't have to do anything!" Empyrean Sacred Altar waved his hand. His dantian suddenly caved in and a powerful aura began to condense within. The other two saint Empyreans were the same …
Boom! Rumble! Rumble!
These three Empyreans stimulated the last trace of true essence in their dantians and blew themselves up.
With the strength of an Empyrean, the power of their self-detonation should have been terrifying. But, the three of them were already at the end of their rope. Their self-detonation didn't pose any threat to anyone present …
As Empyrean Sacred Altar self-detonated, the Sacred Altar also slowly vanished. Even the little golden monkey lying on top of the Sacred Altar creaked and viciously glared at everyone. Then, it unwillingly dissipated before everyone's eyes.
Even if an Empyrean died, the Mandate of Heaven wouldn't disappear. The Mandate of Heaven would return to the Great Yan Universe's Heavenly Dao …
This was the first battle between the Great Yan Universe and the Great Yan Universe. After several years of patiently waiting, this sudden attack was able to kill a third of the Saint Race's Empyreans. The entire process could be said to be extremely smooth. Only two Empyreans had died, and the old Patriarch didn't even need to do anything.
Everyone's hearts were heavy. After all, the true opponent was still in the Great Yan Universe.
Once the Saint Race opened the channel to the Wall of Sighs, the Great Yan Universe would have to face the crazy retaliation of the Saint Race. This was an invisible pressure.
This war between Empyreans had already caused the entire Sunflower World to be torn apart. Even if an Empyrean were to help, it would still be impossible to repair it. However, the universe originally had the ability to regenerate itself. Over the endless years, the wounds of this great world would slowly heal. But in the end, it was impossible for it to become a complete great world. It could only be like the Orange Sea World, a broken great world.
But this was already the best result. When four or five Empyreans fought, the Orange Sea World had been torn apart. Now, over a hundred Empyreans were fighting. If it weren't for Startail imprisoning the Saint Race Empyreans, then it wouldn't have just been the Sunflower World that would have been destroyed, but hundreds or even thousands of great worlds.
Currently, there were still many saint martial artists in the Great Yan Universe.
But without the support of the Empyreans, these saint martial artists wouldn't be able to live for long in the Great Yan Universe. The various natural disasters that came from the universe were more than enough to clean up these saint martial artists. It would only take a certain amount of time.
Right now, what they needed to consider was how to deal with the counterattack of the saints.
At this time, Startail appeared in front of everyone once more, a bitter smile on his face. "The saints … have already begun to break through the boundary."
… …
… …
Breaking through the Wall of Sighs was extremely difficult.
The saints had begun their operation several hundred years ago. If it weren't for Mu Haiji's assistance, it would have taken several hundred more years.
But, Startail had only used a dark star and the Star Sealing Art to temporarily seal the Wall of Sighs. As a True Divinity, Startail's methods were fierce, but this wasn't an insurmountable obstacle!
At this time, in front of the Wall of Sighs …
The saint Great Empyreans and many saint Empyreans were still standing there, their faces filled with grief and sorrow …
The Heavenly Dao had been shaking for a long time. According to their speculations, all the saint Empyreans that went to the Great Yan Universe should have perished without a single survivor!
The saint Empyreans were divided into many factions and there were also disputes between them. But, as fellow Empyreans, they had extremely close personal relationships with each other. In the blink of an eye, so many Empyreans had died in another universe, so they naturally felt sad.
In front of these Empyreans was a black-clothed youth floating in the air. This youth exuded a faintly discernible aura. It was very gentle, but it left a deep impression on others. This aura was' potential '. This black-clothed youth was one of Mu Haiji's disciples, True Divinity Mu Han.
All of Mu Han's inheritances came from Mu Haiji. Thus, his' potential 'was very similar to the' potential 'emitted by the Great Empyreans that collected the heavens. But, if one were to carefully distinguish them, there would be many differences.
"Hoh, it's just a seal placed with the help of the power of the stars. What an insignificant skill!" Looking at the blocked passage, Mu Han chuckled.
"Lord Mu Han, can you hurry up? Our saint race …" The saint race's Empyrean Silver Wing anxiously said. This Empyrean's son and daughter had joined the expeditionary army and it was unknown if they were still alive.
"What are you rushing me for?" Mu Han indifferently glanced at the Empyrean.
"Silver Wing, don't be rude to Lord Mu Han!" The Great Empyrean reprimanded.
"But …" Exalted Celestial Master Silver Wing looked resentful.
Their saint race was working for Mu Haiji. Mu Haiji was the ruler of the entire universe. The saint race didn't have the qualifications to refuse any of Mu Haiji's requests. But, this didn't mean that the saint race didn't have any other thoughts.
Even if they helped Mu Haiji destroy the entire Great Yan Universe, what benefit would it bring to the saint race?
These Empyreans were working for Mu Haiji, but Mu Han was Mu Haiji's disciple. He acted so leisurely, as if he didn't care at all. It was inevitable that the Empyreans present would have other thoughts …
However, Mu Han didn't care at all. In the eyes of the beings of the Realm of the Gods, these secondary life forms in the universe weren't true life forms at all. They were just 'things' formed from true essence. He didn't care how many died!
With a light leap, Mu Han jumped into the passage of the Wall of Sighs.
Then, a pale yellow beam of light shot out from the passage. This beam of light reflected off the passage and shot into the sky …
The numerous Empyreans heard Mu Han shout, "Break!"
Bang!
There was a dull sound as air was constantly sucked in. The passage that spanned across the two great universes opened once more.

Chapter 1683
The mass of the dark star itself was astonishingly great.
The tail of the star pushed the dark star down from the depths of space at an extremely high speed. Then, with a terrifying momentum, it smashed into the channel!
Before this, Great Empyreans had borrowed the 'potential' of the Great Extreme Space to move the dark star away, but they had failed.
They never imagined that Mu Han would forcefully break it apart …
Bang!
Within the Great Extreme Space, the dark star was blasted into a channel. It drew a parabola through the air before smashing into the great world.
How could this great world withstand the mass of a dark star?
The dark star smashed into the mud as if it had fallen into water. It sank downwards and soon vanished without a trace.
Mu Han walked out of the tunnel and took a deep breath. When he sensed the sealing power of the Great World, he frowned.
The Empyreans that followed Mu Han also felt a strong binding force.
Some Empyreans tried to condense their Space Laws, but just as they did, the power of the Laws collapsed and vanished without a trace …
"This feeling makes me very unhappy." Mu Han lightly said.
Undoing the seal of a great world was a rather troublesome matter, but Mu Han didn't even think about undoing the seal.
He stretched out a foot and gently stepped on the ground. A gentle aura spread out from the tip of his foot.
Rumble rumble rumble rumble …
Faint waves spread out from Mu Han's foot and rapidly spread across the entire great world. Then, the great world began to shake along with this frequency …
In this great world, there were still many Sacred Race cultivators and many mortals who managed to survive.
At this moment, they could see tiny cracks appearing beneath their feet. This wasn't like an ordinary earthquake. Ordinary earthquakes would cause the earth to shake and the mountains to tremble violently. But at this moment, the shaking was very gentle, but the frequency of the shaking was extremely high …
This shaking only lasted for around ten breaths of time before a piercing sound echoed throughout the great world.
Ka ka ka ka …
This huge great world instantly disintegrated into trillions of watermelon-sized chunks of earth that scattered downwards.
All of the mortals also fell down with these chunks of earth. Only a small number of martial artists who could fly were able to escape this calamity. But for these martial artists with lower cultivations, it was simply impossible for them to cross over a great world. Once they exhausted all of their true essence in the air, they would fall to the ground and shatter into pieces!
The Star Sealing Art had been used on this great world. Once this great world was destroyed, the Star Sealing Art would naturally collapse as well …
When the saint Grand Empyreans led their clansmen to see this, their eyes revealed looks of horror.
With the methods of an Empyrean, they could also destroy a great world, but it was difficult for them to destroy a great world to such a degree. What was even more terrifying was that Mu Han had only gently stepped on this great world. this great world world.....
"Is this the power of a True God?"
"Empyrean Empyrean.................
"Although a Grand Empyrean has a heaven-suppressing treasure, in the end they are only borrowing it. That 'force' doesn't belong to the Grand Empyrean himself …"
The saint Empyreans discussed amongst themselves. Even the Empyreans that were accustomed to grand spectacles were incomparably shocked.
Mu Han looked up towards the depths of the great world and then casually said, "Everyone, follow me …"
After he finished speaking, he hurtled forwards. As he flew forwards, he didn't cause any energy fluctuations. In just a few dozen feet, he turned into a blazing white silhouette that vanished in front of the saint Empyreans.
"Follow him!"
A Grand Empyrean waved his hand and led the saint Empyreans into the depths of the great world using greater teleportation!
In the shattered Sunflower Water Realm …
Startail sensed that the saints were breaking through his Star Sealing Art and had already informed Luo Zheng and the others ahead of time.
But in just half an incense stick of time, Startail informed everyone once more, "They have already broken through the seal …"
"How could they be so fast!?"
"That is the seal that Lord Startail laid down!"
"Did the other side's True Divinity take action?"
Everyone originally thought that with Lord Startail's attainments, the seal would last at least several months. Even if it didn't last that long, several days should be enough. No one thought that it would last for only an incense stick of time!
After killing over 50 saint Empyreans in the Sunflower Water Realm, the powerhouses of the Great Expansion Universe didn't even have time to rest before they had to face an even more brutal battle. Moreover, it could be said that the saints had turned out in full force and there were even True Divinity powerhouses amongst them.
Everyone in the Sunflower Water Realm understood that this would be a tough battle.
"Since we have taken action this time, there is no longer any path of retreat." The Old Patriarch's calm voice sounded out.
"That's right, we have no path of retreat. We can only go forwards and fight with them." Empyrean Thunder Punishment nodded.
"Words are useless. We will welcome them here …" Empyrean Oracle's eyes were filled with determination. After cultivating to the level of an Empyrean, she had already seen through life and death. She knew that only at the moment of her death would she be able to give meaning to her death!
"Lay down the array!" Empyrean Original Sin immediately took action. He joined forces with the patriarchs of the four great families and laid down the "Star Annihilation" array …
Empyrean Cloud Fox gathered Luo Zheng and Xun to her side. She was about to use the Spatial Love Lock to link the two of them together. Luo Zheng and Xun were strong, but they were only Realm Lords in the end. When fighting against an Empyrean, there was a natural shortcoming. Only by cooperating with Empyrean Cloud Fox's destiny could they display their strongest combat strength.
But at this time …
Whoosh whoosh …
On the majestic battleship, Xi Youqin was still sitting upright. She had been controlling the God Slaughtering Sword Array at the edge of the Sunflower Water Realm. Although the battleship's speed wasn't slow, it was still much slower than an Empyrean. It was only now that she rushed over.
"Luo Zheng!"
Ever since she stepped into the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, she had been searching for Luo Zheng. In the end, she found nothing. Then, she was taken away by a mysterious person and received the complete inheritance of the God Slaughtering Sword Array …
There were many warriors who died in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Xi Youqin's journey was quite smooth, but she was still unhappy. According to her own thoughts, she naturally wanted to cultivate with Luo Zheng in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. However, she hadn't seen Luo Zheng even once until she returned to the universe. Now that she saw Luo Zheng again, she naturally hoped that she could get closer to him.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
After Xi Youqin called out, she jumped down from the battleship high in the sky. She was wearing a purple robe, and the smoky muslin ribbon on her back was dragged. After drawing a trajectory in the air, she accurately jumped into Luo Zheng's arms.
On the side, Xun had just established a link with Celestial Venerable Cloud Fox's Hollow Love Lock. When she saw this scene, a dark red light suddenly flashed in the depths of Xun's eyes, and the killing intent contained in the power of faith was released.
Xi Youqin leaned half of her head on Luo Zheng's chest and whispered about her experience after going to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, as well as her longing for Luo Zheng. However, her eyes passed over Luo Zheng's shoulder and fell on Xun.
Perhaps sensing the baleful aura in Xun's eyes, Xi Youqin's eyes curved into crescent moons as a surprised expression emerged from her mouth. "Big sister Xun, you've finally found your body?"
Women were generally very sensitive when it came to emotions. From the time Luo Zheng met Xi Youqin until now, Xun had always existed in the form of a soul, so Xi Youqin naturally did not view her as an opponent. However, now that Xun had a complete physical body, it was naturally different from before.
Hearing Xi Youqin's surprised and mocking tone, Luo Zheng could not help but feel a headache. What time was it? Xi Youqin's mind was still filled with these things.
Just as he was about to speak, Xun coldly snorted and turned around. She was too lazy to pay attention to Xi Youqin.
Xun was a person of few words, and unlike Ning Yudie's temperament, she naturally did not like Xi Youqin, but she would definitely not quarrel with Xi Youqin for Luo Zheng …
"Alright, let's go back quickly. True God experts can appear in the universe in an instant. We need you to preside over the sword array and end this war. Then, we will have time to spend together." Luo Zheng gently patted Xi Youqin's back. After a while of persuasion, Xi Youqin reluctantly let go of Luo Zheng. She suddenly remembered a certain agreement in her mind, and she smiled sweetly at Luo Zheng. Then, she turned around and flew towards the battleship.
However, the moment she slowly ascended, a blazing white human silhouette suddenly appeared in mid-air!
After the Law of Space on this human silhouette dissipated, a black-clothed young man appeared. This was Mu Han, who had arrived first. He glanced indifferently at Xi Youqin, who was close at hand. Xi Youqin also blinked. She thought this black-clothed young man was a Celestial Master of the Human Clan.
"Hey …"

Chapter 1684
Facing the sudden appearance of Mu Han, Luo Zheng, Xun, and many other Celestial Venerables were stunned.
After Mu Han broke Xi Youqin's neck, he looked down at the cultivators below. The corners of his mouth curled up, revealing a cold smile. He lifted Xi Youqin's slumped neck and carefully examined it for a while. "Not bad. It's a pity to kill you like this …"
After saying that, he casually threw Xi Youqin's body down.
Looking at Xi Youqin's soft body that was like a piece of paper, Luo Zheng's heart thumped wildly, as if it was being pricked by needles.
When they were in the Central Domain, she didn't hesitate to follow him all the way to the Cloud Hall.
After ascending, she also cultivated a powerful sword array and searched for him in the huge Illusory Battlefield.
She was still the same when she went to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
After returning, Xi Youqin was quickly brought back to the Demi-Immortal Clan. She wanted to find Luo Zheng, but she was too busy practicing the God Slaying Sword Array. In the end, she couldn't get away.
In her heart, everything in the world didn't seem to exist. Even herself didn't exist. Her love for Luo Zheng was almost distorted …
This woman had been desperately chasing after him. However, compared to Xun and Ning Yudie, Luo Zheng's feelings for her were the most indifferent. Many times, he even treated her with respect. Perhaps he was just being responsible for his impulsiveness in anger.
However, at this moment, when he saw Xi Youqin's neck being broken, an intense pain emerged in his heart. This pain was so deep that it almost made his heart burst!
Whoosh!
Before he could vent his anger, Luo Zheng flew straight to Xi Youqin's floating body and hugged Xi Youqin in his arms.
It was only her neck that had been crushed, but Luo Zheng felt that Xi Youqin's life force had been completely extinguished. Hugging her soft body, a trace of sourness gathered in his nose, and Luo Zheng's figure in the air looked somewhat lonely.
Xi Youqin's unexpected death was indeed shocking. The God-Slaying Sword Array was very important to the Demi-Immortal Tribe. After her death, no one could control it …
However, this wasn't the focus of everyone's attention. They were all focused on the black-clothed young man.
This black-clothed youth exuded a gentle aura, as if he didn't have any sharpness, but no one dared to ignore this youth's existence!
That gentle aura was the "influence" of a true god. This person should be that true god in the Universe of Extremities.
This thought flashed through the minds of the Empyreans, and their eyes all revealed looks of fear …
"I didn't think that a bunch of bugs formed from true essence would bring so much trouble to Master. As expected … relying on those bugs in the Great Ultimate Universe is not a reliable thing to do." Mu Han looked down at the Celestial Venerables and smiled faintly.
Mu Han's inner world was quite complete, and there were even Empyreans in his body. It was just that the Empyreans in his inner world were far inferior to those in the universe. However, in Mu Han's eyes, whether it was the living beings in the Universe of Extremities or the living beings in the Universe of Extremities, they were all secondary living beings formed from true essence. They couldn't be called real living beings at all.
"Hu …"
At this moment, Xing Wei stepped forward and stood in front of Mu Han. Faint starlight flashed in his eyes. "We've fought before."
On the Sacred Race's Soaring Sky Mountain, Mu Han had used the secret technique of nine Sacred Race Empyreans to send out a palm strike across the universe. However, he was ultimately forced back by Xing Wei!
"I have a bad memory." Mu Han shook his head and smiled.
"You are not my match." Xing Wei's expression was extremely serious.
Xing Wei was extremely confident in his own strength. Although he was Luo Xiao's last disciple and also the youngest, his talent was also the most outstanding. Naturally, he was deeply favored by Luo Xiao.
His relationship with Luo Xiao was also the deepest. Thus, he was arranged to stay in the Universe of Extremities and help his master overcome this crisis.
However, the flow of time in the Universe of Extremities was far too fast. After hiding here for so many years, his skeletal age had become extremely old. However, True Gods possessed an eternal lifespan, so the word 'old' wasn't suitable for Xing Wei. As long as he wanted, he could restore his youthful appearance with a single thought. However, he wanted to use his current appearance to remind himself of the time he had spent in the Universe of Extremities, so he didn't transform into his youthful appearance.
A True God of the Universe of Extremities had finally appeared, but Xing Wei said that this True God wasn't his match.
These words caused the Empyreans present to rejoice. If Xing Wei could kill this black-clothed youth, then the pressure on these Empyreans would be much less. And if Xing Wei could kill this black-clothed youth and still have the strength to help them, then their Universe of Extremities might be able to obtain a complete victory!
Mu Han's next words caused the hearts of the Empyreans present to plummet to the bottom of the abyss. They heard Mu Han openly admit, "Yes, I alone am not your match, but what about adding my senior-apprentice brother and junior-apprentice brother?"
If Mu Han was a True Divinity, then his senior-apprentice brother and junior-apprentice brother must also be True Divinities!
In other words, the Universe of Extremities had sent three True Divinities here!
Xing Wei's complexion darkened. "This doesn't conform to the rules of the Holy Hall. Mu Hai, you cannot do this!"
The Holy Hall had developed for countless divine epochs and had long since possessed a perfect set of rules. Even Saints couldn't easily surpass these rules.
For instance, a Saint couldn't directly attack another Saint's universe!
It was also because of this rule that Mu Hai couldn't personally step into the Universe of Extremities. Otherwise, with Mu Hai's strength, it would only take a short period of time for him to shatter this universe. Why would he spend so much time and effort to nurture the saints and place the ancient sorcerer race and monster night clan here?
Another rule was that if two universes fought each other, only a single True Divinity could be sent.
Luo Xiao had many disciples, but in the end he had only placed Xing Wei in the universe. This was because of this rule!
Mu Han let out a light breath. "The rules can be changed. Honorable Master spent a great deal of effort to change this rule. You can also ask for help. Isn't Luo Xiao's chief disciple, Yu Taibai, very strong?"
Xing Wei's gaze grew colder and colder. Changing the rules of the Holy Hall was definitely not an easy task. Since Mu Hai was willing to pay such a great price to change it, how could he allow Luo Xiao's disciples to casually step into the Universe of Extremities?
"I'll give you a chance to live. Get out of the Universe of Extremities. If you see me in the divine territory in the future, you'll have to take a detour," Mu Han continued with a smile. Although he admitted that he wasn't Xing Wei's match, he had his Honorable Master as his backing.
Now that Luo Xiao had basically fallen, Luo Xiao's disciples in the divine territory didn't have a good time. Naturally, he wouldn't be afraid of Xing Wei.
Xing Wei sneered. "None of Honorable Master's disciples will back down. Cut the crap."
"That's a pity. I can only reluctantly summon my fellow disciples to bestow death upon you …" Mu Han shook his head and said. With the passage of the Wall of Sighs unobstructed, a True Divinity could easily cross from one universe to another.
However, before Mu Han could finish his sentence, an extremely violent aura exploded from below. Following the explosion of the aura, a golden light shot toward Mu Han ferociously. At the same time, a loud roar exploded in the sky.
"You'll die first!"
Luo Zheng's entire body was shining with a golden light. Numerous Emperor Soul Seals appeared! Under the crazy burning of his soul, his Soul Power was released without reservation!
When the aura spread out, the Old Clan Leader, Heavenly Sovereign Original Sin, Heavenly Sovereign Oracle, and the others were shocked. As Heavenly Sovereigns, they also possessed Golden Fighting Souls. However, at that moment, when facing the Soul Power that was released by Luo Zheng's Soul Power, it created a huge pressure. Moreover, they were only affected by the aura that was released by Luo Zheng. The one who was facing Luo Zheng's Soul Power was the True Divinity!
"Luo Zheng, stop!"
Xing Wei was shocked. He didn't expect Luo Zheng to act so recklessly. It was too late for him to stop him.
Mu Han felt Luo Zheng's aura and was shocked. This brat was only a Sector Lord. How could the power of the burning of his soul make him feel pressured?
One had to know that a True Divinity's Soul Power had reached another level. That level was integrated with the Godhead. The True Divinity in the Soul Power had far surpassed that of a Golden Fighting Soul!
However, Mu Han was only admiring Luo Zheng's aura. As for Luo Zheng's rash charge toward him, his eyes were filled with disdain. He extended his hand and spread his five fingers toward Luo Zheng. "Secondary living beings are reckless. However, their courage is commendable …"
He looked down at Luo Zheng, who was rushing toward him. He wanted to crush Luo Zheng's sword with one hand before crushing him.

Chapter 1685
Luo Zheng's strike was filled with hatred.
Nearly a third of the energy in his inner world poured into his arm!
Although Luo Zheng's body was extremely sturdy, it was still unable to withstand such a powerful force. Whether it was his tendons, veins, or bones, they were all on the verge of collapse. However, he was too angry to care about that.
The other 70% of the power was frozen around the sword by Luo Zheng in the way of "Transcendence". Exerting the power in such a way would consume a considerable amount of energy, which could be considered a drawback of the "Transcendence Magical Power". Therefore, Luo Zheng also understood that if he wanted to exert the power source in his Inner World to the extreme, he could only strengthen his physical body.

Swish, swish, swish …

Ka ka …

Chapter 1686

The Heavenly Dao in the two great universes constantly shook …

In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Luo Zheng had chosen to cultivate the Great Wish Art!

Chapter 1687
Once the Great Wish Technique was used, it could greatly increase the power of the person who made the wish.
However, the wish had to be fulfilled. Before the wish was fulfilled, the cultivation of the person who made the wish wouldn't improve.
Generally speaking, people who weren't mortal enemies wouldn't make the Great Wish Technique. After Luo Zheng made the Great Wish Technique, he had to kill Mu Han!
Luo Zheng had cut off one of Mu Han's arms as soon as he arrived. This had already infuriated him. He didn't have the time to find trouble with this brat.
Now, he was going all out to deal with Xingwei. At the critical moment, this brat actually attacked again …
What made Mu Han even more speechless was that this brat actually knew how to use the Great Wish Technique!

This power didn't come from somewhere else. It was given to him by himself!
When he believed that he had this power, he really did have it … In the end, this was a kind of idealistic power!
However, this power could only be used on that mutated beast. If Luo Zheng gave up on killing that mutated beast and changed targets, he would instantly return to the real world and lose this idealistic power … When Luo Zheng killed that mutated beast and fulfilled the wish, this wonderful feeling also disappeared. The entire world returned to normal!
Now, Luo Zheng was in the same situation when he used the Great Wish Technique on Mu Han.
In his eyes, the entire world no longer existed. There was only Mu Han left.
"Hu …"
Luo Zheng's reaction speed also increased by several times...

All the distracting thoughts in Luo Zheng's mind dissipated. The Yi God's Sword was even purer and sharper!

"Ding ding ding ding …"

Two breaths …
Three breaths …
Mu Han originally planned to end Luo Zheng's life in one breath's time and then kill Xing Wei with all his strength.
However, Luo Zheng once again displayed strength that far exceeded Mu Han's estimation …

Because of Luo Zheng's torment, Xing Wei had won precious time.

The Five Ultimate Qi Seal was one of the most mysterious profound meanings of the Five Elements Divine Path. Although there were many True Gods who cultivated the Five Elements Divine Path, it was very rare for a True God to be able to use the Five Ultimate Qi Seal. This was because it was related to one's constitution.
After being trapped by the Five Ultimate Qi Seal, Xing Wei was anxious and hesitant. He wanted to break free.
However, he also understood that the outcome of a battle between True Gods was usually decided in one ten-thousandth of a breath's time. If he could break free from the Five Ultimate Qi Seal, it would be enough time for Mu Han to kill him ten times over!
At this critical moment, Luo Zheng unexpectedly interfered. He actually had a great ambition and even held Mu Han back …
Xing Wei was surprised and gratified. He seized the time to break the seal!
To mortals, ten breaths of time was only enough for them to drink a cup of water. However, to Xing Wei, it was enough to save his life!
With a flick of his hands, five rays of starlight were flicked out.
He wasn't proficient in the Five Elements Divine Path, but he had cultivated the Great Sealing Technique. The Five Ultimate Qi Seal was a seal that used the "five elements engender each other." If he wanted to break the Five Ultimate Qi Seal, he had to use the "five elements counter each other!"
The five rays of starlight flicked out from his hands were of different colors. They were the five stars of metal, wood, water, fire, and earth!
Mu Han's five eyeballs engendered each other, while Xing Wei's five stars countered each other. The five eyeballs revolved in the right direction, while the five stars revolved in the opposite direction …
"Ka ka ka ka …"

Metal engendered water, fire countered metal. That star collided with the golden eye!
Water countered wood, earth countered water. That star collided with the water eye …
Wood engendered fire, metal countered wood. That star collided with the wood eye …
…
…
Xing Wei was able to unravel the Five Extreme Qi Seal in an unusually smooth manner. It only took him nine breaths of time to unravel the Five Extreme Qi Seal. At this time, Mu Han was still controlling the small five-colored sword to fight with Luo Zheng!

After all, the Five Colored Divine Swords formed by the five-colored swords could be used to kill a True God!

Chapter 1689
Luo Zheng had used the "Gen" token to escape into the Immortal Mansion.

Even if Luo Zheng successfully used Greater Teleportation, he would only be able to move a distance of a thousand miles at most. Through this space, Luo Zheng could freely teleport to any Great Realm in the universe. However, using the teleportation token was slower than using greater teleportation. The third escape method was to use the "Gen" token. The "Gen" token was a projection of the Immortal Mansion's door. The moment it was activated, there would be no fluctuations of Space Law and he would be directly pulled into the Immortal Mansion.
Therefore, Luo Zheng chose to use the "Gen" token.

Chapter 1690
In the long years, Afo had always been in a sealed state.
His puppet body didn't decay, but the body had a real soul. Endless years meant endless loneliness.
But the time when Luo Zheng broke in was the happiest time of his life.
In these tens of years, Luo Zheng had entered the immortal residence a few times, and each time he had gained something. The further he walked on the path of true extinction, the stronger his control over the immortal residence became.
Later on, Luo Zheng settled his family in the residence. That little fellow Luo Nian brought him and the immortal in the book a lot of joy, and they loved him very much.
Afo never asked about the outside world, but that didn't mean that Afo didn't know about the Great Evolution Realm, and knew that Luo Zheng was facing a huge test.
But Afo was very confident in Luo Zheng. Being able to become master's disciple, how could he fail so easily?
Seeing Luo Zheng suddenly appear in the immortal residence, lying on the ground covered in blood, Afo's face was still filled with worry. Only after Afo checked carefully, did he know what was going on.
Luo Zheng's wounds looked scary, but they didn't hurt his vitals.
After reaching the sector lord realm, a fighter's only vitals were the soul and dantian. As long as these two weren't too damaged, they would heal quickly.
Plus, Luo Zheng's body was already different from normal people, and now he was quickly recovering!
The warm current in Luo Zheng's body was constantly flowing around the wounds all over his body. Some of the wounds were deeper and directly penetrated his internal organs. At this moment, they were wriggling at a visible speed. New granulation was rapidly growing out of the wounds, connecting them to each other.
Not long later, Luo Zheng slowly woke up. The moment he opened his eyes, a sharp light flashed, and he flipped over.
"Pu pu pu …"

If he couldn't defeat them, then Luo Zheng would have to go to the path of extinction.

It wasn't impossible for him to die if he wasn't careful.

The remaining three tokens were the most difficult to obtain in the immortal mansion!

"Glazed Thunder Pagoda!"
