Chapter 1691
As soon as Mu Jiu's voice fell, the Soul Searching Banner shot out beams of gray light.
As soon as these gray lights appeared, they brought a heavy pressure to the five Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region Empyreans!
After cultivating a Golden Battle Soul, the plasticity of one's soul would greatly increase. Ordinary methods would find it difficult to injure a Golden Battle Soul. Some cultivators who specialized in cultivating the soul would use their soul to defend against their enemies, and some would even mold their soul into a weapon!
It was precisely because of the strength of a Golden Battle Soul that Empyreans rarely used the power of their soul when they fought. This was because neither side could injure the other's soul.
However, the Soul Searching Banner was a grandmist artifact, and the person controlling it was a True Divinity. He could display the full potential of this grandmist artifact.
Mu Jiu's understanding of the soul was on a whole new level compared to the other Empyreans. Therefore, even though Thunder Punishment Empyrean and the others had Golden Battle Souls, it was still difficult for them to resist the gray lights of the Soul Searching Banner!
When this gray light shined onto Empyrean Thunder Punishment's body, he felt as if the heavens and earth were turning upside down in his mind. His soul involuntarily wanted to leave his mortal body. This gray light was actually trying to suck away his soul!
Empyrean Thunder Punishment was like this. The four Empyreans behind him, Wuxin, Wuyuan, Wugen, and Wuhui were also like this.
At this moment, the five Empyreans' faces were distorted. Sometimes their eyes rolled back, and sometimes their mouths were agape. All kinds of strange and terrifying expressions were constantly changing on their faces. This was because the souls in their minds were frantically resisting this gray light.
"Great Pagoda … Suppress my heart!"
As he continued to struggle, Empyrean Thunder Punishment vaguely spat out these words.
The Ten Thousand Buddhas Return to Origins Great Pagoda that the five of them had arranged was a sharp weapon that could attack and defend!
As his voice fell, the Ten Thousand Buddhas Return to Origins Great Pagoda behind them suddenly emitted a golden Buddhist light. At the same time, there were bursts of Buddhist hymns, as if the gods and Buddhas were chanting in a low voice.
This low Buddhist hymn spread over a considerable distance. It could be heard in dozens of neighboring great worlds.
And this hymn contained the demonic power of a divine artifact. Hearing it, one's mind would calm down, and one's entire being would become lazy and tranquil. Even their mind would become clearer!
As for the souls of the five Empyreans, they were constantly emitting this golden Buddhist light!
Under the illumination of this Buddhist light, it forcibly resisted the grey light. The eyes of the Thunder Punishment Exalted Celestial, Wuxin, Wuyuan, Wugen, and Wuhui quickly regained their clarity. They looked at the Soul Searching Banner with a trace of fear in their eyes.
Celestial Venerables were also living beings, and they were also afraid of death. This Soul Searching Banner was meant to directly take away their souls. If his soul was really taken into the banner, who knew what kind of inhumane treatment he would receive? It was just that most of the secret techniques of the Soul Dao were known for their cruelty. Presumably, his end would also be extremely miserable.
Seeing Empyrean Thunder Punishment use the Ten Thousand Buddhas Return to Origin Stupa to block his soul searching flag, Mu Jiu couldn't help but sigh. "What's the point? No matter how hard you struggle, the final result will never change …"
As soon as he finished speaking, he shook the Soul Searching Flag once more!
The flag on the Soul Searching Banner fluttered in the air, and rays of gray light shot out again!
His Soul Searching Banner had collected countless souls!
Ordinary living creatures had tens of millions of souls. There were more than a million souls at the Jade Battle Soul level. There were more than a hundred thousand souls at the Silver Battle Soul level. There were more than three thousand Golden Battle Souls and there were seven True God Souls that were of a higher level than the Golden Battle Soul!
After a soul was taken into the Soul Searching Banner, it was equivalent to stepping into a cruel hell. In the world of the Soul Searching Banner, countless Yin Souls fought and devoured each other. They constantly grew stronger, but in the end, they couldn't escape the fate of being enslaved.
As for Mu Jiu, he enslaved thousands of Gold Battle Souls and charged towards Empyrean Thunder Punishment Exalted Celestial and company!
These Gold Battle Souls came in different shapes and sizes. They were all living beings that had fallen in front of the Soul Searching Banner. Among them were human cultivators, and there were also various forms of mystical beasts. There were even two True Dragon Souls …
"Wu wu wu …"
These enslaved Gold Battle Souls all possessed great strength when they were alive. Now that they were charging over, the power they exuded was more than ten times stronger than before!
Seeing these demon-like Gold Battle Souls, the hearts of Empyrean Thunder Punishment Exalted Celestial and company sank …
Wuxin, Wuyuan, Wuren, and Wuhui's expressions were even more disheartened.
Before this, they still had a certain level of fantasy in their hearts. After all, Empyreans were able to compete with True Gods in many situations. Although the strength of a True God was terrifying, Empyreans relied on the destiny of a Saint to not be too weak.
But after seeing Mu Jiu use the Soul Searching Banner's techniques, they realized that the gap between them and a True God … was just too great.
In fact, these Empyreans could not be blamed for this!
The difference in strength between True Gods was like the difference between clouds and mud.
In the Divine Realm, there were a large number of True Gods. But those True Gods that were able to become disciples of Saints were all the most outstanding of True Gods.
Just like how Mu Jiu alone could deal with an ordinary lower True God, Mu Jiu did not place these several Empyreans in his eyes at all!
"I'll risk it all!"
Empyrean Thunder Punishment Exalted Celestial clenched his teeth. The dark red mole between his eyebrows emitted a blood red light!
"Zi zi zi …"
Wisps of blood-red lightning escaped from the red mole!
He was one of the four upper Empyreans in the Great Yan Universe. What his destiny carried was the strongest thunder profound that Luo Xiao cultivated – the Blood Refining True Thunder!
The blood within Empyrean Thunder Punishment Exalted Celestial began to rapidly boil. Under the crazy burning of his blood, he stimulated streaks of blood red lightning.
"Pipa, pipa …"
The streaks of lightning formed rings of lightning that spiraled towards those souls!
At the same time, Wuxin, Wuyuan, Wuren, and Wuhui, the four Empyreans, sat cross-legged in the air. They began to mutter incantations as they stimulated their own destinies to fight against those demon-like golden battle souls …
"Wu …"
"Ao …"
"Hou hou …"
All sorts of strange cries came from the skies.
Thunder-attribute cultivation methods were originally extremely effective against evil yin spirits. Although the souls that Mu Jiu stimulated were golden battle souls, they were still unable to withstand the power of the Blood Refining True Thunder.
Among the four upper Empyreans in the Great Yan Universe, Empyrean Oracle's strange self-destruction art was feared. The Old Patriarch was the strongest by borrowing the power of the universe. Empyrean Original Sin's cultivation was the deepest and most unfathomable. However, Empyrean Thunder Punishment's Blood Refining True Thunder was the sharpest. He once flew to the edge of the universe and relied on this Blood Refining True Thunder to melt a large hole in the Wall of Sighs!
The thickness of the Wall of Sighs was extremely exaggerated. If Empyrean Thunder Punishment Exalted Celestial wanted to break through the Wall of Sighs, he would need tens of thousands of years. He had no reason to do so. However, in this universe, the only Empyrean that could break through the Wall of Sighs was likely him …
"Pipa …"
A single Blood Refining True Thunder shot out, tearing apart dozens of souls.
However, there were simply too many golden battle souls surging towards him. He constantly burned his blood essence and stimulated the Blood Refining True Thunder, but the number of golden battle souls still increased …
"It really isn't worth it …"
Mu Jiu shook his head. Collecting so many golden battle souls wasn't an easy task. This Empyrean Thunder Punishment's Blood Refining True Thunder was truly unique. Even Mu Jiu didn't dare to test just how strong it was.
At this time, the soul searching flag in his hand swayed slightly, as if something was trying to break free from it.
Seeing this, the corners of his lips curled up into a smile. "Alright, you haven't come out for a long time. It's good to let you out for some fresh air!"
"Whoosh …"
With a slight tremble of the soul searching flag, a gray light floated out from the flag!
The speed of this gray light was incomparably fast. After rushing out, it drew a gray line in the air at an inconceivable speed. And this gray line was about ten billion miles long, the equivalent of crossing two great worlds.
Using greater teleportation to cross two great worlds wasn't difficult for an Empyrean. By using the Space Laws, they could compress a distance of ten billion miles into an extremely short period of time.
But this gray light didn't use the Space Laws. It completely relied on its own speed!
Although a soul body had no weight and its flying speed was naturally extremely fast, this gray light used two breaths of time to cross a distance of ten billion miles. This speed already surpassed the imagination of any living being in the Great Evolution Universe!
"I let you out for some fresh air, not for you to run so far. Come back here!" Mu Jiu coldly shouted as he held the soul searching flag.
So after two breaths of time, it circled back at an inconceivable speed. At the same time, it accurately passed between Empyrean Thoughtless's eyebrows …
Empyrean Thoughtless still sat cross-legged, floating in the air. But his mouth was wide open and the light in his eyes was gradually fading. The soul in his mind had been completely swallowed up. He maintained this posture and sank down!
Another violent tremor came from the universe. Empyrean Thoughtless had fallen.

Chapter 1692
That gray dot had been flying at an extremely high speed. Even after Heavenly Sovereign Wu Hui had fallen, no one had been able to see what it was!
In everyone's eyes, this gray dot was just a gray track.
However, after this gray dot killed Heavenly Sovereign Wu Hui, it circled around and landed on Mu Jiu's shoulder.
Only then did Heavenly Sovereign Thunder Punishment and the others see it clearly. It was a gray bat!
This bat was also a soul body. However, its soul was not a Golden Fighting Soul. Instead, it was the soul of a True God. This bat was famous in the Realm of the Gods. It was the fastest True Spirit – the Light-Grazing Bat.
Even when this bat had a body, its speed was shockingly fast. Now that it was only a soul body, its speed could only be faster. Therefore, it was a sacred object in the eyes of some evil cultivators who refined and drove away souls!
However, the number of True Spirits was extremely rare even in the Realm of the Gods. Wanting to capture this Light-Grazing Bat was extremely difficult. If an ordinary True God encountered a Light-Grazing Bat, forget about capturing it, they would be burning incense if they were not killed by this little thing.
This Light-Grazing Bat was naturally not captured by Mu Jiu. He did not have such ability. This Soul Searching Flag was originally a treasure that Mu Haiji had used before. It could be said that the Soul Searching Flag's power was not the flag itself, but the many souls imprisoned within it!
"Are you full?"
Mu Jiu stretched out his finger and gently stroked the little bat on his shoulder.
The little bat rubbed Mu Jiu's finger and made a "da da da" sound …
"Not full? Then let's continue. "
Mu Jiu had originally planned to collect more Golden Fighting Souls into the Soul Searching Flag. However, since he had invited the Light-Grazing Bat out, he would naturally let this little guy eat as much as he wanted.
As soon as his voice fell, the Light-Grazing Bat turned into a gray track and rushed out.
Heavenly Sovereign Thunder Punishment and the others were originally in a state of high alert. Seeing the Light-Grazing Bat rushing over, they could not react in time. They only saw a gray track flash across their field of vision and then it disappeared completely.
The several Empyreans looked left and right, trying to lock onto the Light-Grazing Bat. Empyrean Fateless lowered his head and finally found the Light-Grazing Bat's trail. "This one …" Empyrean Fateless had barely uttered these two words when the Light-Grazing Bat had already drilled into his body and exited through the top of his head.
Empyrean Thunder Punishment turned his head and saw the gray track shoot out from Empyrean Fateless' head. Empyrean Fateless' divine sense had already dissipated. His eyes began to dim and his entire body silently fell to the ground.
Fateless Sky Master's body was unharmed, but his soul had been devoured by the Light Sweeping Bat.
"Stay close to me!"
Empyrean Thunder Punishment shouted as he saw this. Then, he had Empyrean Heartless and Rootless approach him. The moment the three Empyreans gathered together, a red light flashed between Empyrean Thunder Punishment's eyebrows and a Blood Refining True Thunder spread out!
This Blood Refining True Thunder turned into a shield of thunder that spread outwards. Countless blood red arcs of thunder wove together, protecting the three Empyreans within.
This little bat was only the size of a palm. Generally speaking, creatures that relied on speed to win were often very fragile. Empyrean Thunder Punishment felt that this Light-Grazing Bat wouldn't be able to penetrate through the shield of thunder formed by the Blood Refining True Thunder!
Just as Empyrean Thunder Punishment had predicted, the Light-Grazing Bat soared into the skies. In several breaths of time, it reached the peak of the Great Evolution Space. Then, it turned back and swooped down towards Empyrean Thunder Punishment and the others.
As this little Light-Grazing Bat arrived below Empyrean Thunder Punishment's head, it suddenly changed directions and swept towards the other side. It really did fear the Blood Refining True Thunder!
This gray track kept spiraling towards the blood-red ball of lightning. There were a few times when it was extremely close to the lightning shield and even attracted a few Blood Refinement True Thunders, but it relied on its speed to quickly get rid of them.
"This thing doesn't dare to come in!"
"A single Blood Refining True Thunder is enough to erase this little thing!"
Empyrean Heartless and Empyrean Rootless said as they stared at the constantly circling gray track.
Empyrean Thunder Punishment remained silent. The Blood Refining True Thunder was the Mandate of Heaven that could only be stimulated by burning one's blood essence. Although he was an Empyrean and had a rich blood energy within his body, the amount of blood essence he possessed far surpassed that of an ordinary person. But, his blood essence wasn't infinite!
The Blood Refining True Thunder consumed an extreme amount of blood essence. In just several dozen breaths of time, over half of his blood essence had been burned away!
Mu Jiu hugged the soul searching flag and faintly looked at the three Empyreans within the shield of thunder. His face had a bored expression, as if his struggle was meaningless. Their guess was correct. The little bat relied on its extreme speed. A single Blood Refining True Thunder was enough to kill it. But, the problem was that there was no one in this universe capable of catching up to its speed.
Moreover, the Light-Grazing Bat could use the 'Soul Realm Light Refraction'. Under the protection of this Light Refraction, the Light-Grazing Bat could force its way in!
It was just that the Light-Grazing Bat loved to swallow souls that were struggling in pain. Once it was done tormenting them, it would naturally become serious.
Soon, Empyrean Thunder Punishment's blood essence was completely burned away …
Zi zi zi …
The Blood Refining True Thunder also began to dim.
In order to maintain the Blood Refining True Thunder, Empyrean Thunder Punishment began to burn his ordinary blood!
If a mortal lost a third of their blood, it would endanger their life.
For example, if an Empyrean powerhouse burned all of their blood in a short period of time, as long as they replenished it in time, it wouldn't be too much of a problem.
An Empyrean's blood was incomparably condensed. But, when used to maintain the Blood Refining True Thunder, it burned even faster than blood essence. If this continued, Empyrean Thunder Punishment would sooner or later burn away every last drop of blood in his body …
"I'll risk it all!"
When Empyrean Thunder Punishment said these words, he had already lost all confidence. His blood essence had already been burned away. He understood that he no longer had the qualifications to risk his life.
As the shield of thunder gradually dissipated, Mu Jiu gently waved his hand. A gray trajectory spiraled forwards, piercing through the heads of Empyrean Rootless and Empyrean Thunder Punishment. Two Empyreans had fallen … and Empyrean Thunder Punishment was the first upper Empyrean to fall!
As for Empyrean Thoughtless, he had used greater teleportation to escape at the last moment.
Unfortunately, he had only crossed the distance of a great world. As he prepared to use greater teleportation to escape again, the Light Sweeping Bat had already arrived and pierced through Empyrean Thoughtless' head once more.
Huu …
After a breath of time, the Light Sweeping Bat came to a stop on Mu Jiu's shoulder once more.
"Alright, there are still many gold war souls in the Great Yan Universe. Consider them your food." Mu Jiu lightly said as he grasped the Soul Searching Flag. Then, he turned his gaze towards the other Empyreans!
Da da da! The little bat cheered on Mu Jiu's shoulder.
As the two True Divinities joined the battle of the Empyreans, the situation suddenly reversed.
The Empyreans of the Great Yan Universe had previously held the absolute advantage, but now they were forced back again and again, with Empyreans constantly falling.
With Mu Jiu and Mu Han taking action, the situation of the saints was completely reversed. The entire Great Yan Universe began to fall into a precarious situation once more!
…
…
On the True Extinction Path …
Yin and yang energies constantly condensed into saint martial artists.
The saint martial artists on the True Extinction Path were very similar to the saint martial artists in the Great Yan Universe. In terms of appearance, there was almost no difference.
However, Luo Zheng could sense the fundamental difference between the two.
The saint martial artists in the Great Yan Universe were formed from Mu Haiji's True Yuan, while the true saint martial artists were formed from Yin and Yang energies.
Yin and Yang energies were formed from chaos. Then, who was the one who transformed chaos into Yin and Yang energies? The living beings in Luo Zheng's inner world were formed directly from chaos, so they didn't experience the process of Yin and Yang energies.
Now, the saint martial artists that Luo Zheng was challenging were almost all at the Sector Lord level.
"Kill!"
Luo Zheng didn't hold back in the slightest against the saint martial artists that were constantly attacking him.
Pu pu pu …
Most of the saint martial artists were torn apart alive by Luo Zheng, then disintegrated into Yin and Yang energies and dissipated on the True Extinction Path.
Luo Zheng had consumed 90% of the nine stars' power source, but from the moment Luo Zheng entered the immortal estate until now, he relied on chaos energy to continuously transform and increase it.
To slaughter these saint martial artists, 10% was enough!
After fighting for an hour, all the saint martial artists were finally wiped out by Luo Zheng.
"I passed?"
Luo Zheng wasn't in the mood to train himself on the True Extinction Path. He just wanted to quickly pass through and get the Qian token!
As the saint race sector lords disappeared, the yin and yang energies revolved once more, forming two huge black and white whirlpools on both sides of the path. These whirlpools continued to gather the yin and yang energies, finally forming two saint race sector lords in tattered robes.
"The final challenge is still two saint martial artists?" Luo Zheng was dazed. To Luo Zheng, this was a good thing.

Chapter 1693
The auras of these two saint World Lords were very strange. Their bodies showed signs of decay, but their life force was very strong, giving off a very contradictory feeling.
"Ancient Dao Enlightened Ones?"
Although Luo Zheng was in a hurry to pass through the True Path of Extinction, he knew that there were many things that couldn't be rushed, so he didn't rush forward.
"They are the first batch of martial artists to comprehend the Divine Dao." Afo said.
At the beginning of the Divine Realm, besides those famous ancient saints, there were also a large number of low level races.
The saint race was one of them!
The reason why the saint race was ahead of the other races was because of these ancient Dao Enlightened Ones.
It could be said that the Divine Realm's 3,000 Divine Daos system was created by the saint race.
When the Good Fortune Axe split open the Divine Realm, it created 3,000 Divine Daos, but these Divine Daos weren't directly passed down to the world. Instead, they were erected all over the Divine Realm in the form of Dao tablets!
Besides a few Divine Daos that were passed down by the ancient saints, the majority of the 3,000 Divine Daos were comprehended by the saint race. They were widely spread among the saint race, then slowly spread to other races, finally spreading to the entire Divine Realm.
For example, the Five Elements Divine Dao, Truth Divine Dao, Star Divine Dao …
With these Divine Daos being passed down, the latecomers had a way to peek into them. Naturally, it was much easier, but these pioneers had to put in a lot of effort!
The Divine Realm's "Dao Tablets" were not ordinary tablets of laws.
Luo Zheng had more or less come into contact with them in the Cloud Hall, True Dragon World, and the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
Normally, one only needed to comprehend the laws within to gradually control the power of laws within. The entire process didn't have any risks or costs.
But comprehending a Dao Tablet required one to undergo a harsh test. In the process of comprehension, one's body would be constantly worn down, their soul would be constantly destroyed, and even the inner world in their dantian would be thrown into chaos!
So every ancient Dao Enlightened Ones were pioneers of the Divine Realm. Moreover, these ancient Dao Enlightened Ones had a much deeper understanding of the Divine Dao than ordinary Dao Children!
Although these two saint race martial artists were only at the World Lord level and could be considered Dao Children after comprehending the Divine Dao, their abilities were much stronger than ordinary Dao Children.
"Master, isn't this too much?" Afo noticed that a few more wounds on Luo Zheng's body had opened up, and golden blood was flowing out from the wounds.
Previously, Luo Zheng's body's self-healing ability was extremely terrifying. However, after being tempered by the Craftsman Saint, his body had reached a new height. After such a sturdy body was damaged, its healing speed became much slower.
Naturally, Afo wanted Luo Zheng to retreat first and rest for a while before attempting the True Path of Extinction.
Although these two ancient Enlightened Ones were powerful, it would not be difficult for Luo Zheng to kill them with his current strength. Now that Luo Zheng was not in a good state, it was still too risky.
"No!"
Luo Zheng's face showed determination.
The Grand Evolution Universe was in imminent danger, and this matter was related to his father's fate. How could he slack off in the slightest?
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng's figure fluttered, and he raised the Great Thousand Sword above his head with both hands. Then, he slashed down at one of the ancient Dao Comprehenders.
"Yi Shen Sword!"
After comprehending the essence of the Sword of Beginning, Luo Zheng's casual slash contained deep meaning.
This slash was originally very easy to dodge. After all, the opponent could easily dodge it from the back, left, or right. However, this simple slash made it difficult to determine the direction of the slash.
Naturally, the ancient Dao Comprehender also noticed this. He found that no matter which direction he dodged in, he could not dodge it. After realizing this, the ancient Dao Comprehender revealed a relaxed smile on his face. Then, he stretched out a withered hand.
The thumb and middle finger of his hand were intertwined, making a strange gesture.
Although Luo Zheng noticed the strangeness, once the Yi Shen Sword was slashed out, there was no way out. His body was affected by the Emotion Severing Divine Path, so he did not hesitate to slash down!
The Great Thousand Sword turned into a sharp fan-shaped sword light in the air. It was mixed with sharp sword essence as it slashed down. When it was less than an inch away from the ancient Dao Comprehender, a crisp sound suddenly came from the ancient Dao Comprehender's fingers.
"Pa!"
The ancient Dao Comprehender actually snapped his fingers.
"Hmm?"
Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. A strange feeling pervaded his whole body. He felt that everything was moving back quickly!
The Great Thousand Sword in his hand was moving back, his body was moving back, and even his thoughts were moving back …
"Phew …"
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng returned to his original position. The Great Thousand Sword was also tightly held in his right hand. This was actually his state in the span of two breaths!
"This …"
Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of surprise.
Although he had returned to his original position, his memory still existed. It was just that the order of the memory was slightly disordered.
Just a moment ago, he seemed to have returned to the past.
"Sigh, this person comprehended the Time Divine Dao. He's the most troublesome person …" Afo sighed, feeling a little depressed. Gu Bei had already mastered the Yin and Yang Qi to the extreme. The living beings formed by the Yin and Yang Qi were almost exactly the same as they were when they were alive. However, the difficulty of the True Path of Extinction was still too high. It wouldn't be easy for Luo Zheng to completely pass through it!
In the universe, the Space Law and the Space Law were the two most heaven-defying laws. Although these two laws could be cultivated to the tenth level, the higher the level of the Space Law, the further the greater the distance of greater teleportation.
However, greater teleportation was the most important technique for warriors. Thus, everyone would cultivate it. After stepping into the Sector Lord realm, everyone would try to comprehend the perfect Space Law.
The Time Law was also extremely important. All Sector Lords and Celestial Venerables would choose to cultivate it. However, in ordinary battles, the Time Law was rarely used. This was because when you used the Time Law, your opponent would also use it. Moreover, the Time Law comprehended by Celestial Venerables and Sector Lords was not very different. Even if you cultivated it to the peak, you could still compress a portion of time.
This was because Saints had very strict control over time and space. Almost no Saints would pass down the ultimate understanding of these two great laws as the will of heaven, and Luo Xiao was no exception. These two great laws were too powerful, and ordinary living beings would mess up time and space in the universe after cultivating them.
However, the Divine Realm had no restrictions on the Time Law. Moreover, this ancient Dao Enlightened One relied on the Dao Tablet to comprehend the Time Law.
"Heh," seeing Luo Zheng's surprised face, the ancient Dao Enlightened One cracked a smile.
Perhaps because the Great Evolution Universe was invaded by those "fake Saints", Luo Zheng had the urge to rush up and crush them to death whenever he saw them. He couldn't tolerate this guy's mockery!
Huu ….
His figure shot forward, stirring up gusts of air. Luo Zheng's sword moved along with this momentum, slashing horizontally at the ancient Dao Enlightened One!
"Yi God's Sword!"
This time, Luo Zheng released a sword light ahead of time!
As the fan-shaped sword qi spread out, it formed a 100-meter-long crescent that swept out at an extremely fast speed.
This fellow was able to make Luo Zheng return to the past. Could he also make his sword go back to the past?
If so, then there was no need to rush through the Path of True Extinction. Luo Zheng could leave now ….
The ancient Dao Enlightened One didn't use the Divine Dao of Time again. Instead, he leapt up and dodged Luo Zheng's slash. But as he lightly tapped the air, he actually threw himself at Luo Zheng!
"You want to fight me head-on?" An imperceptible smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face. But not long after, that smile froze on his face.
Just as Luo Zheng leapt up to meet the attack, the ancient Dao Enlightened One's figure sank. Unexpectedly, he scuttled below Luo Zheng, avoiding a direct confrontation with Luo Zheng.
At the same time, a crisp "pa" sound rang out. Luo Zheng once again returned to the past, appearing at the position he was at two breaths ago. The ancient Dao Enlightened One had already calculated Luo Zheng's position. His hands formed fists and fiercely smashed Luo Zheng's body. At this time, Luo Zheng was still puzzled ….
Dong dong!
Following the two enormous forces, Luo Zheng was instantly sent flying dozens of feet ….
Fortunately, Luo Zheng's current body was no longer the same as before. Coupled with the protection of the Heart Connecting Robe, even a Heavenly Venerable would find it difficult to injure him.

Chapter 1694
These sacred race fighters were lifelike, no different from real people.
But they were ultimately formed by Gu Bei's yin and yang energies, and were created according to Gu Bei's ideas. They were just puppets, and definitely not real existences!
Luo Zheng had explored the true path of extinction several times, and already knew the ins and outs of this place.
On this Path of True Extinction, the only one who could be said to have independent thinking was probably that "Second Senior Brother".
Some of the sacred race fighters would take the initiative to attack, while others would just wait for Luo Zheng to come.
Just like now, Luo Zheng was facing these two ancient dao comprehenders, according to Gu Bei's arrangement, they wouldn't take the initiative to attack.
Luo Zheng challenged the ancient dao comprehender on the right, while the one on the left just stood there in a daze …
"Hu …"
With a gust of wind, the ancient dao comprehender pounced towards Luo Zheng again.
Even though he knew he couldn't dodge, Luo Zheng still forced himself to move to the other side …
"Pa!"
Following a finger snap, Luo Zheng was once again bound by time laws, and returned to two breaths of time.
"Peng!"
A heavy punch landed on Luo Zheng's stomach.
Ordinary Sector Lords would find it hard to face this ancient Dao Enlightened One. Even if they could withstand these punches, they would most likely lose half their life.
But to Luo Zheng, it wouldn't cause any harm, so he had plenty of chances to test it out.
"As expected, I can only go back to two breaths of time each time." Luo Zheng climbed up from the ground, feeling more and more certain.
This path of extinction was extremely difficult, but it definitely wasn't a path of extinction!
As Luo Zheng continued to experiment, the ancient dao comprehender used the same trick again and again. No matter how Luo Zheng dodged, or how to attack, it could always rewind Luo Zheng to two breaths of time, and the timing was extremely precise, not a bit more, not a bit less.
After taking a dozen punches, Luo Zheng became more and more confident.
"Master, are you okay?"
Afo's voice came from outside the path of extinction again …
"I'm fine." Luo Zheng's voice was full of strength.
He suddenly felt that this True Path of Despair was a game that Gu Bei was playing with him. This path not only tested his strength and perseverance, but also his intelligence.
"Phew …"
Luo Zheng watched as the Ancient Enlightened One lunged at him again, but this time, he suddenly swung his sword in front of him!
This sword was not aimed at the Ancient Enlightened One but at the void!
Fu, who was outside the True Impasse Path, was slightly taken aback when he saw this scene. He was momentarily unable to figure out the purpose of Luo Zheng's strike … However, Luo Zheng had suffered so many punches just now. He must have thought of something; otherwise, it was impossible for him to be so confident.
Luo Zheng didn't stop at all after thrusting his sword. With a stomp of his feet, he pounced towards the ancient Enlightened One.
He had grasped the timing quite accurately. This series of movements was not fast, but it happened to be stuck in the time of two breaths!
"Snap!"
As Luo Zheng expected, the Ancient Enlightened One snapped his fingers again. The invisible law of time was applied to Luo Zheng's body. At the same time, the Ancient Enlightened One rushed to the other side, where Luo Zheng was two breaths ago!
However, in the span of two breaths, Luo Zheng thrust his sword forward!
So when Luo Zheng "returned to the past", his sword accurately pierced through the Ancient Enlightened One's body …
"Pfft!"
Even though the Heavy Sword of the Great Thousand Swords did not have a sharp edge, it was still indestructible due to its immense mass. There was nothing it could not pierce through. This sword easily pierced through the physical body of the ancient Dao Enlightened One.
"Hiss …"
The Ancient Enlightened One's body began to disintegrate, turning into black and white yin and yang energy that merged into the two sides of the True Path of Despair.
"Clap clap clap …"
Afo clapped his hands outside the True Path of Despair. At this moment, Afo finally understood the purpose of Luo Zheng's sword.
If the law of time was used properly, it was extremely difficult to break.
In fact, there were many warriors who could compress time in the Divine Realm, but those who could reverse time were pitifully few. Even some powerful True Gods could only reverse time to a limited extent.
If this Ancient Dao Enlightened One wasn't created by Gu Bei and was a real Saint Race World Lord, Luo Zheng wouldn't be his opponent. An expert at this level could do many things in two breaths of time.
After killing the first Ancient Enlightened One, Luo Zheng's gaze focused on the one on the left.
The first Ancient Enlightened One used the Divine Dao of Time. Then what about the other?
Luo Zheng maintained a trace of vigilance as he walked towards the Ancient Enlightened One.
When he was relatively far away, the Ancient Enlightened One had no intention of attacking him. But when Luo Zheng slowly approached, his posture changed. He bowed slightly, already in a state of alert …
In order to pass through the True Path of Despair faster, Luo Zheng began to study the details of the True Path of Despair.
After noticing the subtle changes in the ancient Enlightened One, Luo Zheng stopped in his tracks and took a small step back.
The Ancient Enlightened One immediately returned to his previous standing posture, but his eyes were coldly staring at him.
After Luo Zheng took a step forward, the Ancient Enlightened One's body bowed slightly and became alert again, as if ready to attack at any moment.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng revealed a trace of a smile.
He took a step forward, then a step back. The Ancient Enlightened One was very cooperative, sometimes alert, sometimes relaxed. The only thing that did not change was that wooden face and cold gaze.
"Master, what are you doing?" Afo was also full of questions. Just now, Luo Zheng was in a hurry to pass through the True Path of Despair. Why was he not in a hurry now? Instead, he was toying with the Ancient Enlightened One …
"One hundred and twenty meters." Luo Zheng estimated the distance in his mind. If he maintained this distance, the Ancient Enlightened One would not take the initiative to attack him, but once he attacked, the Ancient Enlightened One would pursue him.
When martial artists fought, they always maintained vigilance against the opponent at all times, no matter if it was close at hand or far away. After all, once they fought, there were too many uncertainties.
However, this Ancient Enlightened One was like a puppet, without independent thought. Luo Zheng wanted to take advantage of this loophole.
Standing one hundred and twenty meters away, Luo Zheng took a deep breath and retreated a certain distance. Then, he began to sprint, the Great Thousand Heavy Sword spinning like a windmill in his hand.

But soon, Luo Zheng obtained an answer from Afo. In truth, the concept of Empyrean originated from the saint race. At that time, the saint race held an absolute leading position in the Realm of the Gods. The strength they comprehended from the Dao tablets was called the Mandate of Heaven. Moreover, at that time, every time the saint race comprehended a Dao tablet, they would seal it away, striving to become a unique existence!
Thus, once an ancient Dao Comprehender comprehended the strength of a Dao tablet, they would be called an Empyrean …
However, with the inexplicable disappearance of the Saint Race, the seals on the Dao tablets also lost their effectiveness. Afterwards, more and more martial artists comprehended the Divine Dao, and more and more life forms became True Divinities. The concepts of Empyreans and Mandate of Heaven also gradually disappeared.
It wasn't until the later generations cultivated to the Sage Realm that they created the universe that they transmitted their life's comprehensions to the world. Thus, "Celestial Lords" once again appeared in the Inner Worlds of the Great Sages.
The final challenge of this section of the True Extinction Path was a saint race ancient Empyrean. In truth, what he cultivated was the Divine Dao of Annihilation. Although he had completely stepped into the Divine Dao, he still hadn't condensed his godhood, thus he couldn't be considered a True Divinity.
The Annihilation Divine Dao was an extremely destructive power. However, Luo Zheng had the protection of the Concentric Garment and had just swallowed the Nine-Headed Snake Blood Elixir. Thus, he wasn't at a disadvantage in this confrontation. Luo Zheng was most likely not afraid of a head-on confrontation.
Moreover, Luo Zheng's nine star strength source had been restored by over half. As soon as he found a chance, he attacked and cut down this ancient saint Empyrean into yin and yang energies!
This time, after Luo Zheng passed through the True Extinction Path, his state of mind calmed down …
There were still two tokens left in the True Extinction Path, the Heaven Token and the Earth Token. The Earth Token was the key to opening the Cultivation Pagoda. It wasn't of much use to the current Luo Zheng. After all, distant water couldn't quench a present thirst. Luo Zheng didn't believe that he would be able to go out and slaughter everyone in the universe after entering the Cultivation Pagoda.
Now, he could only resign himself to fate.
A golden light fell from the sky. Luo Zheng casually waved his hand and caught the golden light in his palm. When he turned his hand over, Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly. Then, a light flashed from his eyes and a faint smile appeared on his face.
In the center of this token was a large "Heaven" character.
"Not bad, you finally got this token." On the side, Afo also showed a gratified expression.
Actually, Afo was still worried. What if Luo Zheng got the "Earth" Token …
After collecting all eight tokens, he would be able to completely control this Holy Realm Dweller's Mansion. Luo Zheng could even remove the enchantment protecting this Holy Realm Dweller's Mansion and step into the huge forest.
However, the last part of True Path of Extinction was definitely not easy to pass. The living creatures that guarded the last part of True Path of Extinction were comparable to Real God's abilities. With Luo Zheng's current abilities, it would be difficult for him to escape from Real God's hands, let alone kill a Real God.
Luo Zheng held the "Heaven" Token in his hand and followed the path back to the pavilion. Then, he walked along the side of the pavilion and went into the backyard of the Holy Realm Dweller's Mansion.
Compared to the magnificent main courtyard of the Holy Realm Dweller's Mansion, the backyard was much more dilapidated. There was only a row of broken fences in front of Luo Zheng …
But there were faint golden lights floating above the fences. Within these lights, there were small golden people holding long swords. Luo Zheng could feel a faint power from these small golden people.
Although the power was not strong, Luo Zheng did not doubt the power of the divine patterns at first glance. If he triggered these divine pattern restrictions, he would probably be killed instantly!
These divine pattern restrictions were not targeted at Luo Zheng. All the small golden people were facing the backyard. Obviously, they were guarding against something in the backyard.
Thinking of what his father had said to him, Luo Zheng's heart trembled slightly.
Actually, Luo Zheng had already made sufficient mental preparations for the True Extinction Path. Luo Xiao had told him that there were some extremely vicious criminals locked up in the dungeon in the backyard of Holy Realm Dweller's Mansion.
Although these guys were not as strong as Sages, they were still top-notch True Gods.
Luo Zheng could ask these guys for help if the Great Evolution Path was unable to defeat the Sacred Clan at the last step …
If Luo Zheng had reached this step, it meant that Luo Xiao was dead for sure. Because once these guys made a move, they would be violating the rules of the Holy Realm Dweller. Mu Hai would have a reason to act. It would not be difficult to crush the Great Evolution Path.
At that time, Luo Xiao did not know that the rules of the Holy Realm Dweller had changed, but now Luo Zheng knew. Therefore, Luo Zheng did not have any misgivings about this matter.
However, these guys locked up in the dungeon were not so easy to deal with. Just look at how Afo had been suffering in the Holy Realm Dweller's Mansion. However, Afo was still able to move about freely in the Holy Realm Dweller's Mansion and take care of it.
Those guys in the dungeon had truly been trapped here for countless years. Who knew what their state of mind had become? Perhaps they had all become lunatics and would tear him to pieces as soon as he opened the dungeon.
Everything had its risks. This time, Luo Zheng had no choice but to take this risk …
Standing in front of the dungeon, Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment before activating the "Qian" Token in his hand.
"Creak!"
A section of the fence in front of the backyard rotated, and the simple fence was opened.
The moment the fence was opened, an ear-piercing roar was heard. At the same time, a heart-palpitating aura was transmitted from the backyard!
This aura was extremely vicious, but it was unknown what kind of creature had emitted it.
Luo Zheng had sensed this aura in the Holy Realm Dweller's Mansion a long time ago. At that time, Afo had told Luo Zheng that it was a dog raised in the Holy Realm Dweller's Mansion …

Chapter 1699
This huge creature didn't look like a dog, but more like a huge lion with long fur.
Every strand of hair was flickering with a black light, looking as beautiful as satin.
"Woof …"
Just as Luo Zheng stepped into the backyard, the big dog in the kennel suddenly stood up and charged straight at Luo Zheng!
Its two feet dug into the ground, instantly creating two half-foot deep grooves.
When the dog's barking reached Luo Zheng's ears, Luo Zheng's body suddenly shook violently. He seemed to have a natural fear of the dog's barking. His whole body stood there like a block of wood, unable to move or think.
Luo Zheng had already cultivated his heart of martial arts to perfection in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. His state of mind was very difficult to shake. Even when facing a True God, he didn't shrink back in the slightest!
But the fear in front of him was something Luo Zheng couldn't resist. This big dog seemed to have an innate talent in this aspect. Any living creature that faced its barking would involuntarily feel extreme fear, even Luo Zheng was no exception.
At this moment, Luo Zheng thought he was dead for sure.
"Ding ding dang dang …"
The moment the big dog rushed out, it pulled on a shiny silver chain. It was unknown what this chain was made of, but since it was used to lock the big dog, it was extremely sturdy.
When the big dog rushed in front of Luo Zheng, the shiny silver chain was stretched taut. At this moment, the dog was less than a foot away from Luo Zheng!
"Woof woof woof woof woof …"
The big black dog stuck out its scarlet tongue, which had barbs growing on it, and rolled towards Luo Zheng. If one was rolled by this tongue, one would probably suffer terrible injuries. But in the end, it was only one or two inches away from Luo Zheng!
A hot breath with a fishy and sweet smell came out of the big dog's mouth, causing Luo Zheng to feel dizzy. Although he wanted to escape with all his strength, the dog's barking made him completely unable to move …
"Big Black! Get back! "
Afo, who had rushed over, shouted coldly.
When the big black dog saw Afo, its originally violent and manic aura immediately dissipated. It even let out a whimper as it walked back into the doghouse.
After the big black dog entered the dog house, Luo Zheng began to pant. His whole body was soaked in sweat.
It had been a long time since he had experienced such fear.
The strength of this big black dog was too terrifying. With Luo Zheng's state of mind, even with his Gold Fighting Soul, he was still frightened to this extent. If it was a slightly weaker warrior, he would have died from this big black dog's roar!
"This is a Soul Shocking Mastiff, a True Spirit that master once subdued. Later on, it has been guarding the Immortal Mansion. This Soul Shocking Mastiff's talent is" Shocking ". Under its roar, all evil spirits will be annihilated. Even a True God will fall into extreme fear," Afo said.
Hearing this explanation, Luo Zheng nodded with lingering fear. This big black dog's talent was indeed powerful. Under the dog's barking, he had no room to resist at all.
"Guard the house? This dog is chained up, how can it guard the house? "Luo Zheng asked.
"Hey," Afo smiled faintly, "The Soul Shocking Mastiff guards things outside the boundary. If this boundary is opened and something enters the Immortal Mansion, the Immortal Essence Chain will naturally break and release Big Black …"
Luo Zheng suddenly understood.
This Immortal Mansion itself was not located in the Great Yan Universe, but in this strange forest.
People like Luo Zheng who came from the Great Yan Universe didn't need to be on guard. Even if a Celestial Venerable broke into the Immortal Mansion, he wouldn't have the right to casually barge in, because even a Celestial Venerable wouldn't be able to open any of the restrictions in the Immortal Mansion!
This forest should be located in a special place in the Divine Realm. If the boundary was really broken, the Immortal Mansion naturally had a way to deal with it, and this Soul Shocking Mastiff was one of them.
Although "Big Black" had returned to the dog house after Afo's scolding, Luo Zheng still didn't dare to go over. If the dog pounced over again, Luo Zheng might be able to block it with his current strength, but the dog's barking could almost make Luo Zheng lose his ability to move.
"Don't be afraid, Big Black is actually very docile. It won't attack you," Afo comforted when he saw Luo Zheng standing still.
Luo Zheng nodded, but he still stared at the big dog with a face full of vigilance, and carefully walked over.
Just like what Fu had said, Dahei was very docile under Fu's lead. The burly lion-like body even rolled around on the ground and kept making friendly noises towards Luo Zheng. "Whimper, whimper".
"He's the new master of our Immortal Mansion, remember him!" Afo pointed at Luo Zheng and said.
Although Big Black had terrifying strength, the intelligence of many creatures was not proportional to their strength. This Big Black's intelligence was obviously not high, but it still understood Afo's words.
It rolled all the way from the dog house to Luo Zheng, and its pillar-like limbs kept waving to the sky, acting coquettishly to Luo Zheng …
"This …"
Luo Zheng was rendered speechless by the ferocious fellow who was behaving in such a manner in front of him.
Afo said with a smile, "It means that you can touch it."
Looking at Big Black's shiny black fur, Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment. Then, he reached out and stroked Big Black's body. He felt as if his hand was brushing across satin, giving him a cold and smooth feeling …
"Woo …"
Big Black's head was on the ground, and it let out a soft cry, as if it was responding to Luo Zheng's touch.
"Hey, you're finally here …"
A faint voice suddenly came from the depths of the backyard …
The entire backyard was immersed in darkness, so Luo Zheng didn't pay attention to it just now.
Hearing this voice, he looked up and saw a long-haired man sitting not far in front of him. This man sat on the ground with his upper body naked, and his hands and feet were wrapped in shining silver chains. These chains were the Immortal Essence Chains that Fu had mentioned!
Behind the long-haired man, there was a narrow entrance. This entrance must be the prison of Immortal Mansion.
After seeing the long-haired man, Luo Zheng took a breath and walked towards him.
His father told Luo Zheng that the martial artists imprisoned in Immortal Mansion's prison were all extremely vicious in the Realm of the Gods, and their only desire was freedom.
If Luo Zheng wanted them to act, he could only exchange their freedom for them.
But these people might not keep their promises, and if he casually released them, he might suffer a backlash from them. So everything depended on Luo Zheng.
This suggestion was only because Luo Xiao had no other choice. As long as the Realm of Expansion didn't reach a point of no return, he didn't recommend Luo Zheng to do this.
Luo Zheng understood that there seemed to be no one left to rely on in the Realm of Expansion. The three True Gods sent by the Sacred Clan had already formed an absolute suppression, and he had no other choice. Moreover, he even knew the new rules of the Hall of Saints, so having no other choice became the best choice.
Everything was up to these fellows!
Luo Zheng adjusted his state of mind, and with a smile on his face, he walked in front of the long-haired man. "Senior, I am the master of this Immortal Mansion …"
"I know." Without waiting for Luo Zheng to finish, the long-haired man waved his hand and interrupted him. "When you barged into this Immortal Mansion when you were still in the Xiantian stage, I already knew …"
"But the speed of your progress is really surprising." The long-haired man looked at Luo Zheng with a trace of amazement in his eyes.
Kubei had promised these people!
If someone succeeded in inheriting this Immortal Mansion, they would release all the people in the prison.
These people had been waiting for this day for who knew how many billions of years!
The long-haired man's expression was admittedly calm, but in his heart, he was extremely excited. They had been imprisoned for so long, and it was finally time for them to see the light of day again!
"Hmph, what's there to be surprised about? This brat also made us wait for almost twenty years!" A hoarse voice came from inside the prison.
From the first time Luo Zheng stepped into Immortal Mansion until now, it had been almost twenty years.
On the side, Afo said disapprovingly, "A few years ago, who said that it would take over a thousand years for Luo Zheng to take control of Immortal Mansion?"

Chapter 1700
Hearing the conversation between Afo and this group of people, Luo Zheng could guess their intentions.
These people were indeed in a hurry to leave this place. This was also the only bargaining chip Luo Zheng had with them.
"I'll let you go," Luo Zheng suddenly said.
When Luo Zheng said this, the long-haired man's eyes flashed with a blazing light, and the corners of his eyes twitched violently.
It was obvious that this long-haired man was extremely excited, but he was trying his best to control his emotions.
"Swoosh …"
From the entrance of the dungeon, several fierce auras suddenly erupted. Those auras were full of excitement …
"Speak, what do you want?"
"I can personally teach you the Path of Extreme Evil!"
"Hmph, I can help you cultivate the Devouring Sky Technique. This Devouring Sky Divine Path is much better than the Path of Extreme Evil …"
"Big mouth, what do you mean? Do you want to compete with us? "
"Hahaha, Old Man of Extreme Evil, you were no match for me back then. If we leave this place, you'll still be no match for me!"
The two people in the dungeon actually started to argue. It seemed like they wanted to see who was better.
After arguing for a while, Luo Zheng was ignored. After waiting for a while, Luo Zheng interjected, "I only have one request for you to be released."
This time, no one said anything.
Luo Zheng thought for a while. "Help me kill three True Gods."
In the silence, the old voice sounded again. "There's a huge difference in the strength of a True God. Is it lower, middle, or upper? Or peak? "
"It's three lower True Gods," Luo Zheng replied.
"Merely three Lower True Gods, and you can kill them with a wave of your hand … What are their names?" the old voice asked.
"Mu Han, Mu Jiu, Mu Tian," Luo Zheng said the names of the three True Gods.
"Nameless juniors that I've never heard of. Tsk tsk, this is an easy task," the voice in the dungeon said again.
These people had been imprisoned here for many years. They did not know some of the new True Gods in the Divine Territory.
"Wait, why are they all surnamed Mu?" Another person asked, "Could these three be related to Mu Haiji?"
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.
The long-haired man frowned. "Are these three Mu Haiji's disciples?"
Luo Zheng nodded. "Yes!"
Thus, everyone remained silent once more …
These people belonged to the type of True Gods who were extremely vicious and evil in the Divine Territory. They were also extremely powerful, but that didn't mean that they really had no scruples.
Back then, they had offended Gu Bei, which was why they were locked up in this dungeon. After spending countless years in this dungeon without seeing the light of day, they were afraid of being locked up!
Gu Bei was considered one of the more benevolent Sages. Some Sages would not imprison them, and would usually kill them directly!
Now that Luo Zheng had ordered them to deal with three of Mu Haiji's disciples, they would definitely suffer Mu Haiji's vengeance. Even if they returned to God's Domain, they would most likely be hunted down by the All Saints. Mu Haiji was definitely an existence they couldn't afford to offend.
"Change your request, kid! Everything else, everything you want, everything you want, I'll give it all to you! "
"Yes, come cultivate my Path of Extreme Evil and become my disciple. I'll definitely make you famous in God's Domain!"
"Hmph, the Devouring Sky Technique is your first choice."
"My Path of Extreme Evil …"
The two people in the dungeon actually wanted to take him in as a disciple.
From their point of view, although Luo Zheng controlled Gu Bei's immortal residence, he was still a Realm Lord. With their strength, taking Luo Zheng in as a disciple was more than enough.
Seeing the Extreme Evil Old Man and the "Big Mouth Monster" arguing in the dungeon, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a fierce light. He shouted, "Stop arguing."
Although Luo Zheng controlled the freedom of these people, this group of people obviously did not put Luo Zheng in their eyes. Luo Zheng suddenly shouted. Whether it was the people in the dungeon or the long-haired man in the dungeon, they were all stunned.
"My master is the North Sage. Do you all think you're stronger than the North Sage?" Luo Zheng said coldly.
"North Sage? Gu Bei took in another disciple? "
"Didn't he say he didn't take in any disciples!"
"Kid, where did you come from? You actually managed to become Gu Bei's disciple!"
This group of people was extremely fearful of Gu Bei, but at the same time, they worshipped him. They didn't think that Luo Zheng would be taken in as a disciple by Gu Bei.
Luo Zheng didn't want to use Gu Bei's identity to suppress them, but there was no use in dealing with this group of villains. Revealing his identity now was also a form of demonstration.
"I said, help me kill those three True Gods, and I'll immediately release you. There's no need to talk about other conditions. I don't need you to give me anything," Luo Zheng said.
After a while, the old voice in the dungeon transmitted, "Since it's like this, this old man can only reluctantly take action!"
Although Mu Haiji wasn't someone to be trifled with, it was still better than being locked up here forever. Their lifespan was unlimited, and they could live for a long time, but such a boring life was worse than death!
However, although these people agreed, Luo Zheng didn't know what they were thinking. Once they were released, Luo Zheng would lose control.
"Before I release you, swear on your Inner Demons that you will listen to my orders for the rest of your life. If you disobey, you will die from the backlash of your Inner Demons!" Luo Zheng said again at this moment.
Hearing this, the long-haired man frowned slightly, and a dissatisfied voice transmitted from the dungeon again.
The restriction of the Inner Demons was very strange. The higher the cultivation, the stronger the restriction. Many low level warriors swore on their Inner Demons, but it had almost no effect. However, after cultivating to the Life and Death Realm, the influence of the state of mind on the warrior would become greater and greater. Just like the Great Wish Technique, it was also a kind of Inner Demon Oath.
"Isn't your request a bit too much?" A faint voice transmitted from the dungeon again. Luo Zheng had never heard this voice before, and he didn't know how many people were locked up in the dungeon.
"Yes, we can swear on our Inner Demons to help you kill Mu Tian, Mu Jiu, and Mu Han, but we don't need to listen to your orders!" The old voice immediately said.
Listening to Luo Zheng's orders was equivalent to following Luo Zheng's orders. These people were well-known characters in the Realm of the Gods, so how could they be willing to be restricted like this? Moreover, the other party was a youth with a skeletal age of less than a hundred years!
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly, "Then there's no other way. Since everyone can't come to an agreement, there's no need to continue talking!"
After saying this, Luo Zheng turned around and left.
He was very clear about his bargaining chips, and these people were almost unable to refuse his conditions.
Moreover, if these people weren't willing to swear on their Inner Demons, even if they killed Mu Hai, Mu Jiu, and Mu Tian, they would still ultimately devour him!
Seeing Luo Zheng quickly walk away, the long-haired man suddenly stood up, "I, Zhan Beihai, swear on my Inner Demons that I will listen to your orders for the rest of my life. If I violate them, I will die from the backlash of my Inner Demons!"
After the long-haired man said this, Luo Zheng's footsteps suddenly stopped. He then turned his head and smiled at the long-haired man, "I'll let you go for now."
After saying this, Luo Zheng walked towards the long-haired man and activated the "Qian" Token in his hand. Soon after, a faint gray light shot out from the token!
"Whoosh!"
When the Immortal Essence Chain came into contact with the gray light, the entire chain's buckle cracked layer by layer and disappeared from his hand.
"Senior Zhan, since you've sworn on your Inner Demons, then follow me," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
The moment the Immortal Essence Chain broke, the long-haired man suddenly moved his muscles and bones. Soon after, he rushed out with a "whoosh" and actually started running wildly in the backyard. His face revealed a deranged expression!
"Hahahaha, I'm finally free! You've locked me up for so many years! " Zhan Beihai's face was full of ecstasy and excitement. As he ran, the bones all over his body crackled, and he looked very satisfied.
As for the old guys in the dungeon, when they heard this, they could only be envious and jealous. They didn't want to miss this opportunity, but they also didn't want to swear such a serious Inner Demons oath!
